💜pg 1💜

Yarinyace Yar kimanin 14
Years atsaye a kichin tana aiki idan kaganta baza kai tunanin ita kewannan aikinba ynd kmshi k tashi akichin dn dole ya haddasa mk ynwr bazata da kwadayin son dandana abnda ake sarrafawa agrn tarufe miyar dagarjajjiya hantar tare da saukewa don tagama soyuwa takashe gas tagoge ko ina  kmr baayi komadaeba tadauki kyn da tabata tana kokarin wankewa taji momy na kwala mata kira d sunanda take kiranta dashi wato kee jikinta har rawa yake tamike hrda tuntubenta Allah yasa bats fadiba ta isa falo tatsugunna murya na rawa tace ga gani momy 
Wata matace Yar kimanin 45 years ta hakimce cikin 1 daga ckn luntsuma2n kujerunda sukayiwa falon kawanya taci ado wanda ya boye shekarunta gefanta           yarinyace bazata wuce 13 years ba ta juyo tana hararar SUHAILAT tsawon minti 2 kfn ta fara mgn ckn fada sbd kin rainani ina kiranki amma saida kk gadama kfn kk zoko? takarasa tana me zare mata ido ita dai tnd tashigo falon bata kara koda daga kntaba ballema taga abinda take mata
Ai bata zataba sai saukar rankwashi taji a tsakiyr knta hkn yasa ta zabura da Sauri tare da dafe grin wani irn radadi takeji agurin momy tace tashi kibani guri banza mayya kawai kuma kije maza ki yiwa isah farfe sun kifi don      yana hanya ta tashi tana goge kwallar idonta jiki asanyaye tafara takawa kubra tafincikota tai baya zata fadi sai tai saurin rike hannun kujera tana kallon yarinyar wacce take mata dry haushi yakamata tasan d aunty yasmeen tana guri n ba ta isa tai mata hknba tajuya da sauri akokarinta nason tabr falon taji momy  na fdn to knji me kubran keso kiyi matane zaki tafi? Cak ta tsaya ba tareda juyowaba cikin shagwaba kubra ke cewa ni Momy bazanci abnciba wainar fulawa zanci momy tace karki damu auta ynzu zatayi miki dai-dai lokacin yasmeen tashigo falon hannunta dauke da takardu dama tn daki karatun jarabawa take wacce zasu fara, da sallama ta shigo tanemi kujera ta zauna momy ta kalleta sannu ya karatun tai krmn tsaki wlh ngj shi yasama nafito in huta momy tace daman ai ke kike tkr kanki kfn lokacin tuni suhailat ta  fita ta fara aikinda aka sata yasmeen tace ai dole inyi krt in bn son insamu matsala tamike tsaye bari inga mezan samu akichin ta fada tn brn falon momy tabita da kallo sarai tasan zuwa zatai taya mayyar yarinyar can aiki ta hanata amma taki hanuwa tajaa tsaki tare da shingida kan kujerar da take zaune.
Koda yasmeen tashiga kichin ta sameta taci rabin aikin tace sannu agogo srkn aiki Suhailat tace aunty ke nan kullum sai kin tsokaneni zaki ji dadi? To inban tsokanekiba wa kk so ntsokana tamayr mata tana kokarin karbar wukar hannunta kawo kigani yi wani abun lah aunty kibarshi ki huta nasn yau kn gaji da krt a'a kibani dai ta mikamata suka cigaba da aikinsu Suna hira abinsu ko ba komai zata rage zmn kadaici don agidan Indai ba aunty yasmeen ba babu mai hira da,ita sai y sadiq shi kuma ba mazauni bane.

Momy yaranta 4, 2maza 2mata y sadiq ne babba yana da she kara 28 sai aunty yasmeen me shekara 24 sai isah dayakeda 20 kubra auta13
Momy macece me bakin kishi hakan yasa tatsani marainiyar Allah suhailat wacce mahaifiyar ta tarasu tabrta tun tana da shekara 2 a duniya, da farko ba'a hannun momy take ba tana grn kakarsu tun byn rasuwartane ta dawo gidan babanta tana da 10  anan ne tahadu da masifar momy take azbtar da ita ada bata brn tayi girki sai dai duk aikin gdn itace daga baya talura yarinyar ta iya komai domin kakarta ta koya mata tun sannan tahutar da kaltume me aikinsu yazamana ita keyin komai agidan har itama kaltumen   take mata abinda taga dama kuma momy bata hanaba sai ita idan tanuna    bataji dadiba momy takaramata da duka .
Yaran momy kuwa daga y sadiq sai aunty yasmeen su kadaine suke sonta suna tausayamata kuma suna fadawa momy gsky amma sai dai tarufesu da fada kubra kuwa ta raina suhailat rashinkunya sosai take mata duk da ta bata she kara 1 kuma ba ruwan momy saima goyon bayanta da takeyi 
TO MASU KARTU KUBIYONI YANZUAKAFARA

💜Pg 2💜

Ana kiraye2n sallar magrib suka kammala komai suka gyara kichin din tsaf sannan kowa tanufi dakinta don gabatar da sallah 
Saida tafarayin wanka kfn ta dauro alwala ta gabatar da sallarta ckn nutsuwa tai addu,a sannan ta mike tare da dauko doguwar Riga ckn jakar kayanta tasa a ckn irin kayanda aunty yasmeen take bata dukda rigar taimata yawa hkn be hana tai mata kyau ba tasa karamin hijabi tafito ckn sauri take tfy saboda bata son momy ta fito falo batare da takawo musu abinciba
Doka ce a kanta kullum ita zatai girki sannan ita zata kawo musu har falo idan angama ta kwashe kayan shi yasama take sauri don tn gudun masifar momy
Tana isa kichin din gabanta ya fadi sakamakon ganin kaltume Yar aikinsu wacce tafi Yar gida don ita ynx ba aikin da take agidan sai dai momy ta aike ta amma babu wani aiki da tk yi, ganin ta tayi aciki ta zuba abinci tana ci, Jikinta a sanyaye ta karasa  takalleta kfn tace haba kaltume yazaki zo ki zuba abinci kina ci anan ko so kk momy tace nice nadiba tmn fada kaltume tahadiye lomar bakinta kfn ta ce ai ko batace ke bace ni zan  fada mata naganki kina ci suhailat ta ce a Allah yana gani kuma shi zai Sak... Kafin ta karasa taji saukr naushi abakinta Wanda hakan shi yai sanadiyyar fashe war bakinta nan jini yfr yoyo hanci d bakinta tai kasa tana kuka dai-dai lokacin y sadiq yashigo jin hayaniyar da kaltume keyi ne yasa shi karasowa kichin din,
Kaltume na ganinsa ta rikice tana Neman hanyar guduwa sbd tsoransa take ji dakatar da ita yai ta hanyar daga mata hannu ta tsaya tana rawar jiki
Yakaraso inda SUHAILAT   ke tsugunne dafe da bakinta Wanda har y fr kumbura ya tasheta tsaye ganin irin abinda kaltume tai mata ransa yabaci ya juyo ahasale yawanka mata mari hannu 2 jikake tas2 karar Marin ne yasa aunty yasmeen d take kokarin shiga kichin din karsowa aguje a tunaninta momy ce ke dukan SUHAILAT tana shiga ta fara raba ido nan taga yanda hanci d bakin SUHAILAT suna zubar jini da sauri ta karasa gareta tana fdn y sadiq me yfr ya juyo yana nuna mata inda kaltume ketsaye dafe da kunci sai alokacinma yasmeen taganta don hankalinta nakan Yar kanwarta 
Kfn tai magana kaltume tai saurin ficewa akichin dn sbd tasan zata iya kara mata daman ba shiri sukeba duk saboda SUHAILAT
Kwafa aunty yasmeen tayi tare da kama hannun SUHAILAT tace zo ki wanke bakinki sadiq yace wai ina momy ne nashiga bangantaba yafada yana kokarin fita yasmeen tace aisun tafi dauko isah ksn zaizo Hutu dn tabe baki yai batareda yai magn ba ya fice suma fita sukai 
Hardaki aunty yasmeen takaita tashiga bath room tawanke bakinta sannan ta dauro alwala ta nafitowa aunty yasmeen tashigo dkn hannunta dauke da magani da ruwa ta mika mata karba tayi tana yamutsa fuska Sam batason shan magani ganin yanda aunty yasmeen ta tsareta da ido yasa tasha tankokarin kwanciya kan Yar katifarta aunty yasmeen ta CE a'a tashi kiyi sallar isha,i inkinci abinci sai ki
 kwanta ba musu ta tashi gabanta yana faduwa sbd tuno abinda ya faru 
Saida aunty yasmeen ta ga tafara sallah sannan tafita falo tanufa tana zuwa taga sundawo dn murmshi tai kfn ta CE mutanen turai kenan tafada tana me kallon isah wanda rayuwa da yanayin cimaka tasa yazama kato kmr ba yanzu ne yake shiga 20 year's ba ya dago yana Sosa gashin kansa  Wanda rabine an aske Rabin km an rinashi yazama brown yace yadai sis kuna lpy mamaki ne ya hana yasmeen magana ganin irin yanda kaninta yakoma sai ka rantse shine babba 
Momy ta katse mata tunani ta hanyar fdn ina mayyar  yarinyar can tashige daki tabar kaltume da aiki byn tagama yimata rashin kunyako? Amatukar fusace aunty yasmeen ta dago wato kaltume tazo taiwa SUHAILAT sharri ko momy bata baata damar maganaba tacewa kaltume jeki maza ki kiramin ita.
TO READERS KUBIYONI DON JIN YANDA ZATA KAYA UMMU MASH KUR CE

💜 Pg 3 💜

Momy bata bawa yasmeen damar magana ba tacewa kaltume jeki maza ki kira ita ,
 
Ckn sauri kaltume tanufi dakin SUHAILAT cike da frn cikin momy zata rama mata abinda y sadiq yai mata don ba karya Marin ya shigeta don har ynz da shatin hannunsa akuncinta

Dakarfi ta tura kofar SUHAILAT dake  kwance zazzabi ya rufeta sakamakon kunbura da bakinta yai kanta ne kemata mugun ciwo ta zabura da sauri atunaninta momy ce, nan take jikinta y fra kyarma ta zubawa kofar dakin ido tana jiran ganin me shigowa kaltume ta fado dakin kamar wacce aka turo tana mata wani banzan kallo kfn ta CE ai saiki tashi hjy tn kiranki banza kawai yau zaki gane ban- bancin da ke tsakaninmu,
fargabar kiran da akace momy ta namata shi ya hanata jin abinda kaltume ta ke fada, ta Mike dasauri jknta na rawa tabi byan kaltume datake mata dry tana shiga ta tsugunna agaban momy ganin bakinta akumbure bai sa momy taji tausayinta ba kfn tai magana taji saukar rankwashi Wanda yake barazanar tarwatsa mata kwa-kwalwa sbd tsananin ciwo da kanta yake mata aibata San time dn da takife akasaba kukanma yaki zuwa sbd azaba aunty yasmeen ta runtse idonta cike da tausayi d takaicin abinda momy ta aikata ga marainiyar Allah,idonta yakawo kwallah tadubi momy
 Ta ce haba momy wai me yasa kk tsani yarinyar nan baki tanbayi ba'asiba kawai ki fara dukanta?koda bata da laifi amma agurinki itace me laifi bakya bincike?,momy ta juyo tana kallon yasmeen, a fusace ta ce na daketa dn in kuma zaki rama mata ne to bismillah yasmeen tace a,a momy bance zan rama mataba Amman wlh duk ranar da makirar matar nan takara sawa kk daketa aknta wlh ranta sai ya baci takarsa fada tana antayawa kaltume harara dai-dai lokacin y sadiq ya shigo yana tanbayar abinda yake faruwa ganinsu duk afusace ga kuma SUHAILAT tunda ta kife akasa ta kasa tashi sbd jiri2 take gani aunty yasmeen ta ce y sadiq ba Kaine kasamu matarnan tana dukan SUHAILAT ba harma ta fasa mata baki? gyada kai yai alamar eh,yasmeen taci gaba to amma taje tacewa momy wai itace tai mata rashin kunya ita kuma momy ba bincike tafara dukanta ,


Y sadiq ransa ya baci ya kalli momy d take Harar yasmeen yace momy gaskiya abinda kk bakya kyautawa saboda Allah yarinyar nan marainiyace kuma amana dady ya baki kuma kk karba to me zaisa bayan idonsa aringa zaluntarta momy kisani ko da ace dady baya gani Allah yana ji kuma tana ganink---dakata sadiq ni zakaiwa rashin kunya akan shegiyar yarinyar nan momy ta fada tana nuna inda SUHAILAT ke durkushe wacce magan-ganunsu ke kara jefata mawuyacin hali,


Sadiq yace barashin kunya bace momy gskiya nake fada miki, a matsayina na danki zanso kizamo mai aikata al-khairi ,
Wata tsawa momy tadaka masa tare da fadn ya isheni Ku fita Kuban guri kuma muddin kk cigaba da tare mata zanyi fushi daku sannan tare mata da kukeyi kusani kara jefa tsanarta kk azuciya ta banzaye da Baku San ciwon kankuba  sadiq bai kara magana ba yasa kai ya fice dg falon itama aunty yasmeen stugunnawa tai tare da kamo SUHAILAT sukabar  falon,
To KUCI GABA DA BINA SANNU AHANKALI TAKU UMMU MASH KUR

💜 Pg 4 💜

Yasmin tsugunnawa tai tare da kamo hannun SUHAILAT suka bar falon.

Momy ta ja tsaki tare da binsu da harara sannan ta juyo tana kallon sauran yaran nata kfn tace  wlh duk Wanda zai Shiga harkata akan yarinyar nan nikuma zan hada harshi inci mutuncinsa ta karasa fada tana huci,


Kubra tace momy ni ai tun lokacinda dady yakawota naji nima natsaneta saboda tafini kyau ni ,kuma na tsani ganin anfini kyau takarasa a sha gwabe,

Momy tace kar kidamu auta I ita jinin mayu CE duk kyanta sai kin fita frn jini, murmushi kubra tai tana kokarin kwanciya bisa cinyar momy,


Isah kuwa Wanda tun farkon abun bai samu bakin magana ba sbd shi baima san SUHAILAT ba don sanda aka kawota baya nan shekara 4 kenan kuma a lokacinne yamastawa dady akan yanason zuwa England karatu ganin yawanci Friends din sa duk can suke tafiya, sam dady beson tura yaransa waje sbd yasan matsalar tura yaran waje don yanzu hakama yana kasar maleshia kan harkar business din sa yana yawo kasa she yana ganin yanda wasu  yaran ba karatun suke ba sai sha-shanci,hakanema yasa duk cikin yaransa ba wanda yai karatu awaje,
Shi sadiq bama shida ra,ayin karatu awaje amma isah yasa momy agaba yana mata kuka,hankalinta ya tashi babu abinda ta tsana irin damuwar yaranta gashi dady baya magana 2 momy harda hawayenta domin indai akan yarantane  zata iya komai,acewarta kudinsa bashida anfani indai yaransa basu moraba,
To dakyar dai tasamu yayarda ta hanyar saka bakin babban abokinsa bisa sharadin be yadda da shiriritaba  nanfa isah yaita murna kuma amince da sharadin dady.



To da farko dai ya na karatunsa sosai ammafa dg baya friends dinsa suka hire masa kunne suna shar holiyarsu da yanmatansu yanzu babu abinda isah bayayi tundaga shaye-shaye,Neman mata,kai harda maza ma (Allah dai yashiryamana zuri,a ameen)


Atunaninsa SUHAILAT Yar aikinsu ce shi yasa momy takemata haka  yata6e baki ko ba komai babyn ta hadu ahakan ma dan batada lpy yasan zatafi haka kyau idan tasamu lfy da Hutu,ya sauke wata boyayyir ajiyar zuciya sannan ya kalli momy kfn yace mom ina kk samu wannan beauty babyn? Takaici ne ya hana momy bashi amsa saima cewa da tayi tashi maza kaje dakinka ka huta nasan ka gaji next mayi maganar yamike ba tare da yakara magana ba yanufi hanyar dakinsa yana kara hango fuskar SUHAILAT a zuci Yarsa, Tabbas Yar aikinsu CE inkuwa hakane sai ya kai mata far maki don babyn ta yi masa da wannan tunanin ya isa dakinsa.




Suna isa dakin aunty yasmeen ta kwantar da ita sannan ta komai dakinta ta dauko cake da lemo dama bata rabo dasu ta dawo dakin SUHAILAT tana zuwa tasamu har tafara bacci tashinta tayi tace wanke bakinki kici girgiza kai tayi kfn ta ce nakoshi aunty yasmeen bazan iya cin komai ba a,abazaiyiwu ki kwantar dayunwa ba tashi maza mikewa tati badon tana soba sai don bazata iya yiwa aunty yasmeen musuba don yanzu itace ta mayemata gurbin uwa.

 💜Pg 5💜

Da kyar tasamu taiya wanke bakinta tafito aunty yasmeen ta zaunar da ita a baki tadinga bata hk take karba don ba yanda zatayi saida yasmeen ta ga tana shirin amai kfn ta kyaleta tare dabata magani sannan ta barta ta kewanta saida taga bacci yafara fuzgarta sannan ta fice tare da jamata kofar dakin tana addu,a axuciyarta Allah kb yarinyar nan lpy,batayi tunanin komawa parlour ba sbd damuwar da take ciki kai tsaye dakinta ta wuce itama cake din taci ta kwanta.




       WASHE GARI
Kiran sallar farko akunnen SUHAILAT a kayi ta mike tana mamakin irin baccinda tayi ko farkawa batayiba yau ko sallar da tasabayi da daddare bataiba don tun kfn takai 
haka kakarta inna take tashinta suyi sallah hkn ne yasa tasaba duk dare sai tayi sallah,
Mika tayi tare da karanto addu,ar tashi dg bacci(alhamdu lillahil lathi ahyaa naa ba,ada maa a maa tana wa ilaihinnu shur) mikewa tayi tanufi bathroom ta dauro alwala ta reda yin brush sannan tafito tai sallah byn ta idar ta mike dauko azkar tanayi duk wannan tarbiyyar inna ce shi yasa take kuka duk sanda ta tuna da ita tanuna mata gata sosai ammafa hkn be hana tai mata kya-kyawar tarbiyyaba duk wani abun da uwa zatayi don ganin ta bada ingantacciyar tarbiyya inna tayi mata shi,sai dai tai mata addu,a Allah yajikanta tun tasowarta inna tako yamata yiwa mahaifiyarta adu'a don haka yanzu take hadawa da inna cikin kowace addu'arta.




Mike wa tayi byn ta gama azkar da addu'ointa tanufi hanyar fita daga dkn don kuwa ta sani koda suma take momy bazata iya daga mata kafaba sai tai mata aiki gashi yau Monday zasuje school batason makara dan kullum sai ta makara idan momy ta ga dama ma sai tahanata zuwa tabata wani aikin itakuma yaranta duk sun tafi,donma SUHAILAT yarinyace me haxaka duk abinda aka koya mata sai 1 yazauna a kwa-kwalwarta ko laifi taimaka da ka nuna ba kaso zata kiyaye shi yasa ta ke kaffa-kaffa da momy indai ba cinzalinta zata yiba to kfn tai lefi tana dadewa,momy bataso haka ba taso ace yarinyar tana da rawarkai ko ba komai zata jibgeta ammafa duk dahaka idan tanason ta ci zalinta saita kir-kiro laifi bata jiba bata gani ba don kawai ta daketa,
Tana bude kofar aunty yasmeen ta shigo tana mata kallon tuhuma kfn tace to uwar aiki ina kuma zakije ke da ba lpy ce dake ba ta fada tana me daure fuska don tasan yanzu zata iya cemata aiki zata fita tayi kallon fuskar aunty yasmeen ta yi ganin yanda ta wani hade rai, dry abin yaso bata tai murmushi kfn ta ce aunty kenan ainaji sauki zan iya aikin kng kr momy ta yi fada ai daman nasan abinda kk tsoro ba wani sauki da kkji kinga bari inje inyi aikin ke kije ki huta in anjima xan tasheki kishirya ki tafi makaranta  a'a aunty wlh zan iya naji sauki sosai tace to shike nan zomuje,



Kichin din suka nufa don su duba time table dn abinda zasu dafa, momy Ce ta tsara abinta kullum da abinda za'a girka break past tsarinshi daban lunch dabn dinner daban sai anti kmr wata take canzawa,yaukam tea d soyayyiyar plantain sai kuma farfesun kayan ciki dayake suna da komai aikin baya bata wuya kuma gata da zafin nama duk yawan aiki bata jinsa tana farawa a yan mintuna zata gama amma mafi yawanci dama tare sukeyi da aunty yasmeen,dukda momy ta na hana yasmeen dn amma taki don ita momy ko girkin bata koyawa yaranta ba itama yasmeen dn a nove,ls take karantawa kfn zuwan SUHAILAT gidan duk girkinda takaranta saita gwada zuwan SUHAILAT yasa ta k'ara dagewa tana koya agurinta bata nuna girman kai acewarta idan tai aure bata bukatar y'ar aiki a gidanta shi yasa tadage wajen kyan girki SUHAILAT nakara koya mata wasu musamman na gargajiya don ita tafi kwarewa anan tunda goyon tsuhuwace hkn be hanata iya girkin zamaniba ko wanne kalane da annuna mata shikenan ta iya.







Tare suke aikin ckn nustuwa suke gabatar da komai kfn 7:00am sun gama break past koda suka shigo dashi parlourn ba kowa suma kowacce tanufi dakinta don shiryawa wanka SUHAILAT bayan tagama mai  kayan makarantarta tasa sunyi mata kyau sosai dukda tsufa hoda kawai tashafa tana k'ara kallon bakinta da har yanzu be gama sacewaba dukda batajin ciwon komai amma Sam jikinta ba dadi haka dai ta k'arasa shirinta jikinta  amatuk'ar sanyaye,dakin aunty yasmeen ta shiga tana zuwa ta tarar itama tagama shirinta cikin Riga da skirt na wata atanfa super pinch colour da ratsin blue mayafi d takalminta duk blue colour ne taci makeup tai masifar kyau yasmeen kenan y'argayuce sosai tana da kyanta dai-dai misali Wanda yawan kwalliyarta ke k'ara fito dashi,




Dasallamarta ta shigo dkn ganin yanda aunty yasmeen tai kyau batasan sanda tace masha Allah  wlh aunty kinyi kyau murmushi yasmeen ta yi kfn tace Allah kanwata ammafa ngd murmushin itama tayi Wanda ya haddasa lotsawar gefan kumatunta kfn tace yau idan y farouq ya ganki sai yayi loosing control dn sa dagudu ta biyota tana fadin yarinyar nan kin rainani ta kwasa da  gudu tai hanyar parlour ita kuma ta biyo ta,cak ta tsaya jin motsin su momy a parlourn koda yasmeen ta karaso saida suka saita nutsu warsu kfn suka shiga da  sallama dakyar momy wacce ta ci ado cikin tsa da dden less ta iya amsawa can kasan makoshinta kubra nagefanta cikin shigar kabasajinaranta sababbi sunsha guga sai daukar ido suke fuskarnan anci makeup duk da cewa an hanasu amakaranta amma kubra da kawayenta basa jin magana,


Makarantarsu 1 da SUHAILAT amma ba class1 ba ita tana j,ss1c SUHAILAT tana j,ss 3a sunkusa zana placement,kubra batada wani k'ok'kari sai wasa k'awayentama duk many an y'anmatane basu da aiki sai zan can samarinsu abinda ma yasa suka barta a cikinsu don tana kashemusu kudi don kullum momy sai tabata 1k kudin makaranta SUHAILAT ko 5 bata baata aganinta gaataa takkewa kubra batasan rusa kanta take ba, kubra batada aiki a makaranta sai ciwa SUHAILAT mutunci idan kawayenta sun tanbayeta sai tace Yar aikinsu wata rana wata kawarta me suna mee mee tace wai me yasa yawancin yan aiki sai ki gansu kyawawa?? tabe baki kubra tai kfn ta Ce ai ita wannan jinin mayu ce inji momy na tafada tana kallon SUHAILAT da take zaune agurin da littafi a hannunta tana dubawa ko kallon su batayi suka karaci  abunsu sukai gaba,





Bayan sun gaida momy suka wuce dining ereia don su karya don su momy tuni suka gama sai faman latsa wayar hannunta take da alama kira zata yi kubra ta mike momy zan tafi kudi ta miko mata kamar kullum tare da cewa sai kindawo auta karba tayi tare da saurin fita sai duk dan kar akaisu tare da SUHAILAT ita dama tasaba zuwa a kasa don haka ko a jikinta momy ta kira isah awaya wanda karar wayarce ta tasheshi yamika ya sauki wayar tana tsaki ganin momy Ce yasa yadaga ba tare da tayi magana ba momy ta Ce naga bama fito bane ko duk gajiyarce???yamutsa fuska yayi kmr yana gabanta kfn yace yea,mom nagajifa over don har yanzu ban gama bacciba call dn kine ya tasheni  momy tace kayi sallah kuwa??no yanzu dai znyi,OK inka gama sai ka shigo kayi break momy ta fada tana kokarin katse kiran yace mom me kuka dafane?fada masa momy tayi yace noo mom banaci kunun gyada nake so d kosai na dade banci suna tun kfn intafi kisa wannan babyn ta kawomin daki na ya fadi haka ne don kawai tasamu damar kebewa da SUHAILAT momy ta Ce OK yanzu zata kawo maka.

Momy ta Ce OK yanzu zata kawo maka tafada tana kokarin kashe wayar tajuya Inda SUHAILAT ke zaune tana karyawa cikin sauri2 take komai saboda bata son tamakara a makaranta gabanta ne ya fadi lokacin da taji momy ta na fadin za'akawo maka ko ba'a fada ba tasan d ita momy ta ke fatanta Allah yasa kar a hanata zuwa makaranta ba ta gama tunanin ba taji muryar momy na fadin kee!sunanta ke nan abakin momy, ahankali tamike ta isa gurin momy gabanta na tsananta bugawa muryar momy ta tsinta akunnenta kije kiyiwa isah   kunun gyada da kosai shi yake sha'awa saurin dago kanta tayi zuciyarta tana mata matsanancin zafi wata irin tsawa momy ta buga mata uban me km kallo daba zaki wuce abinda na sakakiba???




Haba momy makaranta fa zata tafi kuma kinga koynzu ta makara ki barta ta tafi ni zanyi masa yasmeen tafada tana karasowa inda suke harara momy ta watsa mata kfn ta Ce bake nake bukatar kiyiba ita dn Ce zatayi km wlh idan har nakara sakata aiki kk mn irin haka sai ranki ya baci ,batare data Kara magana ba jikinta a matukar sanyaye tabi bayan SUHAILAT wacce tunda yasmeen ta Fara magana ta bar gurin don tasan momy ba zata yardaba .




A kichin din tasameta tana aikin tana kuka bata damu da share hawayen fuskarta ba bakincikinta 1 momy ta hana ta zuwa makaranta duk sbd isah, take taji ta tsaneshi momy bata damu da y sadiq ba ko yaci abinci ko bai ciba babu abinda ya dameta amma gashi daga dawowar wannan isah n duk momy ta kara takura mata,
Bataji motsin shigowar aunty yasmeen ba sai ji tayi tana goge mata hawaye ta dago idonta tana kokarin k'akalo murmushi da sauri kuma tadafe kirjinta ganin aunty yasmeen dnta itama tana hawaye abinda bata tab'a ganiba ta Ce nashiga 3 aunty me akayi miki kk kuka??zuciyar yasmeen ta kara karyewa ta na kuka take fadin kiyi hakuri SUHAILAT komai me wuce wane narasa abinda yasa momy ta ke miki haka don Allah kiyi hakuri kinji ta fada tana dafa kagadar SUHAILAT wacce zuwa yanzu kuka take sosaie.





Ckn kukan tace haba aunty yasmeen ke cefa me lallashina to yanzu me ya karya miki zuciya kisani in kina kuka nima bazan daina kuka ba har abada don Allah ki daina kukan momy kuma don ta isa dani ne take min haka ni wlh agurina babu komai rashin zuwana makaranta shine yake damu na har kikaga ina kuka amma don Allah ki hakuri ki daina kuka in kina kuka nikuma ya kk so nayi?? 
Yasmeen ta goge hawayenta tana yaba irin fasahar yarinyar yarinyace me hankali nutsuwa da hangen nesa amma me yasa momy ta ke daukar alhakinta kallonta tayi kfn tace kiyi hakuri kinji kanwata idan kinje makaranta ki karbi books dn yar class dn ku ki copy dn duk abinda akayi bakya nan nikuma idan kin dawo Zn koya miki kinji,ckn murna tace ngd aunty na,no karki mn gdy wajibine inkoyamiki itama ta fada ta na me jin dadin yanda take fara'a,ai dole inmiki gdy Allah yabarmin kee ameen aunty yasmeen ta amsa tana me tayata aikin.







Nan da nan suka gama aikinsu ckn farin ciki da kaunar juna aunty yasmeen ta Ce dauki maza kikai mishi ni bari inje in dan duba book dina don inada paper 10:30 gashi time yana tafiya to aunty Allah y bada sa'a amma fa ni bansan dakinsa ba don jiya ma ko ganin sa banyi ba, nuna mata dakinsa tayi kfn ta wuce nata dakin SUHAILAT ta bada komai ckn nutsuwa take tafiya har ta isa kofar dakin nocking tayi ahankali amma taji shiru saida ta karayi kfn ta ji ance come in, ahankali ta tura kofar dakin tashiga da sallama be amsa sallamar ba saima binta yai da wani irin mayen kallo kamar me shirin ganin wani Abu ajikinna ta.......

Ckn rashin gane inda maganar sa ta dosa tace ban fahimce kaba, shima ckn rashin damuwa da abinda zaifada yace me zai hana muyi yarje-jeniya dake zan hana momy wahalar dake amatsayinki na Yar aikin agidan nan zansa tamayar dake me cikakken gata kamar kina hannun iyayenki da suka turoki aikatau nikuma abinda zaki mn shine duk lokacinda nakiraki dakina zaki zo batare da kn bata lokaci ba ki na dai ganin yanda Mom take sona komai nake bukatar tayi min zata yi min,hope kinga ne ya karasa fadin haka ckn murmushi yariga yasani irin wahalar da take sha dolene ma ta amince ana sa tunanin.



Mamaki ne da rudani suka hana SUHAILAT magana dukda kasancewarta  yarinya hakan be sa takasa fahimtar inda ya dosaba don kuwa Inna babu abinda bata sanar da itaba abinda yadaure mata kai be wuce yanda yake kiranta da Yar aikinsu ba hakan yanuna baima San alakar da take tsakaninsuba shi yasa ma yake mata wani banzan maganar sa ta wofi,tunda take bata taba yiwa wani rashin kunya ba ita ko ckn sa'anninta bata fada amma yau zata fara akan y isah tunda shi ya yarda girmansa, ganin da yayi tayi shiru ne yabashi damar takowa yazo inda take yana kokarin kama hannunta, dakatar dashi tai ta hanyar daga masa hannu kfn ta kalle shi ido ckn ido abinda bata taba yiwa wani ba tace dakata isah yanzu kai a banzan tunanin ka kana ganin na aminta da wannan bagidajiyar maganar taka to in kai baka San illar abinda kk shirin aikatawa ba ni ba jahila bace da ilimina kuma da kk cewa momy ta na ban wahala ai wuya bata kisa sai kwana ya kare kuma sau 1000 gwara wuyar da momy ta ke bani da wannan jahilin tayin naka da bashida maraba da halayyar dabbobi sannan da kk kirana da Yar aiki shin baka San alakar dake tsakaninmu bane Yakama ta kaje ka tanbaya don kuwa na baka assignment, tana zuwa nan tabuga uban tsaki tawu ce ckn sauri ta bude kofar duk da atsorace take don karya biyo bayanta.

Zama tayi a kujera kfn ta motsa bakinta d yayi mata nauyi tace to duk me yakawo wannan tanbayar shiru isah yayi, alamuun rashin gaskiya suka bayyana a fuskar shi amma dayake dn duniya ne kuma bayajin kunyar momy don yasan kome yayi bazata ga laifinsa ba
Sai dai ma tagoya mai baya hkn ce tasa bakunya yace mom wai don na dn kula yarinyar shinefa take gaya mn magan-ganun banza shine harda cewa wai muna da alaka ni kuma ban Santa ba yakara sa fadar hakan yana kallon momy da ta dafe saitin zuciyarta da ta ji tana mata wani mahaukacin bugu,dakyar tasamu ta k'wa-k'wak'ulo maganar da ta mak'ale mata a wuya  tace kana nufin sonta kk??tafada tana kallon sa ganin yanda ya kafeta da ido, no mom ba wai ina sonta bane kawai burgeni ta danyi shine ni kuma na....katseshi momy ta yi don kuwa ta gane abinda yake nufi tace indai don wannan ne ba kada matsala kaje koda karfin tsiya ne kayi abinda kaga dama ckn murna yace, yess mom amma da farko me yasakaki ckn damuwa naga kamar hankalinki ya tashi muramushi ta kir kiro don kuwa har yanzu bata gama dawowa dai-dai ba tace I nazaci sonta kk shi yasa don kuwa bazan yarda ba, haba mom kamar ni nakare da yar aiki I ba class ya karasa fadin haka yan nuna kansa momy tace yauwa yarona haka nake bukatar naji, kallon ta yai kfn yace to kenan Mom ba mu dawata alaka da ita karya taimin??on expected taji tanbayar tadauka baze kara yi mata tanbayar ba hkn yasa tai shiru kfn daga baya tai magana ba ta re da dogon tunani ba ta Ce babu wata alaka a tsakaninku, mikewa yayi yanufi hanyar fita yana fadin ok Mom ba ri inje,to kawai momy ta iya cewa don kuwa tan ckn damuwa isah ya tuno mata da abinda take b'oyewa shekara da she karu.



Babu Wanda yasan abinda momy ta ke b'oyewa daga ita sai Allah d kuma mahaifiyar ta wacce ta dade da rasuwa sai kuma Wanda suka aikata abin dashi,shi yasa koda isah yazo mata da wannan zancen hankalinta yai matukar tashi tasani idan asirinta ya tonu tashiga 9 ma ba 3 ba ammafa tayi ganganci da tace masa basu da alaka anya kuwa bata dab'awa kanta wuk'aba karfa taje garin son a cutar da yarinyar nan asirinta ya tonu taja doguwar ajiyar zuciya to ko dai ta kirashi ta hanashi abinda yakeso yayi haka dai tai ta tunani ita kadai a parloun,tana ckn wannan halin yasmeen ta fito ckn shirinta na makaranta don tana da paper,kallon momy tayi ganin ta ckn damuwa tace momy lpy kuwa?? lpy kalau ta fada ranta a hade aunty yasmeen ta wuce tana fadin sai nadawo don kuwa abinda momy ta yi mata be dametaba don kuwa idan da sabo ta saba tun lokacinda aka kawo SUHAILAT momy ta ga suna bata kulawa ita da y sadiq ta canxa musu fuska.



To ko me nene momy take b'oyewa take tsoron kar asirinta yatonu??kuma me yasa tace masa babu alaka a tsakaninsu to lokaci ne dai zai nuna Ku cigaba da bi na.




SUHAILAT tafarka daga bacci, bude idonta tai ahankali tamike ta nufi bathroom wanka tayi sannan ta dauro alwala tafito a gefen katifarta taga wata takarda anyi short not ajiki dauka tayi tana dubawa,    
     Slm kanwata dptn knj sauki naga knyi bacci shiyasa ban ta sheki ba na tafi school bazan dadeba karki shiga kichin ki bari sai na dawo tunda baki da lpy from your sister.
Rungume takardar tayi tana jin wata irin kaunar auntynta a zuciyarta tace Allah sarki aunty na I nasamu lpy ke dai Allah ya biyaki ameen lokacin 11:15am don haka Qur'aninta ta dauko tana karanta wa.





Isah kuwa yana fita dakinsa yanufa yana shi ga baibi takan break fast din da aka kawo masa ba kwanciya yai bisa make ken gadonsa magan-ganun yarinyar ne suke dawo masa tunda yake Neman y'anmata babu wacce ta tab'a kallon kwyar idonsa ta fada masa magana sai wannan kwailar ai kuwa ta jawa kanta sai ya ganar da ita kus kurenda ta tafka tsaki yayi da yatuna abinda tace wai ta bashi assignment to shi class work zai bata ya dade yana tsara irin muguntar da zai mata tunda mom ta bashi dama kfn bacci yayi awon gaba da shi.




Momy kuwa gaba daya bata ckn sense din ta don kuwa isah ya tuno mata da abinda ta dade da binneshi a ckn zuciyarta gashi batada abokin shawara tunani tayi a kan kawai taje gurin bokanta ko zai Samar musu da mafita tunda yasan komai da wannan shawarar da zuciyarta tabata ta Mike tanufi dakinta mayafi da kudi ta dauko sai key na motarta ckn sauri2 take fitowa don tasan tafiyar dake gabanta bata wasa bace haka ta shiga motarta ta dauki hanya.



Tafiya CE me nisan gaske don saida ta fita daga ckn gari ta Shiga daji adajin ma tafiya sosai tayi kfn tafito daga motar  lokacin rana ta kwallare haka dai momy ta ci gaba da ku tsawa cikin jejin ta gaji matukar gajiya hanyar ma ta b'ace mata don rabonta da gurin har ta manta tafiya dai ta ki karewa gashi Sam bata hango kogon dutsen da boka yakeba, zama tayi a kar-kashin wata bishiya tana maids numfashi ranta duk a dagule kukane kawai bata yiba tana nan zaune sai kuwa ta hango wasu natahowa kai da ganinsu kasan sun jigata da sauri ta mike kasa jiransu su karaso tayi,takarasa inda suke,mata ne guda 2 kowacce da kullin abu a hannunta, kallon su tayi kfn tai magana 1 tace kema gurin me garkuwa zaki??momy bata samu iya magana ba sai daga kanta tai da sauri suka kalli juna kfn suce lallai kina da aiki dakyar ta iya cewa ya ta shine a'a layi ne me yawa muma kinga anan muka kwana sai yau muka samu,girgiza kai momy tayi tace Ku taimakeni Ku nunamin kogon da yake cikin wlh hanyar ce ta bace min, nuna mata sukayi tai godiya suka wuce.










Saida takara tafita sosai kfn ta fara hango mata masu uban yawa kamar ana biki ko wacce akan layi gabanta ya fadi babu Wanda tasanarwa ta fita gashi yanzu bata San ranar komawarta gida ba ta karasa jikinta yai bala in sanyi kallon mutanen gurin kawai ta ke,matane masu yawa sai yan tsirarun maza, wata matace tai mata magana ta sannu baiwar Allah, kallon ta momy ta yi kfn tace yauwa! matar tace ke sai yanzu gashi ma yashiga Hutu,kallon ta momy tayi lallai me garkuwa ya sha hara a da babu layi kuma babu wani Shiga hutu dayakeyi,yanzu yaushe zai fito ta tanbayi matar,I yadade yanzu zaki ga yafito,rufe bakinta ke da wuya sukaji wata mahaukaciyar kara alamar fitowar boka kenan gurin yai duhu yanda ko hannunka bazaka iya ganin saba,bayan kamar 2 minit gurin ya dawo dai-dai momy kam rudewa tayi ta kankame jikin saida taji shiru sannan ta dago kanta wani mutum tagani tsaye a kanta,yace kece rabi daga kanta tayi yace boka na kira da mamaki tamike tsaye ilahirin mutanen gurin ita suke kallo ganin itace ta karshe amma an aiko kiranta itama tunanin ya akayi haka tafaru take,haka dai tabi bayansa suka Shiga.



Suna Shiga taga gurin yafi da abin tsoro gaba daya gurin yazama kamar juji sbd yawan tarkace ga wasu ja jayen abubuwa kmr gidan matsafa komai na gurin baki da ja ne hatta bokan ya koma kamar wani aljani sbd rashin kyan gani ga kuma tsufa duk farin gemu ya bai bayeshi runtse idonta tai lokacin da ta tuno wani abu da ya faru a baya tsakaninta da wannan halittar, wata mahaukaciyar dariya boka yafara tsawon 5 minutes kfn yai shiru yana zaro mata kwala-kwalan idonsa yace.keee!rabiiii matsiyaciya,tsinanniya,bankadaddiya,meyasa kk daina zuwa gurin tsinanne,bankadadde,wato niii,duk da muna da alaka a atsakaninmu ckn wata irin razananniyar murya yake mata magana jikinta yafara kyarma cikin rawar murya tace...........

Jikinta yafara kyarma cikin rawar murya tace tuba nake bokan duniya ina ftn za'a karbi tu bana yanzu ma abinda yakawoni shine....katseta yai ta hanyar saka katon yastansa abakin sa yace nasan abinda yake tafe dake la'ananniya kinzone kan maganar yaron wajenmu ko ba haka bane,ha,ha,ha, yafada yana daka mata tsawa, fitsarin datake makalewa ne ya kufce mata nan take tasake shi, dariyar sa me ban tsoro yasaki kfn daga baya ya murtuke,kaf,ceceyir fuskar shi yana zaro mata kwala-kwalan idonshi Momy dai banda gumi da rawar jiki babu abinda take, tsawa ya kuma daka mata ko ba haka bane tsinanniya??bakinta na rawa tace, ha..hakane bokan duniya ina tsoro ne kar tanbayar dayake min tasa asiri na ya tonu boka kasani isah ba dan alhaji bane kaga kuwa basu da alaka da yarinyar nan tsoro na 1 kar ya dawo yace sonta yakeyi kaga a kwai  matsala,don kuwa asiri na zai tonu,kinyi kuskure matsiyaciya kinyi kuskure,ha,ha,badan kin yi saurin zuwa nan ba da tuni asirinki ya tonu,meyasa kk ce masa basu da alaka, to ki sani idan har kika bari yaimata fyade to kisani soyayyarta me karfi zata shiga zuciyarsa kuma kee baki isa ki hanashi ba ha,ha,ha.

 Nashiga uku bokan duniya kabani mafita kallon ta yake da jajayen idanunsa,yanzu kam tashin hankalin da take ciki ya dakushe tsoronda take masa hakan yasa ta tuno irin kirarin dasuke masa lokacin da suna zuwa da mamanta nan da nan ta fara don kuwa ta San yana matukar son kirarin, shedan ya taimakeka boka me zamani

Hanashi yai mata fyade yana hannun ki duk yadda za'ayi yau ki koma gida don kuwa yaron yau yai niyyar yin komai in biki komaba komai zai iya faruwa,sannan ita wannan yarinyar aljani baze iya shiga ji kinta ba natura mata Wanda zai sata bin maza amma yadawo yace daya doshi jikinta zafi yakeji saboda bata wasa da ibada kuma tana da tsari na musamman a jikinta, yanzu ga wannan ya miko mata wani magani kitabbatar kinsa mata a ckn abinci,indai taci shi angama hannunta na rawa takarb'a sannan ta ce nagode gafalalle,yace tashi ki b'ace min daga nan kuma kici gaba da kula da ajiya ta,bata kara magana ba tafita da mugun sauri don warin gurin barazanar kumbura mata ciki yake gashi tana son ta isa gida dawuri don ta hana isah abinda yake shirinyi tunda ba iya yarinyar zaicutarba ita rayuwarta ma zai tarwatsa mata gashi yanzu har magriba tayi,ita ba ta sallolinda ba tayi ba ta ke tunanin ta kawai shine Allah yasa takoma gida da wuri.

Saida ta kai karshen aya sannan ta amsa sallamar ta juyo tana murmushi ta Ce sannu da dawowa aunty,yasmeen takalleta murmushin itama tayi kfn tace yauwa kanwata ya jikinnaki da sauki aunty ai naga alama tunda gashi kina ta karatu, dariya tai kfn tace to aunty kidan huta bari inshiga kichin, yasmeen ta Ce to zaki iya aikin kuwa kodai ki bari in watsa ruwa inzo,? ah,haba aunty xan iya kije ki huta nasan kin gaji dayawa, ta fada tana kokarin barin dakin, murmushi yasmeen ta yi itama tabar dakin ta nufi dakinta don ta watsa ruwa,ita kuwa SUHAILAT kichin tashiga tana duba time table don ganin me zata dafa  nan da nan kuwa ta fara aikin ta ckn matukar kwarewa Wanda idan kaganta baxakai zaton zata iya irin wannan aikin ba.

Momy tafito daga wajen boka burinta 1 ta isa inda ta ajiye motarta sauri take sosai kamar me gashi duhun magriba ya fara,sai dai mee tana zuwa gurin mota tace daukeni in kin ganni,tashin hankali!zubewa tai aguri tana kuka waye wannan daya daukar mata mota? amma dai ko ma waye tsinanne ne tafada dakarfi kamar wata mahaukaciya,yanzu ya zatayi tariga tasani ko da mota ba lallai ne ta isa gida da wuriba donba tafiyar wasa bace tafiya ce me yawan gaske, kuma gashi boka yasanar mata idan bata isa gida da wuriba komai zai iya faruwa, gabanta y fadi da ta tuno magan-ganun boka,kuka sosai take ita kadai a dokar daji ganin bata da mafita ne yasa taci gaba da tafiya tana yi tana kuka har dai ta samu ta fito bakin titi damuwar tatarbata sanarwa da yaranta zata fita ba tasani dolene hankalinsu ya tashi musamman kubra da tariga tasaba duk inda zata tare suke tafiya, tana tsaye duk motar da ta tsayar bata tsaya wa gashi kuma wayarta ma tana cikin motarta barema takira sadiq yazo ya dauke ta Allah ma yasa ta dauki kudi,haka dai momy taci gaba da tsaiwa amma har yanzu shiru gashi dare yana Kara yi.



____________________gida kuwa hankalinsu ya tashi saboda momy bata tab'a kai wannan lokacin bata gida ba kubra tayi kuka har ta gaji ita kan ta aunty yasmeen din dauriya kawai ta ke amma fa hankalinta ba karamin tashi yayi ba shima y sadiq ya shiga ckn matsananciyar damuwa gashi wayar ta bata Shiga SUHAILAT ma tana ckn damuwa musamman da ta gansu a damuwar tausayin kanta ya kamata yanzu fa su ganin mahaifiyarsune basuyiba suke a ckn wannan damuwar ita kuwa ta ma rasa tata mahaifiyar har Abadan, nan dai suka zauna suna ta ji mami har wajen karfe 10 :45 shiru y sadiq har ya fara bada cigiya aunty yasmeen ta yi shiru tana tunani ba shakka kfn ta tafi school taganta ckn damuwa haushin kanta ya kamata meyasa bata tsaya taji damuwar mahaifiyar ta ba koda yake momyn bata baata fuska ba tunda sai da ta tanbaye ta amma tace babu komai,haka dai suka cigaba da zaman jira kubra kam tagaji da kuka har tayi bacci acinyar kaltume da take ta faman lallashinta,y sadiq ne ya shigo ya dawo daga shigr da report na batan momy nan yai musu bayani sannan yace kowa yaje ya kwanta kafin gobe aga abinda Allah zaiyi, haka kowa ya nufi dakinsa ba don suna jin bacci sai don babu yanda zasu yi.
________________________Momy dai ta na nan tsaye har ma tagaji da kukan idonta duk ya kumbura don kuwa rabonta da kuka har ta manta gasanyi yana ta busa ta tasan badon yarinyar nan ba babu abinda zai sa taje gurin boka gashi yanzu ta janyo mata bala'i kuma ba lallai ne bukatar tama tabiya ba,tsanar SUHAILAT takara ninkuwa a zuciyarta tana jin komai zata iya yi don kawai ta ruguza rayuwar yarinyar,jikinta banda rawar sanyi babu abinda yake tahade jikinta guri 1 sai faman kyarma ta ke ckn ikon Allah saiga wata motar daukar kaya ta doso inda take ita harma ta gaji da tsayar da mota don duk motar da tazo wucewa sai ta tsaida ta amma basa tsaya wa ckn sa'a tana tsayar da wannan sai taga ta tsaya ckn rawar jiki momy ta karasa gurin motar tana musu baya nin inda zata tayi sa'a kuwa suma can haka zasu je don haka suka Ce ta hau nanfa momy ta takar-kare tahaye saman mota ckn kaya,motar da ko wanine yace zata hau sai ta mari mutum sai gashi yau da rawar jikinta take hawanta, nan dai aka fara tafiya sanyi kuwa da iska suka ta kuramata ta dukun kune jikinta da mayafinta ana ta tsala gudu,koda suka ido ckn gari dare ya tsala sosai babu wani abin Hawan da zai kaita gida,to dayake drivern yana da kirki sai ya kaita gidansa yace da safe sai ta tafi gida dayake matarsa ta saba yana irin wannan taimakon hakan tasa ta  karbeta hannu 2 tabata abinci Wanda da kyar  momy ta iya cinsa wanka kuwa kasa yinsa tayi a bayinsu sbd kyan-kyami duk da babu kazanta a ckn sa,sai da taram sallo linda ake binta sannan ta kwanta a wani daki da aka sauketa a cikin sa zuciyarta Sam babu dadi.

💜Pg11💜

Dagudu ta koma ta dauko hijabinta hakan ya bashi damar kutsa kan shi ckn dakin yana kara bin dakin da kallo kfn daga baya yazuba mata lumsheshen idonsa da sukai jajir saboda fitina ckn rawar jiki da murya ta fara fadin kayi hakuri don Allah ka dubi maraici na karka kwa cemin abinda ko wacce mace ke tunkaho dashi,

Murmushin mugunta yayi mata kfn yace." ayyah yarinya kin makaro bake karamar Mara kunya ba to yau zakiga yanda ake rashin kunya, kara kusanto inda take yakeyi ita kuma tana ja da baya tana girgiza mishi kanta hawye na anbaliya akuncinta,


Haka ya cigaba da binta har jikin bango kfn ya fara kokarin kai hannunsa yana son rabata da hijabin ta kan- kame jikinta tayi  ganin yasamu damar cire hijabin, jikinta yana wata irin kyarma tunda ta ke bata ta6a Shiga irin wannan tashin hankalin ba,


Shi kuwa ko ajikinsa kokarin sa kawai yacimma burinsa a kanta duk da irin magiya da kukan da take masa memakon yaji tausayin ta sai ma zuciyarsa ce ke kara tunatar da shi bakaken magan-ganun ta kamar yanzu ne take fada masa, hakan yakara sashi dagewa akan abinda yake kokarin yi,



Ganin da tayi idan tai wasa babu shakka zai maida ita fanko yasa ta dage iyakacin karfinta ta gan- tsara masa cizo a hannu, bashiri ya saketa yana yarfe hannun wata azaba hannun ke masa,ganin jini yana fita a hannun ransa yakara 6aci ya juyo inda take da mugun sauri,sai dai yana juyo wa yahangota tana kokarin shiga bathroom nan take ya mika zankala-zankalan kafafunsa taku3 ya cafko ta da mugun karfi ya fizgota inda tai masauki bisa kirjinsa,bai tsaya 6atawa kansa lokaci ba ya wurga ta kan y'ar katifarta da take dakin,


Wata razananniyar kara ta saki wacce tai amsa kuwwa a gidan gaba daya kasancewar dare ya fara nisa ckn matsanan cin tashin hankali aunty yasmeen wacce ta kasa bacci tunanin inda momy ta Shiga take,tafito a guje ta nufi dakin SUHAILAT sai dai ga mamkinta dakin a bude yake amma an kashe wutar dakin kuma ga dukkan alamu ba bacci ta ke ba ckn lalube ta kunna wutar dakin, me za ta gani isah ne ke kokarin 6atawa yarinyar nan rayuwa, ai bata tsaya tunanin komai ba ta yi kansa tana zunduma masa wani uban zagi Wanda bata ta6ayiba,




Shi kuwa tun bayan I hunda SUHAILAT tayi yasamu hijabinta ya tura mata a baki yanda ba zata iya yin komai ba hatta numfashinta da kyar yake fita baima San lokacin da aunty yasmeen ta shigo ba hasken da ya gani baisa ya bar abinda yake ba,jin saukar abu a tsakiyar kansa ne yasa shi saurin dagowa,aunty yasmeen ya gani tsaye a kan shi rike da wani takalmi a hannunta ganin tana kokarin kara buga masa yasa ya mike da sauri dafe da kan shi,wajen suhailat yasmeen ta nufa wacce ta matukar gala baita sakamakon toshe mata baki dayayi a zuciyarta kuma gdy takewa Allah da ya kawo mata dauki wannan mugun bai samu ya cika burinsa a kan taba,



Cire mata hijabin ta yi daga bakinta tasaka mata shi a jikinta kfn ta juyo wajen isah yana tsaye ya kasa tafiya sai muzurai yake na marasa gasky,sai yanzu bakinta ya bude don farko tsabar rikicewa kasa magana ta yi,tafara fadin, kai wane irin mahaukacin jaki ne dama abinda ka koyo kenan halayyar dabbobi in banda dabba waye zaiyi yunkurin yiwa kanwarsa fyade,kai wane irin dakikine ba'asan inda mahaifiyar ka take ba amman kazo kana shirmen banza kana son 6ata rayuwar marainiyar Allah to kasani ta6a yarinyar nan dai-dai yake da kata6a kubra don matsayinsu1 a wajenka non senses kawai fice kaban guri kfn in Tara maka jama'ar gidan nan ta fada ckn hargagi,bashiri yabar dakin ko ba komai ba zaiso atara masa mutaneba,




Wajen suhailat ta nufa wacce ta ke faman kuka idonta yai jajir saboda kukan da ta dade tana yi,tace kiyi hakuri daina wannan kukan karki jawo wa kanki wata matsalar ckn shashekar kuka tace aunty yasmeen nagode wa Allah dayasa kk zo da bakizo ba da yanzu shi kenan ya...katseta tayi da cewa kinga ki daina wannan maganar don Allah tunda dai Allah yakiyaye ki daga sharrinsa taso muje dakina ,hannunta takama suka wuce dakin yasmeen din,saida ta watsa ruwa sannan suka kwanta yasmeen cike d mamakin wannan lamari yanzu ace kamar isah yasan yaiwa yarinya fyade kai duniya allah ya shirya mana zuri'a ameen da wannan tunanin bacci yai awon gaba da ita.



______________________Momy kam kasa bacci ta yi saboda bata saba da kwana ckn irin wannan local dakin ba ga zafi ga sauro allah-allah take gari ya waye ta tafi gida, haka dai taita juye-juye bisa katifa har taji an kwala kiran sallah ganin masu gidan basu fito ba yasa ta hakura saida taji fitar me gidan masallaci kfn tafito alwala ta yi sannan ta koma dakin da aka sauketa,aso samunta tafi so tana idar da sallah ta tafi amma me gidan yace ta bari gari ya dan waye,



 ba don son ranta ba ta hakura ko ba komai mutumin ya tai maka mata,ba donshi ba ba tasan ya zata yi ba,haka dai tabari saida garin ya waye sosae sannan tai musu sallama ta reda gdy ta tafi sunyi-sunyi ta tsaya ta karya amma taki toko ta tsaya ba iya cin abincinsu zatayi ba,tana fitowa titi tasamu abin hawa ta fada masa Inda zata ya dauke ta suka kama hanya.........

💜pg12💜

A dai-dai get dn gidan tasauka tabiyashi hakkinsa sannan ta fara nocking baba me gadi ya leka ganin hjy ce da sauri ya bude kofar yana mata sannu bata amsa shiba sai daga mishi hannu tayi ta wuce tana Shiga parloun ta ci karo da yaranta sunyi cirko-cirko suna shawarar neman mafita,kallon su tayi gaba dayansu suna gurin banda isah, 


Kubra ce tafara lura da shigowar ta wani ihu taxuba ta nufeta aguje hakane yasa kowa ya maida hankalinsa gurin, ckn mamaki suke kallon momy, kubra tazo ta rungume ta cike da farinciki,sai kuma ta fashe da kukan shagwaba,zama momy ta yi tafara lallashinta, 1bayan1 sukayi mata sannu da zuwa tana amsa wa koda suhailat ta yi mata sannu da zuwa kin amsa wa tayi saima binta da tai da harara wata irin tsanar yarinyar ce take kara huda zuciyarta gani take duk itace ta jamata wannan wahalar,y Sadiq d aunty yasmeen basu ji dadin abinda momy ta yi wa suhailat ba yarinyar ta nuna damuwar ta akan ta duk da irin abinda take mata,hakan hana tayi murna da dawowar momyn ba,




Sadiq yace bari inje in janye report dn da na shigar ya fada yana kokarin barin parloun itama yasmeen hannun suhailat ta Kama tana fadin bari mu barki ki huts momy, suma suka fice babu Wanda ta tankawa a cikin su don ta fahimci abinda ta yiwa yarinyar nan ne ya6ata musu rai,



Aka barta daga ita sai kubra da kaltume suna bata labarin irin tashin hankalinda suka shiga jiya, nan take tanbayar su ina isah suka ce tunjiya basu ganshi ba bai dawo gida da wuri ba,tunani take allah dai yasa bai 6ata mata shiri ba ko da yake ba ta ga alamar wani abu ya faru da yarinyar ba tunda ga tanan lpy klw,tai a ajiyar zuciya, sannan ta mike tana fadin bari na watsa ruwa to momy sai kin fito cewar kubra a fito lpy hajiya kaltume itama ta fada.

Dakin momy ya nufa Inda ya sameta a parloun ta tana break fast gurin ta ya karasa da murmushi a fuskar shi yana zuwa yace good morning mom,morning son,ta bashi amsa tana masa wani irin kallo kamar me son gano wani abu atare dashi, lpy kuwa mom ya dawo da ita daga duniyar tunanin da ta Shiga, ah ba komai, kayi break fast ne tai kokarin kauda zancen dayi masa wannan tanbayar, kansa ya gyada mata alamar a'a da kanta tai servn dn shi,bukatar ta yagama ta tanbaye shi akan abinda ya faru tsakaninsa da yarinyar don ba taga alamun wani abu ya sameta ba, shi kuwa tanbayar da take zuciyarsa yake so yayi mata don abin ya shige masa duhu,be wani ci dayawa ba ya ture,momy ta kalleshi har ka gama kenan, eh,mom magana nake so muyi dake,saida gabanta y fadi kfn ta saita nu tsuwarta tace ina jinka ,yauwa Mom akan yarinyar nan me aikin... dakatar dashi ta yi,da fadin ba mungama magana akan ta ba fadamin meya faru a tsakaninku jiya??ransa ya 6aci daya tuno yace babu wani abinda ya faru aunty yasmeen ce shiga dakin a lokacin, wata 6oyayyiyar ajiyar zuciya ta sauke yauce ranar da taji dadin kare yarinyar nan da yasmeen keyi a karo na farko, ko ba komai asirinta bazai tonuba,ckn salon dabara da wayo tace karka damu yarona indai wannan yarinyar ce dakanta zata kawo maka kanta, in shock yaji maganar, da sauri ya dago yana kallon ta maganinda boka ya bata ta nuna mai tace in dai akan cikar burina da na yarana ne babu abinda bazan iya aikatawa ba.




Da mamaki yake bin abin da kallo kfn yace me kk nufi Mom?? kaga wannan abin ta Kara daga masa maganin yabi hannunta da kallo yana son Karin bayani,taci gaba duk yarinyar da taci shi,tazama karuwa da kanta zata fara neman maza, kaga kenan ina zuba mata a abinci shike nan ba ma sai kaje Inda take ba, zata nemeka da kanta,takarasa fadar haka ckn murmushi, shima murmushin yayi yana aiyana irin abinda zai mata duk ranar da takai kanta gareshi.

Yasmeen ce zaune a dakinta ta re da suhailat ta ba ta hakurin akan abinda momy ke mata har zuwa kan abinda ya faru jiya, tace mata kiyi hakuri kinji suhailat ko wane bawa da irin jarabawar da ubangiji yake masa ki kara hakuri akan ta ki jarabawar ki dauka qaddarar kice haka,kisani allah yana jarabtar bawansa ne domin yaga qarfin imaninsa ke taki jarabawar an sanya miki ita a hannun momy,ni kuma allah ya jarabceni da uwa Mara tsoron allah ,takarasa fadin haka hawaye na gangarowa bisa kuncinta,goge mata hawayen suhailat keyi tana fadin aunty na ki daina kuka in dai baso kk nima inyi ba,kuma ni wannan abubuwan da ake min na dauke su matsayin qaddara watarana baza'ayi min ba,duniyar ma nawa take yanda mahaifiya ta tarasu nima wataran dole zanbita,ni fatana kullum shine allah ya bani ikon cin wannan jarabawar, aunty kici gaba dayiwa momy addu'a zata daina abinda take insha'allah,nima zan tayaki,amma don Allah kibar kukan nan,shiru yasmeen ta yi tana yaba hankalin yarinyar kfn daga baya tace nagode kanwata kuma daga yanzu nabar kukan addu'a zan cigaba dayi insha'allah, yauwa aunty na nima nagode, haka dai suka cigaba da hira daga baya yasmeen tace Mata zataje gurin momy.

💜pg14💜

Ganin abinda yasmeen ta yi ne yabata damar fashewa daga cikar da tayi,a harzuke cike d masifa tace


Waike yasmeen wane sabon salon kini bibi kk tsiro min dashi yau??saboda kin rainani kowa yasan gurin zamanta don mazaunanta basu da muhalli a kujerun parloun nan,ki gaggawar dagata tun kfn na hadaku naci ubanku,kallon momy y sadiq keyi cike d takaicin halinta sannan yabude baki yace,


Haba momy me yasa kk haka ne yanzu saboda Allah me yarinyar nan ta tare miki d kk tsaneta??kin maida ita tamkar wata baiwa y'ar aiki ma ta fita daraja a gurin ki,nasani da wannan shagwab'ab'b'iyar y'ar taki ce baza kimata hakan ba ya karasa fadin haka yana nuna kubra da yatsansa.

Dakata kar kara hadamin yarinya ta da wannan aljanar kuma abinda zan fada muku shine daga kai har yasmeen Ku fita harkar yarinyar nan inhar ba kwason 6acin raina  sannan babu ruwanku da duk wani abinda zanmata idan kukai haka zamu zauna lpy daku amma muddin kk qi bin umarnina to kusani zanfita daga sabgarku duk da Ku dn y'ay'ana ne,zanbar mata ku tunda ta fini a gurinku,ta karasa maganar ta tana kallon su don taji abinda zasu fada.



Yasmeen ta mike daga inda take tazo wajen momyn kneels dn ta ta sauke kasa sannan ta fara magana cike karyewar zuciya ji take kamar ta fashe da kuka juyo wa tayi ta kalli suhailat wacce tun fara masifar momy ta sauka a kujerar kanta a kasa tana faman zubar da hawaye,


Tausayin yarinyar ya kara narkar mata da zuciya hawayen idonta take kokarin mayarwa sannan ta samu damar fara magana tace,momy Allah SWA yace mubi iyaye musamman uwa amma idan babu sa6on ubangiji a ciki,Momy abinda kk umartarmu da shi sa6o ubangiji ne nikuma bazan ta6a sa6awa mahaliccina ba saboda in faranta miki b...momy tayi saurin katseta, lallai yasmeen wuyanki ya isa yanka yanzu ni kk sa a gaba kina fada min wa y'annan magan-ganun to cewa nayi ki min wa'azi to tun kfn kizo duniya nasan haka kuma tunda kin za6i ki 6atamin akan wannan shike nan maza ki tashi ki dauketa kubar  part dinnan ba nason sake ganin kafarku a parlour na Ku koma can BQ ko gaisuwarki bana bukata 


Yasmeen ckn kuka take fadin momy kiyi hakuri karki yanke mana wannan hunkun cin don Allah tana hada hannunta guri 1 tana rokonta,a zafafe momyn ke fadin badai kin za6i sa6a minba to nima zan iya hakura dake muddin baki canza ra'ayinki ba,mikewa yasmeen tayi daga durkushen da take, gurin suhailat ta nufa wacce take ta faman kuka ta dago ta sannan ta juyo ta kalli momy idonta yai jajir tace shike nan momy zamu koma can dn tunda haka kk so ke kuma zan ci gaba dayi miki addu'a Allah ya ganar dake tana gama fadin haka taja hannun suhailat suka bar parloun da sauri, da harara momy ta rakasu idonta kamar zai fado sannan ta juyo gurin y sadiq tace ina jinka ka zakabi umarni na ko kaima ban isa da kaiba??

Sadiq Wanda kansa ke kasa ya dago yana kallon Momy cike d tausayi shi tausayin ta ma yake ganin ta dage akan sai anmata biyayya akan sa6a umarnin mahaliccinta,ckn sassanyar murya ya fara magana, Momy ke uwa ce kina da y'ay'a maza da mata idan akaiwa danki k y'arki abinda kk wa wannan yarinyar ba zakiji dadiba,momy yakamata ki zama uwa mai dora y'ay'anta akan turbar gaskiya,abinda kk nuna mana muma dashi zamu tarbiyanci namu y'ay'an amma wannan abinda kk umartarmu da shi ba abu ne me kyauba muyi shiru muna kallo ana zalunci,annabi SAW yace idan kaga ana aikata mummu nan abu kahana da karfinka ko kahana d harshenka idan duk baza ka iya ba to kaki abin a zuciyarka,kuma shine mafi raunin imani,momy muna da karfin da zamu hanki abinda kk yi amma muna da harshen fada miki gaskiya, don tarbiyyar da kk dora mu akai gurguwa ce batada kafar da zata tseratar damu ranar lahira don Allah Momy ki daina abinda kk ba don ni ba ko ba komai ke mahaifiyar muce baza muso kina aikata ba dai-dai ba alhalin Muna sane,yakara sa doguwar maganar sa yana kallon ta.






Momy da tayi zugudy tana jin abinda dan cikin ta ke fada mata tasan gsky ya fada mata amma ina Wanda yai nisa baya jin kira,duk da cewa jikinta yayi sanyi hkn bai hana ta yi masa masifar ta ba,to sannu ubana zaka zo kawani sani a gaba kanamin magan-ganu kai me ilimi to bazan daina ba, kuuma da kk kini saboda bare nima daga yau na cire ku daga ckn jerin y'ay'a na ku kaddara yanda uwarta ta mutu kuma baku da uwa don ni duk Wanda bazaibi ra'ayina ba zan iya rabuwa  dashi ko waye,don haka kaima ka wuce bana son kara ganin ka a part dinnan, ta karasa maganar ta tana nuna masa hanya bai kara magana ba ya fice cike da tsana nin 6acin rai yana addu'ar Allah ya shiryi Momy.

mishi Peck a cheek dn shi tace my sweat ina zuwa kansa ya daga mata don a halinda yake ciki magana zata yi mishi wuya, fitowa waje tayi ta hango kubra raku6e a jikin bango dafe da kanta da alama abin ya taso mata,tintsirewa tayi da dariya har tana rike ciki,ita dai binta take da ido daga karshema tsaiwar ta ga gareta ta durkushe a kasa dafe da cikin ta, saida meimy tayi dariya mai isarta sannan tazo kusa da ita tace,me ya kawo ki bayan kinsan ke jarababbiyace gashi yanzu kinga abinda yafi karfin ki,saida nace ki bari gobe kizo amma kk ki,kinzo kin katse mu muna tsakiyar love, ita kam ko jinta ba tayi gaba daya hankalinta baya jikinta abinda take ji a yanzu yafi na kullum,(abinda bata sani ba maganin da yake yawo a jikinta yafi tasiri time dn da mutum yaga irin abinda ta gani) ita kam yanzu gaba 1 bata ckn nutsuwarta,lura da hakan tasa meimy koma wa ckn parloun,wajen wata kujera ta nufa tana murmushi, baisan tana tahowa ba saboda attention dn sa nakan wayar sa, tafa hannunta ta yi,wani hadadden guy ne meji da kansa,yadago ya zuba mata kwayar idonsa kashe masa ido tayi ya dan juya ya kalli inda ammar yake ganin sa yayi ya kafa musu na mujiya,ya juyo yana murmushin sa mai tsada yace me ya kawoki wajena kinsa wan can sarkin kishin ya mai damu tvn shi,juya wa tayi ta kalli ammar yanda ya wani tsaresu da ido,hannunta ta mannawa kiss ta hura masa,gaba daya suka sa dariya,najib yace to ai saika bar kallon mu haka tunda kaga ba abinda kk zargi bane,ammar yace, kai dinne,dan duniya ne ina zaune zaka iya yi min kwace,tuno wa tayi da halin da tabar kubra aciki kallon najib tayi tace tsuntsu ne daga sama gasa shshe inkana so kallon rashin fahimta yake mata tace bakaga shigowar wataba,ta6e baki yayi to koku da kk ckn parloun bansan abinda kk ba bare naga Najib shine mutum na farko daya fara raba kubra da budaurcinta tun tana karamarta da taimakon mahaifiyarta da kuma kawarta, tun bayan da tayi jinyar jikinta suka kuma kullewa dashi suna sheke ayarsu yanzu ko makaranta tafita sai sun hadu gidan su meimy wacce itama suke tasu badalar da ammar tunda babu me hana su auntynta bata damuwa da duk wani abinda zasu yi a cewarta bazata shiga rayuwar kowaba ra'ayinta kenan hakan ce tasa suka samu lasisin sheke ayarsu a gidan.










Momy tana ta zuba ido akan suhailat duk da cewa ba guru 1 suke ba amma hakan be hanata jiran tsammaniba gashi boka yace ckn kwana 2maganin yake tasiri kuma inta fara babu mai iya rabata da abin amma har yanzu kusan 3 weak balabari ta matsu taji mummunan labari game da yarinyar ko dayake tasani koda tafara dn su yasmeen zasu iya rufa mata asiri amma idan suka ga abin yafi karfin su dole ne su sanar ,da wannan tunanin hankalinta ya kwanta a ganin ta suhailat ta fara wannan mummunan halaiyar,murmushi tayi duk ranar da suka zo mata da zancen ba yarinyar ba harsu da suka rufa mata asiri sai ta tayar musu da hankali,saboda har yanzu abinda suka yi mata akan yarinyar yana damunta har rashin lpy tayi saboda ita mace ce me bala,in son yaranta amma saboda son zuciyarta ta danne son nasu don kawai ta tozarta rayuwar marainiyar Allah,


Tayi nadama akan wannan batai zaton yaranta zasu mata hakan ba ita da take so ta wahalar da yarinyar, amma gashi yanzu rashin hakurin ta yajamata ko aikin da yarinyar ke mata yanzu bata morarta, dole kaltume ta koma aikinta ita kuma ba komai ta iya ba wani abin idan momy naso sai dai ta shiga kichin da kanta, amma ita yarinyar babu abinda bata iya ba, daman ba maganar kubra ake ba ita ruwan zafi kawai zata iya dafawa ko shi dinma bata ta6a gwadawa ba,







Yanzu suhailat rayuwa take me cike d farin ciki gani take kamar bata da wata matsala, tun lokacin da abin ya faru basu daina zuwa gaida momy ba kullum da safe sai sunje sai dai ko sunje din haka zasu taso momy bata kula su,watarana da suka jema cewa tayi muddin suka kara zuwa Inda take sai ta tsine musu, hakan ce tasa y sadiq yace su daina zuwa sai ta huce,yanzu kam basa zuwa Inda take,kowa rayuwar sa yake baruwan kowa da kowa abinci kam a kwai komai a store can suke diba suna anfani dashi, suhailat ta goge ta murje tayi kyau kyanta ya kara fitowa har wata kiba tayi 6ul-6ul da ita,gashi ta koma makaranta yanzu ko makara ba tayi bare fashi aunty yasmeen ta hana ta aikin komai,ita keyi don yanzu ita babu Inda take zuwa,sun gama exam dn su sai jiran results,sai weekend take aiki shima ba wani mai yawa ba,sannan yanzu ta kware wajen iya makeup aunty yasmeen ce ta dage ta koya mata a cewarta mace ba kwalliya duk kyanta ta zama namiji shi yasa ta dage ta koyawa kanwarta,dayake tana da kwanya ckn kwanaki kadan ta iya yanzu ko a makaranta babu meyimata kallon banza,abinda ta lura shine Sam basa haduwa da kubra, koda yake ita kubra driver ke kaita ita kuma y sadiq ne kullum yake kaita sai ya wuce gurin aikin sa amma yana bata kudin dawowa dana break abinda bata saba ba shi yasa bata siyan komai sai ya dawo ta bashi, baya kar6a yace idan bata bukata ta zubar jin hakan yasa take 6oyewa a wajen ta tunda babu abinda zata nema tarasa komai suna yi mata,sai dai Allah yasaka musu da alkhairi.






Kubra ta zama babbar mace ta bude ta cika tai fam daka ganta kaga me aure yanzu idan ka ganta zaka dauka itace babba daman ita girman jiki ne da ita,yasmeen kuma karamin jiki ne da ita gashi yanzu tana samun sinadarin da yasa ta mugun girma na fitar hankali, duk da irin mugun giman da kubra tayi momy bata kawo komai aranta ba saim farin cikinta data nuna game da girman y'ar tata.

Story by MARYAM A SALIS (UMMU MASH KUR)

💜pg18💜

3 months suhailat ta canza ta murje don har wata kiba tayi ta kwanciyar hankali ba aikin momy babu hantararta kullum tana tare da y'an uwanta masoyanta y sadiq da aunty yasmeen suna rayuwarsu ckn farin ciki da kwanciyar hankali, ptn su Allah ya dawo da dady lpy,yaga abinda momy ta aikata musu,sun San halin dady baya daukar irin abinda momy keyi ita kanta momyn tana mugun tsoron shi, yanzu ma ganin baya nan ne yasa take yin abinda take,amma a gabanshi bata nuna komai game da suhailat ba.



Lokacin da ya kawo mata suhailat duk da ranta bai so ba,amma bata nuna masaba harda cewa Allah ya bata ikon rikewa ,yana tafiya ta fito da mugayen halayen ta akan yarinyar, shi yasa suke ptn Allah y dawo dashi lpy yaga irin rukon amanar da tayiwa  y'ar marainiyar Allah tunda yace yana nan tafe ckn week dn nnan.







***************************
Karatu suhailat keyi ba wasa daga islamiyyah har bokon,daman gata da kwa-kwalwa kuma yanzu bata fashi karatu baya wuce ta,sannan a gida aunty yasmeen bata barinta tayi wani aiki ta dauke mata aikin komai, yanzu tana da haddar 30 hizf na alqur'ani mai girma, sannan sun zana  jarabawar placements,duk fadin school dn su babu Wanda yaci mark dn da taci hakan ce tasanya principal da hukumar makaranta suka yanke shawarar yi mata jumping saboda irin kokarin da take dashi, aka ce ta turo mahaifinta a shawarce shi,y sadiq ne yaje a madadinsa suka yi mishi bayani akan zasu kaita s.s3 yace gsky 3 yayi nisa amma ya amince a kaita s.s 2 haka kuwa akayi, yanzu tana s.s2, nan ta kara dagewa da karatun ta su yasmeen da y sadiq suna mata special lesson a gida,hakan ba karamin tai maka ta yake Sam bata samu wani problems da jumping dn da aka mata ba.







***************************
A ckn wannan week dn dady yasanar da momy akan yana hanya, duk da cewa bai sanar mata ranar zuwan nasa ba,ammafa hankalinta ba karamin tashi yayi ba tafi kowa sanin halin alhj mutum ne me zafi da saurin fushi muddin ya dawo ya tarar da abinda ta aikata ga yaransa tasani komai zai iya faruwa don haka dole ta nemawa kanta mafita, gashi yaran ma ta hana su zuwa inda take bare ta nuna musu ta hakura su dawo wajen ta ko ba komai zata cigaba da wahalar da yarinyar ko da a 6oye ne,to yanzu ko zuwa zanyi wajen su ta fadi hakan a zuciyarta in kk je ai kinzubar da girman ki gurin yaranki wata zuciyar ta gargadeta,zama tayi tana shawarar yanda zata yi duk abinda ta aiyana zata yi sai taga ba mafita bace daga karshe ta yanke shawarar koma wa gurin bokanta kila shi zai Samar mata da mafita kuma daman tana bukatar sanin ko yarinyar ta fara abinda take buri tayi, tasani me garkuwa zai sanar da ita komai,da wannan shawarar ta zuciyarta ta amince akan gobe zata yi sammakon zuwa wajen bokanta, (Allah ya ganar dake).







***************************
Kubra yanzu abin ta yakara yawa don yanzu ba da najib kadai take harka ba ta hadu da wasu mazan y'an duniya,tunda daman  mutum 1 baze ta6a isarta ba, a yanda ta sha maganin gurin boka daman badon mutum 1 akayi ba,don ta dinga bin maza ne,sai Allah ya tseratar da marainiyar Allah, yanzu kubra duk namijin da taga yayi mata da kanta zata kai masa kanta kyau ta, idan kuma abin ya taso mata hankalinta barin jikinta ya keyi kowa ta samu zata iya mu'amala dashi,watarana ckn dare abun ya taso mata ta gigice ta fita haiya  cinta gashi dare ya tsala,ai bata San lokacin tanufi dakin bala drivern gidan su ba daman shi ya dade da sanin abinda take aikatawa tunda shine yake kaita duk inda take zuwa, har nuna mata yayi shima yana harkar amma taki saurarsa ganin me aikin gidan su ne kuma baya 1 daga ckn mazan dake burgeta amma da taga batada mafita dolenta taje gurin sa sukayi badalarsu, tunda ga ranar suka kulle dashi ammafa sai taga bata da mafita take zuwa gurinsa,(Allah sarki mahaifiyar ki ce taja miki wannan bala'in,da yanzu marainiyar Allah ce a ckn wannan halin)










***************************
Da asubah momy ta shirya don ziyartar boka me garkuwa da sauri2 take komai don bata son yau tayi irin na wancan time dn koda yake yau da wuri zata fita wancan lokacin kuwa saida yamma shi yasa tasamu problems, babu Wanda tasanarwa zata fita daga kaltume sai kubra koda yake yanzu ai su kadai ne a part dn ta isah kam yanzu sai yaga dama yake kwana a gidan, hutunsa tun tuni ya kare amma yaki tafiya a cewarshi sai dady y dawo sannan zai koma,


Batayi garajen tafiya da mota ba tunda waccen mortar tata a can ta 6ata driver tasa ya kaita inda zata hau motar kauyen da bokan yake, ckn sa'a suna zuwa ta samu mota babu wani 6ata lokaci suka dau hanya,


Sai wajen 7:30am suka isa inda zata sauka ashe suna da yawa nan mata suka dinga fitowa masu kudi d talakawa,da alama suma gurin bokan suka nufa,haka akayi kuwa kusan su 10 duk can dn zasu don haka suka dunguma ckn dajin don karasa wa inda yake suna tafe suna hirar irin layin da ake samu a gurin sa momy bata damu da wannan ba tunda tasan ita bata bin layi da kansa yake sawa a kirata. 



Tafiya mai nisa sukayi kfn su iso gurin nasa,yau ba kamar rannan ba bubu layi sosae haka suka zauna zaman jiran fitowarshi,kusan 1 hours sannan suka ji alamar fitowarshi gurin yai baki kirin ko tafin hannun ka baka iya gani ga wata irin kara mai ban tsoro, wannan karon momy bata tsorata sosae ba, bayan ya wuce da y'an min tuna aka fara kiran mutane,saida aka sallami kusan mutum 10 sannan aka kira momy,koda ta shiga bataji wani tsoro ba kamar wancan lokacin, nan ta fara jero masa kirari kamar yanda tasaba aikuwa nan da nan yace ta fadi bukatar ta ckn sauri ta fara fadin, bokan duniya me garkuwa ka taimakeni ina ckn damuwa nayi anfani da abinda kabani yanda kace kuma taci sai dai har yanzu ba nida tabbacin tafara bin mazan kuma gashi mahaifinsu zai dawo kasanshi da taurin zuciya shine nake so agama min dashi yazama na duk abinda na fada mishi ya yarda dashi ya kuma dinga bin umarnina inaso yazama a tafin hannu na sai yanda nayi dashi ya la'ananne me aiki da cika wa.






Wani irin ihu bokan yake mai tsananin karfi Wanda ya tilastawa momy toshe kunnenta da ya tsunta ganin yana barazanar tarwatsa mata dodon kunne,bayan yayi shiru sai ya dauko wata kwarya ya zuba wani abu a Viki take saiga hoton najib lokacin da yake raba kubra da budurcinta amma ba'a iya hango ta shi kanshi bokan baiga fuskar ta ba sai dai zaka iya fahimtar mace da namiji ne suke aikata badalarsu, ya fito momy yake da zankala-zankalan hannunsa yana nuna mata ckn kwaryar da sauri ta leka wani irin farin ciki ya mamaye zuciyarta ashe dai burinta ya cika akan yarinyar amma yaranta suka 6oye mata kara leka wa tayi tace bokan duniya da gaske ita ce nake gani??ya bushe da dariya indai kin tabbata ita kk bawa magani to bashakka itace, murnar ta ta kasa 6oyuwa tunda tasan ba Wanda yaci maganin inba yarinyar ba,ckn farin ciki tace ngd me garkuwa aikin ka yana kyau, yanzu sai maganar alhj ina so in mallake shi sai abinda nace,shiru yayi na 5seconds kfn ya kalleta da jajayen idonsa yace kin San wannan matsiyacin mijinnaki baya jin asiri saimu 6ata lokacin mu idan muka tura masa 10 sai munyi babbbar sa'a 1 yake tasiri a jikinsa, muddin kina son mallakeshi sai kin kuma aikata abinda kk yi shekarun da suka ga bata.





Ckn faduwar gaba tace kamin rai me garkuwa bazan iya kara aikata wa domin har yanzu ina nadama akan Wanda na aikata a baya, duk da bukata ta biya amma nima na gamu da abinda banji dadin sa,shedan ya taimakeka idan a kwai wata hanyar ba wannan ba kafada min komai wahalar ta zanyi, dariyar sa mai ban tsoro ya saki,ita kam rudanin da take ciki bai bata damar tsorata ba,saida yayi mai isarsa sannan ya kalleta da kwala-kwalan idonsa yace mafita 1 ce kuma ko yaya kk yi kuskure bukatar ki bazata biyaba dole sai kin dawo kinyi wan can abinda ba kyaso ckn rawar jiki tace fada min naji zankiyaye bokan duniya, yace, 1, karki kuskura kiyi magana dashi koda a waya ne har sai ya dawo, kuma idan ya kiraki karki daga kuma karki kashe wayar ki barta sai ta mutu da kanta, 2,ungo wannan ki tabbata yaci idan ki kayi hakan kin gama mallakarshi sai abinda kk so zaiyi, ckn murna ta kar6i abinda yake bata sannan ta debo kudi masu yawan gaske ta bashi tana faman zabga masa gdy, da haka tafito daga gurin sa.



Koda tafito taga mutane sun karu hakan bai dametaba tunda ita tabiya bukatar ta haka ta kutsa kanta ckn dajin don ta samu abin hawa sauri take don ta tuna wancen lokacin da yanda tasha wahala, aikuwa tana fitowa titi tai sa'a an sauke wasu mata daga wata mota nan da nan ta hau suka dau hanya....

bata samu kowa a part dn ba sai kaltume da take zaune tana kallo, sannu tayi mata ta amsa ta wuce bedroom dn ta,tana shiga wardrobe dn ta tabude ta adana maganin da boka ya bata,sannan ta fada bathroom don tayi wanka saboda gajuyar da ta kwaso,

 Ckn taushin murya me sigar lallashi  yace haba mutumi na me yake faruwa ne a gidan kk so ka 6oye wa dadynka ko kana son hankali na ya tashi don gsky yanda yanayinka ya canza lokaci guda nasan a kwai problems pls my son kasanar dani ji kina yana fada min ba lpy ba,don nasan halin mahaifiyar ku ciki da waje,kila akwai abinda kk 6oye min game da ita,
Shiru y sadiq ya kuma yi baya son ya tayarwa da mahaifinsu hankali ya riga yasan halin sa da saurin fushi yanzu idan ya sanar dashi ya sani har yazo gida ransa a 6ace yake, jin muryar dady yai yana fadin, kai nake sauraro abubakar, ckn damuwa yace, tabbas dady a kwai problems amma don Allah kabari idan ka iso gida lpy zan sanar da kai komai yanzu naga kana hanya kuma bana son in 6ata maka rai ko hankalinka ya tashi, please dady ka kwantar da hankalinka da zarar ka dawo zan sanar da kai bazan 6oye maka komai ba.



Da alama ran dady yafara 6aci saboda ckn zafi2 yace kaga barshi kawai idan na dawoma bana bukatar sani karike kayan ka,yakara sa maganar yana kokarin katse kiran, plsssssss dady kar ka kashe wlh zan sanar da kai don Allah karka yi fushi dani plsss my special dady yakara sa fadin haka muryar shi a matukar raunane kamar zaiyi kuka yasan idan ba hakan yayi ba dady bazai ta6a saurararsa ba tunda ya fara jin haushi, yarasa wane irin hali ne da mahaifinnasu kamar ba yanzu yagama magana ckn sanyin murya ba amma yanzu ya rikide masa,


Shiru yaji dadyn yayi ba tare da ya kashe wayar ba hakane yasa ya fahimci yana sauraronsa duk da baiyi magana ba, take ya fara sanar da dady wasu daga ckn abinda suka faru tun bayan tafiyar sa, dif dady yayi yana sauraron yaronsa, y sadiq bai iya fada mishi komai ba amma ya sanar dashi muhimman ciki, musamman abinda ya faru tsakaninsu da momy,bayanin y sadiq ba karamin dugunzuma hankalin dady yayi ba, dakatar dashi yayi da fadin kana jiko abubakar ka barshi idan na dawo saiku karasa sanar dani komai,amma yanzu abinda nake so da kai bana bukatar kasanar da ita zuwana ba kace yanzu kuna part dina ba??hakane dady y sadiq ya amsa to in hakane ku kyaleta kawai idan na sauka lpy zan San abin yi kagane ko eh,nagane dady OK sai na iso karka manta time dn saukar jirgin kasan bana son jira,to dady Allah ya kawo ka lpy plss dady karka saka damuwa a ranka kaji  ya karasa fada yana langa6ar da kai kamar yana gaban mahaifinna shi OK my son karka damu Zan kula da kaina ya fada tare da kashe wayar, bin wayar da kallo y sadiq yayi yana tuno abubuwan da suka faru yanzu tsakaninsa da dady. 





Ya dade tsaye yana tunani kfn daga baya yanufi part dn su don sanar da su yasmeen maganar dawowar dady, da yanda sukayi dashi,sunji dadin dawowar tasa amma har ga Allah basu ji dadin hukuncin da ya yanke akan lamarinba wannan ma kfn ya dawo yaji sauran abinda sadiq bai fada masa ba, idan kuwa yaji sun sani saiya dauki mummunan mataki ,aunty yasmeen ta dauki aniyar baza ta 6oyewa dady komai ba, duk kuwa irin matakin da zai dauka tunda mahaifiyar tasu ta zama Zuma Shanta saida wuta kila hakan tasa momyn ta gyara halayenta sanadiyyar hukuncin da dadyn zai mata,ita kam suhailat tsoro ne ma ya kamata jin irin matakin da dady ya fara dauka tun kfn ya dawo tasani babu wani canza halin da momy zata yi saima dai ta kara tsanar ta tunda saboda ita dadyn zaiyi hakan,muryar y sadiq ce ta katse su daga tunanin da kowaccensu keyi yace to ai saiku tashi ku fara shirin tarar dady tunda anan zai sauka,tare suka mike suka fara aikinsu na tarar dadyn kasancewar weekend ne suhailat na gida babu school sai islamiyyah ita kuma sai 3:00pm take zuwa a cewarta yau bazata je ba tunda dady yana hanya.

Aunty yasmeen sun gama gyara ko ina tun daga dakin da zai sauka har zuwa ko ina, sannan suka zauna suka tsara masa girki special Wanda sun San ba karamin dadin abincin zai ji ba saboda ya dade baici irinsa andade ba'a hadu ba ,saida suka gama komai kfn daga baya ko wacce ta san6ala wanka sannan suka zauna suka tsan-tsara makeup abinka da kwararru nanfa sukai bala'in kyau kamar masu zuwa party sunfi son dady ya gansu fes-fes alamar suna ckn kwanciyar hankali, parlour suka dawo suna jiran time dn dauko dady a airport ya cika don sunce baza'a basu a baya ba dasu za'a je,


Hira suke cike da birgewa,aunty yasmeen bata yarda da wata kawa ba kawar ta itace kanwarta babu abinda take iya 6oye mata ko tsakaninta da faroq wani abu ya faru ita take fadawa kuma ta bata shawara me kyau shi yasa Sam bata da wata kawa ko aminiya da ta wuce suhailat to itama dn bata da kawa sai yayarta yasmeen duk abinda ya sameta tana zuwa ta fada mata itama tana yin iya bakin kokarin ta wajen ganin tabata shawara me kyau, a haka y sadiq yasa mesu shima yaci tashi kwalliyar yace oya Ku tashi muje kunsan dady baya son jira,tare suka Mike don daukar hijabai sun sani indai da y sadiq zasu fita basa ta6a daukar mayafi don ko ka dauka sai ka maida shi,a tare suka jero gwanin sha'awa, y sadiq harda yi musu pic yanda suka saka komai nasu iri 1kamr twins dayake aunty yasmeen ba jiki gareta ba, suna isa airport suka nemi guri suka zauna don suyi waiting saukar jirgin basu yi 30 minit ba jirgin ya sauka cike d fasinja,kowa kagani a wajen ya kurawa jirgin ido saboda son ganin Wanda kazo dauka, ciki kuwa harda su yasmeen, can saiga dady na saukowa a hankali ckn nutsuwa, mikewa sukayi a tare su 3n suna kallon dady ya gama saukowa yana y'an waige-waige da alama su yake nema da sauri y sadiq ya kama hannayensu suka nufi Inda dady yake kowanne da smyln a face dn shi, bai kula da ta howarsu ba saiji yayi y sadiq ya hugging dn shi ta baya da sauri ya juyo shima tasa face dn cike da murmushin annuri ya rike y sadiq yana fadin my son Kaine haka kazamo msgi danci ina mamana me yasa baka ta homin dasu ba nuna masa gefen sa yayi, dady ya juya yasmeen da suhailat ya gani sunci adi suna masa smyln matsawa yayi kusa dasu yai hogging ko waccensu i damunsa sun ciko da kwallah daya tuna abinda rabi taiwa gudan jininsa wacce duk ckn yaransa yafi jinta a ransa saboda sunan mahaifiyar shi
