[1/10, 8:06 AM] Ummu Mashkur: 💜💜SUHAILAT💜💜

Story and written by MARYAM A SALIS (UMMU MASH KUR)

Labarine me cike d tausayi soyayya kishi zamantakewar rayuwa ds.


Maman sayyid da sayyada wannan naki ne.


💜pg22💜

Suhailat ckn kuka take kallon su tace don Allah Ku hana dady nasani a ckn wannan yanayin zai iya aikata wa momy komai, su kansu sun tsorata da yanayinsa amma dai babu mai iya tun karar dady,ganin sunyi shiru kuma babu Wanda yayi yunkurin tashi yasa ta kwasa a guje tabi bayan sa time dn yakusa zuwa part dn momy kasancewar da y'ar tazara tsakanin part dn 2 saida tayi da gsk ta iya shan gabansa lokacin har ya kai hannunsa handle dn kofar part dn, faduwa tayi a gabanshi ta rike masa kafa, ta fara magana ckn kuka mai girgiza zuciyar Wanda ake wa,tace don Allah dady kayi hakuri ka sassauta zuciyarka karka yanke hukunci alhalin kana ckn fushi manzon Allah SAW yace kada muyi fushi, sannan yace duk sanda bawa yaji shi ckn fushi, idan a tsaye yake ya zauna idan a zaune yake ya kwanta,dady mai karfi bashine Wanda yake daukar mataki lokacin da akayi masa laifi ba mai karfi shine wanda yake iya tankwara zuciyarsa yayin fushin sa dady ban hana ka daukar mataki ba amma don Allah kabari kasamu nutsuwa zuwan ka ckn wannan halin zai iya haifar da komai,ta dora kanta bisa kafarsa taci gaba da kukanta jikinsa yayi bala'in sanyi saboda yanda yake jin kukan ta na ratsashi,lokacin yakara tabbatar da cewa suhailat rainon innarsa ce don duk wannan hadisan a bakin Inna yasaba jinsu tun yana yaro idan yai irin wannan fushin su take fada masa har zuwa girman sa, jikinsa yakara yin sanyi daya tuno da hakan,baiyi magana ba yadago ta ya goge mata hawayen fuskar ta, sannan Yakama hannunta suka bar gurin suka juya part dn su.



Suna shiga suka tarar dasu sunyi tsuru-tsuru kamar wanda sukaiwa sarki karya ba tare da ya musu magana ba yawuce dakinsa rike da hannun suhailat,da ido suka rakasu sannan y sadiq yace gsky suhailat tana da matsayi mai yawa a gurin dady,don tunda nake ban ta6a ganin yayi fushi lokaci 1 ya sauko ba balle ma irin wannan fushin da har saida ya zubar da hawayen sa,yasmeen tace, ai dolene dady yasota kaga dai tarasa mahaifiyar ta sannan sunan mahaifiyar sa ce da ita,bayan haka yarinyar tana da shiga rai ga hankali duba kaga komu yanda muke sonta natabbata bama son kubra haka, y sadiq ckn zolaya yace to ai koke sai dai ki biyo bayanta a gurina,dariya tayi y sadiq kenan ni kam indai suhailat ce na yafe ka hada da nawa duk kabata ,dariya yayi tsokanarki kawai nake duk 1kuke a gurina, wai intanbayeki y akayi kk samu dabarar yin recording kuma baki ta6a fada min ba,hm,y sadiq halin momy nasani yanzu zata juya abinnan y kasance mune bamu da gsky kaga record kuma ga muryar ta bazata karyata muba sai dai taji haushinmu nikam akan gsky ta bana tsoron komai duk da ina tsoron irin hukuncin dady a kanta don nasan ba wai ya hakura bane, tunda ita tajawa kanta sai dai in bata hakuri kila ta gyara halin ta,yace maganar ki haka take tsaf momy zata iya juya abun mu zama mune masu laifi gwanda da kk tanadarmana hujja nan dai suka cigaba da hirarsu kamar babu wani abinda yake faruwa amma rabin hankalinsu nakan kofar dakin dady.





Yana shiga bai zarce ko ina ba sai wajen karamin fridge da yake dakin ruwa ya dauko me sanyi, tunda y kafa battle dn a bakinsa bai cire ba saida yaji babu komai ya dauko wata itama saida takare kfn ya ajiye still hannun ta rike da nashi,ita dai duk inda yajata binsa take, bakin katafaren ganin ya zaunar da ita,tana kallon sa ya nufi bathroom, ka'idane idan ransa ya 6aci saiya sha ruwa me sanyi sannan yai wanka,to hakane kawai kesa yadan samu nutsuwa, bare ma yau dabeyi abinda yai niyyaba a bathroom ya dade yana sakarwa kansa shower sai faman sauke ajiyar zuciya yake akai-akai,saida ya tabbatar da yasamu nutsuwa sannan ya fito daga bathroom dn, hango suhailat yayi tahada hannuwanta biyu a kuncinta alamar tayi zurfi ckn tunanin ta har yagama shirya wa yazo Inda take bata saniba tausayin yarinyar ya kara kama shi yanzu kamar suhailat ace tana irin wannan tunanin yarinya karama,da ita saboda maraici saurin kawar da tunanin yayi a ransa don idan yabari yai nisa yanzu ransa zai kuma 6aci,dafa shoulder dn ta yayi ta dago da sauri, murmushi yasakar mata yace tunanin me kk mamana?? itama murmushin tayi masa ganin kamar ya huce daga fushin sa tace ba komai dady fushin kane ya bani tsoro, smyln dn yakara yi sannan yace karki damu ni haka Allah ya halicceni idan an6atamin rai bana iya control dn kaina ki dinga tayani addu'a ni kaina abun na damu na, amma nasan mamana ita bahaka takeba tayi gadon sanyin hali gurin Inna ko??ya canza salon maganar da fadar haka don baya son ta kuma tuno masa yariga yabar abun a zuciyarsa amma babu makawa sai ya yiwa rabi hukunci dai-dai da abinda ta aikata masa amma dai ya barwa zuciyarsa irin hukuncin da zai mata,suhailat tace dady insha'allah zan taya ka addu'a Allah ya yayemaka,Inna ma kafin tarasu addu'arta kenan a kanka,hakane mamana to ngd tunda kema zaki dinga yimin taso muje parlour munbar yayunki,ba musu tamike tsaye Yakama hannunta suka wuce parloun, y sadiq ne yalura da fitowarsu ya ta6o kafar yasmeen yana nuna mata,mamakin sune ya karu ganin face dn dady wasai ba kamar yanda ya shiga ba da alama dariya ma yakeyi har suka zo gurin su basu bar kallon su ba saida dady ya musu tafi yace me kk kallo haka kamar Wanda aka janza mu,yafada yana kokarin zama kan sofa fuskar shi cike da wal-wala,a tare suka ajiye hearts suna gyara zamansu still basu daina bin su da kallo ba,hakan ba karamar darya yabawa suhailat aikuwa ta kwashe da dariya, dady yace dolene kiyi dariya mamana sun samu gaba kamar TV suna kallo, y sadiq yai murmushi yace gani muka yi dady ka waremu kabata abun dadi mu muna nan muna zuba ido,dady AF ni na manta ma da tsarabarku maza jeka ka kwaso min jakankunan can in baku naku,


Manya-manyan akwatuna ne guda 5 dady babu Wanda baiyi wa tsara ba ba harda baba me gadi da bala driver kai harda kaltume, nan yabawa kowa tasa tsara bar me uban yawa sannan y nuna wata madai-daiciyar a kwati yace mamana dauki ki kara da naki tsarabar rabi ce nafasa bayarwa,da kallo suka bishi suna mamaki amma babu damar magana, suhailat dn cema tace dady kayan sunyi min yawa kayi hakuri ka bata,kamar ckn zafi yace ki zubar in bakya so bata kara magana ba don tasan dama kadan dady yake jira nan dai suka yi masa godiya mai yawa sannan kowa yanufi dakinsa don sun San dady yana da gajiya a jikinsa suka barshi ya huta.

💜pg23💜

Momy tana gida hankalinta kwance kamar babu wani abinda yake faruwa hirarsu ma suke dasu kubra, momy tace yau dai nayi maganin wannan shegiyar yarinyar, ai naso ace munkaita lahira kubra tace mayya ba go bema takara yimin munafurci kaltume tace aiduk baba me gadi ne ya cucemu da yanzu munkaita lahira matsalar hjy takare,kwafa momy tayi tace ko yanzu irin wannan bakin dukan da taci da wuya bata mutu ba,hm hjy kenan wlh tana numfashinta sai dai sama-sama ne wannan me shegen taurin ran da nice ke hjy tuni na dade da kawar da y'ar banza daga doron kasa,dama don kawai kar kice na fiye wuce gona da iri ne,da yanzu na baki mafita, momy tace karki damu kaltume ai mun zama 1 ni kanwa na dauke ki fada min wacce mafita ce wannan, gyara zamanta tayi sannan tace,hjy yanzu ma kin samu wata dama asibitin za'a bisu a karasa tsinanniya ba daga yasmeen sai sadiq ne a gurin ta ba to musan dabarar da za'a yi musu mu dura mata guba kinga shi kenan kin huta alhj na dawowa saiki ce masa hatsari tayi, bazai kara tada zancen ba,murmushi momy tasaki tana yabawa da wannan shawarar ta kaltume, sannan tace haka za'a yi kin kawo I dea tunani Sam bai kai can ba, abinda za'a yi shine zanyi girki sai kuje ke da kubra kusan yanda zakuyi wa yasmeen dabara ku bata abincin, kubra tace a'a gsky nikam ina tsoron aunty yasmeen yanzu nasan muna zuwa zata iya yimana rashin mutunci a bainar jama'a kaltume tace ba haka za'a yi ba hjy wannan aikin ba namu bane kinga dai yaran nan y'ay'anki ne to da kissa zaki bisu tunda idan kk je haka kawai aka kai musu abinci dolene hankalinsu bazai kwanta dashi ba tunda sun san yanda kk tsani yarinyar, nuna musu zaki kinyi kuskure kinyi nadama akan abinda kk yi shi yasa ma kk kaxo don kiga halin da take ciki tofa da haka zakiyi musu dabara harki samu burinki ya cika,momy tace wlh akan haka ni har kuka zan iya yi musu nasan dolene idan suka ga nayi kuka su yarda cewa nayi nadama tunda ban ta6a aikata wani abu makamancin wannan daga baya nazo nayi nadama ba,yauwa hjy ashe kin gano bakin zaren momy tayi wata dariya mu indai kissa da makirci ne ai muna gaba-gaba,dariya suka kwashe da ita,nan dai suka cigaba da tsara yanda zasu karasa kashe SUHAILAT gobe ba kunya ba tsoron Allah basu San cewa dady yana asibitin ba.







***************************
Kusan 3 hours doctors suna kanta suna kokarin ceto rayuwarta dakyar aka samu nasarar dai-daitar numfashinta da taimakon oxygen, doctor muhseen kabeer adam shine ya kar6i nauyin kula da ita,da taimakon wasu manyan doctors,doctor muhseen ya fito daga dakin yana goge gumin face dn shi da handkerchief, gaba dayansu tare suke tanbayar shi abinda ake ciki yace su wuce office, bayan sun zauna ya dubi dady yace alhj wane irin accident tayi, dady ya goge kwallar dake kwance a idonsa yace doctor wlh ba accident tayi ba,rashin imani ta hadu dashi duka ne,aka yi mata na hauka ckn karaji,doctor yace wannan wane Mara imani ne,ya aikata hakan, doctor plss abar wannan maganar zan dau mataki yanzu dai fada min halin da yariya ta take ciki,ckn damuwa doctor muhseen yace to yanzu dai munsamu nasarar dawowar numfashinta da taimakon oxygen sannan mun gano cewa sakamakon wannan mugun dukan tasamu karaya a hannunta, bayan buguwa da kashin bayanta yayi duk sanadiyyar rashin imanin da aka gwada a kanta,yanzu dai abinda ya kamata gareku kutaya mu addu'a insha'allah zamu samu ta dawo normal, gaba dayansu hankalinsu yakara tashi aunty yasmeen kuka take sosai ji take inama tana da dama ta maida ciwon jikinta, yanzu doctor yaushe zamu samu damar ganinta, y sadiq ne yasamu karfin halin yin tambaya, eh,to maganar gsky muna sa ran sai nan da 12 hours zata farka shine muke saka ran insha'allah zata farka ckn hankalinta, yakamata dai Ku kasance ckn shiri don sai tsakar dare zata farka kenan,kuma anfi so taga wani blood sister ko brth a gurin karka damu doctor babu inda zamu muna nan insha'allah yasmeen ta bashi amsa ckn kuka, nan dai doctor muhseen ya sallame su suka koma reception sallah kadai ke ta shinsu, babu Wanda yayi tunanin koma wa gida dauko kaya, hijabi kam y sadiq yasiyawa yasmeen don ko shi babu a jikintah

Haka dai suka cigaba da zaman jira har dare yayi dukan su sunce bazasu tafi gida ba tunda basu ga farka warta ba,abinci ma basu samu sukunin cinsa ba, aunty yasmeen ce a dakin da suhailat take y sadiq da dady a masallaci zasu kwana na asibitin sai dai sunce duk time dn da ta farka yasmeen tasanar dasu,


Sai misalin 3:34 ta farka da fanka idonta yafara tozali tana juyawa sannu a hankali idonta yafara ganin duk abinda ke ckn dakin bin ko ina take da ido,har idonta ya hango mata auntynta na sallah a gefenta take abinda yafaru ya fara dawo mata kwa-kwalwa, kara ta Saki tana yunkurin tashi hannun ta kuma na sanye da drip, aunty yasmeen bata San time dn da ta katse sallar ba, tayo kanta a guje lokacin har ta koma ta kwanta idonta a rufe tana zubar da wasu hawaye masu radadi sakamakon zugi da ciwon da taji bayanta da hannunta suna bala'in yi mata,da gudu yasmeen tafita ta kira doctor bayan yazo yadubata,yace kar ayi mata wata hayaniya zasu iya ganin ta yanzu wayarta ta dauko takira y sadiq da suka kusan kwana suna sallah da addu'a ga marainiyar Allah wayar na katse wa sai gasu kamar jira suke, bata iya yiwa kowa magana ba sai ido take binsu dashi sune ke faman jera mata sannu daga baya doctor ya kuma yimata allurar bacci ta koma baccinta su kuma suka koma masallaci, ita kam yasmeen bata iya cigaba da sallar ba sai kuka ta zauna tana yi mara sauti tana kara tsanar hali irin na momy.

Tun safe momy ke shirye-shiryen zuwa asibitin da makircin da suka kullah girki ta zauna tayi mai kyau sannan tayi farfesun hanta Wanda a ckn sa tasa gubar,bayan tagama shirya wa tasa driver ya kaita har ckn asibitin ya kaita, da tanbaya tasamu dakin tashiga da sallamar ta fuskar ta dauke da alhini,babu kowa a dakin sai yasmeen data zuba uban tagumi tana kallon yanda numfashin SUHAILAT ke fita a hankali, kwatsam taji muryar momy na sallama, zabura tayi da sauri ta mike tana kallon momy cike da matsanancin mamaki, time dn su dady sun tafi nemo Wanda zai gyara mata karaya tunda doctor muhseen ya basu shawarar ayi gyaran gida, murmushi ne kwance a face dn momy sannan ta karasa shigowa dakin tayi wa kanta matsugunni a 1 daga ckn kujerun dake dakin ta kalli yasmeen tace......

💜pg24💜

Haba yasmeen wane irin kallo kk bina dashi kamar kinga wata bare"?

Ajiyar zuciya yasmeen tayi sannan tace."dolene momy inyi miki wannan kallon saboda bansan dame kk kazo ba ganin ki kadai babban tashin hankali ne a garemu". 
Shiru Momy tayi tana kyafta idonta a kokarin ta nason ta kwa-kulo hawayen munafurci, tayi sa'a kuwa idon nata yaciko da hawaye taki gogewa burinta yasmeen wacce ta sunkuyar da kanta, ta dago taganshi.
Ita kam yasmeen kwayar idonta ma bata bukatar kallon Momy domin irin yanda take jin wani irin tukuki a zuciyarta ta tabbata wannan matar dake gabanta da ba mahaifiyar ta bace da yanzu tayi maganin ta, to me tazo yi musu bayan itace silar komai "?.

Muryar Momy ta tsinta akunnenta cike d rauni,tunda take bata ta6a jin muryar momy haka ba, da sauri ta dago,idonta ta sauke akan momy ga mamakin ta hawaye tagani kwance a face dn momy,abinda ta manta rabonta da taga kukan momy.dai-dai nan y sadiq ya shigo dady na can suna magana da doctor muhseen dakuma ma gyaran karya ganin Momy  da yanayinta ba karamin tsorata yayi ba amma ya danne ba tare da yayi wa kowa magana ba ya nemi guri ya zauna yana kallon yand momy ke matso kwallah." Momy tace. "Yarana, Ku yafe min nayi nadama akan abinda na aikata wa yarinyar nan don Allah kuyi hakuri Ku yafe min ban ta6a jin nayi kuskure akan abinda nake aikata wa ba sai akan wannan, "taja majina tana kara matso kwallar da takusa karewa". Kana gani kasan da karfi ake janyo hawayen, taci gaba da cewa". Jiya bayan na kwanta nayi mafarki wasu manyan halittu ne masu ban tsoro suka tsorata ni akan abinda nake yi sannan suka ce inje in nemi yafiyar ku data yarinyar, na tsorata sosai yanda naga halittun ban ta6a ganin halitta mai girma da muninsu ba ,don Allah Ku yafe min ko zasu barni na huta,mamaki ne ya hanasu magana ckn su babu Wanda ya yadda da abinda momy ke fada sun riga sun san irin makircinta,sai dai abinda ya daure musu kai hawayen dake fita a idonta abinda mamaki, zuba musu ido tayi don ganin yanda suka kar6i lamarin, ganin sunyi shiru babu Wanda ya iya mata magana yasa ta kara canza salon makircin a tunanin ta shirun da sukayi sun yarda da ita,ta tashi daga inda take ta nufi bakin gadon da suhailat ke kwance kamar bata numfashi .

Ganin hakan tasa yasmeen mikewa tsaye tana kafe momy da ido, dan murmushi tayi,tace". Yasmeen baki yadda dani ba ko"? Babu abinda zanyi mata duba jikinta kawai zanyi takai hannunta jikin suhailat ".tace Allah sarki ki yafe min nayi nadama akan abinda na aikata miki wayyo Allah na ya zanyi idan ban samu yafiyarta ba nasan halittu nan kashe ni zasu yi, takara matso wata guntuwar kwallah tana kyafta ido kamar munafuka.


Y sadiq ganin abinda momy ke yi yake kamar a film, ganin ta dage wai ita dole tayi nadama, gashi ko alamar hakan babu a kwayar idonta, da yaga zata 6ata lokaci kuma gashi anytime doctor da dady suna iya shigowa, yasani muddin momy sukayi 4 eyes da dady zai iya yimata mugun tozarci koda kuwa a gaban waye,shi kam bazai so hakan ba koba komai mahaifiyar sace bazai so a tozarta ta gaban mutane ba,koma wane irin hukunci dady zai mata bazai damu ba amma dai zaifi so idan a gida ne,hakane yasa shi karasa wa inda take rike da hannun suhailat tana fadin ta yafe mata,yakalleta "yace.


Momy doctor yace bata bukatar hayaniya ki bari idan tasamu sauki saiki nemi yafiyar amma yanzu zaki iya komawa gida tunda muna kula da ita, momy kallon sa tayi kfn tace".nasan baku yarda dani ba amma ina so kuyi tunani tunda muke ban ta6a aikata wani abu daga baya nazo ina nadama ba,sai akan   ya kamata kuyi tunani, wlh nayi nadama, y sadiq yace to munji kuma mun yadda amma don Allah mony ki koma gida akwai matsala muddin kk tsaya anan ,mamaki ne ya kama momy to me sadiq ke nufi da zai matsa akan ta tafi ko dai korarta yake"?.


Kamar sadiq ya san abinda ke zuciyar ta yace momy ba korarki nake ba doctor ne yace,"kar a dameta da hayaniya kuma kema tunda kinyi nadama baza ki so wani abun yakara samunta ba,takaici ya kamata ji take kamar ta shake yarinyar amma babu damar hakan, abincin da tazo dashi ta mika musu tace". Nasan baku iya cin komai ba shi yasa na yo muku girki ita kuma ga farfesun hanta nayi mata nasan zata fi jin dadinsa".


Yasmeen tun zuwan Momy bata iya yin magana ba sai yanzu, ".tace ".a'a momy kibar abincin nan mu bata shi muke ba ta lpyr ta muke, y sadiq yai saurin kar6ar abincin yana fadin ai dolene muci abinci tunda mu bamu zamu bata lpy ba addu'ar mu kawai take bukata,zuwa yanzu yasmeen tafara jin haushin y sadiq gani take kamar yana goyawa momy baya, komawa tayi ta zauna ba tare da ta furta komai ba don tasan muddin tai magana ba mai dadi bace shi yasa taja bakinta ta tsuke, nan y sadiq ya lalla6a Momy akan ta tafi idan suhailat ta farka sai tazo ta nemi yafiyar da take ikrarin ita take nema ,duk a kokarin sa nason tabar asibitin ba tare da dady ya ganta ba, to dakyar dai ya samu nasarar yi mata wayo ammafa tana kara jadda da mishi ya tabbatar suhailat taci farfesun a cewarta hakan zaisa ta samu karfin jikinta, saida ya tabbatar mata da kansa zai bata sannan tabar dakin, bin bayanta yayi don ganin yanda zata kasance ptn sa Allah yasa kar dady ya ganta.




Babu alamar dady a gurin hakan tasa y sadiq ya dauke 6oyayyir ajiyar zuciya, wajen driver tanufa sai dai ga mamakin ta babu motar ba drivern bata kawo komai a ranta ba ta tari keke nape ta tafi gida cike da Farinciki burinta zai cika tunda taga alamar sadiq ya yadda da ita, duk da yasmeen bata nuna ta yarda ba amma taga jikinta yayi sanyi kuma tunda sadiq yace zai bata farfesun da kansa tasan bazai mata karya ba, nishadi take sosai, ckn zuciyar ta tana fadin yau zaki bar duniya kibi uwar ki, mayyar yariya kawai, a haka har aka kawota gida, motar da aka kaita asibitin ta gani a parking space mamaki ya kamata ,to me yasa bala driver ya dawo ba tare da ya jirataba"?gurin zamansa ta kalla baya nan tayi kwafa tana fadin zaka dawo ne saika fada min dalilinka na tafiya, sannan ta wuce ckn gadan







Tun bayan sun gama tattaunawa da doctor da mai gyara karaya,dady ya nufi hanyar dakin da yaransa ke ciki tun kfn ya karasa shiga yaji muryar Momy a ciki zuciyar shi ta dinga ingiza shi akan ya shiga amma ya daure yasani muddin ya shiga dakin idonsa yai tozali da nata komai zai iya faruwa,bazai so hakan tafaru ba kar hakan ya jawowa suhailat wata matsalar tunda doctor "yace ".bata bukatar hayaniya, da wannan ya samu damar sarrafa zuciyarsa, ya nufi gurin bala driver gidan sa wanda sai bayan ya fito ya lura dashi, shi kansa bala yayi mamaki don bai San da dawowar alhj ba,gida dady yace ya kaishi ,ba tare da 6ata time ba ya kunna motar suka tafi,bayan sun zo dady ".yace baya son ya fadawa kowa ya ganshi ckn girmamawa bala ya amsa sannan ya ajiye motar ya fice, don yasan muddin hjy ta same shi a gidan saita 6ata masa.




Kai tsaye part dn momy dady ya nufa babu kowa a parloun kubra bata gidan ma kaltume ce a daki tana bacci abinta,a parloun dady ya tsaya ya goya  hannunsa a ba ya yana zirga-zirga da alama yana ckn matukar fushi, zuwan Momy kawai yake jira.


Tana sanya kafarta parloun sukayi 4 eyes da dady,wani shocking taji tunda ga tsakiyar kanta har zuwa tafin kafarta,jikinta yafara kyarma hannunta ta daga tana nuna dady ckn in ina tace".al alhj yaushe ka dawo ba sanarwa, jiyowa yai gaba 1 yana takowa inda take, tsananin tsoro da firgici suka dirar mata time 1 ganin yanda face dn alhj babu alamar rahama ganin ya kusa zuwa inda take sai ta juya da sauri a kokarin ta na son guduwa tunda taga kamar ba a haiyacinsa yake ba,kfn tayi wani yunkuri tuni ya cafko ta ya hada kanta da bango,wata iriyar razananniyar kara tasaki wacce tayi amsa kuwwa gidan gaba daya ya amsa, kfn tai zaman da6aro a gurin kanta na mata barazanar tarwa tsewa tsabar radadi da azaba,abinda zuciyarta ke fada mata kenan aikin boka baiyi tasiri a kansa ba gashi daga dawowar shi ya rufeta da duka abinda ta manta yaushe rabonta dashi, yanzu akan rashin daga wayar sa da bata yi ba ya yanke mata wannan hukuncin to ina ga yasamu labarin abinda ta aikatawa gudan jininsa tun bayan tafiyar sa har zuwa yau da takai guba don a bata taci ta mut...kfn ta karasa tunanin ta taji saukar wasu maruka 6arin makauniya masu barazanar tarwatsa mata dodon kunne,tashi take so tayi amma ta kasa saboda azabar da take ji,dady kam idonsa ya rufe babu abinda yake bukata illah ya kassara lpyr momy ko ba komai itama tayi jinya kamar yanda gudan jininsa tayi........

[1/8, 10:56 AM] Misseey: 💜💜SUHAILAT💜💜

Story and written by MARYAM A SALIS (UMMU MASH KUR)


Labarine me cike d tausayi soyayya kishi zamantakewar rayuwa ds.




Bismillahir rahmanirrahim

💜pg25💜

Saida yaga shigar momy napep sannan ya dawo daki yana godewa Allah da yasa basu,hadu da dady ba,zama yayi kusa da yasmeen wacce ta hade rai don ita a tunanin ta y sadiq ya yadda da Momy, kallon ta yayi sannan yace."fushin me kanwata take. "? Kallon sa tayi,da idonta wanda yai ja saboda rashin samun bacci tace."yanzu y sadiq kana nufin ka yadda da momy."? Murmushin karfin hali yayi mata kfn yace." Ba wai na yadda da ita bane nayi hakane saboda kinsan dady yana ckn hospital dn nan kuma muddin ya ganta zai iya yin komai, shi yasa na lalla6ata ta tafi kinga ko kema bazaki so yayi mata wani abun a bainar jama'a ba"shiru yasmeen tayi tasan maganar y sadiq gsky ce,yanda dady yayi matsanancin fushi da momy babu abinda zai hana yayi mata komai a gaban kowa, su kuma baza suji dadin hakan ba tunda ko ba komai Momy mahaifiyar su ce, zasu fi son duk hukuncin da zaiyi yayi a gida tunda ita tajawa kanta.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke kfn ta kalli y sadiq tace. "Hakane kam nasan yanda ta 6ata ran dady muddin ya ganta zai iya aikata komai tunda dady bai iya sarrafa fushin sa ba,amma y sadiq me yasa ka kar6i wannan abincin, nifa gsky ban yadda da abincin nan ba" y sadiq ya kalleta "to ke banda abinki don na kar6i wannan abincin ai bashi yake nuna zamuyi anfani dashi ba, ni kaina ina zargin da akwai wani abu tunda kk ga momy tana kara jadda da min akan na tabbata na bata paper soup dn nan"yai shiru yana kallon food placs dn da abincin ke ciki sannan yace." Kinga ni har naji yunwa ma rabona da abinci tun breakfast bari na tsallaka restaurant dn can in kar6o mana nasan kema zaki ji yunwa,amma wannan ki ajiye shi idan na dawo zan San yanda zanyi dashi don bana son dady ya dawo ya ganshi".fita yayi don kar6o musu abincin,don gsky suna bukatar shi.

 yasmeen ta bude cooler, paper soup dn wani hadadden kamshi ya doki hancinta,yawunta yai bala'in tsinkewa,hannunta tasa ta dauko ta kai bakinta har tafara taunawa taji gabanta na matsanancin faduwa, a lokaci 1 ta tuno maganar y sadiq ai bata San time dn da ta furzo shi daga bakinta ba tashiga bathroom ta wanke bakinta, saida ta tabbatar babu sauran test dn a bakinta, sannan ta shiga bathtub tayi wanka don rabonta da wanka tun jiya.


Y sadiq ya shigo dakin hannunsa rike da leda wacce take cike da kaya,baiga kowa a dakin ba kasancewar yasmeen bata fito daga bathroom ba, juyawar da zaiyi sai ganin muzuru irin Wanda ake ajiye wa a hospitals yana cin paper soup dn da yasmeen ta bude ta manta bata rufe ba,bai hana shi ba tunda yasan ba anfani zasu yi dashi ba,

Zama yayi a 1 daga ckn kujerun dake dakin ya fito da abincin sa yana ci,byn yasmeen ta fito daga bathroom sanye da doguwar riga sabuwa saboda y sadiq duk ya siyo musu abinda zasu bukata a zaman hospital dn, gurin sa ta nufa tana fadin "wlh y sadiq sai yanzu ma naji yunwar tana takuramin amma jiya ko alamunta ba naji" saida ya hadiye na bakinsa kfn yace. "To ai jiya hankalinmu ba'a kwance yake ba, ni kaina ganin abincin momy ne ya tayar min da tsohuwar yunwa". 

Zama tayi itama ta dauki abinda zata ci ta fara ci sannan tace." Wai ya maganar gyaran karayar karfa jikinta yai tsami ba'a gyara ba"sadiq ya kalleta yana goge bakinsa da handkerchief da alama ya gama cin abincin,yace. "To ni dai bansan yanda ake ciki ba mun taho da mai gyaran na barsu suna magana da dady da kuma doctor, yanzu bansan yanda sukayi ba,gashi dady shiru har yanzu ko ina ya tafi oho".

Nan dai suka cigaba da tattauna wa akan abinda yake damunsu,yasmeen na jera kayan da ya shigo dasu a gurin da ake ajiye wa, kamar ance ta kalli karka shin kujera, ta hango gawar katon muzuru,salati tasaki tana kiran y sadiq tare da nuna masa,lekawa yayi,muzurun dazu ya hango kwance ya mimmike alamar babu rai a jikinsa, tashin hankalinsa yafi na yasmeen tunda shi yasan dalilin mutuwar muzurun,saboda yasha paper soup dn momy ne,hawaye ne suka fara xuba daga idonsa zuwa kan cheek dn sa,wani irin rashin imani momy take dashi haka, yanzu da sun bawa yarinyar nan haka zata mutu."?  Mamaki ya kama yasmeen ganin y sadiq na hawaye daga ganin gawar muzuru, ckn damuwa tace." Y sadiq menene yasa ka kuka daga ganin gawar muzuru. "?

Ckn kukan da yasmeen bata ta6a tsammani ba taji sadiq na fadin,"Momy baki kyauta mana ba kincuce kanki kin cutar damu". ckn rashin fahimta yasmeen tace." Y sadiq kasanar dani abinda yake faruwa kasani ckn duhu." Zuwa time dn itama ta fara kuka don tasan ba karamin abu ne zaisa y sadiq irin wannan kukan ba,ckn kukan shima yake sanar da ita,abinda ya gani yakara da cewa."wlh da nasan guba momy tazuba ckn abincin nan da ban hana ta hadu da dady ba duk ma abinda zaimata yayi mata".ita kam yasmeen mutuwar tsaye tayi,jin abinda y sadiq yafada kuma gashi babu karya saida muzurun yagama cinyewa sannan ya mutu sai yanzu ta hango placs dn a gefe ta tuna da yanzu itace taci paper soup dn, wani irin kuka ne ya kwace mata,nan dai aka rasa mai lallashin wani a ckn su, kowa zuciyarsa zafi take masa suna danasanin kasancewar momy mahaifiyar su.





***************************dukan ta kawai yake idonsa ya rufe momy banda kururuwa babu abinda take, tana neman dauki,baba megadi yana ji amma yaki zuwa saboda shi kansa da yana da dama hakan zai mata,don ba karamin haushi ta bashi ba yanda ta gwada rashin imanin ta akan karamar yarinya, saida yaji alhj na shirin yin kisa sannan ya fita waje yasamu wasu mazan don yasan shi kadai alhj bazai saurare shiba,

Koda suka shiga momy ta gama galabaita amma duk da haka dady bai kyaleta ba,dakyar suka samu nasarar rike shi,da rarrafe momy ke tafiya zata gudu dakinta, kwatsam taji muryar dady yana fadin. "Ki gaggawar fitar min a gida don kuwa na sakeki saki 2 bazan iya zama da wacce bazata iya rike min jinina ba".ckn mamaki baba me gadi ke fadin.
" Ahsha haba alhj me yasa ka yanke wannan danyen hukuncin ckn fushi. "?

Dady ya kalleshi har yanzu jikinsa rawa yake irin ta tsananin 6acin rai, yace.
"Malam musa wlh ba don haramcin saki 3 lokaci 1 ba da shi zanyi mata" ya juya ya barsu a gurin ya nufi part dn sa, suma dole suka bar parloun.


Momy kam dukan da dady yayi mata bai dameta ba irin hukuncin sakin da yayi mata,kuma duk akan wannan tsinanniyar yarinyar, tuno da cewa ta kai mata guba kuma tasan babu shakkah sadiq zai bata,yasa duk da irin zugi da radadi da zuciyarta da jikinta ke mata hakan bai hana tayi murmushi ba Wanda idan mutum ya gani sai yaji tsoro saboda yanda dady ya canza mata kamanni,tashi take son tayi amma jikinta yayi mata masifar nauyi,

kaltume da kubra da suke la6e tun farkon abin suka kasa fitowa don kar a hada dasu ita kubra bata ma gidan dawowa tayi ta tarar da abinda ke faruwa,ita kuwa kaltume bacci ma take ihun momy ne ya tasheta, karaso wa suka yi suka kamata dakyar suka iya kaita daki kaltume ta hada mata ruwan zafi don ta gasa jikinta haka ta shiga tana dingishi,koda taje gurin wanka kasa daga hannunta tayi sai yanzu ta lura da kumbura da yayi, ga wani irin zugi dayake mata,da alamar tasamu targade ko gocewar kashi,kuka ta saki tana kara tsinewa dady da ma suhailat tunda duk saboda ita alhj yayi mata hakan gashi harda tsarabar Saki bayan yafi kowa sanin bata da kowa kuma bata da inda zata je,tunawa tayi da yanayinsa tasan muddin batayi gaggawar fitar masa a gida ba to zai iya dawowa, don haka ta lalla6a ta gasa jikinta sannan ta fito samun su tayi sunyi cirko-cirko cike da jimamin abinda ya faru,......

💜pg26💜

Kallon su momy tayi tana kara rike hannunta dake mata azabar radadi, ba tare da tayi wai kowa magana ba tawuce,wajen wardrobe dn ta, kayanta tasa ckn dabara kasancewar hannunta yana mata zafi,gaba 1 hankalinta baya jikinta tunanin inda zata je kawai take,kubra ta watso mata tanbayar da tasa ta kasa magana tace. "Yanzu Momy ina zaki tafi."? Kallon ta kawai Momy keyi don bata da amsar da zata bata,gane hakan tasa kaltume tace." Hjy da zaki amince mu tafi garinmu dake ki huta daga baya sai mu san abin yi,dolene a dauki mataki akan wannan matsalar data tun kareki" 
Shiru Momy tayi tana tunanin maganar kaltume,bata da wata mafita data wuce amince wa, saboda ita bata da inda zata je mahaifanta duk sun rasu,y'an uwanta kuwa babu Wanda take masa abin arziki, tun suna zuwa wajen ta tana musu wulakanci har kowa ya gaji ya watsar da ita tasan yanzu duk wanda ta dosa ba kar 6arta zasuyi ba, don haka tace. "Ba komai kaltume na amince zan biki garin naku tunda ni yanzu bani da kowa, amma wlh dolene sai alhj ya dawo a tafin hannu na ko zanyi yawo tsirara,sai na rama wannan rashin mutuncin da ya min a gaban me gadi da makota kuma wai duk akan wannan tsinanniyar y'ar tasa"kaltume tace. "karki damu hjy ina da Wanda zai miki maganin shi, da duk Wanda kk so ya dinga bin umarninki" ckn sauri Momy tace. "Da gsk kk kaltume aikuwa da naji dadi, ammafa alhj yana da matukar taurin kai asiri bai fiye kamashi ba" murmushi kaltume tayi sannan tace. "Ai hjy komai taurin kansa wannan bokan zai iya maganin sa don shi ba asiri yake ba tsafi yake kashe mutum ko ya maida shi wata dabbar baya masa wuya bare kuma wannan dn karamin aikin naki"

 Duk da momy tana ckn matukar tashin hankali hakan bai hana ta saki fara'a ba jin cewar burinta zai cika,kallon kaltume tayi tace. "Na dauki alkawari idan komai nawa zai kare a duniyar nan saina mallaki zuciyar alhj koda zanyi yawo tsirara " 

Sallamar me gadi suka ji a bakin kofa kubra ce tasamu damar amsawa,yace. "Hjy alhj yace Ku fito zai kulle gidan" kallon juna suke cike d mamaki,ckn ranta take fadin," Ashe da gsk alhj yake ya sake ni to nufinsa har su kubra zamu bar masa gidan."? Tayi wa kanta tanbayar da bata da babu amsa kfn daga baya tasamu damar bawa mai gadi amsa,tace. "To gamu nan fitowa" sannan ta dubi kubra tace. "Kema dole ki bimu tunda kinga yace rufe gidan sa zai yi" kubra ta kalli Momy sannan tace. "Gsky momy bazan biku ba, zan tafi gidan su meimy har ku dawo, kinga idan na tafi za'a wuce ni a makaranta"

 momy tace."to shi kenan kije ki shirya, saimu fita tare"kubra ta tafi dakinta don ta shirya itama kaltume ta nufi nata dakin,yayin da suka bar Momy tana shiya kayanta a wata matsakaiciyar handbag, duk abinda tasan zai mata anfani saida ta dauka,sannan tafito daga dakin tana zubda kwallar takaici wai itace yau aka saka kuma bata da gurin zuwa, tsanar suhailat ta Kara d'arsuwa a zuciyarta, ji take da zata samu dama da babu abinda zai hana taje har hospital dn taga abinda ke faruwa, shin sun bata paper soup dn ko kuma basu bata ba, tasake dabara amma yanzu duk ba tada wannan damar.



Magana taji ana yi mata,ta dago kubra da kaltume ta gani ko wacce rike da bags da alamun sun gama shirya wa, kubra ta matso tana goge wa Momy kwallar idonta sannan tace. "Kiyi hakuri momy nasan kina ckn damuwa amma kiyi hakuri  lokaci ne nasan ba zaki dawo gidan nan da wannan damuwar ba sai dai farin ciki"kaltume tace." musamman idan nakaita gurin dn tsitaka nakan dutse ai magana ta kare"
Momy tana son taji maganar cikar burinta,ko da ckn damuwa take yanzu zata samu wal-wala,don haka tace."wlh da zarar burina ya cika kaltume daga ke har iyayenki zan muku babbar kyauta wacce zaku dade kuna mamaki "

Itama kaltume ckn farinciki tace. "Tabbas hjy nasan zaki alfahari dani idan na kaiki gurin sa"

Wayar kubra ta fara ringing alamar tana bukatar dauki, sallama tayi musu ba tare da ta dauki wayar ba don tasan najib ne ke kiranta, saboda ta fada mishi yazo ya dauke ta zata kai masa ziyara gidan sa.   

bayan sunyi sallama suma suka fita don samun abin hawa don kuwa garin su kaltume ba karamin nisa ne dashi ba,can ckn kauyen illorin, basu sha wata wahala ba suka samu motar da zata kaisu tasha,suna zuwa nanma babu 6ata time suka samu motar illorin, driver yaja suka dau hanya, momy sai kallon hanya take don bata ta6a zuwa garin  ba.







***************************mlm musa megadi yana ganin sun fita daga gidan ya garkama katon kwado a part dn Momy wannan umarnin dady ne,ya dawo wajen zaman sa ya zauna yana tuna irin abinda yaji su Momy na fada sanda yaje sanar dasu sakon dady,tabbas wannan matar bata da hankali, koda yake daman hausawa sunce Wanda yayi nisa baya ta6a jin kira,kalli irin duka da hukuncin da alhj ya yanke mata amma duk hakan baisa tayi nadama ba sai ma sabon shirin da takeyi akan wannan bawan Allah, kai wasu matan babu Allah a zuciyar su, haka baba me gadi ya cigaba da tunanin sa wannan kenan.





***************************
Dady bayan yagama dukan Momy wanka yaje yayi sannan yasanar da me gadi ya fadawa rabi tafitar masa a gida bayan ya bashi umarnin datse part dn da zarar sun fita.

Hospital dn ya koma yasamu lokacin ana ckn yiwa suhailat gyaran karayar da ta samu duk da an mata allurar kashe zugi hakan baisa ta daina jin kamar ana zare mata numfashinta ba,ihu take sosai ko muryar ta bata fita saboda tsananin azaba.

Aunty yasmeen da y sadiq suna reception suma ckn damuwa da tashin hankalin da momy ta haddasa musu, sai dai sunyi alkawarin baza su sanar da dady ba,don kar hankalinsa ya kara tashi  ya kuma tsanar momy,saboda su basu San ya ga momyn ba a hospital dn tunanin su wani gurin yaje, sai dai ganinsa ya canza kaya ba karamin mamaki abun ya basu ba.

Gurin da suke ya karasa yana kallon yanayinsu kamar a kwai wata sabuwar damuwa  a tare dasu, tanbayar su yafara yi,suka ce masa ba komai, tausayin suhailat suke wacce zuwa wannan lokacin angama gyara mata karayar an mata allurar bacci dakyar baccin ya dauke ta saboda tsabar azabar da tasha.


Dady ya nufi office dn  doctor fahad Wanda yanzu shine ke kula da lafiyar suhailat tun bayan da doctor muhseen yai tafiya,koda ya shiga yasame shi suna magana da Wanda ya gyara mata karayar,ya shiga office dn da sallamar sa,suka amsa yasamu gurin zama, yadubi,doctor fahad yace. "Doctor ya ake ciki."?doctor yace. " Alhamdu lillahi an gyara mata karayar kuma insha'allah muna ptn samun nasara akan wannan aikin, amma nan da gobe zamu fahimci aikin yayi kyau ko da matsala wacce bama fata".
Dady yace."muna ptn Allah yasa asamu nasarar aikin". gaba 1 suka hada baki wajen fadin "ameen".
Kallon me gyaran dady yayi kfn yace." To mlm me za'a baka na aikin."?.

Mlm yace."ai doctor fahad ya sallame ni". Ckn mamaki dady ke kallon doctor fahad,kfn yayi magana doctor ya riga shi da cewa. "Karka damu dady mudai ptn mu Allah y bata lpy". Suka amsa da ameen.


 To bayan dady yafito daga office, dakin da suhailat ke kwance ya nufa anan yasamu su yasmeen sun sakata gaba suna kallon yanda aka daure mata hannunta, shima ckn tausayi yake binta da kallo ckn kwana 2 duk ta fita haiyacinta y'ar kibar da tayi duk ta zube, goge y'ar kwallar takaici yayi, kfn daga baya ya nemi guri ya zauna,ya kalli yasmeen yace." Yakamata kije gida ki dauko duk abinda zaku bukata yanzu nima daga can nake,tunda muna nan zamu kula da ita kfn ki dawo ". 

Da mamaki suke kallon sa musamman da yace daga gida yake, to ko dai sun  hadu da momy ne."?.tanbayar dake ckn ransu ke nan amma babu damar yi, a hankali yasmeen ta mike ta shirya ta nufi gida .





Tun farkon shigar ta gidan tasan wani abu ya faru saboda jin shiru yayi yawa baba megadi ne kawai a gidan, bayan ta gaishe shi ta wuce part dn su tanufa ,saida ta yi wanka sannan ta hada musu duk abinda zasu bukata ta fito, har ta kusa zuwa bakin get, sai kuma ta koma, wannan Karon part dn momy ta nufa,tun daga nesa ta hango katon kwado,abinda bata ta6a gani ba,koda Momy bata nan,ba'a rufe part dn.

Jikinta a matukar sanyaye tanufi gurin baba megadi tace." Baba megadi wani abun ya faru a gidan nan zuwan dady."?

Mlm musa,yace."kwarai kuwa zuwan alhj gidan nan yau ya tayar mana da hankali don gaba 1 hankalinsa baya jikinsa" .

Samun guri yasmeen tayi ta zauna don tana son jin abinda ya faru tace. "Me ya faru."?. Baba megadi ya fara bata labarin duk abinda ya faru.....

💜pg27💜

Babu abinda baba megadi ya 6oyewa yasmeen tun shigowar dady gidan har zuwa abinda ya faru tsakaninsa da Momy, da irin shawarar da yaji Momy da kaltume su nayi lokacin da yaje sanar musu sakon dady,duk saida ya fada mata.

Damuwa da 6acin rai babu Wanda yasmeen batayi ba, Sam bata damu akan abinda dady yayi wa Momy ba,a cewarta momyn ce tajawa kanta, damuwar ta yanda baba megadi ya sanar da ita wai Momy tabi kaltume kaltume garinsu kuma ga dukkan alamu yanayin magan-ganun da baba megadi yaji suna yi batayi nadama ba, wacce irin Mahaifiya Allah ya basu mai yawan son kanta bata duba lahirarta.

Hawayen da take makalewa ya samu nasarar zubowa kan cheek dn ta,bata damu da goge wa ba saboda kukan ne kadai zai sa tasamu nutsuwar zuciyar ta, haka hawaye yaci gaba d zuba kamar an bude fanfo.

Mlm musa baiyi yunkurin hana taba saboda yasan kukan zai rage mata wata damuwar, shi kansa da yana da damar kukan yi zaiyi saboda tunda yake a rayuwar duniya bai ta6a ganin mace me taurin kai da rashin imani da tausayi irin hjy ba yanzu duk irin wannan mummunan dukan da alhj yayi mata bata risuna ba saima kar dulmiya kanta ckn 6ata take,Allah ya kyauta ya fada a zuciyar sa ya juyo yana kallon inda yasmeen ke zaune idonta rufe hawaye na ambaliya a kuncinta, ganin yanda take wannan kukan zai iya haddasa mata wata matsalar yasa ya fara lallashinta tare da yimata nasiha mai ratsa zuciya, wacce ta sanya jikinta yai bala'in sanyi, yakara da cewa.


"Wannan kukan naki bashi ne mafita ba akan wannan al'amari addu'a itace mafita Ku dage wajen yiwa mahaifinku daku kanku addu'a akan Allah y kare shi daga dukkan abin ki,sannan ita ma mahaifiyar taku ku dinga yi mata addu'a akan Allah y shiryeta ta dawo kan madai-daiciyar hanya, amma kuka bazai ta6a zama mafita a gareku ba,nima zan taya ku addu'ar kiyi hakuri kinji kibar kukan".


Yasmeen ta dago kwayar idonta da ta rikide ta koma jajur maimakon fara qal,saboda tsabar kuka kanta yana wani mugun Sara mata ta kalli baba megadi tace." Mungode sosae baba insha'allah zamu dage da addu'a ngd da nasiharka a gareni Allah y kara girma ".

Baba megadi yace." Ba komai y'ata patana Allah yaba suhailat lpy yarinya mai hankali da ganin girman manta".yasmeen tace."Ameen baba"ta mike ta koma dakinsu bathroom ta shiga ta wanke face dn ta,tafito ko kallon inda mirror yake ba tayi ba,yasmeen sarkin makeup amma yau kusan 3 days kenan bata kalli face dn ta jikin mirror ba idan wani ne yace zata kwana1 ba tayi kwalliya ba zata karyata amma yau gata kwana 3 ba kwalliya, lallai kam komai samun dama ne.


Ta fito daga part dn rike da kayan da ta dauko tazo gurin baba megadi tace. "To baba ni zan koma hospital dn" mlm musa ya kalle ta cike da tausayin ta, har ga Allah yana tausayin yaran nan yanda Allah ya jarabce su da Momy a matsayin uwa,yace. "To don Allah ki gaida min da me jikin ina mata sannu kfn nazo"tace." To baba insha'allah zan sanar da ita mungode ".tafada tana kokarin fita daga gidan.






***************************
Kubra tun bayan sunyi sallama da su Momy, hanyar da suka saba haduwa da najib ta nufa tana tafe tana waiwaye na rashin gsky tun daga nesa ta hango motar sa,da saurin ta takarasa fadawa ckn motar daman already ya bude door dn motar tana shiga ya figi motar suka bar gurin, saida sukayi nisa kfn ya juyo inda take makale a gefensa,yana murmushi yace." To yanzu sweet ina muka nufa ko gidan su meimy zamu je"?ya karasa maganar yana wani dage gira sama,kallon sa tayi tana murmushi tace."Haba hony yanzu ai mun wuce ajin zuwa gidan su meimy gidan ka zaka kaini,ziyara na kawo maka ta musamman "itama dage girar tayi kamar yanda yayi,yace." Da gsk sweet"?. daga masa kai tayi,yace "kice zamu huta" batayi magana ba sai hannunta da ta sakalo a wuyansa.

Saida ya biya wani restaurant yayi musu order abincin da zasu ci don ya dade da sanin kubra bata iya komai a 6angaren kichin ba,bayan ya fito daga gurin, bai shiga motar ba sai ya leka,yace. "Sweet ko a kwai abinda kk bukata"?. Lumshe mayatattun idonta tai da alama abin nata ya taso mata,tace," a halin yanzu babu abinda nake bukata sai kai hony".murmushin jin dadi yayi daman yasan za'a rina tunda yaga tana wani lumshe idonta yasan jarabarta ta motsa,shiga ckn motar yayi ya xauna tare da kullo kofar motar,dora kanta tayi bisa  cinyarsa, shi kuma ya sunkoyo yana shin shina wuyanta, kamshin turarensa da kuma irin salon da yake mata  ya kara je fata ckn matsanancin son kasancewa dashi, bata San lokacin da ta cafko lips dn sa ba itama tana aika masa da irin nata salon, sun dauki tsawon lokaci a haka kfn daga baya yayi kokarin jan motar subar gurin, ita kam gaba 1 jikinta ya gama mutuwa tayi lamo kan cinyarsa, sunkuyawa yayi dai-dai saitin kunnenta yace. "Kwana nawa zaki yi min"?. bata dago ba balle tayi yunkurin bashi amsa don a yanayin da take ciki ko magana bazata iya ba.


Bai damu ba saboda ya riga yasan yanayin mutuniyar tashi idan ta shiga wannan yanayin hankalinta ma ba'a jikinta yake ba, shi kanshi ya matsu su karasa gidan, don shima baya gajiya da ita,dukda bada ita kadai yake mu'amala ba amma yasan ita dn ta musamman ce a gurinsa koba komai shine mutum na farko daya fara kusantarta ya koyar da ita duk wani abu da yake so mace tayi masa domin jin dadin sa,kuma duk ta dauki karatun nasa,shi yasa yafi jin dadin harka da ita.

Yana isa gidan ya nufi madai-daicin parking space  dn gidan Wanda ba zai wuce a perker mota 3 ba,  ya perker motar, har zuwa lokacin idonta a rufe yake bata San yazo gidan ba,saiji tayi yadaga ta sama kamar baby,kai tsaye bedroom dn sa yanufa direct yana zuwa yai mata muhalli bisa madai-daicin gadonsa,jinta akan gado ya sanya ta dan bude idonta a hankali, zuwa lokacin ya shiga bathroom, hakane ya bata damar karewa dakin kallo kasancewar bata ta6a zuwa gidan ba,gidan ba wani babba bane sai dai ankashe kudi wajen qawata shi,haka dakin babu wani shirgi,amma yanda aka tsara shi ya mugun birgeta,dai-dai lokacin ya fito daga bathroom dn kai tsaye inda take ya nufa,daman shi daga shi boxes, ba tare da 6ata lokaci ba suka fara gabatar da abinda ya tarasu a gurin (Allah ya shirya).







***************************
Momy an isa illorin lpy, tun saukar su daga mota take faman raba idanu,kamar Mara gsky, musamman yanda taga kabilu,daban-daban kowa yana harkar gabanshi, kaltume ta kama hannunta suka nufi wata rumfa,suka zauna, da mamaki momy ke kallon ta kfn tace."Zaman me zamuyi bayan munzo,garin yanzu ba gidanku zamu tafi ba"?.murmushi kaltume tayi sannan tace. "Hjy ai sai munjira motar kauyen mu saida yamma take zuwa,kinga wadannan mutanen duk ita suke jira".da mamaki momy ke kallon runfar da take nuna mata,mutane sunfi 50 amma yanzu ace mota 1 suke jira wannan wacce irin babbar mota ce.


Kallon kaltume tayi sannan tace." Wannan motar amma babba ce kalli mutanen gurin nan amma kice mota 1 ake jira,zanso inga girman mortar nan".ta karasa maganar tana dora hannunta kan bakinta alamar mamaki, dariya sosai kaltume tayi,sannan tace mata."Hjy mortar fa ba wata babba bace,irin motar nan ce wacce zaki ga samanta a bude,ina nufin akori kura wani kan wani haka ake zamanta kuma maza da mata".ai momy bata San lokacin da ta mike tsaye ba,tana zare ido kamar ta ace ta kare da hawan irin wannan motar gsky ba zai yiwu ba,dolene asamu mafita, itama kaltume mikewa tayi tana kallon yanda lokaci 1 hankalin hjy ya tashi, daga jin yanda ake hawa mota, kuma a hakan ne take cewa zata iya zuwa wajen boka dn tsurku nakan dutse,gsy a kwai matsala


Momy ta kalle ta cike da damuwa tace." yanzu babu wata mafita sai mun hau motar nan"?. Shiru kaltume tayi don ita duk zuwa garinsu da take a wannan motar take zuwa, can kuma ta tuna da wata dabara tasan idan tasamu sarkin tasha zai iya Samar musu da mafita sai dai tasan zasu ci kudi,murmushi tayi da tuno da cewa itama zata samu rabonta don tunda take da hjy bata ta6a daukar kudi ta bata da sunan kyauta ba,don haka dolene itama tasamu rabonta, dagowa tayi tare da kallon momy wacce ta zuba mata ido tana jiran taji abinda zata ce,murmushi kaltume tayi kfn tace. "Nasamu mafita hjy zanje wajen sarkin tasha nasan bazai rasa yanda za'a yi ba,ammafa nasan zasu caji kudi da yawa". Murmushin jin dadi Momy tayi jin za'a Samar musu mafita, tace." Haba kaltume kinsan dai kudi not in my problem don haka kawai kije kiji yanda zakuyi dashi ko nawa suke bukata zan basu indai bazan hau motar daukar shanu ba".dariya kaltume tayi sannan ta wuce wajen neman sarkin tasha,ita kuma momy ta samu guri ta zauna tana jiran dawowar kaltume.


Bata sha wuya wajen neman shi ba, daman sun dade da sanin juna,kfn kaltume ta tafi aikatau,suna y'an harkokinsu dashi tsohon dn duniya ne, yana ganin ta ya sallama mutanen gurin ya karaso yana washe hakora,hade ranta tayi sosae saboda ita yanzu ta wuce class dn sa a cewarta, kfn tayi magana ya riga ta da cewa. "Kaltume manyan mata anje kano an manta damu". Kara tsuke face dn ta tayi wai ita a lallai kar ya rainata, ckn ya tsina da gadara da nuna cewa itama fa wata ce,tayi masa bayani, yace." indai da kudi babu matsala ko su kadai suke so a dauka to za'a Kaisu".nan dai suka yi ciniki yace 5k zasu biya kaltume ta dage da kyar ya bar mata, 4k aikuwa a take tabude Jakarta,tana sane ta dauko kudin da yawa don Tara kudin albashinta take tunda babu abinda ta nema ta rasa,ganin irin yawan kudin sarkin tasha yace. "Ah,lallai kaltume yanzu kk babbar mace,irin wannan manyan kudi haka dolene ki guje mu". Cike da gadara take mika mishi 4k dn kar6a yayi yana juya su a hannunsa, sannan yace." Ranki ya dade hajjaju ki dn karo ko 500 ce".jin yanda ya girmama ta,daman burinta kenan ta zama hjy ta juya ckn nera,1k ta wurga mishi cike da isa,ya dauka yana kiran wani driver Wanda zai kaisu har ckn kauyen.

Juya wa tayi don sanar da Momy yanda sukayi dashi, samunta tayi inda ta barta, zaune tana faman kalle2 kaltume ta zauna kusa da ita tana murmushi tace. "Hjy mun samu motar amma sunce 20k za'a basu tunda mu kadai za'a dauka, sannan hjy nayi miki wani karanbani,nace su suyo mana kayan abinci masu kyau tunda nasan idan muka je bazaki iya cin abincin mu ba tunda baki saba ba kuma ba'a samun kayan abincin da kk so sai anan kiyi hakuri idan bakiji dadin hakan ba".


Ckn murmushi Momy tace karki damu daman inason tanbayar ki abinda za'a yiwa iyayenki tsaraba to kinga shi kenan sai akai musu abincin idan ma Wanda kk ce a kawo bai isa ba to a karo". Ta Ciro kudi 100k ta bata tace." Kije dasu duk abinda kk ga ya dace ki siya ".murna kamar me haka kaltume ta tafi da kudin,ta dai siyi kayan na 30k sauran ta zuba Jakarta,bayan driven da zai Kaisu ya gama loda kayan suma suka hau motar, babu laifi motar tana da rufin sama amma itama ta daukar kaya ce,haka suka fara tafiya suka fara shiga ckn daji.............

Kuma ya kudiri niyyah ko ba yanzu ba saiya sameta...

Alhamdu lillahi jikin suhailat yayi kyau tana kara samun sauki sosae don yanzu tana iya tashi zaune da kanta harma ayi hira da ita,wannan yasa hankalinsu yafara kwanciya, ganin ta fara samun sauki, kulawa sosae suke bata kowa burinsa tayi farinciki, ganin ta dn samu lpy yasa dady yace sadiq ya koma aikinsa,dakyar ya yadda ya koma sai dai kullum kfn ya tafi saiya zo yaga jikinta haka idan ya dawo.

Aunty yasmeen ce ke zuwa gida tayi musu abinci don kuwa dady baya son cin abinci a waje sai dole,shi kam dady babu inda yake matsawa kullum yana makale da gudan jininsa mafi soyuwa a gareshi,kasancewar su tare koda yaushe ya haifar musu da wata sabuwar shakuwa ta musamman,wacce duk cikin y'ay'an sa babu Wanda sukayi irin wannan shakuwar.

Doctor fahad yana matukar kula da ita da zarar time dn shan maganin ta yayi da kansa yake zuwa ya bata don ya lura Sam bata kaunar shan maganin, haka zaizo da wasa da tsokanarta,saiya tabbatar da tasha sannan zai kyaleta allura kam sai yayi da gsk kfn ya samu yayi mata,yana gama mata zai zauna yaita tsokanarta, hakan tasa, dady yake sonshi,ko abinci taki ci sai dady ya Kira shi aikuwa ckn ikon Allah zai zauna yana mata bar kwanci,har bata San lokacin da zata cinye abincin ba, ita kanta tana son doctorn ya shigo don ko ba komai zaisa tayi nishadi da abubuwan ban dariyarsa.




Doctor fahad kabeer shine sunan sa,wanda friends dn sa suka sa masa FK mutum ne muskili first class 
kfn kaji yayi magana za'a dade,bare kaga dariyar sa,kyakkyawa ne bashi da wata makusa yana da kowa ne irin qoleties da mace zata mato a kansa, Sam baya kula y'an mata, sune ke bibiyar sa baya kulasu, amma tun lokacin da ya kar6i files dn suhailat yaji yarinyar ta bashi tausayi, saboda halin da yaganta a ckn sa, musamman da aka sanar dashi dalilin faruwar lamarin,shi yasa yake sakar,mata fuska,jininsa ya hadu da nata,abin har mamaki yake bashi  saboda ya saba ganin marasa lpy kala-kala wadanda suka fi ta abin tausayi amma bai ta6a jin tausayi, irin yanda yake jin nata ba.



Zamansa da ita yana tuna baya lokacin yana ckn farinciki da wal-wala babu abinda yake damunsa,kfn yanzu shi mutum ne me raha da barkwanci,bashi da wata matsala, tun wata rana da wani mutum ya fada masa wata magana ya shiga ckn kunci da damuwa, ya zama miskili baya kula kowa,aikin sa kawai yasa a gaba, ita kanta mahaifiyar sa,canzawar da taga danta yayi abin ya dameta,kuma ta tanbayeshi yaki sanar da ita abinda yake damunsa amma ckn y'an wanakinnan wal-wala ta maye gurbin miskilancin, musamman idan ya dawo daga aiki tana lura dashi har smyln zaka ga yanayi shi kadai, canjin nasa yasa itama hankalinta ya kwanta don yanzu har zama yake suyi hira abinda ta manta rabnda yayi hakan.



***************************
Kubra ansha soyayya an more don kusan wuni sukai manne suna bawa Kansu kulawa,kfn daga baya su hakura ba don komai ba sai don yunwar da tafara da munsu,tare sukayi wanka sannan najib ya dauko musu abincin sukayi feeding junansu,bayan sun gama babu Wanda a ckn su yai maganar sallah, suka zauna a parlour suna kallo, abinsu,suna ckn kallon ammar ya masa waya yana tanbayar inda yake ba tare da 6ata lokaci ba yasanar masa yana gida tare da kubra, wani ihu ammar yasa ta ckn wayar kfn yace,"Shege mutumina kace yau ka gwan-gwaje ni ina can ina fama da shago".saida najib ya saci kallon kubra wacce tayi matashi da cinyarsa kfn yace. "Ai mutumina in ana sallah ba'a magana, yau kam kamar sabon ango haka nake". Ammar yai kasa damurya alamar roko,yace, "oga ko nima in shigo da tawa babyn mu dan gyagije"?. Da yake najib mahaifinsa babban dn kasuwa ne hakan tasa mahaifin nashi ya bude mishi babban shagon provision ya zuba masa kaya masu kyau da tsada,amma Sam najib baya zama a shagon saiya wakilta ammar da yake shi iyayensa basu da hali da wannan yake ciyar dasu, sauran yayi sharholiyarsa dasu, shi kam najib idan ka ganshi a shago to kudi zai kar6a yaje ya kashe wa y'an mata, bai damu da lissafin abinda ya shigo dawanda zai fita ba,ya riga ya yadda da ammar, shima kuma ammar dn baya cutar shi ya rike mishi amana saboda shima yasan ya taimaka masa a rayuwa, ammar yana da halin kirki sai dai wannan miguwar dabi'ar ta bin mata itama najib ne ya dora shi a hanyar, duk lokacin da ammar yake son wani abun a gurin najib haka yake kwantar da murya, yana kiransa oga,hakan yana yiwa najib dadi don shi mutum ne mai son a girmamashi,shi yasa duk bukatar da ammar yazo da ita indai ya kira shi da oga zai iya yi masa komai.

Wannan karon ma bukatar tasa ta samu kar6uwa,don yace masa suzo mana ba damuwa kashe wayar yayi, ya sunkuya yana kallon ta yanda ta rufe idonta kamar me bacci, murmushi yayi sannan yace,"Sweet kinyi bacci ne"?.ya fada yana shafa gashin kanta, najib ya matukar iya soyayya in mace tana tare dashi bazata so su rabuba,don yanzu haka kubra ji take kamar su biyu ne kadai a duniya, saboda tsabar shiga ckn shauki, a hankali take bude idonta ta kalle shi tace, "banyi bacci ba ina nan sai kawata tazo tu kunna". Yace, " ai yau babu bacci a tsarina don haka kema bazaki yi shi ba"haka dai suka cigaba da hirarsu har su ammar suka zo bayan sun dade suna hira kowa yaja budurwarsa daki suka cigaba da abinda suka saba.(Allah ya shirya)





***************************
Momy ana ta bakunta,batada matsalar komai don yanzu babu abinda yake gabanta sai muradinta na zuwa wajen dn tsurku, musamman da taji yanda kaltume da inna suke kara zu zuta mata aikin sa,shi yasa ma kwata-kwata, bata damu da matsalon gidan ba,tunda burinta zai cika ko a inane zata iya rayuwa...

Haka suka fara tafiya suka fara shiga daji...

Continue

Tafiya ce mai nisa sukayi kfn suka iso, dn karamin kauyen su kaltume,momy sai kallo take sha abinta, kaltume ce,ta dinga nuna wa driven hanya, har suka zo,kofar gidan su, juyo wa momy tayi,jin kubra tace wa drivern ya tsaya, idonta ya sauka akan wani karamin gida, ginin kasa, wanda rabin gatangar ya zube,da ace ba'a saka kara ba,mutum yana waje zai iya hango na ckn gidan,takaici da haushin kanta ya kamata da ace batayi wa danginta wulakanci ba da tasan zasu kar6eta amma yanzu dubi gurin da ta kawo kanta ko fakirin ckn zuri'arsu yafi karfin sa wai a hakan ma taimakon ta akayi.

 Kaltume ce ta fara sauka daga motar,sannan ta kalli momy,taga yanda take bin gidan su da wani irin mayataccen kallo ko dauke idonta ba tayi, haushi ya kamata aranta  tace."a ckn sa dai zaki zauna sai sanda naga dama zamu tafi". A fili kuma zuwa tayi kusa da ita tace. "Hjy kin gaji ko"?. "Ki sauko mun karaso gidan". Firgit momy ta dawo daga diniyar tunanin da ta fada, sannan ta kwakulo murmushin karfin hali tace." To bari na sauko kin san da gajiya don tunda nake ban ta6a zuwa garin nan ba".bayan ta sauko,suka shiga gidan, driver da yaran kauyen wadanda tunda suka ga tsayuwar motar,suka zagaye ta,suna kallo kamar yau suka fara ganin irnta.


Da sallama suka shiga gidan momy kuwa jikinta ya mutu murus,kamar kazar da kwai ya fashe mata, wata dattijuwa suka gani tana,tuka tuwo, kamanninsu da kaltume shi zai baka, damar gane ita dn mahaiya ce a gareta,da dn gudunta ta karasa cike da murna, tana fadin Inna ta, gani na dawo itama innar farinciki ne,kwance a fuskar ta,don kusan shekara kenan y'ar tata bata zo ganinsu ba.


Ita kam Momy tana tsaye inda take tana Kara bin gidan da kallo, Sam hankalin Inna bai kai,kanta ba, hankalinta nakan kayan da ake shigowa dasu,bude baki tayi tana kallon kaltume kfn tace. "Keko yar nan ina kk samu kudin siyan wannan kaya masu uban yawa haka"?. Sai yanzu kaltume ta tuno tare da hjy suke, ta juyo Inda Momy ke tsaye, tace." Lah,hjy kiyi hakuri wlh duk ganin Inna ne,yasa na manta, tare muke".Momy tace."ba komai nasan kunyi kewar juna ne".inna wacce sai yanzu ta kula da momy ta karaso inda suke tana fadin."lale barka da zuwa ke kaltume wane irin sakarci ne wannan kin San ba ke 1 bace amma kk zo kina shirme kin barta a tsaye ". Murmushi Momy tayi tace ba komai wlh ". Sabuwar ta barmar kaba,Inna ta dauko,momy na tsaye still saida ta share gurin kfn ta shinfida ta barmar,Momy ta zauna cike da kyankyami don dai babu yadda ta iya, da bazata zauna ba.


Sai bayan ta zauna sannan inna itama ta zauna a kujera y'ar tsugonno,suka gaisa,kaltume tace." Inna itace hjy rabi wacce nake aiki a gudanta,tace tana so taga garinmu, sannan zan raka ta gurin boka dn tsurku".inna tace."ah to madallah gashi bamu San da zuwan ku ba,ai da mun tanadi gurin da zata kwana da abinda zata ci,nasan bazata iya cin irin abincin mu ba".kaltume tace "karki damu inna,indai gurin kwana ne bazai wuya ba yanzu zan gyara mata wancan dakin, abinci kuma gashi nan mun taho da abinmu". Inna tace, "to ba komai tashi ki gyara mata saiki dauko katifar sunusi (kanin kaltume) saboda sabuwa ce kwanan baya ya siyeta". Mikewa kaltume tayi ta fada dakin ta fara gyaran ta barsu tsakar gida, Inna tana dan jan momy da hira,ita kam Sam hankalinta baya gurinta tana can ta fada duniyar tunanin halin da take ciki.


Inna ta dubeta tace." hjy ke kuwa mai yasa kk son zuwa wajen boka dn tsurku,gashi ke babu abinda kk nema kk rasa, ni kam na dauka zuwa wajen boka saimu ta lakawa ba irinku da kk taka nera ba".duk ckn hirar da take mata babu wacce taja hankalinta irin wannan, domin kuwa abu ne da take bukatar Karin bayani akai,shin bokan zai iya cika mata burinta kuwa,to taga itama tsohuwar y'ar hannu ce ashe kaltume gadon halinta tayi,dn muskutawa tayi tana gyara zamanta kfn tace. "Inna ai muma muna da bukatar mu mallaki zuciyar mazajenmu yanda zamu samu yanda muke so ba tare da samun matsala ba". Inna tace,"gskyr ki,muma da muke auren talakawa muke so mu mallaki zuciyar mazan mu to bare fa ku,ni kin ganni nan kfn baban su kaltume ya rasu,to ko,kara na aje masa bai isa ya tsallaka ba,kuma a gidan nan sai abinda nace shi za'a yi". Momy fa saboda murna ganin innar take kamar itace bokan cike da zakuwa tace," Inna kuma shi wannan bokan ne yayi miki aikin "?.Inna tayi wata shewa irin ta tsofaffin y'an duniya tace. "Hjy ke nan shine mana, ai shi aikin sa kamar yankan wuka ne,ammafa da akwai wahala indai zaki iya". Gajiyar da tayi bai hana murmushi ya kufce mata ba,jin cewa burinta yana gab da cika, kallon inna tayi tace,"Karki damu inna zan iya kashe ko na wane, kuma zan iya jure ko wacce irin wahala indai buri na zai cika". Inna tace," haka ake so a tsarinmu ba ma son me karaya,inama ace tsufa bai riskeni ba aida tare zamu je gurin dn tsurkun nace masa yayi miki irin aiki na,amma ko yanzu tunda yasan kaltume zaiyi miki aiki mai kyau".momy tace aikuwa da nafi kowa farin ciki,kuma ba kaltume ba har ke sai kinji dadi".Inna iyayen son abin duniya aka wargaje baki wanda tsabar cin goro hakurin ya dafe,dai-dai lokacin kaltume ta gama gyara dakin babu laifi ya danyi kyau ta dauki kayan momy ta shigar mata dasu,sannan itama momyn ta shiga dakin cike da farinciki burinta zai cika, wanka take so tayi kaltume ta nuna mata ma kewayi,wanda aka za gayeshi da kara,idan mutum wanka yake bashi da ikon mikewa tsaye don na waje zasu iya hango ka.


Farincikin da take ciki baisa taji kyan-kyamin bandakin ba,haka tayo wanka ta fito, kaltume kam tana can tana tanadar musu abinda zasuci,a zuciyarta take fadin wlh ba wahalar banza nake miki ba sai kin biya,Inna kam raba can makota tana bada labarin tayi bakuwa me kudi, tuwon da suka samu tana yi tun kfn ta fita ta kira yara ta basu don itama yau tafi karfin cin tuwo😂😂🤔.


***************************Yasmeen tana zuwa ta samu suhailat ta farka har suna dn hira da y sadiq lokacin dady baya dakin, da murmushin ta takarasa inda take tace. "Sannu y'ar uwata ya jikin naki"?.cike da karfin hali suhailat ta kir-kiro murmushi tace," da sauki aunty na"ckn muryar marasa lpy,yasmeen tace, "yanzu menene yake miki ciwo"?. Hannunta ta kalla da yasha dauri tace," aunty hannu na yana ciwo da kuma bayana"cike da tausayin kanwar tata tace, "ayyah kiyi hakuri to zai daina kinji"daga kanta tayi don tafara gjy da magana, y sadiq yace,"yasmeen zo mu fita waje naga kamar tana son komawa bacci karmu hanata " yasmeen tace "to y sadiq" tana mikewa tare da gyara hijabin ta, tun kfn ma subar dakin bacci yayi gaba da suhailat.


Suna fita suka zauna a reception inda suka saba zama, nan y sadiq ya kalli yasmeen yace, "Na matsu inji abinda ya faru a gida tsakanin Momy da dady,hankali na yaki kwanciya tunda dady yace daga gidan yake, nasan dolene wani abu ya faru". Shiru yasmeen tayi gaba 1 yanayinta ya canza tuno lbrn da baba megadi ya bata,ckn lokaci kadan hawaye suka fara zuba wanda bata San time dn da suka fara zuba ba,y sadiq yace." Subhanallahi meya faru haka daga tanbaya sai kuka, haba yasmeen feel free idan baki fada min damuwar kiba to wa zaki fadawa, kinsan abokin kuka ba'a 6oye masa mutuwa, don haka ki kwantar da hankalinki ki fada min abinda ya faru plss".kokarin hadiye kukan take amma yaki,tafiya sai dai ta samu nasarar tsayar da sheshekar kukan ammafa hawaye yaki daina zuba, tace. "Y sadiq wlh momy ta cucemu, yanzu ace kamar momy wai tabi kaltume y'ar aikinmu garinsu".


Da sauri ya dago maganar tazo masa in shock yace," me kk nufi yasmeen don Allah kibar kukan nan ki sanar dani komai ".tace, y sadiq bani da ikon tsaida hawaye na don basu yi shawara dani ba lokacin da zasu fito, amma zan fada maka duk lbrn da baba megadi ya fada min". Nan ta sanar dashi komai ba tare da ta 6oye masa komai, shima yayi bakinciki sosae, badon baya son kara tayarwa da yasmeen hankali ba da shima kukan zaiyi don shi ya dace dasu.


***************************
     WAI  INA  ISAH


Yana can ya koma makaranta ko ince shiririta tun lokacin da yasamu tsohuwar budurwar sa suna sheke ayarsu kwatsam tafiya ta kama ta kuma can kasar da yake karatun, ai ba tare da 6ata lokaci ba suka tafi babu Wanda ya sani saida ga baya yakira momy ya sanar da ita,har ya tafi zuciyar sa bata daina son kasancewa da suhailat ba,kuma ya kuduri niyyah ko ba yanzu ba dolene ya sameta.........

Tunda burinta zai cika ko inane zata iya rayuwa...

*continues*
Bata da wata matsala game da rayuwarta gidan su kaltume matsalar ta bazata wuce 1-3 na farko yanda bata iya using da ban dakin su,kyankyaminshi take har yanzu ta kasa sabawa,duk lokacin da ta shiga idan ta fito saita yi amai,na biyu,yanda inna ta fiye son kudi kullum saita kirkiro abinda zata bata kudi kuma masu yawa Wanda tana ganin muddin aka cigaba a haka to tabbas zasu karya mata tattalin arzikinta,abu na 3 Wanda shine yafi damunta yanda yau kwana 4 da zuwan su amma har yanzu sunki yi mata maganar zuwa wajen boka dn tsurku Wanda hakan ba karamin damuwa yasa ta ba har tana tunanin anya ba karya suke mata yanda suke zu zuta mata aikinsa"?.

Yau kam ta kudiri aniyar zata tare su da maganar, don ita burinta kawai shine dady ya zamo a tafin hannunta,kuma ta matsu hakan ta kasance, tana ckn wannan tunanin kaltume ta shigo dakin, kallon ta momy tayi kfn tace, "yauwa kaltume magana nake so muyi dake"
Ba tare da tayi magana ba ta zauna tana kallon momy, don yanzu tarage irin girmamawar da take mata,kallon ta tayi kfn tace "uhmm ina jinki meye matsalar ki"?. Ta fada tana wani kallon ta a dage.

Mamaki ya kusan kashe momy a zaune, yanzu kaltume ke mata wannan abun don tana ta kamar ta kawota wannan matsiyacin gidan nasu,lallai mutum bashi da tabbas duk irin alkhairin da tayi mata shine tun yanzu ba'a je ko ina ba zata mata butulci,(nace kadan ma tagani). 

Ganin yanda hjy tayi nisa a tunani yasa kaltume cewa," haba hjy kin zaunar dani kince zamuyi magana amma kinyi shiru,to idan bazaki fada min matsalar ki ba ni inada abin yi ". Ta yunkura zata tashi da sauri momy tace, " zan fada miki zauna tunda ni na damu" ta koma ta zauna tare da juyar da kanta can gefe,kada kai momy tayi sannan tace, "dama akan maganar zuwa wajen boka dn tsurku ne,naga har yanzu baki ce na shirya muje ba"?. Murmushi kaltume tayi,don dama abinda ya kawota kenan, abinda yasa tayi wa momy haka,  tayi tunanin akan ban dakin gidan zata yi mata magana tunda suna kallo duk lkcn da ta shiga saita yi amai, ita kuma bata son a raina mata muhalli duk da tasan tabbas a hakan ma Momy tana kokari da take iya shiga ban dakin, in tayi la'akari da bathroom dn momyn kamar daki yanda aka tsara shi.

Kallon ta tayi har yanzu da smyln a face dn ta,sannan tace," hjy kenan me kk ci na baka na zuba, bari nayi ki dn huta tunda naga baki ta6a irin wannan tfyr ba,amma yanzu tunda kin matsu to ki shirya gobe tun karfe 4:00am na dare,zamu tafi don haka saiki kwana da shirin cikar burinki ".

Kasa 6oye murnarta tayi musamman da taji yanda kaltume ta kara Sosa mata inda yake mata kaikayi, tini ta manta abinda kaltume tayi mata yanzu, da murmushi ta kalleta tace,"ammafa naji dadi kuma ngd da taimakon ku a gareni,na dauki alkawarin indai burina ya cika zanyi miki abinda zaki ji dadi sosae" murmushin mugunta kaltume tayi kfn tace,"hjy ai nima indai wannan burin naki ya cika nima nayi miki alkawarin saina yi miki abinda baza ki ta6a manta wa ba a rayuwarki".

Bata fahimci maganar ta ba kuma bata nemi Karin bayani ba,don farincikin da take ciki ya dakushe komai a zuciyarta har hango ta take, a gidan ta tana juya kowa yanda ranta ke so, suna ckn wannan halin inna ta same su,nan dai ta zauna suna kara fadawa Momy yanda aikin bokan yake kamar yankan wuka.







***************************
Suhailat kam jiki yana kyau sosae don yanzu har tashi tsaye take da kanta,tafiya ce dai sai a hankali saboda buguwar da ta samu a kashin bayanta, doctor FK yace a dinga dn kamata ana dn zagayawa da ita,don hakan zaisa ta damar iya yin tfy da kanta, doctor FK da kansa yake aikin zagayawar da ita da taimakon aunty yasmeen, wannan ne ya bawa ma'aikatan hospital dn damar yin gulmarsa kasancewar kowa yasan halinsa,baya shiga harkar kowane mutum aikinsa kawai yasa a gaba duk inda suka wuce sai anbi su da kallo, ana gulma kasa-kasa don babu Wanda zai ga fuska balle a tsokane shi,daman doctor muhseen ne,kuma baya nan,wasu suce kanwarsa ce wasu kuma suce y'ar uwarsa ce,don pbasu ta6a ganin yana bawa,petiante irin wannan kyakkyawar kula ba.


Ckn lokaci kadan tafara iya tafiya da kanta sannan tana dadewa ana hira da ita,jikinta yayi kyau sosae saboda irin kulawar da take samu daga y'an uwanta,mahaifinsu, da kuma doctor FK har wata y'ar kiba tayi,kowa a ckn su burinsa ya sata farinciki.









***************************
Ammy ce zaune a katafaren parloun ta, hannunta rike da hisnul-muslim tana azkar na yamma, hankalinta gaba 1 yana kan abinda take yi,sallamar tilon danta ta doki dodon kunnenta, ta dago da sauri tana kallon sa don ta gano da wane irin yanayi ya dawo,smyln ne kwance a kyakkyawar face dn sa,Wanda  ya fito da kwarjini haibarsa da cikar kamalarsa,karasowa wajenta yayi tare da ajiye briefcase dn sa a kan kujerar dake gefansa ya dora kansa bisa cinyar ammyn sa, yana sauraron yanda take karanta adduo'inta cike da kwarewa,saida ta kai karshe kfn ta rufe littafin ta dube shi tace, "ina ptn yarona ya dawo ckn koshin lpy da kwanciyar hankali"?.

Murmushi yayi kfn ya bata amsa da cewa," lpy ammy na, ina ftn nasamu ammy na lpy"?. Shafa yalwa tacciyar sumar kansa wacce tasha gyara tayi sannan tace, "lpy lw fahdy na ". Zun6ura baki yayi alamar shagwaba sannan yace,"gsky ammy na kina 6atawa wannan guy dn suna''. waiwaya wa tayi tana neman guy dn,FK ya kwashe da dry yace, "ammy kinga guy dn anan". Ya fada yana nuna kirjinsa da hannu, dukan wasa takai masa tana fadin "ja'iri, kamaida ni kakarka". Da gudu ya bar parloun yana fadin" ammy na ki shirya ya a kwai story bari na watsa ruwa ".

Ya karasa fada yana kokarin shiga dakinsa, murmushi ammy tayi, tana gdy ga Allah da yasa yaronta ya dawo kamar yanda yake a da,mutum me wasa da dry........

UMMU MASH KUR CE

Pg 31

Tun da asuba Momy ta gama shiryawa daman dai ba wani dogon bacci tayi ba saboda zumud'i burin ta zai cika, lokacin da kaltume ta shigo ta sameta a shirye don haka suka rankaya suka fito waje har zaure inna ta rakosu tana k'ara fad'awa momy irin yanda aikin d'an tsurku yk, momy ta matsu ta ganta a gabansa ta sanar dashi buk'atar ta, da haka suka yi sallama da inna suka tafi, tfy sk ckn dokar daji ba gida gaba ba gida baya,tun momy tana iya jure tfyr har ta fara k'orafi,kaltume tace" ai ko rabi bamuyi ba". zaro ido Momy tayi ganin irin bak'ar wahalar tfyr da suka sha amma ace wai har yanxu ko rabi basuyi ba, ckn tsananin gjy tace"na shiga 3 kaltume wlh na gaji gsky mu nemi abun hawa ko nawane xn biya amma muddin muka ci gaba da wannan tfyr sai dai kikai masa gawa ta don baxan iya ba, gashi hannu na kamar zai cire gyran da akayi sam baiyi ba".

Kaltume tace."to ke yanxu zaki iya hawa jaki."? K'walalo ido momy tayi kfn ta maimaita."jaki fa kk ce." Jaki kuwa don shi kad'ai ne abun hawan da xai iya kaimu har kuma shi kansa jakin sai munyi sa'a kfn mu sameshi."  Hankalin momy ya tashi tunda tk a duniya bata ta6a hawa doki bama bare kuma wani jaki tsoron sa ma tk, haka suka cigaba da tfy, zuwa yanxu ba momy ba hatta kaltume ta gaji saboda itama ta dad'e rabonta da yin irin wannan tfyr, hakan tasa suka samu guri suka xauna suna huta wa.
Kaltume ta dubi momy tace."hjy hutun jaki da kaya muke yakamata mu samu mafita kinga ga wani me jaki can bari nayi masa magana daure wa xakiyi ki hau ai daman hausawa sunce ranar biyan buk'ata rai ba a bakin komai yk ba." Shiru momy tayi tasani yanda kaltume ke fad'in nisan tfyr nan bazata iya ba don haka dole ta daure ta hau jakin, haka kaltume tayi wa me jaki magana yaxo  suka daidaita sannan suka hau da kyar momy ta iya hawa tsigar jikinta sai tashi tk idonta a kulle don tsoron jakin tk, ta k'ankame bayan kaltume da tk gaba me jaki ya kora suka tafi daman yasan inda zasu tunda aikinsa ne kai tsaye can ya nufa dasu.
