Ammy ta mike zuwa kitchen don samawa tilon dn ta abinda zai ci ckn 50 minutes ta gama dafa masa hadaddiyar jalouop wacce tasha kayan hadi sai k'amshi tk har ta gama jerawa a dining be fito ba,ganin haka ta mik'e da nufin lek'awa taga abinda ya rik'eshi, k'amshin sa ne ya dakata da ita ta zubawa hanya ido tana murmushi, ya fito ckn shigar k'ana nan kaya wadanda sukai matukar yi masa kyau shima fuskar tasa dauke tk da murmushi, ya k'araso kai tsaye dining ya nufa yana shafa ckn sa alamar yana jin yunwa, ammy kam kallonsa tk cike da jin dadi,, zama yayi bisa 1 daga ckn kujerun da suka zagaye dining dn yana jiran ammy tayi serving dn sa, sarai ta fahimci abinda yk nufi amma sai tayi banxa dashi tamkar bata gane ba saima wayarta da ta dauko tana dubawa,fk ya kalli ammy cike da shagwa6a yace."don Allah ammy ki zuba min kar yunwa ta kassara miki tilon danki." Had'e ranta ammy tayi kfn tace."kai ni fa na gaji da sani aiki da kk tun wuri gwanda ma ka nemo mafita." Ta k'are maganar tare da yi masa harar wasa, sosa k'eyar sa yayi don ya fahimci abinda ammy tk nufi ckn xuciyar sa yk cewa."me kk ci na kasa na zuba ammy na komai ya kusa xuwa k'arshe." A fili kam dafe ckn sa yayi tare da xuba wata y'ar siririyar k'ara,da sauri ammy ta mik'e sai gata a gabansa tana tanbayar sa abinda yk faruwa, dariya ya kwashe da ita kfn yace."gashi yanxu kin taso tun d'axu nk jira kiyi serving d'i na amma kk tsaya kina min wata maganar. " duka ammy ta kai masa itama tana dariyar tk cewa." Lallai fahad ka maida ni ka karka." Nan dai tayi serving dn sa yafara cin abincin yana zuba mata santi abinda ya dad'e rabonsa da yayi tun kfn ya shiga ckn damuwa, yau kam jinsa yk ckn wani irin nishad'i Wanda shi kansa ya kasa gane abinda ya sashi ckn irin wannan farin cikin iya kanta ammy yau tana ckn nishad'i musamman da taga yanda tilon dan nata ya xage yaci abinci sosae kfn ya kora da ta taccen lemon abarba sannan yai gdy ga Allah ya kalli ammy yace." Ina mamakin yanda kk iya girkin xamani ammy gashi ke tsohuwa ce shi yasa nk tausayin kaina duk lokacin da ya kasance bana kusa dk saboda bakina ya saba da d'and'anon abincin ki." Dariya ammy tayi kfn tace."wato kai kullum baka da aiki sai wannan maganar na fad'amaka kayi aure sai ka huta nima na huta duk inda xakaje ka tafi da matar ka ba wata matsala." dn k'aramin tsaki yaja sannan yace. "Haba ammy ke kullum maganar ki nayi aure byn na riga na sani babu wacce xata iya kwatanta irin abincin ki." Shafa kansa tayi sannan ckn tausasa murya tace."haba fahad waya fad'a maka bazaka samu wacce xata iya kwatanta irin abinci na ba byn kullum inayi maka addu'a Allah ya baka mace ta gari wacce xata kula min da kai,to ina so kasa a ranka zaka samu wacce ta fini iya duk wani abinda kk buk'ata ni dai fata na ka a mince kayi auren nasan baxakayi nadama ba." Fk yai shiru yana sauraron ammy saida ta gama sannan ya d'ago ya dubeta yace. "Ammy ba wai bana son inyi aure bane kawai dai ina gudun matsalar da xn janyo wa kaina ne bana son abinda ya faru a baya ya k'ara faruwa tunda har yanxu ban samu cikakkiyar amsa daga wajen ki ba ammy." Sunkuyar da kanta ammy tayi tabbas tasan danta yana da gsky amma ya zatayi bata son tuna abinda ya wuce a baya shi yasa ma bata son tabashi labarin hak'ik'anin a salin sa,cike da tausayin danta ta kalleshi sannan tace."kayi hak'uri yaro na insha'allahu na kusa sanar da kai asalinka amma dai ina so kasa a ranka kaima d'a ne kamar kowa kuma ta hanyar aure aka haife ka ba kamar yanda mutane suke zargi ba." Fk yai shiru shi kam bai san dalilin ammy ba da tak'i sanar dashi gsky  kullum idan ya tanbaye ta abinda tk fad'a masa kenan, xuwa yanxu shi kansa ya fara xargin shi dn ba dn sunnah bane tunda ammy ta kasa sanar dashi komai, ajiyar xuciya ya sauke kfn yace."shi kenan ammy na a mince duk lokacin da kk ga ya dace sai ki sanar dani amma ni dai na daina neman aure saboda bana son abinda ya faru a baya ya kara faruwa." Itama ajiyar xuciyar tayi kfn tace." Karka damu yaro na da a kwai lokacin da nk jira lokacin na zuwa zan fad'a maka abinda nk so da kai shine ka cigaba da neman auren nayi maka alk'awari duk lokacin da iyayen yarinyar suka nemi kafito a lokacin zan sanar da kai komai." Da mamaki yk kallon ammy yanxu kam ya fahimci abinda tk nufi, wayo tk so tayi masa saboda ta lura baya son maganar auren shine xata fad'i haka abinda bata sani ba shi kanshi zama ba auren ya fara takura masa don ko yaushe yana ckn azumi saboda gudun fad'awa ckn matsala, murmushi yai sannan ya dubi ammy yace. "Shikenan ammy insha'allah xan nemi auren, ki cigaba da yi min addu'a." Ammy taji dad'i don kuwa ita burin ta bai wuce taga tilon yaronta yayi aure ya tara zuri'a ba, don haka ckn farinciki tace." Karka damu yaro na indai addu'a ce ka gamu da ita dama can inayi sai dai na k'ara dagewa." Kwanciya yai bisa cinyar ammyn ya runtse idon sa yana tunanin shi yanxu wacce yarinya zai aura rabon sa da ya kula mace da sunan soyayya har ya manta, suhailat ce ta fad'o masa a rai haka kurum ya tsinci kansa da fad'uwar gaba yana tausayin yarinyar musamman da yaji marainiya ce bata san dad'in uwa ba, shin dame zai taimaki yarinyar ne ya cire ta daga halin da tk ciki ? Ka aure ta mana wata xuciyar tabashi shawara saurin gigixa kansa yai tare da fad'in "inaa tayi k'ank'anta da yawa." Ammy dk kallon sa tayi saurin cewa."wacece kk cewa tayi k'ank'anta."? Shiru yayi don shi baima san maganar sa ta fito fili ba, ammy ma bata k'ara magana ba, ganin tayi shiru yasa ya d'ago kansa ya kalleta kfn yace. "Daman wata patient ce da aka kawo min ita,( yabata labari tun lokacin da dct muhseen ya bashi file din zuwa yanxu da yk wannan tunanin a kanta) murmushi ammy tayi ta gama fahimtar d'an nata ya kamu da soyayyar yarinyar amma shi baima gane ba sai fad'in tausayin ta yk ji, ita kanta taji tausayin yarinyar musamman da taji dalilin rashin lpyr tata don haka tana ptn inda alkhairi Allah ya tabbatar, kallon fk tayi kfn tace. " Tabbas nima na tausayawa yarinyar kuma ina ptn tausayin da kk mata ya xamo soyayya." da sauri ya kalli ammy jin abinda tace, dariya ammy tayi kfn tace. "Baka san cewa daga tausayi soyayya ke farawa ba." Haba ammy yarinyar fa y'ar k'arama ce." Ya fad'a yana mik'ewa tsaye, "to meye a ciki don k'arama ce to tsohuwa kk so ka kawo min."? Ni dai idan da alkhairi Allah ya tabbatar mana." Tsintar bakinsa yai da fad'in " ameen" ammy tace."ja'iri kama fito ka fad'amin gsky." Da sauri yabar falon yana dariya. 


_____________________________________Momy sunje wajen boka dn tsurku sai dai sunsha bak'ar wahala ita kanta kaltume taji a jikinta balle kuma momy da tunda uwar ta ta haifeta bata ta6a irin wannan wahalalliyar tfyr ba, sun tarar da gurin cike da mutane maza da mata masu neman duniya hakan tasa suka nemi guri suka zauna suna hatawa kfn layi yaxo kansu Momy ce ta dubi kaltume tace. "Yanxu kaltume duk wad'annan mutanen wajen sa suka zo dubi irin sammakon da mukayi amma naga mutane haka." Duk da wahalar tfyr da kaltume tasha hakan bai hanata kwashewa da dariya ba sannan tace. " to ai hjy idan ke sammako kikai wasu anan suka kwana, wasu fa daga wasu garuruwan suke zuwa ke harda ma wad'anda suke xuwa daga wasu k'asashen." Ta6 lallai wannan mutumin ya shahara." Momy ta fad'a cike da mamaki kaltume tace. "Kad'an ma kk gani bari kiga xuwa anjima yanda gurin zai cika." Haka dai suka cigaba da hirar su kusan 1hour  abinda ya tayar da hankalin momy shine yanda taga mutane na hawa da  saukowa daga saman dutse da alama duk mai buk'atar ganin boka dole ne sai ya hau ita kam ina xata iya hawan wannan dutsen me mugun tsayi ckn tashin hankali ta dubi kaltume tace. "Wai yanxu kina nufin muma saman dutsen zamu hau." Kaltume tace. " eh mana ai shi yana ckn wani kogo a saman dutsen kuma duk Wanda yk  so ya ga dn tsurku dole sai ya hau." Na shiga 3 kaltume wlh bazn iya hawa wannan dutsen ba kalli yanda maza ma suke shan wahala." Kaltume ta d'aga kanta ta kalli yanda wani mutum ya dage yana hawan dutsen kamar zai fad'o sannan tace."Kinga wani gabjejen mutum can idan xaki biya shi zai goya ki ya hau dake haka ma saukowa shi sana'ar sa kenan a wajen." Ckn mamaki momy ke kallon mutumin me k'irar samu dawa shi sam baya jin wahalar hawa dutsen, ita kam in dai buk'atar ta xata biya batada matsala xata je ya goyata taje wajen boka, haka kuwa a kayi lokacin da layi yaxo kansu haka momy ta tak'ark'are ba tare da wani tunani ba ta haye bayan k'ato, duk da haka tasha wahala, koda ya ajiyeta gefe ta koma tana maida numfashi sai a lokacin ta ga yanda saman dutsen yk ashe fad'i ne dashi sosae ba ma ka iya ganin k'arshen sa, tana nan zaune tana y'an kalle-kalle aka hawo da kaltume, nan ma sun d'auki dogon lokaci kfn layi yazo kansu, koda layi yaxo Kansu momy ce ta fara shiga don shi boka a k'a idar shi baya ganin mutum 2 sai dai 1 haka momy ta shiga ckn kogon jikinta yana rawa duk da ta aro juriya tasawa kanta amma abubuwan da tagani dole ya hargitsa ta saboda abubuwa ne masu abun tsoro ita dai idan batayi k'arya ba sai tace hadda aljanu don bata ta6a ganin irin wad'annan halittun ba, tana sanya k'afafunta taji an buga mata wata raxananniyar tsawa wacce saida kunnenta ya daina ji na kusan 3 second gumi ta ko ina xubo mata yk a ranta tk fad'in. " na kawo kaina ni rabi na shiga 3." Kfn tayi wani yunk'uri taji an hankad'ata waje sai gata a k'asa ji kk timm! Da wata irin firgitacciyar murya taji ance." tashi la'ananniya da k'afar hagu xaki shigo ko kuma yanxun nan gaxurgumi yai kalace dk." Ai ba shiri momy ta mik'e dukda buguwar da tayi amma jin da tayi ance xa'a yi kalace da ita yasa ko zafin buguwar ba taji ba, yanda aka umarceta haka tayi ta shiga da k'afar hagu, baki 1 ilahirin jikinta rawa yk tunda tk yawon xuwa wajen bokaye bata ta6a had'uwa da irin wannan hatsabibin bokan ba, tsugunnawa tayi kamar me neman gafara ta fara raba idanu wata mahaukaciyar dariya taji anfara da wata gabjejiyar murya muryar kad'ai ta isa ta sanya mutum ya saki fitsari ba tare da ya shirya ba, aikuwa itama momy fitsarin ta saki jikinta na kyarma tana son ganin ma'abocin wannan murya, gurin da tk jin sautin na fita tk kallo wani dn mitsitsin mutum ta hango mak'ale akan wata kujera wacce k'afarsa ma bata sauko k'asa ba saboda tsabar k'ank'anta, tsoro ne yakama ta don ga dukkan alamu wannan k'aramar halittar ce keda wannan gabjejiyar muryar gashin kansa buzu-buzu ga uban gemu da ya kusa rufe y'ar mitsitsiyar fuskar momy dai anan ta daskare ko motsi ta kasayi, kusan 5 minutes dn tsurku ya d'auka yana dariya ba tare da ya tsaya ba, zuwa can wurin yai tsit kamar d'aukewar ruwan sama, momy har ajiyar xuciya ta sauke saboda yanda kunnenta suke baraxanar d'auke wa, tunani tk a ranta indai wannan halittar da tk gani a gabanta shine dn tsurku lallai yaci sunan sa, tana ckn wannan tunanin taji an fara magana da cewa."ke tsinanniya sunanki rabi sunan mijinki alhj Ibrahim yaran ki 4, 2maza 2mata a kwai y'ar mijinki da ta gagareki kk so kiga bayanta sannan kina so ki mallake mijinki, wannan shine abinda yakawoki waje na koba haka bane matsiyaciya." Cikn tsananin mamaki da fargaba cike da in ina tace. " ha hakane boka wannan itace buk'ata ta saboda yarinyar ta shiga rayuwa ta da yawa sannan bana iya juya miji na yanxu haka saboda yarinyar har duka na yayi." Tsoro da fargabar da tk ciki bai hanata fad'a wa boka buk'atar ta ba,  zuwa yanxu tafara sabawa da muryar sa koda yake d'oki da farincikin cikar burinta ya disashe tsoro da fargabar da tk ciki, boka ya kalleta da jajayen idonsa yace. " to yanxu ke me kk buk'atar ayi musu." momy tace. " inaso na mallake alhj yaxama duk abinda na sanya shi zai aikata sannan a sanya masa tsanar yarinyar ta yanda baya so ko ganin ta yayi sannan itama yarinyar a k'ara sanya mata tsoro na." Boka ya kwashe da dariya na y'an mintuna kfn kuma ya duk'ufa aikinsa wata k'warya ya d'auko ya zuba wani garin magani tk wajen yai baki'k'irin ko hannun ka baka iya gani yana wad'an su surutai da d'alasimai kusan 30 minutes gurin yai shiru sannu a hankali haske ya fara bayyana, nuni yai wa momy da hannu akan taxo tagani mik'e wa tayi ta k'ara sa sannan ta lek'a ckn k'waryar alhj ta gani zaune a kujera da alama har yanxu suna hospital, ckn tsananin mamaki tk kallon boka tana son k'arin bayani boka yace. "Lallai wannan matsiyacin mijin naki yana da taurin kai baya wasa da ibadar sa duk abinda muka tura masa ya dawo dak'yar muka samu nasarar kama kurwar sa wannan da kk gani ruhin sa ne." Ya d'auko wata kwalba ya karata a jkn k'waryar take hoton dady dk ciki yaxama hayak'i ya shige ckn kwalbar boka yasanya murfi ya rufe tare da mik'awa momy kwalbar, yace. "Dole ne ki kula da wannan kwalbar saboda itace xata zama makamin ki tabbas xaki juya mijinki yanda kk so muddin kin kula da wannan kwalbar amma ki sani mijinki mutum ne me had'ari sai kin had'a da makirci irin naku mata sannan ita kuma yarinyar gsky bazamu iya cutar da ita ba ta kowane hali saboda itama bata wasa da addinin ta byn haka kuma tun tana k'arama an tsima ta da magunguna Wanda indai asiri ne bazai shigeta ba, abinda zaki yi kawai shine kissa da makirci dasu xaki sanya mijinki ya tsaneta,ni kuma ta 6angare na xanyi kokari na sanya masa tsanarta a ckn wannan ruhin nasa, yanxu idan kin koma kada ki je gidan sa ki samu wani waje inda kk san ba nesa dashi bane da zarar aikin mu ya fara ratsa ckn ruhin sa da kansa xai nemi inda kk, kuma duk lokacin da yai miki gaddama akan wani abu ki murza kwalbar take zai aikata abinda kk so." Ckn tsananin murna momy ta mik'a wa boka kwalbar ya kar6a ya bud'e yaxuba wani bak'in magani a ciki tare da kiran sunan suhailat sau 3 yana gama zubawa ya mik'a mata kwalbar byn ya rufe,yace." Ina k'ara jadda da miki ki kula da wannan kwalbar koda wasa karki bari wani ya ganta duk ranar da kk bari wani ya ganta shi kenan aikin xai lalace gaba d'aya kuma babu hanyar da xamu bi mu k'ara samun nasara akan sa yanxu ma abinda yasa kk ga mun samu ruhin sa saboda yana ckn damuwar abinda ya samu y'ar sa ne da wannan muka samu dama sai ki kula." Momy ta 6oye kwalbar a jkn ta tana faman yiwa boka godiya ta kwaso kud'i masu uban yawa ta juye tare da mik'e wa tana k'ara gdy har ta fice da baya da baya, kallon ta kawai kaltume tayi tasan buk'ata ta biya don haka da murmushi tace. "Boka dn tsurku ya gama aikin sa kenan."? Washe baki momy tayi tare da fad'in." Ke dai shiga ki fito kisha labari." Kaltume ta wuce kasancewar tasan dokokinsa bata samu matsala kamar momy ba dukda itama ta tsorata da abubuwan da ta gani haka ta daure byn ya gama ihun sa da duk abinda yk yi ya dubeta yace. " kinxo ne kina so ki mallake uwar d'akinki duk abinda kk so tayi shi xata yi. " kaltume ta gyad'a kai tare da fad'in haka ne bokan duniya da wannan buk'atar naxo gurin ka nasan buk'ata ta xata biya idan ma zai yiwu har mijin nata a mallaka min shi ." Wani ihu yayi kfn yace. " ke kam taki me sauk'i ce saboda itama wacce kk so ki mallaka tsinanniya ce amma ba zai yiwu ki mallaki mijin taba saboda ita kanta munsha wahala kfn mu samu rihinsa idan kk mallake ta ai kamar kin mallake shi ne tunda itama ta mallake shi ungo wannan." ya mik'a mata kwalba me d'auke da ruhin momy yace ta kula da ita,haka itama ta bashi kud'i masu yawa ta fito, saida suka huta sannan suka nemi wannan jibgegen mutumin da ya hau dasu ya goya su ya sauko dasu suka biya shi daga nan suka nemi wani me jakin suka k'ara hawa don su koma gida wannan karon farin cikin da momy tk ciki yasa ba taji tsoron jakin ba, sai byn magrib suka dawo a matukar gajiye..........


UMMU MASHKUR CE

MANAZARTA ASSOCIATION


Pg 32

Alhamdulillah suhailat jiki yayi sauk'i sosae don yanxu tana iya tashi tayi tfy da kanta yanxu kam bata jin ciwon komai hakan tasa ta takura wa doctor a sallameta don ita ta gaji da xaman hospital dn dak'yar suka lalla6ata ta K'ara kwana 2 to ganin komai normal yasa dole ya rubuta musu sallama ba don yaso ba saboda shi xuwan su hospital dn ya dawo masa da farincikin da yarasa don yanayin shagwa6ar suhailat burgeshi yk musamman idan xa'a bata magani ko zai yi mata allura haka xatai ta matsar k'wallah sai anyi kuma kaji hira har da dariya shi kam hakan ke burgeshi yai ta mata dariya yana tsokanarta, ckn k'ank'anin lokaci shak'uwa me k'arfi ta shiga tsakanin su itama idan bata ganshi ba duk sai taji tana ckn damuwa, aunty yasmeen tuni ta gama gane inda suka nufa sai dai ta lura su basu gane hakan ba don haka duk lokacin da doctor ya shigo sai ta fice ta basu guri, hakan kuwa ba k'aramin dad'i yk wa fk ba, nan zai 6ata lokaci yana tsokanarta ita kuma tai ta shagwa6a don yanxu sam bata jin kunyar yin shagwa6ar ta a gaban sa. Yau ma kamar kullum tun farkawar ta daga bacci byn tayi sallah tayi wanka sannan ta koma gadon ta, ita kad'ai ce a d'akin saboda aunty yasmeen ta tafi gida tayi musu breakfast tun asuba, tunani tk yaushe za'a sallame ta tana ckn wannan halin dady ya shigo da sallama, saukowa tayi daga kan gadon taxo gaban sa ta tsugunna ta gaishe shi shafa kanta yayi cike da k'aunar d'iyar tasa yace. " mama na ya kk tashi.''? Ya kuma k'arfin jikin."? Murmushi tayi sannan tace. " dady ni kam jiki na yayi sauk'i babu abinda yk min ciwo don Allah dady kacewa doctor ya sallame mu". Ta k'ara sa fad'a tare da rik'e hannun dady tana marairaice fuska kamar me shirin yin kuka, murmushi dady yai kfn yace. "Kiyi hak'uri mama na doctor yana matukar kokarin kula dk be kamata mu nuna masa iyakar sa ba kiyi hak'uri muga abinda ya tsara." Dai_dai lokacin y sadeeq ya shigo da sallama dady y amsa kallon suhailat yai sannan ckn tsokana yace. " little sis yaushe zaki sanya doctor ya sallame mu naga jiki yayi kyau." Turo baki tayi kfn tace."ai baxai sallame mu ba so yk sai mun shekara anan." Dariya sukai gaba 1 sannan suka cigaba da hirar su, suna ckn haka doctor yai sallama suka amsa, tunda ya shigo idon sa na kan suhailat wacce tak'i yadda ta kalleshi saboda yau haushin sa tk ji, hannu ya mik'a wa dady da y sadeeq suka gaisa sannan ya k'ara sa gadon da tk xaune da hisnul muslim a hannun ta har yanxu bata d'ago kanta ba, ganin baxata kula shi ba yasa dole yafara magana saboda kada su dady su fahimci wani abu amma ba don haka ba shi kam idan zai kwana yana kallon ta ba xai gaji ba koda ace ba magana suke ba, gyara tsaiwar sa yayi sannan yace. "My patient yau babu ko gaisuwa."? Juyar da kanta tayi tana turo dn cute lips dn ta hakan da tayi ba k'ara min burgeshi tayi ba haka ya tsaya yana kallon ta, "little sis ba magana ake mk ba ki kayi shiru."? Maganar y sadeeq ta dawo dashi ckn haiyacin sa ckn sauri da kunya ya juyo wajen dady yana dn sosa k'eyar sa, yace." Dady ga wannan." Ya mik'a wa dady takaddar dk hannun sa sannan ya cigaba da cewa." Alhamdulillah naga jikin nata yayi sauk'i sosae shine na rubuta mata sallama. " aikuwa sai ganin suhailat suka yi a gaban su tana washe baki mamaki yakama doctor daman dalilin da yasa tak'i kula shi kenan, sai kuwa yace. " amma a kwai wasu allurai da zan k'ara sa mata kfn Ku tafi da sauri ta kalli doctor lokaci 1 murnar da tk yi ta koma ciki nan ta fara k'wak'ulo hawaye dariya gaba 1 suka fara yi mata, ita kam tuni hawaye ya fara xuba, aunty yasmeen ta shigo hannun ta d'auke da kaya ajiye kayan tayi sannan taje wajen suhailat tare da rik'ota tace. Da sauri ta kalle shi murmushi na son k'wace mata ga kuma hawaye, handkerchief dn sa me matuk'ar kyau da k'amshi ya mik'a mata ta kar6a ta goge face dn ta ya shiga tsokanarta tana dariya,, su dai su aunty yasmeen basu san abinda doctor ya fad'a mata ba sai ganin ta suka yi tana faman dariya, dariyar suma suke dady yace."lallai mama na bakya jin kunya kuka da dariya basa yi miki wuya." Ya fad'a yana nuna abincin dk gaban suhailat, ckn sauri ta fara cin abincin ita ba komai yasa ta matsu su bar asibitin ba sai matsa mata da akeyi akan shan magani da kuma allurai da ake mata haka ta dage tana cin abincin suma su dady yasmeen ta had'a musu doctor kam ya fita ya duba jikin sauran patients dn dk jiran sa, byn sun gama suka fara had'a kayan su sun gama shiryawa doctor kawai suke jira, kusan 30 minutes sai gashi ya shigo hannun sa rik'e da files kallon d'akin yai yaga sun had'a komai ya dubi dady yace. Shiru doctor yai yana tuna Kalmar da dady yayi anfani da ita ta YARO NA bai ta6a jin Kalmar daga bakin namiji ba sai ammyn sa yau gashi dady ya kira shi da yaron sa hakan tasa yaji yana kewar mahaifinsa ina ma dady xai maye masa wannan gurbin tabbas da yayi farinciki dn xaman da yayi dasu ya fahimci su dn mutanen kirki ne zai so ya kasance 1 daga ckn wannan ahalin, goge k'wallar da ta taru a gefen idon sa yayi sannan ya juyo wajen dady yace. "Gsky Ku mutane ne masu mutunci da karamci tunda nk aiki ban ta6a had'uwa da mutanen da suka shiga raina lokaci 1 ba har ma nk tunanin rabuwa daku tabbas mara lafiyarku ta samu lfy yanda yakamata amma tunanin rabuwa daku yasa na kasa rubuta muku sallama saboda ina son kasancewa daku zuwa yanxu munyi sabo Wanda nk so yazamo xumunci ne a tsakanin mu har abada da ftn dady xaka kar6e ni a matsayin d'a Ku kuma Ku kar6e ni a matsayin dn uwan ku." tare da tsugunnawa a gaban dady ya rik'e hannun sa sannan ya cigaba da cewa. "Na kasance tunda nk ban sanin soyayyar mahaifi ba, ban ta6a ganin mahaifi na ba hasali ma bani da tabbacin yana raye ko ya mutu, kai bama nida tabbacin ina da mahaifin kamar yanda ammy na ke fad'a, abinda na sani kawai duk lokacin da nayi mata maganar mahaifi na sai tace na kwantar da hankali na, ni dn sunnah ne kamar kowa amma da akwai lokacin da xata fad'a min inda yk ba yanxu ba, ban san menene hujjarta ba amma a halin yanxu ni kaina na fara xargin cewa ni dn ba da aure aka haife ni ba." Ya k'ara sa maganar tare da kifa kansa a cinyar dady yana kuka me tsuma xuciya, gaba 1 jikinsu yayi sanyi musamman suhailat da tausayin doctor ya lulle6eta itama ta fara nata hawayen, yanxu kam ta yadda da ake cewa kowa a duniya yana da k'aluu balen sa shi kuma doctor irin tasa k'addarar kenan, dady ya shafa yalwatacciyar sumar kansa yana lallashinsa, luff doctor yayi a jkn dady yana shak'ar k'amshin sa a ransa yana aiyana inama mahaifin sa ne, saida dady ya tabbatar doctor ya samu nutsuwa sannan ya fara magana da cewa." Babu shakkah yaro na kana ckn jarabawar ubangiji amma ka cigaba da addu'a insha'allah komai xai warware ckn sauk'i sannan ka cigaba da yiwa mahaifiyar ka biyayya tare da lalla6ata ita da kanta xata fad'a maka gskyr lamarin amma ka daina yi mata mummunan xato, ni kuma a 6angare na zan taya ka da addu'a kuma ina me yi maka albishir a madadi na da iyalaina muna farincikin samun ka ckn ahalin mu insha'allah zaka samemu tamkar yanda kk buk'ata, ka kwantar da hankalin ka." Farinciki ya mamaye doctor ya d'ago cike da matsanancin farinciki ya kalli dady yace." Ngd dady Allah ya saka maka da alkhairi kuma nima insha'allah zaka sameni me yi maka biyayya." juyo wa wajen su y sadeeq yayi gaba 1 suka had'a baki suka ce."our well come new broth." Wani farincikin ne ya k'ara lulle6e shi tunda yk a duniya babu Wanda ya ta6a kiran sa da brother amma yau gashi yayi mahaifi da y'an uwa tabbas baxai ta6a manta wa da wannan ranar a tarihin rayuwar saba, nan suka 6ata lokaci suna hira cike da farinciki kfn daga baya dady yai musu umarnin su tafi gida har da fk domin yaga new house dn su,dady ya buk'aci doctor ya sanar dashi abinda aka kashe na magunguna da tests dama gyaran karayar da akayi, FK yace ya biya komai kuma baya buk'atar ayi masa gdy tunda sister dn sa ce,haka babu yanda suka iya suka rabu dashi sai dai dady da yai ta sanya masa albarka, haka suka hau mota sai gida.


Byn sun iso gida kai tsaye kowa d'akinsa ya nufa yayinda y sadeeq ya wuce da FK d'akinsa, tunda suhailat ta shiga gidan tk binsa da kallo y'an kwana kin da tayi bata nan duk ya canxa mata, haka ma da suka shiga dakin su komai yana nan tsaf kamar suna nan koda yake aunty yasmeen tana dawowa kuma tasan baxata barshi da k'axanta ba.




 Wanka dukkansu suka yi sannan suka fita parlour, sun tarar dasu dady suna hira, aunty yasmeen ta wuce kichin don sama musu abinda zasu ci, suhailat taso ta bita suyi aikin tare amma yasmeen ta hana ta dole ta xauna tare dasu amma fa duk jinta tk a takure saboda irin kallon da FK ke jifanta dashi ta rasa na menene duk motsin ta akan idonsa, har aunty yasmeen ta gama abinda tk yi ta dawo ta xauna itama, dady ne ya kallesu gaba 1 sannan yace." FK yace."haka ne dady Allah y jik'an mahaifiyar tata. " gaba 1 suka amsa da."ameen. " sannan dady yafara magana da cewa."


" Ni dn kasuwa ne kuma ina kowanne irin kasuwanci duk abinda nasan xai kawo min cigaba a kasuwanci na shi nakeyi, ina kasuwancin kaya na sawa abinci harkar gwala_gwalai ds, watarana ina zaune wani aboki na yk bani shawarar in shiga harkar dabbobi to Allah da ikonsa na amince da shawarar sa na fara haka ya had'ani da wani bawan Allah alhj kabiru da yk kawo mishi shanu daga Adamawa, nima ya fara kawo min ckn ikon Allah harkar ta kar6e ni har ta kai nima da kaina ina shiga can adamawar don siyen shanu, ina siyan shanaye masu yawa a loda min su a mota a kawo min kano, da haka muka saba da alhj kabiru idan naxo Adamawa a gidan su nk sauka shi yasa nasan kowa a gidan su, Khadija kanwar sa ce yarinya me hankali mun saba da ita sosae idan nazo garin ita ke d'awainiyar dafa min abinci, a kwana a tashi sabon da mukayi ya rikid'e xuwa soyayya me tsananin gsk ni kaina nayi mamakin yanda nk sonta, ba tare da 6ata lokaci ba aka tsaida magana, shi ma alhj kabiru a lokacin baiyi aure ba akwai wacce yk neman aurenta anan kano don haka aka had'a auren lokaci 1. Byn ta tare, na fuskanci tashin hankali daga wajen rabi (Momy) saida na dage sannan na samu saukin abun amma duk da haka Khadija Sam bata jin dad'in zama da rabi don dai kawai tana da tsananin hakuri." Dady ya goge kwallar da ta taru a idon sa sannan ya cigaba."ina jin dad'in rayuwa da Khadija domin ita dn mace ce wacce tasan kanta duk abinda nk so shi tk yi tunda nk da ita bata ta6a 6ata min ba sai dn abinda ba'a rasa ba, inna ta tana sonta saboda yanda itama ta d'auke ta tamkar mahaifiyar ta, sa6anin rabi da ko xuwa inda mahaifiya ta tk bata yi, hakan ya k'ara sanya rabi ta k'ara tsanar Khadija saboda tana ganin taxo ta shiga tsakanin ta da mahaifiya ta.




Ana ckn wannan yanayin Khadija ta samu rabo nayi murna sosae koda yake a lokacin yara na 2 kai abubakar da kuma yasmeen, babu shakkah lokacin da rabi ta fahimci Khadija na da ciki hankalin ta ya tashi musamman da taga ina farinciki da samun shi, to ana haka sai kuma tai 6ari wata 5 kwata-kwata banji dad'in faruwar hakan ba amma babu yanda xanyi haka Allah y nufa, rayuwa ta cigaba a haka da dad'i ba dad'i xuwa lokacin na daina harkar shanu don na fara fita k'asashen duniya akan harkar gwala_gwalai shi yasa na ajiye kowacce irin harka na maida hankali na a kanta gaba 1, ana haka Khadija ta k'ara samun wani rabon saida yai 6months sannan ya fita gsky wannan karon na xargi rabi akan xubewar wannan cikin saboda itace ta bawa khadijan wani magani tace tasha aikuwa tana sha abun ya faru naso na d'auki mataki akan rabi amma ita khadijan da kanta ta dinga bani hakuri haka dole nayi shiru da maganar amma na kafa wa Khadija doka akan kada ta kuma shan duk wani abu da rabi xata bata ko da bata da ciki. 



Khadija mutuniyar kirki ce dukda irin abinda rabi keyi mata na musgunawa amma hakan bai hana ta rik'e mata yaran ta da daraja ba sadeeq da yasmeen duk a wajen ta kuka yi wayo itace ta yi muku tarbiyyah saboda mahaifiyar Ku mace ce wacce bata damu da jan yaro a jikinta ba hakan tasa kunfi sabawa da Khadija ita kam rabi Sam bata damu ba don gani take Khadijan ta zamo tamkar wata y'ar aikin ta, tunda tana yiwa y'ay'an ta bauta ita kuma har yanxu bata haihu ba, ana ckn wannan yanayin ne Khadija ta k'ara samun wani rabon lokacin shekarar mu 6 da aure, wannan karon na sanya ido a kanta da duk wani motsin ta da kuma duk abinda xata ci, rabi ta k'ara bata wannan maganin Wanda ta bata wancan lokacin amma da yake na hana ta sai bata sha ba, haka ciki ya cigaba da girma lokacin da ya kai wata 7 inna tace ta dawo gidan ta saboda ta kula da ita haka kuwa akayi ta koma can, yara na a can suke yin weekend saboda kin zama suke yi don basu saba da mahaifiyar su ba, ni kaina kwanciya ce kawai ke dawo dani gida na,xuwa wannan lokacin itama rabi ta haifi isah tunda ta haifeshi ba ruwanta da mu kullum ta d'anta tk.


 Lokacin da Khadija tafara nak'uda minti 20 ta haifi y'ar ta mace kuma a gida lpy klw, daga baya munje hospital an duba su dukkan su suna ckn k'oshin lpy, munyi farinciki da samun wannan haihuwa ranar suna yarinya taci sunan inna wato Maryam mahaifiyar ta da kanta ta sanya mata SUHAILAT.

Byn anyi suna kowa ya watse y'an Adamawa sun so su tafi da Khadija saboda tayi wanka a gida amma na hana suma ganin mahaifiya ta na matukar kula da ita basu matsa akan al'amarin ba, saida tayi 40 sannan inna ta barta ta koma gida na, munyi matukar murnar dawowar Khadija gidan ni da yara na saboda tamkar marayu muka zama lokacin da bata nan rabi bata damu da matsala ta ba balle ta yaranta duk da a can suke yin weekend, farinciki baya misaltuwa a wajen mu haka ta cigaba da d'awainiya damu ni da yara na ga baby hakan tasa shak'uwa me tsanani ta shiga tsakanin sadeeq yasmeen da kuma baby suhailat suna matukar son yarinyar kamar yanda nima nake jin sonta a raina kuma hakan yana da nasaba da yanda mahaifiyar ta ke bamu kulawa gashi kuma sunan mahaifiya ta ce, mama na ta taso ckn so da k'auna ta wad'annan yayun nata." Dady ya fad'a tare da nuna su y sadeeq murmushi su kayi gaba 1 sannan dady ya cigaba da magana. "




Rabi dai ba ta damu da kasancewar Khadija da yaran ta ba ita a ganin ta khadijan ta rage mata wahala ne, haka rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya, suhailat ta girma tayi wayo harma an yaye ta, yarinya ce me shiga ran kowa sai dai abinda na lura dashi shine rabi bata k'aunar yarinyar amma da yk naga wad'anda ma ta haifa da cikin ta bata damu dasu nima hakan bai dame ni ba, sai na watsar da duk wani tunani.

Zuwa lokacin harkoki na sun k'ara ha66aka na canxa gida kud'i ya xauna na mallaki kadarori masu yawa, a wannan lokacin na fahimci Khadija da rabi duk sun samu rabo sai dai rabi tana gaba da Khadija don ita ta kusa haihuwa, Sam rabi bata San cewa Khadija na d'auke da ciki saida ta lura duk abinda nk siya don haihuwar 2 ne nan fa ta matsa min da tanbaya har saida na fad'a mata gsky, tabbas nayi mamakin abinda na gani a fuskar ta don matsanancin tashin hankali na hango, ckn mamaki na dubeta saboda na kasa hakura nace." Me kk nufi naga canjin yanayi a face dn ki." Shiru tayi kfn daga baya ta dn gyara nutsuwar ta tace. "Nayi mamaki ne da ban fahimci Khadija na d'auke da ciki ba gashi har ta kusa haihuwa." Had'e rai nayi kfn nace. " to meye had'inki da cikin ta don baki fahimta ba abun ya dame ki. " Ta kowa tayi zuwa inda nk sannan ta dn kamo hannu na tace. "Kayi hakuri alhj ba wani abu nk nufi ba kaga ita dn y'ar uwa ta ce kuma gashi tana samun matsalar 6ari ko da shawara xan iya tai maka mata balle ma baxn rasa abinda xn...." 

"Dakata rabi Allah xai raba ta dashi lfy kamar yanda ta haifi mama na lfy kuma bana buk'atar ki tai maka mata da komai." Ina gama fad'in haka na fice na bar mata d'akin.
Kwana 2 da faruwar hakan ita rabin ta haihu d'iya mace ranar suna yarinya taci sunan Khadija, innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraju'un ranar naga tashin hankali don kuwa rabi cewa tayi baxata yiwa Khadija takwara ba haka tak'i shayar da yarinyar cin mutunci kala_kala sai da taga raina ya 6aci saboda a lokacin saida nayi mata barazana idan bata nutsu ba zan iya sakin ta sannan ta nutsu amma tace baxata iya kiran sunan y'ar ta da Khadija ba sai dai tace mata kubra haka na k'yaleta don kawai a xauna lpy.




3week's da haihuwar rabi Khadija ta fara nak'uda ranar bana gida ita kad'ai ke murk'ususun ta a d'aki wuni guda har ta fara fita haiyacin ta, yasmeen ce ta shiga d'akin ta same ta ckn wannan halin tai saurin d'aukar wayar khadija ta kira nomber ta saboda a lokacin tayi wayo sosae, lokacin da naga kiran da sauri na d'aga ina ta magana shiru jin hakan ya tabbatar min ba lpy don haka da sauri nayi hanyar gida, tun Khadija na iya yin yunk'uri na haihuwa har ta kasa ganin haka yasa yasmeen ta ruga da gudu ta sanar da mahaifiyar ta abinda yk faruwa.



Ni dai lokacin da na shigo d'akin na tarar da rabi tana kokarin bata wani abu a kofi ita kam Khadija hankalin ta baya jikinta don haka da rabi ta bata abun haka ta kar6a tasha, ni ma hankali na a matuk'ar tashe nayi wajen ta Khadija ko motsin kirki ba tayi, d'auko ta nayi muka nufi hospital ckn d'an k'ara min lokaci muka isa doctors suka rufu a kanta saida naga an shiga da ita sannan hankali na ya d'an dawo jiki na, inna ta na kira na shaida mata abinda yk faruwa ckn k'ank'anin lokachi sai gata ita da su yasmeen har ma da suhailat, nayi mamaki da banga rabi ta xo hospital dn ba, amma da yk Sam hankali na ba'a kan haka yk ba ban bi ta kanta ba, nan muka xauna muna faman jiran doctors, kusan 1hours wani doctor ya fito muka mik'e da sauri muka je wajen sa, hannu ya mik'a min face dn shi da damuwa yace. "Na tayaka murna matarka ta haifi d'a namiji." ckn sauri nace." Y lpyr me jegon."? Kallo na yayi sannan yace. "Maganar gsky ta dad'e tana nak'uda hakan ne yasa tasha matuk'ar wahala da k'yar ta iya haihuwa da kanta don har mun yanke shawarar aiki xa'a yi mata don ceton rayuwar su, to ckn ikon Allah sai ta haihu da kanta amma dai ita tana ckn matsanancin hali saboda yanxu ma sai da muka yi mata allura sannan tayi bacci amma dai muna da hope xata farka normal, zaku iya shiga Ku ganta amma banda surutu." Gdy nayi wa doctor sannan muka shiga ciki Khadija na kwance tana bacci ga kuma jaririn a gefenta shima baccin yk hannu nasa na d'auki yaron kamarmu 1 dashi sak nayi mishi hud'uba da sunan mahaifin Khadija wato Ahmad sannan na mik'a wa inna shi, yaran ma duk suka nufi wajen inna suna lek'a jaririn.




Kusan awa 2 sannan Khadija ta farka lokacin ni ina masallaci sallar magrib, suhailat da yasmeen ne kawai ke d'akin inna ma sallah take wajen ta suka nufa hannun su ta kamo ta had'a waje 1 ta rik'e su hawaye na fita ta gefen idonta a haka muka shigo muka same su, sadeeq ma wajen ta ya nufa cike da murna, juyo wa tayi tana kallon mu inna tace. "Sannu Khadija y jikin naki." Bata iya amsawa ba sai faman binmu da kallo tk tana k'ok'arin motsa bakin ta alamar magana tk so tayi, a hankali na tako xuwa inda tk na xauna nace." Sannu ina ptn babu abinda yk damun ki yanxu. "?


Sai a lokacin ta samu damar yin magana muryar ta da k'yar take fita tace."alhj ina rok'onka ka yafe min duk abinda nayi maka sannan na baka amanar yara na ka kula min dasu don Allah nasan cewa baxan tashi ba ciki na wani irin ciwo yk." Ckn tashn hankali nace." Ki daina fad'in haka Khadija xaki tashi insha'allah da kanki xaki raini yaran ki a hannun ki zasu girma insha'allah". 


Xuciya ta ta karye na fara kuka inna ce ke mana nasiha, muna ckn wannan halin wani irin amai ya taso mata kfn ta yunk'ura har ya wanke mana jakin mu abun mamaki wani abu ne me wari bak'ik'irin hankali na ya tashi nayi waje da gudu in kira doctor, lokacin da muka shigo tare da likita aman ya koma na jini, tashin hankali Wanda ba'a sa masa rana, nan doctors suka k'ara rufuwa a kanta ni kam babu abinda nk sai ajiyar xuciya da k'yar na iya xuwa na wanke jikina da ya 6aci da amai sannan na dawo , na samu inna da yara na sunyi jugum da jaririn a hannun ta.


Doctor ya fito yana goge gumi face dn sa d'auke da damuwa kfn yace komai na tanbaye shi ckn tsananin damuwa da nuna tausayi ya dafa shoulder dina yace. " kayi hak'uri alhj munyi iyakar kokarin mu don ganin mun ceto rayuwar ta amma abun yaci tura lokacin ta yayi sai dai a gwaje-gwajen da mu kayi mun gano tasha wani abu me kama da guba Wanda shine yai sanadiyyar rayuwar....... Ban k'ara sa jin abinda xai fad'a ba na yanke jiki na fad'i sumamme.


 
Lokacin dana farka an sanya min ruwa xuciyata ta tuno da abinda ya faru take na fixge alluran dk soke a jijiya ta na fito waje, ina fitowa na nufi d'akin da Khadija tk akwance na tarar da gawar ta, su inna sun gewayeta suna kuka, da gudu na k'ara sa inda suke na bud'e fuskarta, tayi wasai kamar xa'a kira ta ta amsa ckn tsananin rud'ani da tashin hankali nace." Me yasa xaki tafi ki barmu Khadija a dai-dai lokacin da mk tsananin buk'atar ki."  Dafa bayana inna tayi sannan tace. "Ba kuka ne abinda ya dace muyi ba mu'axxam addu'a zamuyi mata duk da cewa muna kyautata mata zato Khadija insha'allahu ta dace." Tsaida kuka na nayi a hankali nk kwararo mata addu'oi sai naji na samu nutsuwa, haka aka bamu gawar muka tafi gida zuciyoyin mu Sam babu dad'i.


Gidan inna muka nufa a nan aka shirya ta muka sallace ta aka kaita gidan ta na gsky, nayi kewar Khadija kuma nayi kukan rashin mace ta gari, yarana sunyi rashin uwa ta gari, rabi kuwa ko gaisuwa bata yi mana ba ni kam ban damu da hakan ba saboda ma gsky a lokacin xarginta nk yi akan rasuwar Khadijan saboda tun lokacin da likita ya shaida min binciken su ya nuna musu tasha wani abu me kama da guba na tuna lokacin da naxo gidan nasamu rabi tana bata wani abu a ckn kofi, sannan gashi da ta rasu ko gaisuwa rabin ba tayi ba wannan ne ya k'ara dasa min xargin ta a ckn xuciya ta, amma lokacin da nayiwa inna magana sai tace kada na d'aga maganar idan ma da hannun ta a ciki mu barta da Allah tunda Khadijan ba dawowa xata yi ba, kuma yin maganar zai janyo wata matsalar.



Y'an Adamawa sun xo gaisuwa suma mutuwar ta shigesu musamman mahaifiyar ta haka ta rungume y'ay'an Khadija a jkn ta tana kuka, da kyar aka lallasheta saida akayi 7 suka shirya zasu tafi a lokacin ta buk'aci a bata 1 ckn y'ay'an Khadijan gsky a lokacin ni ban so rabuwa da kowanne daga ckn su ba amma babu yanda xanyi itama tana da hakki akan y'ay'an, inna kam ta riga ta saba da suhailat don haka Ahmad ta bata duk da cewa a yanxu shine abun tausayin haka suka tafi dashi xuciyoyin mu ba dad'i, a can aka cigaba da kula dashi ta hanyar bashi tatacciyar madara.



Byn wata 3 hankali na ya fara kwanciya na fara sabawa da rashin Khadija sannan kuma nasan yaranta ba zasu yi maraici ba muddin kakannin su suna da rai.


Inna tana xuwa Adamawa duk byn wata 5 don ta kai suhailat su ganta sannan itama taga ahmad ni kaina lokaci-lokaci ina xuwa don ganin shi zuwa yanxu duk sun girma sunyi wayo, a lokacin da suhailat ta cika shekara 10 inna kamar yanda ta saba ta shirya ta suka nufi Adamawa saboda anyi hutun makaranta, byn sunje sun gama kwanakin su a hanyar su ta dawowa Allah y aiki musu da had'ari Wanda yai sanadiyyar rasa ran mutane da yawa ciki harda inna, naga tashin hankali Wanda ban ta6a ganin irin sa ba haka aka kirani a waya naje na kar6o gawar inna, kuka kam nayi shi kamar baxan daina ba ita kam suhailat babu abinda ya sameta sai y'an raunuka wad'anda ba xa'a rasa ba, nayi jinyar xuciya ta kfn na dawo dai-dai sai dai babu abinda yk d'aga min hankali irin makomar rayuwar suhailat don yanxu ita kad'ai ce abar tausayi.


Da farko nayi tunanin na maida ita can Adamawa wajen dangin mahaifiyar ta amma sai naga abun baxai yiwu ba saboda yanxu ita kad'ai nk kallah na tuna muhimman mutanen da na rasa a duniya Khadija da inna ta, tana kama da Khadija sannan gashi sunan mahaifiya ta ne da ita wannan dalilin ne yasa na fasa kaita Adamawa, wajen rabi na kawo ta nayi mata nasiha mai ratsa xuciya akan ta rik'e min ita amana, ta nuna min xata rik'e ta amana insha'allah, to ganin yanda ta fara tafiyar da al'amarin ba cutarwa yasa na saki jiki akan baxata cutar min da ita ba.

Lokacin da tfy ta kama ni ba tare da wata fargaba ba na tafi don ni a tunani na baxata ta6a cutar min da yarinya ba sai gashi na dawo na tarar da akasin haka.


Anan dady yai shiru yana goge hawayen dk mak'ale a idon sa, duk parlorn anyi shiru babu Wanda yk iya yin magana masu sharar k'wallah nayi masu goge gumi nayi, suhailat kam share hawaye tk amma face dn ta cike da farinciki koba komai taji tarihin rayuwar ta sannan an tuna mata da d'an uwanta da kuma inna tabbas sai yanxu ta tuna lokacin da sukaje Adamawa na k'arshe don lokacin da wayon ta duk abinda ya faru akan idonta ya faru.

Y sadeeq ne ya katse musu xaman shirun da suka yi yace." Tabbas wannan labari ya tuna min da abubuwa da yawa, Allah sarki umma Allah y jikinta da ita da inna, na tuna yanda ta rik'e mu tsakanin ta da Allah tarbiyyar da muka samu mun sameta ne daga wajen ta, shi yasa muke alfahari da ita kuma muke mata addu'a a koda yaushe Allah y kai rahma kabarin ta." Yasmeen tace. "Ameen dai y sadeeq ni nasan shak'uwa da soyayyar da muka yi da suhailat ba banxa ba mahaifiyar ta ta rik'e mu tamkar y'ay'an ta don haka muma baxamu ta6a bari ta tozarta ba insha'allah." FK yace. "Ni kaina da naji wannan labarin naji tausayin suhailat sannan na jin jina wa mahaifiyar ta da ta rik'e ku tsakanin ta da Allah, Allah dai ya gafarta mata ameen."

Dady ya kallesu cike da farinciki sannan yace."ina so ku had'a kanku yara na Allah yayi muku albarka gaba 1." Suka amsa da "ameen." FK yace. " to yanxu ina Momy da sauran y'an uwan namu."? 

Dady yai murmushin takaici kfn yace. "Gsky ban San inda tk ba saboda na yanke mata hukuncin da ya dace da ita, kubra kam nasan tana tare da ita isah kuwa yana can yana karatu." Subhanallah me kk nufi dady yanxu kana nufin....... "Na sake ta." Dady yak'arasa maganar tare da mik'e wa tsaye "yanxu dai a bar wannan maganar mu tunkari sabuwar rayuwar da tk gaban mu."cewar dady yana kokarin barin parlorn.


Kallon y sadeeq FK yayi sannan yace." Yanxu haka zamu zuba ido ba xamu binciko inda momy tk ba muyi k'ok'ari ta dawo ko Allah zaisa su dai-daita da dady."?

Rausayar da kansa y sadeeq yayi kfn yace. "A yanxu dai addu'a ya dace mu dinga yi saboda a halin yanxu dady yana ckn matsanancin fushi da momy kuma idan yayi fishi da mutum yana dad'e wa kfn ya sauko, sannan mu kanmu abinda Momy tayi bamu ji dad'in sa ba shi yasa muke so sai ta dn horu kfn mu nemo inda tk mu rok'i dady y maida ita amma gsky ba yanxu ba." Girgiza kai FK yayi alamar gamsuwa, sannan suka mik'e suka nufi masallaci don gabatar da sallah.


Yasmeen da suhailat suma suka tafi dkn su don gabatar da sallah, byn sun idar parlorn suka k'ara dawowa suka tarar dasu sun dawo daga masallaci, kai tsaye kichin suka nufa suka d'ebo kayan abincin da yasmeen ta dafa

Kubra kam tana can gidan najib suna rayuwar su tamkar mata da miji, sai dai xaman su tare ya haifar musu da matsala saboda kubra ta gane ba da ita kad'ai najib yk mu'amala ba, hakane yasanya ta tashi hankalin ta, shi kam ko a jikin sa don yanxu yafara gajiya da ita musamman da yk ganin tana takura mishi tana sa masa ido akan al'amarin sa sai kace wata matar sa, abun ne ya dameta saboda yanxu duk wata kulawa da yk bata ya rage shi yasa ta shirya tafiya gidan su miemy don tasanar mata abinda yk faruwa ko zata bata shawara, byn ya sauketa ko shiga baiyi ba ya juya don a kwai wata baby da sukayi magana zasu had'u da ita, kai tsaye ta shiga ckn gidan ba tare da wani tunani ba ta kutsa kanta ckn parlorn gidan, da sauri ta juyo saboda abinda ta gani meimy ce tare da auntyn ta suke iskancin su basuji ma motsin shigowar ta ba, ta dad'e a tsaye tana jin numfashin su kfn taji sunyi shiru hakan ne ya tabbatar mata sun dawo ckn nutsuwar su don haka ta sake d'aga labule ta shiga, yanxu kam sun ganta sai dai har yanxu suna manne da juna, jikinta a sanyaye ta shiga tan bin su da kallo, miemy ta mik'e ta k'araso wajen ta tana wani shu'umin murmushi tare da fad'in ya akayi ta najib ina masoyin naki ne."? 

D'an tsaki kubra taja cike da jin haushi tace. "Ni k'yale ni da wani najib." Sai kuma ta tuna a gaban aunty lubna suke da sauri ta juyo tana kallon inda tk kfn tace. "Sannu aunty ina wuni." Ta amsa, kfn ta mik'e da nufin barin parlorn byn sunyi signa da miemy Wanda su kad'ai suka san abinda suke nufi da hakan, janyo hannun kubra meimy tayi suka xauna akan kujera sannan cike da duniyan ci tace. "Haba kubra me najib dn naki yai miki kuma."? Kubra ta d'an dafa kafad'ar miemy cike da damuwa tace." K'awata najib ya fara juya min baya duk irin soyayyar da nk nuna masa amma saida yaci amana ta, Ashe yana da wasu y'an matan a waje da yk mu'amala dasu  ban sani ba. "Wata mahaukaciyar dariya miemy ta kwashe da ita tsawon 2minutes kfn ta kalleta tace." Au ke a naki haukan kina tunanin ke kad'ai yk kulawa idan kuwa hakane tunanin ki to kinyi babban kuskure don najib tun kfn ya had'u dk yana da y'an matan sa ke harma clubs yk zuwa. " jinjina kai kubra tayi cike da takaici kfn tace. "To yanxu me nene mafita, wlh hankali na duk a tashe yk kinsan Ina son shi da yawa."


"Dallah ni ki min shiru kina son shi to Ina ruwa na nasha fad'a miki ba'a yiwa namiji irin wannan soyayyar bare kuma namijin bariki wad'anda daman basu San wata amana ba, kefa yanxu tamkar karuwa haka kk koma ince dadiro tunda a gidan sa kk dole yai miki duk abinda yaga dama." Ita dai kubra shiru tayi tana jin maganganun miemy, ganin kubra tayi shiru hakan tasa ta bijiro mata da abinda ke ranta ckn murya me sanyi tace. "Shawara 1 xan baki itace ki rabu da najib kixo muyi harka duk kalar macen da kk so aunty lubna xata samo miki kinga shikenan kin huta da matsalar d'a namiji." Da sauri kubra ta kalle ta sai kuma ta tuna a irin mood dn da ta same su lokacin da ta xo gidan ckn in ina tk fad'in." kina nufin abinda naga kunayi da aunty lubna, to wai ma yaushe kuka shirya da aunty lubna har ma na ganku ckn irin wannan halin." Murmushin gefen baki miemy tayi sannan tace. "Labari ne me tsayi idan kina so kiji kiyi maja damu xaki sha labari." Kubra ta juya kai " gsky sai nayi shawara don ina ji a jiki na bazan iya rayuwa babu najib ba." Wani dogon tsaki miemy taja sannan ta mik'e tana fad'in. " To ai sai ki tashi ki koma gidan nasa duk ranar da yayi miki korar kare sai ki nemi gurin fakewa." Tana gama fad'in haka ta bar parlorn ta shige bedroom dn aunty lubna.



Mik'e wa kubra tayi jikinta a sanyaye ta bar gidan, kai tsaye gidan najib dn ta koma nape ta hau ya kaita har kofar gidan, kai tsaye d'akin da tk kwana ta nufa ba tare da tunanin komai ba ta tura kofar ta shiga, da tasan abinda xata gani da bata shiga ba, don kuwa najib ne tare da wata yarinya suna aikata bad'alar su, basu ji shigowar ta ba sai ihun da suka ji ya cika dodon kunnen su, da sauri suka dawo ckn nutsuwar su, kubra ta wawuri glass cup tayi kansu dashi, ckn matukar xafin nama najib ya fixge tare da rik'e ta, kokarin kwacewa tk tana fad'in "ka cuce ni najib kaci amana ta ina soyayyar da kk fad'in kana yi min ashe duk yaudara ce." Yarinyar da tk tunanin kubra matar najib ce tuni ta suturta jikinta hanya kawai tk so ta samu ta fece, najib ya rik'e kubra dk faman xille-xille wai ita dole sai ta daki yarinyar ckn dabara ya bud'e wa yarinyar kofa yai mata nuni da ta fita, a guje ta fice ko takalminta bata tsaya sanya wa ba don ita a tunanin ta kubra matar najib ce shi yasa tk wannan haukan don ta ganshi da mace.


 Sai da najib ya tabbatar mubina ta bar gidan sannan ya saki kubra, aikuwa ta damk'o wuyan sa tana auna masa xagi kala_kala ganin irin haukan da tk masa gashi ta shak'e masa wuya bai san lokacin da yayi ball da ita ba sannan shima ya fara yi mata nasa sababin xagi yk auna mata ta uwa ta uba yana fad'in ta bar masa gidan sa indai ita dn ba mayyah bace, kubra ckn tsananin mamakin tujarar da najib ke mata ta d'ago ido fal hawaye tace. " tabbas najib yau ka nuna min halayyar maza na d'auki soyayyar duniya na damk'a maka amma ka rasa da abinda zaka saka min sai wannan, ka sani ka cutar min da rayuwa ta kaine Wanda ka raba ni da budurci na sannan kayi min alkawari zaka aure ni ashe duk yaudara ce." Ta tsagaita da maganar saboda kuka da yaci k'arfin ta, najib ya dubeta a kai-kaice ya bushe da dariya saida yai mai isarsa sannan yace." Au ke a haukanki kina ganin zan iya aurar karuwa irin ki."? To idan baki sani ba yau ki sani baxan ta6a auran wacce ta zubar da darajarta a waje ba kuma koda wani naga zai aure ki, da gudu zanje na sanar dashi ke dn karuwa ce, kuma da kk maganar budurcin ki ai ba nine naje na same ki ba da kanki kk kawo kanki ina zama na akace na tai maka miki kina ckn wani hali kinga kuwa ni taimako ma nayi." Kalaman najib sunyi wa kubra xafi matuk'a bata ta6a tunanin jinsu daga bakin sa ba lalle miemy ta fad'a mata gsky da tace namijin bariki bashi da tabbas kuka take yi sosai ta kasa magana, ganin ta tsaya tana kuka xata 6ata masa lokaci yace. " ke karuwa maza ki tashi ki bar min gida tun kafin nayi miki korar kare da sauri ta kalleshi saboda ta tuna abinda miemy ta fad'a mata yanxu da tace. " duk ranar da yai miki korar kare saiki nemi gurin fakewa. " Ashe yau dn nan zai mata korar karen, zuwa yanxu ta daina kuka idonta ya k'ek'ashe ta kalleshi cike da tsana sannan tace." Ka cika butulu Wanda bai san abun arzik'i ba kasani zan bar maka gida kuma ni da kai har abada amma ka jira fansa ta duk dad'e wa sai na rama abinda kayi min." Tana gama fad'ar haka ta fice daga gidan a guje ba tare da ta tsaya d'aukar kayanta da suke gidan ba, shi kam ko a jikinsa don a yanxu ba itace a gabansa ba ya rufe babinta yanxu shi yanda zai lallashi mubina yk tunani sabuwar yarinyar sa.

Kai tsaye gidan su miemy ta koma, lokacin da miemy ta ganta ckn wannan halin ta tausaya mata sai dai hakan ba zai hanata fad'a mata abinda yk ranta ba don haka ta kalleta tace. "Ayyah laila me kuma majnoon dn yai miki naga kin dawo haka a firgice."?  Kukan da kubra ke mak'alewa ne ya k'ara kufce mata wannan cin amanar da najib yayi mata baxata ta6a mantawa dashi ba, miemy bata katseta ba saima ts�yawa da tayi tana kallon ta, sai da tayi mai isarta kfn ta dubi miemy tace."miemy najib yayi min abinda ban ta6a tunani ba, wai ni yau najib ya kora daga ckn gidan sa byn na kama shi da mace wai kawai don na nuna masa kishi na shine ya ci min mutunci ya koreni." Ta k'ara sa maganar tana k'ara matso hawaye, miemy tayi dn shiru tana nazarin maganganun kubra kfn daga baya ta fara lallashinta saida tayi shiru sannan miemy ta fara magana da cewa."na fad'a miki namiji ba dn goyo bane ni tuntuni na dawo daga rakiyar su yanxu aunty lubna da k'awayenta sunfi ye min duk wani d'a namiji, don haka kema ki shigo a dama dake kiji dad'in rayuwar ki na tabbata sai kin manta da wani abu wai shi najib." Miemy ta karasa maganar tare da kamo hannun kubra." Ki saki jikinki kin san ba xn cutar dk ba." Gyada kai kubra tayi cike da yadda da k'awar tata, sai dai tana tunanin yanda xata iya yin irin wannan rayuwar da y'ar uwarta mace, kamar miemy tasan tunanin da tk yi sai cewa tayi."karki damu ni da kaina xn koya miki harkar ga kuma aunty lubna ita kam gwana ce a wannan 6angaren." Hmm ke dai bari ai wata rana ne ammar yazo waje na sai na shiga falonta don na kar6i mayafi na a wajen ta, sai naga bata falon sai dai naji motsi da wani irin numfashi na tashi a bedroom dn ta duk da nasan ba abun arzik'i xn gani ba amma haka kawai naji ina son shiga, ina shiga na samesu da k'awarta a irin yanayin da kk ganmu nayi mamaki sosae amma nayi kokarin 6oye mamaki na, na fara k'wala mata kira da hankalin ta ya dawo itama taji tsoron ganin da nayi mata amma sai ta maze ta had'a rai tana tanbaya ta lpy na shigo mata d'aki? Ba tare da naji tsoron komai ba na sanar da ita abinda ya kawo ni wullo min tayi tana watso min harara tabbas nasan taji haushi da tsoron ganin da nayi mata, ni kuwa ina fita wajen ammar naje na shigo dashi har d'aki na tunda naga itama matar gidan abinda taga dama takeyi, muna ckn harkar mu na ga shigowar ta muna had'a ido ta bar d'akin, saboda bata yi tunanin hakan ba ammar yana tfy ta kirani mu kayi magana akan kowa ya rufawa kowa asiri tunda kowa yaga abinda wani kowa yk aikata wa, to tunda ga wannan lokacin aunty lubna bata jin kunyar yin komai a gaba na haka nima a hankali kuma sai ta fara jan ra'ayina akan abinda tk yi saboda wata k'awarta ta yaba da tsari na haka ta fara koya min harkar kuma tana jana gurin k'awayenta ga harka ga kud'i tabbas nasan kina fara wannan abun zaki manta da najib don kuwa nima tuntuni muka yi hannun riga da ammar ba ammar ba duk wani d'a namiji yanxu baya gaba na, kubra ta sauke nannauyar ajiyar xuciya dole ta kar6i tayin da miemy ke mata saboda ta riga ta saba baxata iya zama a haka ba, ta dubi miemy tace. _____________________________Momy kam an dawo gida cike da farinciki babu abinda tk tunawa sai gata tana juya alhj son ranta tana tuna hakan zataji wani dad'i a ranta, byn sun huta sunci abinci nan suka xauna suna bawa innar kaltume labarin wahalar da suka sha a hanya, a ckn wannan daren momy ta shirya tafiya da safe saboda ta matsu ta fara mulki,ita kam kaltume tace sai ta dn k'ara kwana 2 zata dawo, sun dad'e suna hira kfn kowa ya kwanta.



Tun asuba Momy ta shirya kaltume ta rakota har wajen mota bayan tayiwa inna gdy tare da basu kud'i masu yawa sukayi sallama akan xata yiwa kaltumen waya da zarar komai ya dai-dai ta, duk wahalar dk ckn tfyr Momy bata ji ba saboda a ckn farinciki tk, lokacin da suka isa kano dare yay gidani wata k'awarta  hjy lylah ta nufa daman tun tana hanya tq shaida mata xuwan ta, hjy lylah ta kalli momy tace."Hjy rabi me ya sameki ne naganki haka duk a firgice kin rame kinyi baki' wlh ba don tunda k'uruciya muke tare ba ko ganeki ba xanyi ba." Momy tayi ajiyar xuciya sannan tace. " hjy lylah kenan kin dai riga kin sani matsala ta bata wuce alhj to yanxu ma shine yai min abinda ya saba banda duka da nasha ya karya min hannu ke harda saki na yayi kuma kin san duk saboda wannan tsinanniyar yarinyar ne." Kama baki hjy lylah tayi ckn mamaki sannan cike da zuga tace. " to ke wanne irin mataki kk d'auka ko zuba masa ido kk yi ya kashe ki akan wata banxar y'ar sa."?  Ckn dariya Momy tace. "Hmm kin dai sanni ai tayaya zan xauna yanxu ma daga wajen wani hamshak'in boka nk ya bani abinda zan mallake shi sai yanda naga dama dashi." Ckn dariya hjy lylah ta mik'a mata hannu suka tafa "shegiya k'awata amma baki da dama to me yasa baki wuce gidan ba, nifa kinsan ina da gaggawa." hjy lylah ta fad'a tana murmushi.

Momy tace. "Duk gaggawar ki ai baki kaini ba ni da abun ya faru a kaina, boka ne yace min kar na doshi gidan na xauna a kusa da gidan da kanshi zai nemi a maida auren mu kinga daga wannan lokacin na samu tawa damar, to shine naga babu inda ya dace na xauna sai nan kinsan ba nida kowa mahaifiya ta ta rasu y'an uwa kowa ya gujeni koda yk nice ma na guje su yanxu kuwa idan na koma ai da matsala." Hjy lylah tace." Karki damu hjy rabi indai wannan ne baki da matsala kiyi xaman ki har sai ya kawo kanshi." Gdy Momy rayons dama ta sani baxata ta6a samun matsala da k'awarta lylah ba, nan suka cigaba da hirar su kfn hjy lylah ta koma nata d'akin.

Da gudu yk jan motar gani yk kamar bata sauri burin sa 1 ya ganshi a kofar gidan 20minutes ne ya kawo shi gidan baba me gadi ya bud'e masa get kai tsaye ya shiga ckn gidan byn yayi parking a parlor ya samesu gaba 1 suna break fast ckn mamaki suke binshi da kallo irin wannan sammako da yayi, shi kam ko a jikinsa ya k'ara sa wajen dady ya gaida shi sannan ya mik'a wa y sadeeq hannu sukai musabaha aunty yasmeen ta gaida shi cike da girmamawa ita kam suhailat haka kawai ta tsinci kanta da jin nauyin sa don haka a kunyace tace. Shi kansa dady yaso yaje su gaisa da mahaifiyar fahad amma yanda yk jin jikinsa bazai iya zuwa ba sam baya jin dad'i wani irin nauyi kansa yk masa sannan ckn y'an kwanakin nan yana yawan tunanin rabi, zuciyar sa na fad'a masa abinda yayi mata bai kyauta ba, musamman tunda yasan batada iyaye a raye kenan baisan inda zata tafi ba byn korar karen da yai mata, sai dai da zarar ya tuna abinda rabi tayiwa y'ar sa yana jin tsananin 6acin rai, amma kuma can k'asan xuciyar sa yana tunanin inda zai nemo rabi, don ma yaransa suna d'ebe masa kewa suna hana shi yin tunanin.



Yau kama da ya kasance shi kad'ai a gidan ya wuni ckn tunani Wanda ya k'ara tsananta masa ciwon kansa sai dai ya yanke shawarar zai sanar da yaran sa abinda yk damunsa don yasan zasu yi masa maganin matsalarsa.



Ammy ta kar6esu hannu 2 cike da farinciki sai dai lokacin da ta kalli suhailat ta tuna da wad'ansu abubuwa da suka faru a rayuwar ta saboda suhailat dn tayi matuk'ar yin kama da wani jigo a rayuwar ta, saurin kawar da wannan tunanin tayi saboda tasan babu yanda za'ayi abinda tk zargi ya tabbata,  byn sun gama gaisawa ta kalli FK tace." lallai my boy na yaba da new family dn nan naka kuma ina ptn nima zaku sanya ni a ckn sa." Y Sadeeq yace." Wlh ammy munyi matuk'ar farinciki da zamu kasance tare dk kuma muma yanda kk d'aukemu a matsayin y'ay'anki haka muka d'auke ki tamkar mahaifiya a garemu kuma insha'allah zaki same mu masu tsananin biyayya a gareki." Murmushi ammy tayi tare da mayar da k'wallar farinciki wacce tk k'ok'arin zubo mata, ita dn ta kasance me buk'atar y'ay'a da yawa amma k'addara ta sa tun haihuwar fahad bata k'ara haihuwa ba, ta sani da tana tare da mahaifin fahad da yanxu ta tara yara da yawa.


Tayi matuk'ar farinciki da xuwan wad'annan yara tamkar kada su tafi, suma haka suka saki jikinsu kamar sun dad'e da sabawa, tare suka shiga kicheen da ammy sukayi abinci mai ban sha'awa very deletions, ammy tayi mamakin yanda yara kamar su suka iya abinci kala-kala ta lura suhailat ma har tafi yasmeen k'warewa a wannan 6angaren, suma sunyi mamakin yanda tsohuwa kamar ammy ta iya sarrafa abincin zamani haka dukda momy ma gwana ce a wannan 6angaren amma ita ammy sam bata da ganda ba kamar Momy ba wacce ta fiye son jikinkinta.


Byn sun gama shirya dining suka zauna a tare suka yi lunch cike da farinciki yasmeen da Sadeeq suna jin inama ana canxa uwa yau da sai sun canxa ammy ta maye musu gurbin Momy ita kam suhailat ji tk tamkar mahaifiyar ta ce ta dawo duniya, haka suka wuni ckn farinciki sannan da yamma sukayi wa ammy sallama byn ta cika su da tsaraba me yawa sukayi mata godiya tare da alk'awarin xasu dinga kawo mata xiyara akai-akai FK yace shima yana nan zuwa da daddare ya mik'awa y Sadeeq key dn motar sannan ya juya ckn gidan su kuma suka tafi.

_________________________Suna tfy suna hirar irin kar6ar mutunci da ammy tayi musu babu shakkah FK yayi dace da uwa ta gari, suna ckn tattaunawa wayar y Sadeeq ta d'auki ringing dubawa yayi ai bai san lokacin da yaja wani mahaukacin burki ba ganin momy ce ke kiransa juyowa yayi ya kalli yasmeen dk baya yace. " Yasmeen Momy cefa." Itama ckn mamaki da rud'ewa tace." What."  Suhailat dk gefen y Sadeeq tace. " haba mana y Sadeeq ya zaku yi haka momy ce fa ka d'aga mana kaji abinda zata ce maka wata k'ila ta fad'a maka inda tk."  Y Sadeeq kasa d'aga wayar yayi saida Momy ta kira sau 4 sannan ya samu nutsuwar d'aga wa tare da kashe motar yai parking sannan ya sanya speaker, bai iya yin magana ba yai shiru yana jiran abinda Momy zata fad'a sallamar Momy ta karad'e motar duk sunyi shiru suna sauraron ta y Sadeeq bai amsa ba saida ta mai-maita sannan yasamu nutsuwar amsawa muryar sa a hankali, Momy jin Sadeeq ya amsa sallamar sai ta fara magana da cewa."Haba yaro na ban ta6a tunanin zaku iya watsar dani ba akan wani abu ba sai yanxu, to idan ka manta bari na tuna maka ni dn mahaifiyar ku ce kuma ba'a canxawa tuwo suna, yanxu saboda Allah mahaifin Ku yayi min korar Kare byn duka daya nad'a min ya kore ni alhalin yasan bani da gurin da xanje, shine kuma kk watsar dani ko cigiyar inda nk ba kwayi yayi kyau, yanxu kam na gane matsayina a wajen sam baku d'auke ni a matsayin mahaifiyar ku ba." Tayi shiru don taji abinda zaice, Sadeeq ya numfasa a hankali kfn yace. "Kiyi hakuri Momy bazamu ta6a watsar dk ba abinda yasa bamu nemeki ba shine, munga kin d'auki xafi muna tsoron abinda zai biyo baya idan muka neme ki amma kiyi hak'uri har yanxu kina zuciyar mu." Momy taji dad'in maganar Sadeeq don haka ta sassauta murya ckn salo me ban tausayi da nuna nadama tace. " ni kaina nayi matuk'ar nadama akan abinda na aikata, ina neman yafiyar ku yarana ku yafe min sannan ku nema min yafiya a wajen mahaifin Ku koda ba zai maida ni d'aki na ba ya taimaka ya yafemin ko zan samu sassauci don Allah." Sai kawai ta fashe da kuka me tsuma zuciyar me sauraro, tsitt sukayi babu Wanda ya iya yin magana a ckn su, sautin kukan Momy ya cika motar harda shesheka.




Y Sadeeq ne yasamu karfin halin yin magana da cewa. "Haba Momy kiyi hak'uri mana mu mun dad'e da yafe miki dama bamu kk yiwa laifi ba, kuma insha'allah zamu nema miki afuwa wajen dady kiyi shiru don Allah Momy bana jin dad'in wannan kukan da kk yi."  Kallon hjy lylah Momy tayi sukayi murmushi a tare saboda yanda sukaji hankalin dn nata ya tashi don haka takara fashewa da kuka ckn kukan tk fad'in. "Sadeeq ina suhailat marainiyar Allah don Allah itama ta yafe min abinda nayi mata nasan zai yi wuya ta yafe min don ban can-canci ta yafe min ba saboda abinda nayi mata ba k'aramin abu bane,wayyo Allah na ya zanyi da hak'k'in wannan yarinya idan bata yafe min ba nasani hak'k'inta ba zai daina bibiya taba na shiga uku." Tsakanin d'a da uwa sai Allah, kukan da Momy keyi ya tayar musu da hankali suhailat kam itama kukan tk zuciyarta na bugawa bata ta6a tunanin Momy da kanta zata nemi yafiya a wajen ta ba, ita kam indai itace ta yafewa Momy duk abinda tayi mata.


Y Sadeeq da k'yar ya lallashi Momy tayi shiru sannan ya tanbaye ta inda tk yanxu ta shaida masa yai mata sallama byn ya daukar mata alk'awarin zasu zo inda tk insha'allah.

Momy ta kalli hjy lylah wacce ta zuba mata ido tana kallon ta cike da mamakin yanda tk irin wannan kukan byn kuma tasan shirya hakan sukayi, Momy tayi murmushi byn ta goge hawayen face dn ta tace. "K'awata ya naga kin zuba min ido me kk kallo ne."?

Hjy lylah tayi ajiyar xuciya sannan tace.''lalle k'awata ke dn shu'uma ce kuma kin k'ware a iya makirci, dubi yanda kk kuka da hawaye sha6e-sha6e byn kuma ba abinda yk ckn xuciyar ki kenan bah." Momy tayi dariya kfn tace. "Hmm ni dn nan da kk gani gwana ce a 6angaren kissa da makirci babu abinda baxan iya ba indai akan cikar buri na ne yanxu kinga na d'ana musu tarko na farko nasan bazasu ta6a tsallakeshi ba." Hjy lylah tayi dariya kfn tace. "Ai wannan tarkon da kk d'ana musu ko uban nasu ne ba zai iya tsallakeshi ba balle kuma su yara, ni kaina ba don nasan abinda kk shirya ba da sai na rantse kukan gaske kk yi." Momy tace. "Ai kad'an ma kk gani da sannu zan gama dasu dashi kanshi uban nasu."  Haka suka cigaba da tattauna yanda suke so abubuwa su kasance musu.

____________________Y Sadeeq ya juyo ya dubi k'annen sa kowacce tayi shiru tana tunani ya ja ajiyar xuciya sannan yace."Me kk fahimta a maganganun momy." Aunty yasmeen wacce tun lokacin da Momy keyin kuka ta tuno ranar da Momy taje hospital da guba a lokacin ma saida tayi musu kuka akan cewa tayi nadama, yanxu ma kukan da Momy keyi ta tabbata ba har ckn xuciyar ta bane amma idan baka san halinta ba xaka iya rantsuwa har ga Allah tayi nadama ne, gyara xama tayi sannan ta dubi y Sadeeq tace. "Ina da tabbacin Momy batayi nadama ba akwai abinda tk shirya wa don haka kar muyi saurin amince wa da ita mu tsaya muga iya gudun ruwan ta." Y Sadeeq yace. "Nima gsky ban amince da wannan nadamar tata ba saboda na tuna lokacin da taje hospital tana kuka ashe akwai abinda ta shirya sai dai kuma banji dad'in wannan kukan da tayi ba wlh ji nake kamar naje na lallasheta." Suhailat wacce tayi shiru tana goge hawaye ta dubi y Sadeeq tace. "Y Sadeeq da aunty yasmeen kada idanun ku su rufe kuyi abinda bai kamata ba, momy mahaifiyar ku ce kuma duk yanda tk ba kuda tamkar ta don haka addu'a itace tsakanin ku da ita, sannan ni a ganina Momy tayi nadama da gsk saboda tunda nk ban ta6a ji Momy tace a nema mata yafiya a wajena ba amma yau gashi da kanta tk kuka tana fad'in ace na yafe mata ni kam na dad'e da yafe mata, don Allah kuma Ku daina tunanin wani abu kawai ku amince tayi nadamar ne." Y Sadeeq yayi murmushi kfn yace. "Little sis kina da halaye masu kyau irin na mahaifiyar ki wadanda muma dasu muke alfahari mungode da kk yafewa Momy amma ki sani bazamu yi saurin sakin jiki da ita ba har sai mun tabbatar ba wani abun ta shirya ba." Aunty yasmeen tace." Gsky ne y Sadeeq ita bata san Momy bane shi yasa tk tunanin da gsk tayi nadama ne, yanxu dai mu bari muje gida muji abinda dady zzaice." Hakane kinga dama kuma nayi recording sai na kunna masa yaji da kunnensa." Y Sadeeq y fad'a yana k'ok'arin tayar da motar suka fara tfy babu Wanda ya k'ara magana har suka iso gida.




Dady yana kwance a parlour tunani ya dameshi yau haka ya wuni yana tunanin yanda zai nemi inda rabi tk don xuciyar sa na fad'a masa abinda yayi mata bai kyauta ba, sallamar yaransa ce ta dawo dashi daga duniyar tunani ya amsa tare da yunk'urawa ya tashi zaune yana k'ok'arin k'wa-k'ulo murmushi dukda yanda kansa ke masa matsanancin ciwo, da sauri suka k'arasa wajen sa suna masa sannu da jiki y Sadeeq yace. "Kayi hakuri dady mun tafi mun barka kai kad'ai a gida ckn kad'aici." Dady yace. " ba komai yara na kunsamu ammyn taku lpy, nima naso naje mu gaisa amma bana jin dad'in jiki na

." Ayyah dady dama bamu tafi mun barka ba dama mun zauna mun kula da kai." Suhailat ta fad'a tana k'ok'arin ta6a goshin mahaifin nasu Wanda taji ya d'au zafi kallon aunty yasmeen tayi kfn tace. "Jikinsa da xafi aunty." Aunty yasmeen ta k'arasa itama ta dafa wuyan sa tabbas akwai zazza6i a jikinsa don haka ckn sauri  ta dauko masa magani a first aid box ta dawo y Sadeeq yace. " dady kaci abincin da aka bar maka kuwa."? Kansa kawai ya girgixa alamar bai ci ba, suhailat ta had'a masa shayi mai kauri ta kawo masa ya shanye shi tas sannan yasha magani yaji dad'in jikinsa sosae lallai y'ay'a na gari rahama ne ya dube su yace. "Ngd muku yarana Allah yayi muku albarka."  Suka amsa da "Ameen." y Sadeeq yace. "Bari mu barka ka huta dady zuwa anjima akwai maganar da zamuyi da kai."   Karka damu Ku zauna muyi maganar saboda nima da akwai wata maganar da zamu tattauna daku naji sauk'i sosae bana son na sake xama ni kadai saboda ina yawan tunani Wanda shine yk Sanya ni ciwon kai." Kowa ya samu waje ya zauna dady ya kallesu ckn nutsuwa yace." Ck n y'an kwanakin nan ina yawan tunanin rabi xuciya ta tana fad'a min ban kyauta mata ba akan abinda nayi mata, na Kore ta alhalin nasan batada kowa babu inda zataje ban dubi yanda muka tara yara da ita ba yanxu gashi ban san Inda suke ba ita da kubra wannan tunanin ne yk damu na kwana 2 shine yau na yanke shawarar fad'a muku don mu nemi mafita."  Ckn tsananin mamaki suke kallon dady don basu ta6a tunanin zaiyi saurin saukowa haka ba, ganin sunyi shiru suna kallon sa yace."Yanxu babu Wanda yk tunanin yasan inda za'a iya samun ta."? Y Sadeeq y dai-daita nutsuwar sa yace. " gsky dady bazamu iya sanin inda tk ba amma ina ganin abun yazo da sauk'i saboda d'axu ta kira ni a waya tana fad'a min cewa na nema mata gafarar ka tayi nadama wannan dama itace maganar da nace zamu yi da kai, bari kaji yanda mukayi da ita." Ya d'auko wayar sa ya kunna masa recording dn da yayi sai ga maganar Momy tsaff, duk yanda sukayi da ita saida dady yaji, tausayin ta ya kama shi yasan tayi masa babban laifi amma hukuncin da yayi mata yayi tsauri da yawa, girgixa kansa dady yayi byn yagama jin recording dn sannan yace." Yauwa yanxu sai ka kira ta muji inda tk ko." Y Sadeeq y latsa number Momy yai dialing ringing 1 ta d'aga da sallama jin muryar Momy da dady yayi kawai sai yaji gabansa ya fad'i, y Sadeeq ya gaisheta ta amsa sannan ta cigaba da magana. " Sadeeq ka nema min yafiyar kuwa a wajen mahaifin ku ni bance sai ya maida aure na ba amma ya taimaka ya yafe min ko xan samu dn sassauci, sannan ka kawo min suhailat itama na k'ara neman yafiyar ta." Wani dad'i ya kama Momy tabbas tarkon ta ya kama kurciya tunda ta shawo kan yaran ta mahaifin su mai sauk'i ne tasan yana jin muryar ta daga nan komai zai fara, nan take ta fad'a musu inda tk duk sun san gidan hjy lylah don k'awar Momy ce sosae y Sadeeq yace. " insha'allah yau dn nan zaki ganmu momy." Ckn farinciki tace. " naji dad'i Allah ya kawoku lpy yara na amma don Allah ka taho min da suhailat na k'ara neman afuwar ta." Saida ya kalli suhailat dn kfn yace. " to Momy da ita zamu zo." 



Byn ya kashe wayar dady yace. " nima tare dani za'a je." Kallon sa suke cike da farinciki da kuma mamaki bai damu da kallon da suke binsa dashi ba ya mik'e yana fad'in. " kowa yaje ya shirya byn magriba sai mu tafi." Yana gama fad'ar haka ya wuce d'akin sa, suma mik'ewa sukayi don su shirya tunda ankusa kiran sallah.

Wanka suka fara yi sannan sukayi sallah suka shirya, suhailat duk abinda tk yi gabanta fad'uwa yk tasani koda momy tayi nadama bazata iya sakin jikinta da ita ba saboda irin muguwar k'iyayyar da ta nuna mata, ita kam yasmeen har yanxu bata yadda momy tayi nadama ba don tafi kowa sanin halin mahaifiyar ta, zata iya shirya ko wane irin makirci don cikar burinta, haka dai suka shirya zociyoyin su babu dad'i.

Lokacin da suka fito parlour sun tarar da su dady sun dawo daga masallaci don haka tfy kawai sukayi, suna fitowa harabar gidan dai-dai lokacin doctor yana parking motar sa, byn ya fito sukayi masa bayanin inda zasu tafi shima yace ba za'a bar shi ba don haka tare suka tafi.


Basu dad'e suna tfy ba suka iso gidan dama babu wani nisa sosae, yasmeen da suhailat suka shiga ckn gidan da sallamar su, Momy ce da hjy lylah zaune a parlour suna hirar yanda shirin su zai kasance da zarar Momy ta koma ckn gidan, jin muryar su da Momy tayi da sauri ta mik'e ta tare su ckn nuna tsananin farinciki ta rungume su tana fad'in. "Allah sarki yara na nayi missing dn ku, me yasa kk gujeni ina yayan naku." Ta k'arasa maganar tare da sakin su tana lek'a byn su, yasmeen tayi murmushi kfn tace. "Muma munyi missing dn ki Momy, y Sadeeq yana waje tare da dady." On espect Momy taji maganar da sauri ta juya ta kalli hjy lylah suka saki wani 6oyayyen murmushi, hjy lylah tace." To kuje ku shigo dasu mana." A tare suka juya don su fad'a musu, Momy ta kalli hjy lylah wacce ta mik'o mata hannu suka tafa sannan tace. "To saiki bi a hankali kiyi duk yanda zakiyi yau dn nan ku dai-daita." Momy tayi dariya wani irin matsanancin farinciki tk ji yau kam ranar sa'ar ta ce, ta juya ido kfn tace. "Haba k'awata wannan fa wata dama na samu kuma bazan ta6a bari ta wuce ni ba."  Ta k'ara yin wani shu'umin murmushi tace. " Alhj daga yau kazama a k'ark'ashin iko na." Tana gama fad'in haka ta fita daga parlourn, hjy lylah ta k'ara fesa eir freshener ta samu waje ta zauna.


Tare suka shigo da sallama hjy lylah ta amsa byn sun zauna hjy lylah ta gaida dady suma yaran suka gaisheta sannan ta mik'e ta kawo musu lemo da snakes ta fice daga parlourn, Momy ta shigo ckn shigar alfarma sai zuba k'amshi tk, dady da yaran suka bita da ido ta samu guri ta zauna tana gaida dady Wanda tun lokacin da ta shigo k'irjin sa yk wani irin zafi xuciyar sa tana bugawa da k'arfi wani tsoro da kwarjinin ta ya d'arsu a xuciyar sa, da k'yar ya iya motsa bakinsa ya amsa gaisuwar da tk masa, Momy ta lura da yanayin da dady ya shiga don haka ta fara gabatar da shirin su, ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tace."Don Allah alhj ka yafe min laifin da na aikata maka nayi nadama bazan k'ara aikata wani abu makamancin haka ba. " tayi shiru yayin da tafara k'wa-k'ulo hawaye wad'anda basa yi mata wuya nan take kuwa hawayen da ta kira suka fara zuwa da gudu tanayi tana shesheka, wani irin yanayi dady ya tsinci kansa a ciki kansa yai masa nauyi ga xuciyar sa na bugawa haka kawai yaji baya son jin kukan ta rabi wata matsananciyar soyyayar ta na huda xuciyar sa, sai dai har zuwa lokacin bai iya magana ba, Momy ganin alhj yayi shiru ta d'ago face dn ta wacce ta jik'e da hawaye ta kafa masa ido na tsawon second 2 kfn ta had'a hannayen ta waje 1 tace. " ka dubi Allah alhj ka yafe min natub..... Katseta dady yai ta hanyar d'aga hannunsa sai yanxu ya samu damar iya magana yace. "Na yafe miki rabi duniya da lahira kuma ni ma inaso kiyi min afuwa saboda na yanke miki hukunci mai tsauri ban duba tarin yaran dk tsakanin mu ba na yanke miki wannan hukuncin kiyi hak'uri." 


Farinciki ne ya lulle6e xxuciyar Momy, ta goge hawayen munafurcin dk fuskarta sannan ta kalli alhj tace. "Ngd alhj kuma hukuncin da ka yanke yayi dai-dai saboda abinda na aikata maka ba k'aramin abu bane, ngd sosae." Ta juyo gurinda suhailat ke zaune kanta a k'asa, kamo hannun ta momy tayi kfn tace. "Y'ata ki yafe min kinji nayi nadama akan abubuwan da nayi miki." Suhailat wacce k'irjinta yk harbawa ta daure tace. "Na dad'e da yafe miki momy ni ban ta6a rik'eki a xuciya ta ba." Momy ta saki murmushin mugunta tare da samun nasara tace. "Ngd yarinya ta Allah yai miki albarka." A hankali suhailat ta amsa har yanxu gaban ta bai daina fad'uwa ba.

Nan Momy ta dinga jan yaran ta da hira ta tanbaye su waye doctor, dady ne da kansa yayi mata tak'aitaccen bayani a kansa, a fili ta nuna jin dad'in ta da ya kasance tare dasu amma ckn xuciyar ta sam bata yi farinciki ba musamman da taji alak'ar dk tsakanin shi da suhailat sai dai ta d'auki alk'awarin duk Wanda zai kawowa yarinyar nan farinciki sai ta raba ta dashi.

Hjy lylah tayi kiran y Sadeeq tace su bawa Momy da dady waje su tattauna haka kuwa akayi suka tashi suka koma k'aramin parlourn gidan aka bar Momy da dady sai hjy lylah wacce ta shigo a cewar ta sasanci taxo.

To anan dai aka sasanta dady ya maida Momy aka tsayar da sati 1 d'aurin aure saboda ta riga ta gama iddah, murna da farinciki a wajen Momy ba'a cewa komai, shi kam dady baya jin wani farinciki can k'asan xuciyar sa yana jin kamar an tilasta masa ne, haka dai sukayi sallama ya fito xuciyar sa sam babu dad'i duk da cewa yana jin soyayyar rabi a wani 6angare na xuciyar sa, amma bai san abinda yasa baya jin farincikin dawo da ita da zai yi ba,

 yaran ma suka fito Momy har mota ta rakosu tana d'aga musu hannu cike da farinciki har suka bar gidan, a mota yk sanar dasu yanda sukayi dasu Momy, Allah sarki tsakanin d'a da uwa sai Allah duk yanda suke jin basu yadda da tuban da Momy tayi ba hakan bai hana sunji dad'in wannan labarin na mahaifin su ba kowa yayi ptn alkhairi a lamarin, sai dai yasmeen ta k'udurce a ranta da zarar Momy ta dawo ba zasu saki jiki da ita ba har sai sun tabbatar tuban gsky tayi dukda cewa wannan xuwan da sukayi jikinta yayi sanyi don ta fara yadda momyn tayi nadama da gsk musamman da taga yanda ta rik'e hannun suhailat tana roqon ta yafe mata, shi kam y Sadeeq da suhailat tuni suka amince tuban momyn na gsky ne balle dady Wanda yanxu ya zama jani talau sai yanda akayi dashi, FK shima da yk bai San wacece momy ba tausayin ta ma yk ji yanda ta dinga kuka a gaban su tana neman yafiya.................
