MANAZART WRITERS ASSOCIATION


Pg 34

BAYAN AN MAIDA AUREN MOMY

Ranar da aka maida auren a ranar ta koma byn tayi mugayen shirye-shiryen ta don byn aikin da boka dn tsurku yayi mata saida hjy lylah ta kaita wajen nata bokan ya k'ara yi mata wani aikin duk dai akan alhj saboda tana son ta dinga juya shi son ranta, wannan karon tayi nasara don kuwa dady yazama a tafin hannun ta duk abinda ta sanya shi baya mata musu koda kuwa baya so.


Wannan canjin ya girgixa yaran alhj musamman Sadeeq, yasmeen da kuma suhailat hatta FK yayi mamakin yanda yaga dady ya koma sai dai kuma babu Wanda ya isa yayi magana, Momy kam duniya ce sabuwa a wajenta nanfa ta fara zuba mulki yanda tk so dama abinda ta dad'e tana mafarkinsa kenan yanxu kam kaltume da hjy lylah sun cika  mata burinta don haka ta d'auki alk'awarin itama zata saka musu abinda tayi mata, sunyi waya da kaltume tace tana nan dawowa so tk komai ya gama dai-daita.


Kubra kam lokacin da Momy ta kira ta ta fad'a mata dawowar ta cewa tayi ita ba yanxu zata dawo gida ba saboda itama yanxu ta fara yin nisa a harkar lesbian aunty lubna ta kaita can wajen shugabar su wai ita hjy sima tana ganin kubra ta ji tayi mata yanxu kubra an zama millonia saboda hjyr tana sakar mata bakin aljihu kuma byn hjyr ma akwai wasu daga ckn k'awayen aunty lubna wad'anda tk harka dasu.(Allah ya shiryemu ya shirya zuri'armu da zuri'ar dukkan musulmi ameen).


Momy ta canxa salon k'iyayyar da tk yiwa suhailat ta daina fito da ita a fili saboda bata son y'ay'an ta su gane ba tuban gsky tayi ba, soyayyar gsky tk nuna mata a gaban yasmeen da Sadeeq har ma da FK amma da zarar babu idon su tana shan cin mutunci da tsangwama kuma momyn tayi mata mummunan gargad'i akan idan ta bari wani a ckn su ya fuskanta zata gane kuren ta, sai dai  idan momy tayi mata wani abun ta shige bathroom tasha kukan ta, ko da Momy bata hana ta fad'a wa kowa matsalar ta ba, ta d'auki alk'awarin ba  zata sanya kowa ckn wannan lamarin ba saboda ta gaji da sanya y Sadeeq da aunty yasmeen suna 6atawa da mahaifiyar su a kanta zata d'auki k'addarar ta kuma zata dage da addu'a.


Nan da nan suhailat ta fara ramewa y'ar k'ibar da ta farayi duk ta xube, aunty yasmeen tasha tsareta tana mata tanbayoyi akan abinda yk damunta amma sai dai tayi murmushi tace karatu ne da yake ta koma makaranta tana karantar science dashi tk fakewa saboda kowa yasan wahalar shi a xahiri, amma ita kam ba karatun ne yk bata wahala ba illah yanda Momy ta canxa mata ta koma mata xul waj hy ni mai fuska 2 tana nuna mata soyayya da k'iyayya a lokaci 1.


Yau da wuri ta dawo gida saboda ta gama d'aukar lectures dn ta, a gajiye tk lik'is don tun 6:30am ta fita ko karyawa batayi ba, aunty yasmeen ce zaune suna hira da momy akan xuwan da kubra tayi jiya kuma yau da wuri ta tafi a cewar ta ba zata iya zaman gidan don y'an sa ido sunyi yawa, yasmeen tace. "Wai Momy kin San gidan da tk zaune ne? Ni fa da farko nayi xaton kuna tare da ita amma sai naga ashema Sam bakwa waje 1." Momy ta dn gyara zama tace."Wlh tana can gidan su k'awar ta miemy ni ban san me yk damun kubra ba yanxu sam tak'i zaman gidan nan." Ta6e baki yasmeen tayi kfn tace. " Ai duk ke kk 6ata ta momy ba kyayi mata fad'a yanxu saboda Allah dubi shigar da taxo da ita jiya nayi mata magana kince na k'yale ta har tana nema tayi min rashin kunya amma duk baki damu ba, sai lallashinta ma kk wai ta zauna ita kuma tana fad'in ba zata iya zama ba saboda mu y'an sa ido, to ni wlh duk abinda naga tayi bai dace ba bazan iya zuba mata ido ba dole inyi magana. " Momy taja numfashi kfn tace. " ke abinda baki gane ba kubra har yanxu yarinya ce karama dole sai lallashin idan nace zanyi mata fad'a ma zata daina zuwa gidan ma gaba 1 kinga kuwa dole na lalla6a ta du du du nawa tk a wannan watan ne fa ta cika 15 years kinga kuwa tana fama da k'uruciya." Girgixa kanta kawai yasmeen tayi cike da takaici don kuwa tasan duk abinda zata fad'a momy ba zata saurare ta ba saima dai taji haushin ta ita kam a halin yanxu taka tsan-tsan tk da momy don bata son wani abun ya k'ara shiga tsakanin su da ita.

Suhailat ta shiga parlourn da sallamar ta aunty yasmeen ce ta amsa tana tsokanar ta da cewa."A'a Mrs FK yau da wuri kk dawo haka.'' dn murmushi tayi kfn tace. "Kai aunty yasmeen don Allah ki daina tsokanata, kinga na dawo a gajiye ga yunwa da tk faman sanbad'a ta." Ayyah y'ar k'anwata sorry haka karatun ya gada musamman naki me wahala bari nayi serving dn ki a dining dama yanxu na fito daga kitchen. " yasmeen ta fad'a tana k'ok'arin mik'ewa ''ngd aunty na." Suhailat ta fad'a tare da juya wa wajen Momy a tsorace tace. "Sannu da gida Momy."  Momy ta juya ta kalli yasmeen hankalinta na can wajen had'awa suhailat abinci, wata uwar harara ta watsa mata wacce ta sanya y'ay'an hanjin suhailat suka ya mutsa bata tsaya komai ba ta wuce d'akin su, yasmeen da ta gama zuba mata abincin tace."yauwa gashi nan na cika miki plate don haka kiyi sauri ki cinye shi tass yanxu k'ila duk yunwar nan da kk fad'a baifi kici 6 spoon ba kice min kin k'osh....... Maganar ta mak'ale mata lokacin da ta juyo bataga suhailat ba Momy ta kalla wacce tk dariya kfn tayi magana Momy tace. " Ai k'yaleki nayi sai faman zuba kk tun d'axu alhalin ita ta wuce d'aki." K'wafa yasmeen tayi sannan ta juya nufi hanyar d'akin .

Suhailat tana shiga d'akin ta jefar da hand out dn dk hannun ta ta nufi bathroom wani matsanancin kuka na taso mata da sauri ta k'arasa shiga tare da sa key aikuwa kamar jira yk kukan ya 6alle mata sosae tk jin maraici ya dawo mata gata dai ckn matsala amma babu me iya magance mata ita babu Wanda zasu tattauna wannan matsalar balle taji dn sauk'i kuka da addu'a ne kad'ai abinda yk sanya wa taji dn sassauci, bugun da ake wa door dn bathroom dn ne ya sanya tayi shiru, aunty yasmeen tace. " Ki bud'e kofar nan suhailat nasan babu wani abinda kk yi a ckn bathroom dn nan. " jin haka yasa ta kunna tap ruwa ya fara zuba wani kukan ne ke k'ok'arin k'wace mata amma tayi saurin maida shi, aunty yasmeen tace. "Bazaki bud'e min ba ko, nasan akwai abinda yk damun ki amma kin k'i fad'a min to ina jira ko kwana xakiyi a ciki zaki fito ki sameni don yau dole ne ki fad'a min matsalar dk damunki." Ita dai ba tayi magana ba sai kawai ma ta fara wanna saida ta gama sannan ta fito, aunty yasmeen ce zaune tana jiranta k'wa-k'ulo murmushi tayi a k'ok'arin ta na son ta nunawa aunty yasmeen bata ckn damuwa abinda bata sani ba idon ta da ya kumbura yai jajir ya gama tona ta,  aunty yasmeen batayi mata magana ba saida ta gama sanya kaya sannan ta janyo hannun ta suka fito parlour kai tsaye dining area ta ja ta saida ta zaunar da ita kfn tace. "Maza ki cinye min wannan abincin yanxu." Bata tsaya jin abinda suhailat zata fad'a ba ta bar gurin, Momy dai tana zaune tana kallon su batayi magana ba zuwa yanxu ta fara gajiya da 6oye k'iyayyar da tk yiwa wannan mayyar yarinyar, dubi yanda yasmeen ke wani lalla6ata sai kace wata y'ar gwal dole ne ta d'auki mataki don baxata iya zuba ido tana kallon ta cikin farinciki ba.


Tana jin yunwa sosae amma sam ta kasa cin abincin tsakura kawai tk yi bakinta ma sam bayayi mata dad'i, lokacin da aunty yasmeen ta dawo taga ko rabi bata ci ba, kawai sai ta zauna ta kar6i spoon dn ta debo ta mik'a mata ganin babu walwala a face dn yasmeen dole ba don taso ba ta dinga kar6ar abincin saida ta dn ci da yawa sannan ta marai-raice murya cike da shagwa6a tace."don Allah ki barni haka aunty wlh ya ishe ni." Sannan ta k'yale ta, janyo hannun ta tayi suka dawo ainihin parlourn suka zauna a kujerar dk facing Momy ita kam suhailat don babu yanda zata iya ne amma ba don haka ba babu abinda zaisa ta zauna, yasmeen ta kalli Momy kfn ta dawo ta kalli suhailat tace."Momy ki bincika min yarinyar nan me yk damun ta tak'i fad'a min kuma ni na riga na gane ba tun yanxu ba akwai abinda yk damun ta da tk 6oye min ki tanbaye ta ko ke zata fad'a miki. " murmushi Momy tayi tana kallon yasmeen kfn tace. "Banda abin ki yasmeen ai ke kad'ai tk iya fad'a wa damuwar ta kina kallo har yanxu bata saki jikinta dani sosae ba, amma bari na gwada tanbayar ta ko zata fad'a min." Ta kalli suhailat da dn murmushi Wanda bai kai xuciya ba tace."Me yk damun ki ne yarinya ta."? Sunkuyar da kanta suhailat tayi xuciyar ta na harbawa da k'yar ta iya d'aga baki tace. "Babu komai Momy karatu ne kawai ya sako ni a gaba." Momy tace. " to kinji ke yasmeen babu abinda yk damun ta. " yasmeen ta girgixa kai kfn tace. "Wlh Momy akwai abinda yk damun ta yanxu fa da ta shiga d'aki kuka tk yi a bathroom saida taji nayi magana sannan ta fara yin wanka don kar nar fahimta, kuma kalli idonta yanda ya kumbura, saboda kukan da tasha." Gaban suhailat ya fad'i ckn sauri tace. "Lah aunty sabulu ne ya shigar min ckn ido shine fa yasa yayi haka amma don Allah ki yadda dani babu abinda yk damu na." Momy ckn fad'a tace. "Wai ke yasmeen me yk damun ki ne kk matsa mata da tanbaye-tanbaye, yarinya tace miki babu abinda yk damun ta amma kin k'i yadda to so kk tayi miki k'arya ko me."?  "Yi hak'uri Momy ba haka nk nufi ba amma ni na fahimci akwai damuwa a tare da ita shi yasa." Yasmeen ta fad'i ganin yanda Momy ta fara fad'a, "to tunda tace babu ki rabu da ita mana, bana so ki k'ara takura mata da tanbaye-tanbayen nan naki." Ta juya ta kalli suhailat wacce gumi ke faman tsatsafo mata saboda tsabar tsoro tace." Rabu da ita yarinya ta zo nan." Ta fad'a tare da mik'o mata hannun ta babu yanda suhailat zata yi haka ta mik'e taje wajen Momy, yasmeen kuwa ficewa tayi daga parlourn cike da jin haushin an hana ta tanbayi suhailat abinda yk damun ta kuma ita tabbata akwai matsala.


Momy ganin yasmeen ta fita ta juya wajen da suhailat ke zaune ta galla mata harara kfn ta fara magana k'asa-k'asa tace.''Na kula kina so ki tona min asiri a wajen yasmeen kina nuna mata kina da damuwa wato har kuka kk shiga bathroom ki yi, don munafurci ko."? Ta matso ta janyo kunnen ta ta murd'a tsabar azaba saida ta saki wata siririyar k'ara da sauri ta to she bakin ta don kada yasmeen tajiyo, Momy tace."Na kusa raba ki da yasmeen dn sai naga Wanda zaki nunawa kina ckn matsalar sha sha sha kawai."  ta k'arasa maganar tare da kai mata rank'washi. Wata azaba ta ratsa k'wa-kwal war ta, ta durk'ushe a wajen, hawaye na zuba a idon ta, janyo ta Momy tayi tana lallashinta lokacin da ta hango yasmeen ta dawo parlourn, hankalin yasmeen a tashe ta k'araso wajen tana fad'in. "Kin gani ko Momy dama na fad'a miki akwai abinda yk damun ta, kin samu ta fad'a miki ne."?  Momy tace." Eh ta fad'a min wai wasu y'an ajin su ne suke takura mata don kawai ta fi su k'ok'ari, kinsan akwai y'an hassada." Aunty yasmeen ta k'arasa wajen tare da kamo suhailat wacce tayi shiru tana jin irin k'aryar da Momy ta shinfid'a, goge mata hawaye yasmeen ta farayi sannan tace. "Kiyi hak'uri k'anwata haka karatu yk duk Wanda yk yi sai yayi hak'uri zaka had'u mutane kala-kala masu halaye daban-daban dole sai ka sanya juriya musamman idan ka had'u da masu hassada, idan kina sanya damuwa a ranki zaki samu matsala a karatun ki su kuma haka suke so don haka ki dage ki cire duk wata damuwa ki fuskanci karatun ki don muyi alfahari dk a gaba kinji." Kanta kawai ta iya d'aga wa saboda baxata iya magana ba don ji tk kamar kanta zai tsage tsabar ciwo, Momy tayi wani shu'min murmushi kfn tace." Yasmeen ki kaita d'aki ta huta wannan kukan da tayi zai iya sanya mata ciwon kai."  Yasmeen ta kama hannun ta suka nufi d'aki, ita kam suhailat yanxu ta daina mamakin makirci irn na Momy yanxu dubi yanda ta shirya wannan karyar byn duk ita ce ke sanya mata damuwar.



________________Bayan kwana 2 da faruwar haka Momy ta sanya dady ya kira farouq saurayin yasmeen akan maganar auren sa da yasmeen daman shi farouq su kad'ai yk jira don ya gama shirya komai don haka ckn sati d'aya iyayen farouq suka zo aka tsaida maganar auren sati 3 masu zuwa,kfn lokacin ankawo lefe na gani na fad'a, Momy ta duk'ufa shirye-shiryen biki duk abinda ya dace uwa tayi wa y'arta Momy ta tanadeshi ga yasmeen gyara dai kam amarya tasha shi saboda tun daga meduguri wata hamshak'iyar mai gyara taxo musamman don ta gyara amarya ciki da waje, yasmeen ta zamo kamar wata balarabiya tana farincikin xuwan wannan lokacin zata kasance da masoyinta, sai dai kuma duk lokacin da ta tuna cewa lokacin rabuwar ta suhailat yayi sai taji babu dad'i, duk farincikin da tk ciki sai ta nemeshi ta rasa don ma yanxu Momy ta dawo tana son suhailat ai da baxata iya barin ta a gidan ba sai dai ta tafi da ita gidan mijinta tunda tasan farouq baxai hana ba, amma tunda yanxu Momy tayi nadama baxata bari ta tafi da suhailat ba tunda yanxu ita kad'ai zata rage mata me d'ebe mata kewa tunda kubra ta bar gidan.


Ita kam suhailat damuwar ta ta k'aru sai yanxu ta fahimci abinda Momy tk nufi da tace zata raba ta da yasmeen ashe aure zata yi mata lallai kam zata raba su har abada ita kuma zata cigaba da rayuwa ckn rashin y'anci da k'unci, duk wata hidima na gyara da akewa yasmeen tare da suhailat akeyi don yasmeen dn ce da kanta ta d'auki nauyin a gyara mata y'ar kanwar ta, tayi mata d'in kunan fitar biki masu yawa Wanda hakan ba k'aramin ciwo yayiwa Momy ba sai dai ta d'auki alk'awarin duk rintsi sai ta raba ta dasu.

Tun ana saura kwana 4 d'aurin auren yasmeen aka fara biki gida ya cika da mutane wad'anda mafi yawa k'awayen momy ne don duk ta rabu da y'an uwanta saboda bata son kowa ya ra6e ta, haka y'an uwan dady duk ta raba su dashi sai y'an tsiraru wad'anda basa damuwa da irin rashin mutuncin da momy keyi musu,

Anyi shagali me yawa kaltume da kubra duk sunzo, y Sadeeq da FK suma ba'a barsu a baya ba wajen ca6a ado tamkar sune angwaye, FK duk inda suhailat ta sanya k'afa yana biye da ita sai faman kaffa-kaffa yk da ita don kada wani yayi masa k'wace, kubra kam tun lokacin da ta k'yallah ido taga FK sai kawai ta tsinci kanta da sonsa don gayen ba k'aramin yi mata yayi ba, yana da duk wani qualities da tk so a wajen d'a namiji, dukda cewa yanxu ba wannan harkar tk ba amma ganin doctor yasa taji zata iya komawa harka da maxa muddin zata samu abinda tk so a wajen sa, ganin sa da tayi tare da suhailat ba k'aramin d'aga mata hankali yayi ba, ta samu momy ta tanbaye ta alak'ar dk tsakanin FK da suhailat, Momy ta fad'a mata komai kubra ta kalli momy tace. "Gsky gayen ya had'u momy ya za'a yi nasame shi don wlh na kamu da soyayyar shi ckn murmushi Momy ta dubi kubra tace." Indai kinyi min alk'awarin dawowa gida to ni kuma duk abinda zanyi sai nayi don ki same shi. " ckn murna kubra tace."zan dawo wlh momy ki taimaka min kinji. " itama momy ckn farinciki momy tace ."karki damu yarinya ta kisa a ranki tamkar kin same shi. "  da haka suka cigaba da harkokin su sai dai rabin hankalin kubra na kan fk tana lura da duk wani motsin sa, shi kam bai ma san da ita ba harkar gaban sa kawai yk.


An d'aura auren farouq da yasmeen da misalin karfe 10:30 akan sadaki 50k marok'a suka cika unguwar da kirari, suhailat da taji an d'aura auren aunty yasmeen sai kawai taji hawaye na zuba daga idonta shi kenan lokacin raba su yayi, da sauri ta mik'e ta nufi d'akin su ckn kuka, yasmeen itama hankalin ta ya tashi da jin an d'aura auren, suhailat kawai tk ji tasani koda momy baxata yi missing dn ta kamar yanda suhailat zata yi ba, itama d'akin ta nufa sai kawai ta hango suhailat kwance tana kuka tasan kukan suhailat bazai wuce na rabuwa da ita ba don haka ta tafi wajen ta don ta lallasheta amma tana zuwa sai ta kasa itama ta fara nata hawayen nan fa suka dinga kuka babu me lallashi, kubra ce ta sanar da Momy abinda yk faruwa sai ga ta ta shigo, ckn 6acin rai ta dubi yasmeen tace. "Yanxu saboda Allah yasmeen yau ranar farinciki amma ki zauna kina irin wannan kukan kamar wacce za'a yiwa auren dole." Yasmeen ta d'ago face dn ta wacce tayi jage-jage da hawaye tace. "Dole nayi kuka momy na shak'u da yarinyar nan yanda ba kya tunani, yau gashi aure zai raba ni da ita don Allah ki barni nayi kukan nan ko zan samu sassaucin abinda nk ji." Harara momy ta watsa mata sannan tace. "Ai kuwa dole kiyi kuka tunda xaki rabu da uwarki ba." Ckn rashin fahimta yasmeen tace. "Me kk nufi momy"? Suhailat bata cancanta nayi kukan rabuwa da ita ba kome."? Sai a lokacin momy ta gane ta dn kwafsa, sai ta d'an sassauta muryar ta tare da sakin fuska ta janyo yasmeen jikinta tace." Ba haka nk nufi ba yarinya ta ina nufin wannan kukan da kk yi yayi yawa keda yakamata ki lallashe ta amma shine kema kk zauna Ku nayi tare gsky banji dad'i ba." Yasmeen tace. "Kiyi hak'uri Momy wlh ni kaina bansan lokacin da na fara kukan ba, amma don Allah kiyi min alk'awarin zaki kula min da suhailat ni dai momy na baki amanar ta don Allah ki had'a soyayya da kulawar da kk min duk ki bata, ni wlh da zaki amince min ma da kin bani ita mun tafi tare nasan farouq ba zai hana ba." Zuwa yanxu abun ya daina bawa momy haushi sai mamaki saboda ta lura yasmeen da iya gskr ta tk mata magana don haka ta k'ara had'a rai kfn tace. "Lallai yasmeen na lura baki da hankali banda abinki ina kk ta6a jin anyi irin wannan abun, ai ko a da sanda akeyin y'ar zaman d'aki ba gan samemiyar budurwa ake d'auka ba balle yanxu, kuma da kk cewa kin bani amanar ta wato har yanxu baki yarda dani ba kina ganin xan cutar da ita ko, don duk wand kk ji ana bashi amanar abu ba'a yadda dashi bane." Ta k'arasa maganar tana k'ok'arin matso kwallar munafurci.

Hankalin yasmeen ya k'ara tashi tace. " don Allah kiyi hak'uri Momy ba haka nk nufi ba wlh na yadda dk nasan zaki kula da ita fiye dani." Momy ta goge hawayen da bai k'arasa fitowa ba cike da jin dad'i tace. "Karki damu indai akan wannan yarinyar ne xan baki mamakin da baki ta6a zato ba abinda kawai nk so dk ki kwantar da hankalin ki kinji yarinya ta. "? Yasmeen ta d'aga kai tana duban wajen da suhailat ke kwance tayi lamo da alama ba kukan tk ba, janyo ta yasmeen tayi jikinta sannan tace." Kiyi hak'uri y'ar k'anwata nasan zaki yi missing dina amma karki damu ga momy nan zata baki soyayya wacce kk rasa a baya daga wajen ta. " suhailat dai bata iya magana ba kanta kawai ta d'aga saboda ta san babu abinda momy ta tanadar mata sai azaba, momy tayi murmushi tare da d'ora hannun ta a bayan suhailat tace. "Indai nice xan baki duk kulawar da ta dace dk kinji yarinya ta ki daina kuka." Wasu hawaye masu zafi suka K'ara zubowa suhailat tun yanxu ta fara tausayin kanta, ita kam yasmeen murmushi tayi kfn tace."yauwa momy muna gdy." Momy tayi wani shu'umin murmushi kfn tace. "Haba yasmeen menene kuma abun gdy duk fa abinda zanyi mata y'ata ce don haka bana son gdyr ki, yanxu maza ki tashi ki wanke face dn ki, ki canxa kaya bak'i na zuwa yasmeen ta mik'e ta shiga bathroom don ta wanke fuskarta, Momy ta kalli inda suhailat tk wacce tayi shiru kanta a k'asa ckn muryar rad'a momy tace." Munafuka wato kinxo kinsa min ita a gaba kuna kuka saboda tsabar munafurci zaki gamu dani ne bari a gama bikin nan sai na hukunta ki." Tana gama fad'in haka ta mike ta bar d'akin, Yasmeen ta fito daga bathroom ta k'ara shiryawa ckn wasu tsadaddun kaya byn ta gama shirya wa ta kalli suhailat tace. "Kema maza ki tashi ki canxa kaya muje ayi mana photo jiki a sanyaye suhailat ta shirya da taimakon aunty yasmeen, suka fito aka cigaba da hidimar biki.




Lokacin da za'a kai amarya ma sunsha kuka da k'yar aka 6an6are su daga jikin junan su, momy kam takaici ya ishe ta don ta lura yasmenn tafi damuwa da wannan mayyar yarinyar akan kowa, haka dai aka tafi da aunty yasmeen tana kuka momy ta hana a tafi da suhailat itama haka aka bar ta tana hawaye, byn masu tafiya da amarya sun gama tfy momy kaltume da kubra suka had'u suka xage suhailat tass har da dungure mata kai da rank'washi sannan sukayi mata albishir na zama mara y'anci a ckn gidan.


Kwana 2 da kai aunty yasmeen kowa ya watse sai masu gidan a lokacin ne kuma rayuwa ta fara sauyawa kowa na ckn gidan, dady dai yanxu bashi da wani kata6us sai abinda momy ta umarce shi ya zama tamkar rak'umi da akala itama momy hakan ce ta kasance mata domin kuwa da umarnin kaltume tk yin komai don aikin boka ya fara tasiri a kanta kubra kam soyayyar FK ta mata kamun kazar kuku don yanxu koda yaushe bata da lokacin kanta sai na tunani dukda cewa bata daina aikata abinda tk yi ba amma dai ta rage don ta dawo gida sai lokaci zuwa lokaci tk fita idan shugabar su ta nemeta, suhailat baiwar Allah kuwa yanxu bata da duk wata nutsuwa saboda sun takura mata babu Wanda yk sassauta mata a gidan sai y Sadeeq shi kuma yanxu sam baya samun zama don kuwa shine yk kula da harkokin kasuwancin dady saboda shi yanxu dady babu abinda yk gaban sa sai umarnin da momy zata bashi wannan kam jikinsa har rawa yk wajen gabatar dashi.


To muguntar da momy ta fara yiwa suhailat itace hanata zuwa makaranta a cewar ta idan ta bar yarinyar ta zama likita lallai zata gagare ta, wannan abun da momy tayi ya k'ara jefa suhailat ckn matsananciyar damuwa yanxu ta koma tamkar wata y'ar aiki a gidan, ita kad'ai ce ke yin duk wani aiki a gidan girki, shara da wanke-wanke da duk abinda akeyi na aikace-aikace sannan ta k'wace mata kayan sawar ta musamman wad'anda aunty Yasmeen tayi mata da duk wasu kayanta masu kyau sannan ta kore ta daga ainihin ckn gidan ta maida ta can BQ a wani d'aki da ake ajiye shirgi, ranar da momy tayi mata wannan wulak'anci tasha kuka ta gode Allah haka ta hak'ura don babu me lallashinta, ta zage ta gyara d'akin ta ajiye y'an sauran tsumman kayan da momy ta barmata, sannan ta dawo ckn gidan ta fara aikin da yazamar mata wajibi kullum.

 Tana ckn aikin ne taji abinda ya kusa tarwatsa mata k'wak'walwar ta, momy ce suke hira da kubra da farko bata damu da hirar tasu amma daga baya ta maida hankalin ta akan maganar da suke, kubra tace. "Wai momy me kk jira ne har yanxu banga alamar wannan guy dn hankalin sa zai dawo kaina ba duk kwalliyar da nk yi a banxa kullum idan yazo hankalin sa nakan wannan banzar yarinyar." Momy ta dn yi k'asa da murya kfn tace. "Karki damu yarinya ta na gama shirya komai duk ranar da ya sake zuwa gidan nan a ranar xan baki wani magani da zarar kinyi amfani dashi bazai k'ara kula wannan mayyar yarinyar ba." Kubra tayi murmushi sannan tace. "Wlh Momy na tsane ta saboda ta fi ni kyau da tsari amma nasan abinda zanyi mata wlh sai ta raina kanta." Momy tace. "Kwantar da hankalin ki yarinya ta aiko yanxu tasan ke da ita akwai ban-banci kalli fa kiga yanda na maida ta tamkar y'ar aiki." Kaltume ce ta shigo ckn wasu had'ad'd'un kaya shiga ta alfarma ta dubi momy tace."wai rabi wacce magana naji kunayi da kubra ne."? Momy ckn rawar murya tace."akan wani yaro da yk zuwa wajen suhailat ne shine kubra tace tana son shi. " ta6e baki kaltume tayi kfn tace. "Wannan kuma matsalar ku ce, magana nazo muyi don haka ki umarci d'iyarki ta bamu guri." Ckn tsananin mamaki kubra ke kallon kaltume lallai wannan matar ta samu waje dubi yanda tk bawa momy umarni cike da isa da gadara ita kuma momy kamar tsoron ta tk ji duk abinda ta umarce ta shi tk yi, "kubra jeki waje idan mun gama sai ki dawo." Maganar momy ta katse mata tunanin ta, haba momy wai me yasa kk zama kamar wata jani talau akan wannan matar na lura tsoronta ma kk yanxu saboda ko dady sai dai ki bashi umarni amma ki zauna wannan y'ar k'auyen tana juya ki." Cike da tsoro momy ta kalli kaltume wacce ta wani had'e rai tana jira taga irin hukuncin da rabi zata yiwa wannan mara kunyar y'ar tata, ganin haka yasa momy ta d'aga hannu ta kwad'awa kubra mari sannan tace ta fice mata daga d'aki, kubra tayi matuk'ar mamakin wannan abun saboda momy tunda tk bata ta6a dukan ta ba amma dubi akan wannan y'ar k'auyen Momy ta mare ta dole tayi bincike akan lamarin, da gudu ta fito daga d'akin tana kuka bata lura da suhailat dk aiki a parlourn gidan ba, ta wuce d'akin ta.

Kaltume tayi murmushi ganin irin hukuncin da momy tayiwa kubra dama ita kubra ba matsalar ta bace Yasmeen da Sadeeq sune matsalar ta kuma yanxu duk basa gidan irin wannan lokacin ta dad'e tana jira zata ci karanta babu babbaka, wani kallon wulak'anci tayiwa Momy sannan tace. "Duk ckn yaran ki wannan tafi rashin kunya amma ba komai zanyi maganinta." Momy kamar zata yi kuka saboda babu yanda zata yi, wani irin tsoron kaltume ne yk k'ara shiga zuciyar ta bata ta6a jin tsoron wani mutum haka ba ko alhj ba taji tsoron sa haka ba duk xafin sa a baya, kaltume ta dubi momy ganin tayi shiru tace. "Ba wannan maganar ce ta kawo ni wajen ki ba, abinda ya kawo ni shine inaso ki umarci mijin ki ya k'ara aure." A firgice momy ta d'ago tana kallon kaltume sai dai ta kasa cewa komai, harara kaltume ta watsa mata kfn tace. "Kin wani tsareni da ido kina kallo na ko ba xakiyi bane."? Tuni gumi ya fara tsatstsafowa momy a duniya babu abinda ta tsana irin kishiya har yanxu bata rabu da mayyar yarinyar da Khadija ta haifa mata ba, amma shine kaltume zata bata wannan umarnin gsky da wani ne yayi mata irin wannan mahaukaciyar maganar bata san abinda zata yi masa ba amma yau gashi wacce tafi tsoro a duniya itace ke bata wannan umarnin kuma babu damar musawa don baxata iya ba, da zata iya wlh sai ta cusa mata ashar, maimakon hakan sai ta tsinci kanta da cewa."kiyi hak'uri don Allah kinsan babu abinda na tsana irin kishiya ki canxa min duk abinda kk so xanyi." Murmushin samun nasara kaltume tayi ganin yanda aikin boka yk  tasiri akan rabi, ta gyara zama tare da k'ara tsuke fuska tace. "Babu abinda zan canxa miki dolene ki bawa mijinki umarnin ya k'ara aure kuma nice wacce zai aura..............

Ckn tsananin mamaki da tashin hankali momy ta d'ago tana duban kaltume da k'yar ta iya had'iye yawun bakinta, tabbas kaltume ta gama da ita yanxu ya zatayi baxata iya yi mata gaddama ba bud'e bakinta tayi da niyyar rok'on kaltume sai kawai ta tsinci kanta da fad'in." Karki damu indai kece babu matsala zanyi masa magana kuma nasan zai amince tunda yanxu sai abinda na umarce shi yk yi. " ckn farinciki kaltume tace. "Yauwa kiyi gaggawar fad'a masa don bana so a wuce sati ba'a d'aura auren ba." Tana gama fad'in haka ta mik'e tabar momy a d'akin ckn tashin hankali, koda ta fito parlour ta samu suhailat na aiki kaltume ta watsa mata wani kallon banza tayi gaba abinta,ita kam suhailat k'asa tayi da kanta tana mamakin wad'annan abubuwa masu ban mamaki da taji lallai wannan ishara ce Allah ya fara nunawa momy, ckn sauri tabar parlourn jin motsin fitowar momy kada ta fito ta ganta ta huce a kanta.


Momy ta shiga ckn tsananin tashin hankali Wanda bata ta6a riskar kanta a ckn sa ba, wannan shi ake kira ga k'oshi ga kwanan yunwa ta samu alhj fiye da yanda tk so amma gashi kaltume zata kawo mata cikas tasani muddin kaltume ta auri alhj shikenan kuma tata ta k'are ashe dama yaudarar ta kaltume tayi? "Kubra na shiga 3 ban san abinda kaltume tayi min ba wlh wani irin tsoron ta nk ji a ckn xuciya ta gashi ta bani umarnin in sanya alhj wai ya aure ta." Suhailat kam abinda yafi damunta shine taji momy tace duk ranar da FK yaxo zata bawa kubra wani magani tayi amfani dashi, babbar damuwar ta kada a k'wace mata doctor dn ta don haka tana komawa d'aki ta lalubo wayar ta wacce momy bata san tana da ita ba, a 6oye tk amfani da ita, ringing 1 ya d'aga tare da fad'in sorry my patient bari na kira bai jira abinda zata fad'a ba ya kashe, ita kam tura baki ta dn yi saboda ta fad'a mishi bata son wannan sunan amma yak'i daina kiranta dashi, tana ckn haka taji kiransa ya shigo ta ja fasali kfn ta d'aga da sallama ya amsa tare da lumshe ido jin zazzak'ar muryar ta ta doki dodon kunnensa, shiru tayi ta kasa magana har yanxu ba ta daina jin kunyar saba, shi kam tuni ya Saba da halinta don haka yace. Momy ta samu dady da maganar auren kaltume shi kam tamkar yanda tk tunani bai musa ba saboda yanxu duk abinda rabi tace tana so shima yana sonshi, kamar yanda kaltume ta tsara haka abun ya kasance ckn k'asa da sati 1 aka d'aura auren kaltume da dady, momy kam duk yanda bata son abun a baya yanxu itace gaba don kuwa kaltume ta k'ara yi mata wani aikin akan wancan a sanya mata soyayyar kaltume byn mugun tsoro da kwarjinin da tk mata, hatta kubra saida kaltume ta sanya aka yi mata aiki mai kyau a kanta, dady kam abun ya zamar masa 2 ga asirin momy da yk tasiri akan sa sannan kaltume itama ta banko masa nata, yanxu babu Wanda kaltume bata gama dashi a gidan ba sai suhailat wacce itama tayi amma yak'i tasiri akanta hakan ya k'ara janyowa suhailat muguwar k'iyayya a wajen ta, kuma ta d'auki alk'awarin duk abinda zata yi sai taga byn ta.


Y Sadeeq yana k'asar Japan sati 1 da auren yasmeen ya tafi saboda sun zauna sunyi shawara da FK akan ya tafi ya kula da kasuwancin dady tunda shi ya manyanta yakamata ya huta da zuwa k'asashen duniya, haka ne yasa Sadeeq dn ya tafi ita kam aunty yasmeen tana can gidan ta bata san abinda yk faruwa ba idan sunyi waya da momy tana cemata komai lpy saboda kaltume ta gargad'e ta akan kar ta kuskura tasanar da yasmeen abinda yk faruwa, ita kam suhailat bata son hankalin yasmeen ya tashi shi yasa idan sukayi waya bata nuna mata tana ckn damuwa balle ta fad'a mata abinda yk faruwa.


Haka rayuwar wannan gidan ta kasance tsawon lokaci kaltume ke juya kowa da komai na gidan momy kam ta zama kamar wani remote control dn kaltume, don kuwa juya ta tk yanda tk so, dady abun yayi masa yawa gaba 1 sun lalata masa tunani baya tunanin komai sai dai ya faranta ransu don har yanxu aikin momy bai sake shi ba, hatta abinda ya shafi ibadarsa sun hana shi, ya manta yaransa, abokansa, kasuwancin sa da duk wani abu me muhimmanci da yashafi rayuwar sa, amma duk da haka yana kula da sallar farillah da zarar lokacin sallah yayi zai je yayi nafila kam ko tunanin yinta ba yayi.
  
Momy kuwa babu abinda ya canxa na halayyar ta, kyara da tsangwamar da tk yi wa suhailat kamar k'aruwa tk kullum akwai mugun abinda zata yi mata, matsalar ta 1 kaltume da ta zamar mata k'adangaren bakin tulu amma banda haka ta samu alhj yanda tk so.


Kubra da taga yanda rayuwar gidan su ta canxa tana kallo ana cin zarafin mahaifiyar ta amma baxata iya d'aukar mataki ba tunda itama yanxu tsoron kaltume tk, hakan tasa ba shiri ta had'a kayanta ta koma gidan su mimey suka cigaba da rayuwar barikinsu.


To rayuwar dai sam babu dad'i ga suhailat don y'ar aiki ma ta fita daraja a gidan aikin da tk yi tamkar jakar su kuma babu me gode mata, da zarar tayi kuskure kuma tasha duka, alak'ar ta dady kuwa shine idan ta ganshi ta gaida shi,sai tayi kwana 2 ma bata ganshi ba, ta rame tayi bak'i babu me taimaka mata sai Allah sai kuma FK, tun byn dawowarsa daga garin sokoto ya matsa mata yana son zuwa wajen ta amma tak'i amince wa wata rana kawai yai mata xuwan baxata aikuwa baiji dad'in halin da yasameta ba, ckn wasu jemammun kaya ita kanta duk ta jeme ta lalace, aikuwa ya tisa ta da tanbayoyi akan abinda yk faruwa, bata iya 6oye masa komai ba ta fad'a masa duk abinda ta sani, yayi mamaki sosae musamman da yaga yanda dady ya kuma rak'umi da akala a gidan wannan yasa yk dn taimaka mata da abubuwan buk'ata don ya lura babu Wanda yk siya mata, sau 2 ya nemi ganin dady amma momy ta hana shi fitowa sai a masallaci suka had'u, byn an idar da sallah fk yasamu dady suka gaisa abinda ya k'ara tayarwa da fk hankali shine yanda dady ya nuna ya manta dashi kwata-kwata, fk yace. "Dady yanxu baka ganeni ba nine fa d'anka likita." D'aga kansa dady yayi alamar tunani kfn yace. "Gsky yaro ban shaida ka ba kai dn waye ckn yaran abokaina."? Lokacin da fk ya dawo wajen suhailat kuka yk sosae saboda abun ba k'aramin gigita masa tunani yayi lallai wasu matan basu da imani kwata-kwata, dubi yanda aka juyar da tunanin mutum me hankali da kamala irin dady, ya d'auki alk'awarin insha'allah sai ya nema masa mafita don bazai bari y Sadeeq da Yasmeen su san abinda yk faruwa ba, tunda duk basa k'asar, yasmeen sun tafi k'asar saudiyya da mijinta saboda ya k'arasa karatun sa, shi kam y Sadeeq yana can Japan, fk ya dubi suhailat byn yagama sanar da ita yanda sukayi da dady yace. Allah sarki suhailat baiwar Allah bata san abinda aka shirya a kanta ba xuciyar ta 1 tk aikin da ta saba momy ta kira ta, jikinta na rawa ta k'arasa wajen momy ta tsugunna, momy ta kalleta kfn tace." Ki d'auko hijabinki zamu tafi wani guri." Ni." suhailat ta  fad'a ckn rashin fahimta "eh ko bazaki ba."Momy ta fad'a tana zare mata ido, "kiyi hak'uri ba haka nk nufi ba." Ta fad'a tare da mik'ewa ta nufi d'akin ta don cika umarnin momy.

Bata kawo komai a ranta ba sai dai tayi mamaki saboda ko k'ofar gida momy bata ta6a cewa zasu tafi tare ba, hijabinta kawai ta d'auka sai wayar ta dk mak'ale a xanin ta, lokacin da ta fito momy tana kofar d'akin nata don haka suka wuce parking space. Tunda akayi auren aunty yasmeen bata k'ara hawa motar gidan ba sai yau da momy ta janyo ta, gaba momy ta hau ita kam baya ta bud'e ta shiga, saida ta xauna sannan ta fahimci tare da kubra xasu je saboda ganin kubran da tayi a maxaunin driver da alama itace wacce zata tuk'a motar, da gudu kubra ta figi motar kamar zasu kifa, suhailat ta k'ank'ame jikinta waje 1 Momy tace. "Haba kubra kija mu a hankali mana." Bata yi magana ba ta cigaba da tuk'i k'ware wa.


Tafiya mai nisa suka yi ita kam suhailat tunda tk bata ta6a tafiya mai nisa irin haka ba, daga gida sai makaranta gara ma lokacin da aunty yasmeen na gida suna xuwa shopping lokaci-lokaci, abinda yafi tayar mata da hankali shine yanda gaban ta yk fad'uwa ta sani ba abun alkhairi suke shirya wa a kanta ba, tana kallon su suna maganganun su k'asa-k'asa, addu'a tk yi Allah ya ku6utar da ita daga dukkan makircin da suka shirya mata.




Haka suka cigaba da tfy kamar zasu bar garin kano har wani dn daji suka shigo suna tfy, a tsakiyar dajin ta hango wani dank'areren makeken gida ita a tunanin ma wani company ne, kawai sai gani tayi kubra ta kutsa motar ckn harabar gidan byn ta nuna wa sojojin dk gadin gurin wani dn card, a parking space ta parker motar sannan ta fito momy ma fitowa tayi tana k'arewa gidan kallo." lallai hjy sara ta gama mallakar duniya dubi wannan katafaren gida wai duk mallakin mace ne." Momy ta fad'a ckn xuciyar ta, kubra ta kira hjy sara a waya ta shaida mata sun iso, a take ta sallami yarinyar da tk tare da ita sannan ta fad'a bathroom don tayi wanka byn ta bawa kubra umarnin shiga kai tsaye.



Kubra ta juya ta kalli momy wacce ke faman raba idanu tunda tk shiga gidajan masu arxik'i bata ta6a had'u wa da Wanda ya tsaru da dukiya irin wannan ba, murmushi kubra tayi kfn tace. "Momy don Allah ki nutsu karki bada mu mana tace mu shiga." Momy ta dubi kubra sannan tace. "Yanxu kubra wannan gidan ma nata ne."? "Momy kenan ai wannan kad'an ma kk gani tana da manyan gida je a ko wacce jiha dk Nigeria saboda itace mataimakiyar shugabar k'ungiyar y'an lesbian wacce tk can abuja. Girgixa kai momy tayi tana k'ara jin jina al'amarin a ckn ranta, wani security daga ckn sojojin gurin ya matso yace." Hjy me kk buk'ata ne. "? Kubra ta nuna masa kati Wanda yk nuna alamar su dn na gida ne.


Suhailat tana ckn mota tayi shiru sai kalle-kalle tk yi taga lokacin da momy da kubra suka sauka abinda yasa batayi gaggawar fitowa ba shine, ita a tunanin ta wani sak'on zasu kar6a a companyn sannan su dawo su tafi, tana iya hango su ta glass dn motar suna magana amma bata iya jin abinda suke tattauna wa, momy ta bud'e motar ckn 6acin rai kfn tace." Dallah malama ki fito me kk jira byn kina kallo duk mun fito." Suhailat ta fito kanta a k'asa tana bawa momy hak'uri, bata tsaya sauraron ta ba tabi byn kubra wacce tk dab da shiga k'awataccen parlourn gidan, bata da wani xa6i haka tabi byn su.



Yanda aka tsara parlourn dole mutum ya tsaya ya kallah musamman Wanda ya sanya duniya a gaban sa, ga ma'aikata suna faman kai kawo, ckn dn k'ank'anin lokaci aka cika su da kayan motsa baki, kubra ce kawai tk dn ta6a abinda aka kawo musu, momy kuwa parlourn kawai tk bi da kallo, ita kam suhailat kanta na k'asa duk iya tunanin ta bata iya gano abinda yasa momy ta kawo ta wannan wajen ba.



Saida suka shafe kusan 30minute's suna jiran hjy Sara sannan ta sauko daga samanta, wata jibgegiyar mata ce tare da wasu matasan y'an mata a gefenta, guri ta samu ta zauna a d'aya daga ckn luntsuma-luntsuman kujerun da akayi wa parlourn k'awanya dasu, idon ta na kan suhailat wacce tayi k'asa da kanta tana wasa da fingers dn ta, momy da kubra suka kwashi gaisuwata amsa ckn isa da k'asaita sannan tace. "Wannan itace yarinyar na lura babu wayewa a tare da ita tabbas zansha aiki koda yk nafi son irin su ba wad'anda idon su ya bud'e ba." Momy tayi dariya kfn tace. "Hakane hjy yarinyar kifin rijiya ce bata san komai ba, amma da zarar kin koya mata xata iya nasan xaki ji dad'in harka da ita don kuwa tana da biyayya." Murmushi hjy Sara tayi kfn tace."ni dama ai nafi son irin su wad'anda kansu yk ckn tukunya." Ta dubi inda suhailat ke zaune tace. "Babu ko gaisuwa y'an mata."? Ita kam suhailat ta lula duniyar tunani sam ba ta san abinda suke fad'i ba, saida taji momy ta gallara mata mintsini sannan ta d'ago da sauri idon ta yana shirin kawo k'wallah, harara momy ta watsa mata kfn tace." "Ayi hak'uri hjy. " Momy ta fad'a tana dariya, hjy Sara ta d'auko kud'i masu yawa ta k'ara musu tare da yi musu gdy saboda ta yaba da kyan yarinyar irin su tk so, Momy da kubra suka yi gdy tare da yiwa hjyr sallama, suhailat ganin sun mik'e da niyyar tfy itama ta mik'e, momy ta juyo ta kalleta sannan tace. "Ke fa anan zaki zauna kin dawo hannun hjy Sara don haka duk abinda ta umarce ki dashi ko menene shi xakiyi idan kuma kk kuskura kk sa6a mata xanxo har gidan nan na hukunta ki ftn kin gane." Tana ji tana gani Momy da kubra suka tafi, hawaye masu xafi suka xubo mata babu shakkah yanxu ne ta amsa sunan ta na marainiya a baya aunty yasmeen da y Sadeeq sun mantar da ita matsayin ta, hjy Sara ta matso kusa da ita tare da shafo face dn ta tace. "Sorry beauty ki daina kuka nan ma tamkar gida ne ki saki ranki zaki ji dad'in rayuwa damu muddin kina bin dukkan abinda nk so." Ta juya ta kalli d'aya daga ckn y'an matan dk biye da ita tace. "Zeeza kaita special room ta huta ki kai mata duk abinda tk buk'ata." Wacce aka kira da zeeza ta amsa ckn girmamawa sannan ta kamo hannun suhailat suka wuce inda aka umarce ta, ita kam suhailat binta kawai tk ckn rashin haiyaci haka suka wuce tarin d'akuna da parts iri-iri sannan suka isa inda aka tanadar mata zeeza ta sanya key ta bud'e k'ofar d'akin sannan ta juya ta dubi suhailat wacce ke tsaye tamkar gunki tace. "Nan ne d'akin ki beauty ki shiga kiyi wanka ki huta bari na kawo miki abinci." Tana gama fad'in haka ta juya, suhailat ta shiga d'akin xuciyar ta na wani irin bugu, yanda aka tsara d'akin mutum xai iya 6ata lokaci me yawa wajen kallon sa, ita kam xuciyar ta a cunkushe tk bata wani tsaya k'arewa d'akin kallo ba ta nemi inda bathroom yk ta shiga ta d'auro alwala saboda magriba tayi byn ta idar da sallah ta koma bathroom dn don tayi wanka, a dai-dai lokacin zeeza ta dawo hannun ta d'auke da tire Wanda aka jero kayan abinci kala-kala, ganin tana bathroom zeeza ta ajiye tiren akan stool sannan ta juya.

"Naga kinyi wanka amma baki canxa kaya ba." Ai banxo da kayan ba." Suhailat ta bata amsa kai tsaye, zeeza tayi dariya kfn tace. Tana gama fad'in haka ta mik'e ta fice daga d'akin, suhailat ta bita da ido kfn ta mik'e ta nufi wajen da wardrobe dn tk, duba wa ta farayi ko xata samu kayan da zata iya sanya wa, gashi dai wardrobe dn cike tk da kaya amma ko wanne ta d'aga sai taga baxata iya sanya shi ba saboda k'ana nan kaya ne kuma duk shara-shara masu kama jiki, haka ta dinga bincike kayan da k'yar ta samu wata doguwar riga mai dogon hannu itama don babu yanda xatayi ne saboda rigar irin masu bin jikin nan ce lokacin da ta kalli kanta a jikin mirror saida ta tsorata saboda yanda rigar ta fito mata da halittar jikinta da sauri ta d'auki hijabinta ta sanya, ko mai bata shafa ba duk da tarin kayan kwalliyar dk jibge bisa mirror, wajen abincin ta nufa ta fara dubawa dukda yunwar da tk ji cikinta bai bata damar cin abincin da yawa ba saboda a cunkushe yk, haka ta dn tsakuri kad'an ta tashi ta koma bisa makeken bed dn dk d'akin ta kwanta tana karanta wasik'ar jaki.

"

" bata tsaya ta bata amsa ba ta kashe wayar, zeeza ta kalli suhailat kfn tace. "Taso muje beauty shugar mom tana jiran ki." 

 Suhailat bata ce komai ba ta mik'e suka fito dukda cewa dare ya fara yi amma gidan tamkar rana saboda haske haka suka dinga wuce gurare masu yawa sannan suka iso part dn hjy Sara Wanda yk saman bene tsaya wa fad'ar yanda aka tsara gurin da kayan more rayuwa 6ata lokaci ne, zeeza na kula da suhailat sam bata damu da kallon tsarin gidan ba ckn mamaki tk kallon duk wani motsi na suhailat saboda hakan yana daga ckn aikin ta.

Itace ke saukar duk wasu newcomers da suke xuwa gidan kuma duk wacce suka xata kawo wajen hjy Sara tana ganin yanda suke rud'ewa saboda yanda gurin ya tsaru amma ga mamakin ta sai taga suhailat kanta ma yana k'asa, ita kam suhailat burinta kawai suje wajen hjyr watak'ila tasan dalilin da yasa momy ta kawo ta wannan gidan, ba tasan sun iso ba saida zeeza ta ta6a ta sannan ta d'ago da sauri, zeeza tayi nocking a hankali hjy Sara ta bada umarnin shigo wa, suka shiga bakin suhailat d'auke da sallama wacce bata samu Wanda ya amsa mata ba, durk'usa wa tayi k'asa kamar yanda taga zeeza tayi, hjy Sara ta dubi zeeza ckn kallon mamaki kfn tace. "Amma ya akayi na ganta ckn wannan shigar shin baki sanar mata yanda dokar gidan tk ba." Zeeza ta sunkuyar da kanta cike da girmamawa tace. "Na Sanar da ita momcy amma abinda na fahimta ita bata ma san abinda taxo yi a gidan nan ba."

D'aga mata kai suhailat tayi don ita tunda ta kalli mata sau 1 bata k'ara kallon inda tk ba saboda munin shigar dk jikinta a take taji tsanar matar ta shiga ckn xuciyar ta, hjy Sara ta dubi zeeza tace." Idan kin raka ta d'akin ki dawo zamuyi magana. " OK zeeza ta fad'a suna k'ok'arin fita daga d'akin.

"Wacce irin wayewa kk so ki ganni da ita." Suhailat ta tanbaye ta ckn rashin damuwa, "irin wacce koda ba'a yi miki bayanin abinda ya kawo ki gidan nan ba xaki gane da kanki." "Lallai yarinyar nan da alama tana da taurin kai aikuwa xn fad'a wa shugar mom tun wuri don ta san matakin da xata d'auka, da wannan tunanin tayiwa suhailat sallama ta tafi.

Kai tsaye part dn hjy Sara ta nufa tun kfn ta shiga tk jiyo nishi sama-sama, ba sai anyi mata bayanin abinda yk faruwa ba ta riga tasani don haka ta d'an tsaya kfn daga baya ta tuna cewa hjyr tace zasu yi magana don haka tayi nocking shiru kusan 15 minutes tana nocking amma shiru don haka ta tura k'ofar ga mamakin ta sai taji a bud'e kai tsaye ta shiga don ita d'in amintacciyar hjy Sara ce ko tare da wa suke tare zeeza xata iya shiga babu shamaki,

Y'an mata ne guda 2 suke masha'ar su tare da hjyr ajiyar xuciya zeeza ta sauke don itama tana da buk'ata musamman da taga k'irar beauty hakan ya k'ara mata fitina hjy Sara ta mik'o mata hannu kai tsaye ta tafi tare da yin flinging da y'ar yaloluwar rigar dk jikinta suka cigaba da bad'alar su (Subhanallah).

Kaltume taji shiru kwana 2 momy bata sanya an bata 20 millions da ta nema ba, don haka ta tashi da Kanta ta same ta, momy na kwance a parlourn ta tana k'iyasta yanda zasu yi da kud'in da hjy Sara ta basu kawai sai ganin mutum tsaye a kanta tayi saida gaban ta ya fad'i da taga kaltume ce tsaye ta rik'e k'ugu tana mata wani kallon banxa momy ta bud'e baki da niyyar yin magana ckn sauri kaltume ta katse ta ta hanyar d'aga mata hannu sannan tace. "Ai daman nasan duk abinda akayi da jaki sai yaci kara 20 millions fa kawai nace a bani amma saboda son xuciyar ki kink'i yin magana don bak'ar mugunta to ki sani ni d'innan nafi ki iya mugunta zan shiga zan fita na tarwatsa duk wani asiri da kk yiwa alhj sannan na maye gurbin sa da nawa wlh sai kin raina kanki banxa kawai."

Tana gama fad'in haka ta juya ta bar parlourn, a guje momy ta bita tana bata hak'uri saboda tasan kaltume xata iya abinda yafi hakan ma idan kuwa haka ya faru bata san yanda xatayi da rayuwar ta ba, ko kallon ta kaltume batayi ba ta wuce part dn dady Wanda shine part dn ta a yanxu, durk'ushewa momy tayi a k'asa idonta na fitar da hawaye babu shakkah kaltume ta gama da ita ta yaudare ta saida ta amince da ita yanxu gashi ta ci amanar ta, kud'in da kaltume ta nema indai a dukiyar alhj ne ba za'a same su yanxu ba don kuwa komai yana hannun Sadeeq duk wasu kadarori suna hannun sa shi kuma yana can ko wayar sa ba'a samu, ajiyar xuciya ta ja dole ta duba kud'in da hjy Sara ta basu ko zasu isa sai ta bawa kaltume don baxata bari kaltume ta rusa mata aiki ba, da sauri ta mik'e Allah yasa bata kai kud'in banki ba kubra kawai ta bawa 1 million a ciki don itama ta d'an shana, ta d'auko kud'in ta xauna xaman k'irgawa sai gashi sun kusa kaiwa 5 million ne kawai babu don haka ta tattara kud'in ta nufi 6angaren kaltume, tunda ga nesa kaltume ta hango ta don haka da sauri itama ta xo wajen momy dn musamman da taga hannun ta rik'e da leda dukda tayi farinciki da ganin kud'in amma sai ta gimtse fuska tana harar momy sannan a tsawace tace. "Meye kuma yasa kk biyo ni na riga na gama magana don haka kiyi gaggawar barin gurin nan tun kfn ranki ya 6aci." Momy ta durk'usa har k'asa tace. "Kiyi hak'uri kaltume kud'in basu samu duka ba saboda yanxu duk wasu kadarori da kud'ad'en alhj suna hannun Sadeeq sai abinda ba za'a rasa ba." Ta k'arasa maganar tana mik'a mata ledar hannun ta, wani ban xan kallo tabi hannun momy dashi kfn ckn 6acin rai tace. "Nasan wannan duk shirin ki ne kin damk'awa d'anki dukiyar a tunanin ki bazan iya kar6a a wajen sa ba ko? To kisa a ranki alhj da kansa zai kar6i dukiyar ya mallaka min ita ni kuma da zarar na samu abinda nk so na watsar daku na auri yaro mai jini a jika yanda xan more rayuwa ta yanda nk so yayinda ke da y'ay'anki zaku zama tamkar almajirai." 


Ckn tashin hankali momy tace. "Wlh ba nice na sanya shi ya kar6a ba don Allah kada kiyi min abinda kk fad'a ki tuna yanda na rik'e ki a gidan nan tamkar k'anwata na fifita ki akan yarinyar da aka haifa a gidan na d'auki dukkan amana da yadda na damk'a miki amma ki rasa irin sakayyar da zaki yi min sai wannan."?

Kaltume ta bushe da dariya sannan cike da tsiwa tace." Rabi kk kowa me kk ci na baka na xuba, ai ba ki gama ganin irin sakayyar da zanyi miki ba sai na aikata abinda nk fad'a kuma da kk cewa kin rik'e ni kamar k'anwar ki da bakya morata nasan babu abinda zaisa ki rik'e ni da mutunci, kuma na lura aiki na ya fara sanyi a kanki tunda har kk iya duban tsabar ido na ki gaya min magana dole nayi sabon aiki a kanki." Momy ta dage tana rok'on kaltume saida tayi da gaske kfn  kaltume ta kar6i kud'in tace. "Karki yi tunanin don na kar6i wad'annan kud'in zan fasa abinda nayi niyyah zan dai duba na gani idan da yiwuwar a fasa sai a fasa amma banyi miki alk'awari ba." Tana gama fad'in haka ta juya ta bar momy durk'ushe, jiki a sanyaye tabar parlourn.


Kaltume na kallon fitar momy ta zazzage kud'in ta k'irga wani ihun murna ta sanya lallai ba taga ta zama ba dole ta koma wajen dn tsurku ta jik'a shi da kud'i ya warware abinda rabi ta sanya shi yayi wa alhj, sannan ayi mata sabon aiki akan su biyun, wani murmushi mugunta ta saki sannan a fili tace. MANAZARTA WRITERS ASSOCIATION

Saida suka shafe tsawon awa d'aya suna bad'ala kfn ko wacce ta dawo ckn nutsuwar ta, hjy Sara ta d'auki kud'i ta bawa ko waccensu daga ita sai zeeza saboda tace zasu yi magana byn sunyi wanka hjy Sara ta dubi zeeza tace." Wacce hanya kk ganin zamu bi mu janyo hankalin wannan yarinyar don na lura ita dn ta musamman ce dukiya bata rud'ar ta."zeeza ta gyara zama kfn tace. "Gsky ne hjy ne hjy ni shaida ce akan wannan don kuwa kinsan nice ke kula da duk wata newcomer, to wlh tun lokacin da kk d'ora ni akan wannan aikin ban ta6a gamuwa da me irin mood dn ta ba, lokacin da zamu shigo part dn ki duk ckn kud'i da hutun da mutum ya fito dole ya girgixa saboda dukiyar da aka narka a wajen amma wlh momcy yarinyar nan ko a jikinta sunkuyar da kanta ma tayi, kinga kenan bazamu rud'e ta da kud'i ba sannan yarinyar na lura batada tsoro don har baraxana nayi mata akan xata sha wahala muddin tace zatayi mana taurin kai amma sai tace indai wahala ce ta saba da ita ni gsky yanxu bansan abinda yakamata muyi don mu shawo kanta ba." Hjy Sara ta ja dogon numfashi ita kam baxata iya hak'ura da wannan yarinyar ba don haka ko ta wacce hanya zata bi don ta mallake ta, ta dubi zeeza tace. "Kin fad'a mata abinda ya kawo ta gidan nan kuwa."? "A'a momcy saida safe nk so na fad'a mata komai dama na lura zata bamu matsala ne shi yasa nace sai na fad'a miki don mu samu hanyar da zamu bi ki same ta." Hjy Sara ta sauke ajiyar xuciya kfn tace. "Dole ne na samu wannan yarinyar saboda a halin yanxu duk wani buri na shine na mallake ta, don haka na baki sati d'aya kamar yanda na fad'a miki kiyi iyakar k'ok'arin ki wajen janyo hankalin ta, idan kin kasa shawo kanta kuma sai mu nemi wata mafitar, amma tunda kinga kud'i ko baraxana baza suyi tasiri akan ta ba ki bita a hankali da siyasa da lalla6awa har mu samu abinda muke so." "OK momcy yanda kk ce haka za'a yi."  Xeexa ta fad'a ckn murmushi,
"Yauwa zeeza ta, shi yasa nk sonki musamman yanda kk son duk abinda nk so. " hjy ta bata amsa tare da janyo ta jikinta, lumshe ido zeeza tayi kfn tace. "Kece duniya ta momcy kin bani duk wani farinciki da na rasa a duniya kinga kuwa nima dole ne inso abinda kk so." Nan dai suka k'ara shagala da aikata abinda Allah ya haramta.


Suhailat ta kwana ckn tunani da xullumi, a gaban ta hjy ta bawa zeeza umarnin ta sanar da ita abinda aka kawo ta gidan nan tayi amma shiru zeeza bata fad'a mata komai ba, abinda ta lura dashi kawai ba abun arxik'i akeyi a gidan ba saboda ko yanayin shigar yaran gidan da kuma shugabar gidan kad'ai ya isa ya nuna mata hakan, ta runtse ido hawaye masu xafi suka biyo gefen idonta, zata tanbayi zeeza da kanta don ta samu nutsuwar xuciya da wannan tunanin bacci yai awon gaba da ita.



Kiran sallar farko a kunnen suhailat don haka ta mik'e ta d'auro alwala ta fara nafiloli, saida akayi assalatu kfn ta hak'ura da yin nafilar ta gabatar da sallar asubah sannan ta d'aga hannun ta sama tana zuba adduo'i tare da nemawa kanta kariya daga sharrin duk mai sharri tana addu'a hawaye na fita daga idonta haka ta shafa addu'ar cike da tausayin kanta, byn ta idar ta fara rero karatun alk'urani mai girma ba tare da ta samu alk'uranin ba a yanda ma taga yanayin gidan ba lallai suna da shi ba duk kuwa fad'in gidan, da yk ita dn hafixa ce shi yasa hakan bai dameta ba ta cigaba da tilawar ta.



Tun kfn ta shigo wajen tk juyo abinda tunda tk tsawon five years a gidan bata ta6a jinsa ba koda wasa wato karatun alk'urani mai girma ckn wata zazzak'ar murya, sauri ta k'ara don inda tk dosa anan ne karatun yk fita a hankali ta tura k'ofar d'akin ga mamakin ta sai taga ta bud'e jiki a sanyaye ta shiga ckn d'akin xuciyar ta na harbawa a hankali, tsaya wa tayi a bakin k'ofar tana kallon yanda suhailat ke sarrafa d'an k'aramin cute bakinta wajen yin k'ira'a amma idonta a rufe, ta dad'e a tsaye tana kallo da sauraron suhailat ganin tsaiwar baxata yi mata ta sunkuya ta ajiye kayan dk hannun ta, a dai-dai wannan lokacin ne kuma suhailat taji motsin mutum amma hakan bai sa ta bud'e idonta ko ta tsaya da karatun da tk yi ba saida ta kai k'arshen aya sannan ne ta bud'e ido don ganin Wanda ya shigo, zeeza ta gani durk'ushe a gaban kayan da ta shigo dashi jikinta a matuk'ar sanyaye, suhailat ta mik'e daga inda tk dama xaman jiran ta tk yi don ta tanbaye ta, wajen da zeeza ke durk'ushe ta k'arasa tare da fad'in "ya akayi na ganki wani iri ko baki da lpy." Xeexa ta yunk'ura da k'yar ta mik'e tsaye sannan ta kama hannun suhailat tace. "Na kawo miki break fast ne beauty, lokacin da na taho lpy ta klw amma Ina tunkarar d'akin nan na tsinci kaina ckn rashin kuzari musamman da naji kina karatun alk'urani mai girma, na dad'e ina sauraron zazzak'ar muryar ki yayinda ke kuma baki ma san na shigo ba." Zeeza ta saki hannun suhailat sannan ta juya mata baya a k'ok'arin ta na goge hawayen dk shirin xubo mata sannan ta cigaba ba tare da ta juyo ba. "Beauty yau shekara ta 5 a gidan nan amma ban ta6a jin ana wani abu makamancin karatu ba sai yau a dalilin xuwan ki beauty, tabbas ke d'in y'ar baiwa ce." Saida ta gama goge hawayen ta sannan ta juyo wajen da suhailat ke tsaye cike da mamaki, zeeza ta dafa shoulder dn suhailat kfn tace. "Yakamata kije kiyi break fast." Girgixa kai suhailat tayi sannan ckn muryar damuwa tace. "Bana jin xan iya cin komai a halin yanxu saboda ina ckn damuwa har yanxu ban san abinda ya kawo ni wannan gidan ba kuma hjy tace ki fad'a min amma baki sanar dani komai ba." "Karki damu xan sanar dk komai sannan nayi miki alk'awarin zan baki tarihin rayuwa ta ke dai kawai kije kiyi break ina xuwa."
Bata jira abinda xata fad'a ba tayi saurin ficewa daga d'akin, suhailat ta bita da ido ckn mamaki kfn daga baya ta wuce bathroom saida tayi wanka tayi brush sannan ta fito kayan ta na jiya ta mayar daman ta wanke abinta, byn ta gama shirya wa ta janyo tiren da zeeza ta ajiye, tea kawai ta had'a tasha, shima don kada ta zauna da yunwa ne, zeeza ta shigo d'akin har yanxu jikinta a sanyaye yk ta samu guri ta zauna duba abincin tayi taga suhailat bata ta6a komai ba ta dubeta tace. "Beauty ai baki ci komai ba." Suhailat tace. "Bana jin cin komai yanxu don Allah ki fad'a min abinda kk yi min alk'awari zaki fad'a min." Zeeza ta gyara zama ta fara magana kamar haka. "An kawo ki gidan nan ne ba don komai ba sai don a 6ata miki rayuwa, duk wacce kk gani a gidan nan taxo ne saboda wannan dalilin, wasu sune suke kawo kansu, yayinda wasu kuma ake kawo su saboda rashin gatansu." Ta d'an tsagaita da magana yayinda hawaye ke k'ok'arin fara zuba daga idonta, "beauty a gidan nan ana yin duk wani abu na sa6on ubangiji, akwai wata 6oyayyiyar  k'ungiya ta y'an lesbian hjy Sara itace maitaimakiyar shugabar k'ungiyar wacce tk can abuja kuma yanxu haka maganar da nk miki an sanya sunanki ckn y'an k'ungiya ." Ckn tsananin tashin hankali suhailat ta mik'e tsaye jiri na kwasar ta da k'yar zeeza ta iya xaunar da ita babu abinda bakinta yk iya fad'a sai "innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji'un" xeexa ta kalli suhailat yanda duk hankalin ta ya tashi tun kfn taji sauran bayanin, a hankali ta dinga karanta innalillahi har ta d'an samu nutsuwar xuciya, ganin ta d'an nutsu zeeza ta cigaba da yi mata bayani kamar haka. "Byn lesbian anayin kawalcin mata manyan alhazawa da wasu daga ckn y'an siyasa suna zuwa su xa6i duk wacce tayi musu su biya kud'i hjy ta basu ko kwana nawa mutum zai d'auki mace hjy xata fad'a masa kud'in da zai biya ta, byn wannan kuma suna yin safarar mata xuwa k'asashen turawa da larabawa don kawai suyi karuwanci, suna da d'aurin gindi daga manyan gwamnati shi yasa suke yin abinda suka ga dama." 


Ba suhailat ba hatta zeeza kuka suke me tsuma xuciya, ckn shashekar kukan suhailat ke fad'in. "Yanxu irin wannan rayuwar momy ta xa6ar min rayuwa irin ta dabbobi, gsky momy batayi min adalci ba me yasa ta tsaneni da yawa haka, don kawai na kasance y'ar kishiyarta menene laifi na a wannan, ya Allah ka kawo min d'auki." Ta k'arasa maganar tana wani irin gunjin kuka jin xuciyar ta tk kamar xata fashe saboda xafi da rad'ad'in abinda tk ji, zeeza ta janyo ta jikinta tana lallashinta dukda cewa itama kukan tk, ckn kukan itama tace. "Tun lokacin da naga an kawo ki na fahimci kishiyar uwa ce ta kawo ki kamar yanda nima itace ta kawo ni, beauty nasha wahala a rayuwa ta banta6a tunanin xan samu wani farinciki ba, lokacin da aka kawo ni gidan nan na kasance ckn k'unci da tashin hankali musamman lokacin da hjy Sara ta fara bijiro min da buk'atar ta na nuna mata bazan iya aikata abinda tk so na aikata ba, a hankali ckn makirci da shu'umanci ta dinga nuna min muhimmancin kud'in da xan samu idan na amince sannan ta dinga tsoratar dani akan bala'in da zan tsinci kaina muddin nak'i amince wa ta fad'a min xata maida ni gurin inna wato kishiyar mahaifiya ta saboda tasan yanda nk tsoron ta don kuwa matar  ta azabtar dani shi yasa nk matuk'ar tsoron ta, da wannan tayi amfani tayi galaba a kaina ta samu abinda tk so a waje na, daga wannan lokacin ta koyar dani komai na xama ta hannun dama a wajen ta,   amma tana sakar min kud'i ina yin duk abinda naga dama dasu, tuni na manta da matsalar kishiyar mahaifiya ta, na tsunduma ckn wannan harkar." Ta d'an tsagaita tana goge hawayen da suka taru sannan ta cigaba."sunana na gsky shine zainab hjy ce ta sanya min zeeza kamar yanda ta sanya miki beauty, tunda nk zaune a gidan nan ban ta6a jin danasani akan abinda nk aikata wa ba sai yau da nk tare dk, xuciya ta ta karye ne a lokacin da na same ki kina rera karatun alk'urani mai girma a dai-dai wannan lokacin na kamu da tausayin ki kuma zanyi duk yanda zanyi naga na taimaka miki ba'a sanya ki ckn wannan harkar ba, koda kuwa zai kasance abun alkhairi na k'arshe da xan aikata a rayuwa ta." 


Suhailat ta tsaida hawayen dk malala daga idonta ta rik'e hannun zeeza tace. "Don Allah ki taimaka min Xainab na shiga uku me nayi wa momy ta xa6i jefa ni ckn halaka irin wannan."?
Zeeza ta dafa byn ta kfn tace." Zanyi k'ok'arin taimaka miki insha'allah dukda nasan ba k'aramin aiki na kinkimo wa kaina ba, zan fuskanci duk wani k'alubale muddin zaki ku6uta daga hannun wad'annan azzaluman, amma dai yanxu ina so mu shirya wani plan dk yanda hjy baxata gane abinda muke shirya wa ba goge hawayen ki nutsu sosai."

Haka ta goge hawayen ta nutsu tana kallon zeeza, "kamar yanda na fad'a miki zamu shirya plan zanje na sanar da hjy kin amince daxgahasaboda yau dn nan zaki bar gidan nan insha'allah." Suhailat ta d'aga kanta alamar gamsuwa sannan tace. "Ngd sosai xainab amma don Allah ina rok'on ki kema ki daina wannan abun ba abu ne mai kyau ba." Zeeza tayi d'an murmushin takaici kfn tace. "Hmm ki tayani da addu'a beauty amma irin wannan abun da wuya mutum ya saba dashi sannan kuma lokaci 1 ya daina, amma nayi miki alk'awarin zan rage insha'allah." Misalin k'arfe 5:00  suka isa garin yola daga nan kuma suka wuce garin jada nan ne k'auyen su mama a wata ruga mai suna dadiri, ita dai suhailat binsu kawai tk da ido don ganin abun take tamkar a mafarki wai yau itace a garin Adamawa garin aka haifi mahaifiyar ta amma bata san inda zata nemi dangin mahaifiyar tata ba, dukda haka tana godiya ga Allah da ya had'a ta mutanen kirki tasani ba don su ba zata sha wahala kfn ta samu matsugunni, sai byn magriba aka iso ckn k'auyen jada, suna sauka daga mota basu tsaya komai ba suka wuce ainihin gidan iyayen mama, gida ne babba iyaye da y'ay'a rakwacam kowa da 6angaren sa, suhailat sai bin mutanen gidan tk da ido wad'anda suke murnar xuwan gwaggwo indo da d'anta kamalu ga mamakin suhailat sai taji suna yin fulatanci aikuwa sai ta zama y'ar kallo don babu abinda ta sani, byn sun d'an nutsu sannan wani  yaro adamu ya  lura da ita da fulatanci yk tanbayar mama yace. "Gwoggo wannan adda maryam ce ta dawo ba ance ta rasu ba."?  Sai a lokacin suka lura da ita sai kallo ya koma kanta, ita kam tayi tsuru kamar mara gsky saboda bata san abinda suke fad'a ba, mama ta janyo hannun ta tana fad'in." Maryam ce ku bari mu huta sai na fad'a muku labarin ta." Wata y'ar bukkah suka shiga tare da sallama wata tsuhuwa ce ta amsa tana murmushi ta dubi mama tace."use awartu  ja66aba indo." Mama ta amsa itama tana murmushin sannan ta tsugunna har k'asa ta gaida ta itama suhailat haka ta tsugunna ta gaida tsohuwar da hausa ga mamakin ta sai taji dattijiwar ta iya hausa sai dai ba sosae yk fita ba, tsuhuwar ta mik'e tsaye a hankali ta d'auko musu wani k'ok'o cike da madarar shanu sannan ta d'auko wata y'ar k'aramar k'warya itama cike da ruwa me sanyi ta ajiye musu, mama tace."use inna." Suhailat taga karamci irin na y'an k'auye don kfn wani lokaci an cika su da abinci kala-kala sai sun za6i Wanda suke so, shi kam kamal tuni ya shiga gari tare da samarin gidan kamar ba bak'o ba,  byn sun gama cin abinci sun huta sosae lokacin kowa ya hallara mazan gida duk sun dawo an zauna ana ta faman hira da tsokane-tsokane, ita kam suhailat tana raku6e a gefen mama duk jin jikinta tk wani iri, gaban ta yana yawan fad'uwa lokaci-lokaci, inna ce ta dubi d'iyar ta indo ckn yaren hausa tace. "Indo ina kk samo mana wannan yarinyar me kama da zuri'ar mu ni kam da ban san marayama ta rasu ba xance itace don gsky suna yanayi sai dai maryama tafi wannan jiki gaba d'aya gurin akayi shiru ana jiran jin bayani daga bakin indo, mama tayi musu bayani tiryan-tiryan kamar yanda suhailat ta fad'a mata ta K'ara da cewa." Ni gsky yarinyar ta shiga xuciya ta ne lokaci 1 sannan kuma da naji labarin ta sai nake tunanin wani abu a xuciya ta ban sani ba ko a ckn ku akwai wanda yai irin wannan tunanin game da wannan yarinyar. "? Ta k'arasa maganar tana bin yayyen ta maza da ido ta dawo da idonta kan mahaifiyar su sannan ta cigaba da cewa." Inna tun byn rasuwar addah khadija waya k'ara bincikar inda d'iyar ta tk a kano."? Baffah hamxa wanda duk gidan shine babba ya sauri yace. "Gsky ne indo kinyi tunani me kyau amma byn rasuwar dije ai kakar yarinyar ita ke kawo mana ita garin nan har ta rasu itama tunda ga wannan lokacin ne kuma muma bamu k'ara nemanta ba saboda mahaifin yarinyar yak'i yadda ya bamu ita mu had'a ta da d'an uwanta adamu." Inna ta juyo a hankali ta dubi suhailat kfn tace. "Yarinya menene sunan mahaifin ki."? Ckn fargaba tace." Ibrahim. " mahaifiyar ki fa."? Inna ta K'ara tanbayar ta cike da rud'ani ''mahafiya ta, ta rasu sunan ta Khadija. " wayyo ai inna bata san lokacin da taxo gaban suhailat ba tana fad'in."jika ta ce wlh d'iyar khadija ce y'ar uwar adamu." Inna ta rungume suhailat tana kukan murna tana sanbatu da fulatanci tk fad'in. "Ban kyau ta wa y'ay'a na ba sun rasu sun bar min d'iyansu amma na kasa nemo su a duniya nakasa kula dasu kaico na, wannan jini na ce wlh d'iyar dije ce.'' Duk gurin aka rikice masu hamdala nayi masu kuka nayi ita kam suhailat kasa motsi tayi kamar gunki don ita har yanxu ganin abinda yk faruwa tk tamkar a mafarki........

Pg 39

Hjy Sara bata farka ba sai wajen asuba tana bud'e ido taji yayi mata nauyi juya wa tayi gefe sai a lokacin k'wak'walwar ta tafara tuna abinda ya faru, zunbur tayi ta mik'e zaune sai gata a tsaye tana dudduba d'akin ganin da tayi babu kowa a ciki sai cup dn da tasha lemo har bathroom ta lek'a don ta tabbatar da abinda xuciyar ta ke raya mata, indai kuwa gsky ne abinda tk shirin gani lallai ta tafka babbar asara komawa tayi ta zauna jagwab a gefen gado kfn ckn tashin hankali tace. "yarinyar nan ni zata yaudara tayi min wayo sannan ta gudu? Lallai sai na nemo ta duk inda ta shiga don ni bazan ta6a yin asara ba, yanda na siye ta da mak'udan kud'i dolene nayi yanda naga dama da ita, na rantse da Allah saina nemo ta kuma sai tayi nadamar abinda ta aikata min da ita da duk Wanda na same shi da hannu a ciki.'' Ita kad'ai take magana tana sanbatu ckn sauri ta mik'e ta d'auko wayar ta ta danna number xeexa amma abun takaici wayar a kashe, fito wa tayi waje duk Wanda ta had'u dashi sai ta tanbaye shi ko yaga beauty amma babu Wanda ya bata cikakkiyar amsa wacce zata gamsu, haka ta hak'ura ta dawo d'akin ta tana tunanin hanyar da zata bi ta gano inda suhailat tk. 

Ita kam xeexa tana barin tasha gidan wata k'awar ta ta nufa dama tun kfn taxo ta sanar da ita don haka bata samu matsala ba can tai tafiyar ta a can ta kwana byn ta kashe duk wayoyin ta saboda tasan hjy zata nemeta idan ta farka ba taga suhailat ba.


_________________Kaltume  ta koma wajen boka d'an tsurku ta zuba mishi mak'udan kud'i byn ta bashi kanta a take ya warware asirin da yayi wa momy ya maye gurbinsa da nata, tayi wa momy mugun tanadi.

Lokacin da ta dawo ta xamo ita ke fad'a aji a ckn gidan babu Wanda ya isa ya tsallake umarnin ta momy ta zama tamkar wata y'ar aiki a wajen kaltume saboda ita keyin duk wani aikin gidan kubra da taga wulak'ancin kaltume yayi yawa sai ta had'a kayanta ta bar gidan don a cewarta baxata tsaya kallon takaicin da baxata iya maganin sa ba, tsakanin momy da dady sai kallo don babu abinda yk had'a su saboda kaltume ta sanya anyi  farrak'u a tsakanin su, momy ta koma abar tausayi yanxu, mafi yawancin lokaci ytana zama tasha kukan cin amanar da kaltume tayi mata, amma bata ta6a kawowa a ranta alhaki ne yk bibiyarta ba, yaranta yasmeen da Sadeeq ko wayar su bata samu yanxu don ita yanxu wayar ma nema tk ta gagareta don kaltume ta k'wace duk wani abu me daraja da tk dashi, daga k'arshe ma d'akin da momy ta ta6a maida suhailat na BQ nan kaltume ta mayar da momy a ranar da hakan ta faru momy tayi kuka kamar ta ari baki haka dole tanaji tana gani gidan ta da mijin ta suka fi karfin ta gashi y'ay'an ta basa kusa kubra ko xuwa gidan ma ba tayi, ana ckn wannan halin ne kuma hjy Sara ta 6allo wa momy wani tashin hankalin saboda tace ta dawo mata da suhailat ko ta biya ta duk kud'in da ta basu, hankalin  yai mugun tashi saboda ko kashe ta za'a yi bata san inda zata samu kud'in da zata biya hjy Sara ba, kuma tasan matar zata iya yi mata komai a kan 6atan Suhailat, hjy Sara ta bata sati 2 akan ta nemo mata Suhailat idan kuma ba haka ba zata d'auki mataki akan ta, ita kam bata san inda zata nemi yarinyar nan ba don tasan indai ba duniya yarinyar ta shiga ba to bata da inda zata je gashi kubra ko gidan bata xuwa balle su nemi mafita.


_____________________FK yaje garin me duguri lpy yabi duk kwatancen da ammy tayi masa kuma yayi nasarar samun yayyen ammy ya nema mata yafiya sun kuma yafe mata dama sun dad'e da yafe mata don har zuwa gidan ta sukayi abaya amma aka ce musu ta tashi kuma ba'a san inda ta koma ba, dole suka hak'ura amma sunyi nadamar wofantar da k'anwar su guda d'aya jal da sukayi shi yasa fk yana zuwa da wannan maganar suka kar6e shi hannu 2 tare da tarba me kyau, lokacin da zai dawo tare suka taho da d'aya daga ckn yayyen nata, ammy tayi murna harda kukan ta saboda fk yayi mata abinda baxata ta6a manta wa ba, haka suka sha hirar yaushe gamo da d'an uwanta sannan suka yanke shawarar zasu tafi tare da ita don taga sauran dangin ta, shi kam FK zai tafi can Adamawa domin ya nemi nasa dangin daga baya kuma sai ayi maganar auren sa.


Kfn ya tafi saida yaje gidan su suhailat inda yaji mummunan labari Wanda ya tayar masa da hankali wai ba'a San inda tk ba, lokacin da yaga momy yayi matuk'ar mamakin halin da ya same ta dukda cewa yasan ita d'in ba mutuniyar kirki bace amma saida ya tausaya mata da ya ganta, har kuka tayi masa akan ya nemo mata suhailat duk inda ya ganta ya lura bawai don tayi nadama ne tk so taga suhailat d'in ba ya fahimci tana da wata buk'atar daban, sai dai shi tashin hankalin da yk ciki shine abinda yk gaban sa don haka bai sauraron ta ba ya wuce abun sa, ya shiga ckn 
matsanancin tashin hankali da jin labarin 6atan suhailat lokacin da yazo ya sanar da ammy itama taji rashin dad'i sosae sai dai ta bashi shawarar yaje ya fara neman dangin mahaifin sa idan ya same su sai yazo kuma ya binciki inda suhailat tk, babu yanda zaiyi haka ya amince da shawarar ammy ba don ransa yana so ba don shi a son ranshi sai yaga Suhailat sannan ya tafi.


To ckn kwana 2 ya shirya tafiya Adamawa saboda yana so ya gama dasu kfn ya dawo ya nemi inda Suhailat tk, ammy ta bashi duk wasu shaida da zai samu sauk'i wajen gane dangin mahaifin sa, bai samu wata matsala ba ya isa ckn yola ya nemi masauki a wani hotel da yk jimeta kfn gobe ya fara shiga k'auyuka neman dangin mahaifin sa........... Pg40


Rayuwa me dad'i suhailat keyi ckn y'an uwanta kowa burin sa ya nuna mata k'auna,  bata ta6a tunanin dangin mahaifiyar ta suna da yawa haka ba saida kamal ya jata ya dinga yawo da ita ruga-ruga yana nuna musu ita, yanxu kam ta amince itama tana da gata.


Kamal yana bata kula wa fiye da kowa ckn y'an uwanta abinda ya lura dashi shine xuciyar sa ta tsunduma ckn kogin sonta sai dai ya rasa ta wacce hanya zai sanar da ita ta fahimce shi,

 yayan mama baffah haladu ya samu da maganar shi kuma ya sanar da sauran iyayen nasu kowa yaji dad'in wannan al'amari kuma anyi musu ptn alkhairi, mama kam cewa tayi yayi k'ok'ari ya samu soyayyar ta don ba za'a yi mata auren had'i ba, wannan dalilin ne yasa kamal ya fara nuna mata soyayyar sa a fili don baya so ya rasa ta.


Ita kam suhailat tuni ta fahimci inda kamal ya dosa amma tayi kamar bata fahimta ba, saboda har yanxu soyayyar FK na   nan dank'are a ckn xuciyar ta babban takaicin ta shine bata da hanyar da xata sanar dashi inda tk, tasani duk lokacin da doctor ya samu labarin bata gida hankalin sa ba k'aramin tashi zaiyi ba gashi wayar ta ta 6ata babu wata hanya da zata samu ta sanar dashi inda tk muddin ba kano ta koma ba garin da bata tunanin xata k'ara koma wa a rayuwar ta.




Tana ckn wannan tunanin ne ita kad'ai ckn daji saboda yanxu ta fara sabawa don har kiwo tk rako yaran gidan, yauma tare suke da hansatu y'ar gidan baffah hamxa itama budurwa ce sai dai suhailat ta girmeta sosae, 

Gajiya suhailat d'in tayi shi yasa ta zauna tana zama kuma ta fara abinda ta saba wato tunanin fk tana ckn wannan halin taji an d'an ta6a ta da sanda a firgice ta juyo don ganin Wanda ya tsorata ta, kamal ta gani yana mata dariya kwa6e fuska tayi alamar shagwa6a kfn tace. "Gsky y kamal ka tsorata ni dayawa yanxu da na  xuba da gudu fa."?

" nima sai na zuba da gudu har saina dawo dk." 

Turo d'an k'aramin bakinta tayi cike da shagwa6a tace."ai baxaka iya dawo dani ba." 

Yanda tk motsa lips d'in ta tamkar ba ita ke yin maganar ba hakan tasa ya xuba mata ido yana kallon ta komai nata mai kyau ne murmushin ta,shagwa6ar ta, maganar ta shirun ta, dukda bai ta6a ganin fushinta ba yasan shima zaiyi mata kyau, a fili yace. 

"My beautiful Queen"

Tana jinsa sai dai ko d'aga kanta batayi ba balle ta nuna taji abinda ya fad'a, ganin bata tanka ba  ya samu guri a gefen ta ya zauna ya zuba mata ido.

Tana jin yanda idon sa ke    yawo a kanta kunya duk ta rufeta ta kasa ko motsin kirki, ajiyar xuciya ya sauke kfn ya fara magana da cewa. "Maryam yakamata ace izuwa yanxu kin fahimci inda na dosa dukda ban fito na fad'a miki abinda yk ckn xuciya ta ba amma na nuna miki alamar da yakamata ace kin fahimta da kanki, maganar gsky na kamu da soyayyar ki ina ptn bazaki bani kunya ba." 

Shiru suhailat tayi ta kasa magana don ita kam soyayyar ta 1 ce kuma ta riga ta damk'awa fk ita, babu wata kafa da xata sanya soyayyar wani d'a namiji a xuciyar ta, ganin tayi shiru kamal ya dubeta ckn tashin hankali kfn yace. "Kinyi shiru Maryam kice wani abun don Allah."


A hankali ta iya d'ago idonta ta kalle shi sannan ckn raunanniyar murya tace. "Kayi hak'uri y   kamal a halin yanxu gsky babu ra'ayin soyayya a tare dani sai dai ko gaba."


Da mamaki yk dubanta sannan ckn sark'ewar murya yace. "Saboda me Maryam? Kina da ilimi kuma shekarunki sun kai yakamata ace yanxu kina da aure amma shine zaki ce bakida ra'ayin soyayya, ki fad'a min hujjarki ni kuma nayi miki alk'awarin indai na gamsu da ita zan rabu dk."


Kanta a k'asa tace. "Bani da wata hujja sai dai gsky a halin yanxu xuciya ta babu wani gurbi da wani xai shiga, bazan 6oye maka ba y kamal da akwai Wanda xuciya ta da raina suke k'auna kuma babu kafa da suka rage da wani zai shiga."


Tabbas hankalin kamal ya tashi da maganar da suhailat ta fad'a masa amma da yk wayayye ni kuma yasan abinda yk yi sai yace."yanxu to naji amma ai zaki bani labarin masoyin naki ko."?

Kunyar sa ce ta kama Suhailat ta kasa magana,  ya dube ta "karki damu k'anwata ni yayan ki ne ba zanso nayi sanadiyyar jefa ki ckn damuwa ba ki daure ki fad'a min wanene Wanda yai nasarar k'wace min xuciyar ki, xan kasance me yi muku ptn alkhairi."


Hawaye ne taji yana k'ok'arin zuba daga idonta da sauri ta mayar dashi koba komai zata so tasamu Wanda zasu tattauna wannan maganar dashi wata k'ila ya samo mata mafita, amma gsky tana jin nauyin fad'a wa y kamal don tana ganin hakan kamar wani cin fuska ne.

"Ki sanar dani inda yk ko xan nemo miki shi."

Jin abinda ya fad'a ba k'aramin mamaki ya bata ba don saida ta tsare shi da ido na tsawon second 30 abinda bata ta6a yi masa ba tunda tk, ganin da gsk yk babu wasa a fuskar sa yasa ta bashi tak'aitaccen labarin had'uwar su da fk da kuma rayuwar da sukayi ta soyayya, har xuwa lokacin rabuwarsu, tabbas rayuwar fk da Maryam ba k'aramin birgeshi tayi ba kuma insha'allah zaiyi duk k'ok'arin sa don ganin ya sanar da fk inda Suhailat tk saboda yanda yk sonta zai iya sadaukar mata da farincikin sa.


 Cike da tausayawa ya dube ta yace. "Maryam nayi miki  alk'awarin xan nemo miki doctor duk inda yk a fad'in kano  amma ina da sharad'i." Muryar ta a sark'e tace. 

"Ina jinka y kamal wanne irin sharad'i ne na amince indai zaka nemo min shi."

Wani abu me kama da kishi ya soki xuciyar sa amma sai ya danne yace. "Sharad'i na shine idan ban same shi ba ko yak'i zuwa inda kk  zaki cire soyayyar shi daga xuciyar ki sannan ki kar6i tawa shin kin amince."? 

" Na amince y kamal nasan fk ko cewa nayi a nan nk so muyi rayuwa zai zo wlh zai xo. "


Mamakin ta kamal kawai yk lallai wannan fk din ya cika me sa'a da har ya iya sace xuciyar Maryam haka, ''ba komai xan lallashi xuciya ta na bata hak'uri ko don na faranta mata." ya fad'a a xuciyar sa, a fili kuwa cewa yayi. "Tashi mu tafi gida kinga almuru ya fara yi."

Bata iya kallon sa ba don yanxu wata sabuwar kunyar sa tk ji sannan ya k'ara wata daraja da k'ima a idonta, muryar ta a hankali tace."tare da hansatu muka xo."

 Murmushi yayi kfn yace. "Ai tuni hansatu tana gida don ita ce ma ta fad'a min ta gudo ta barki." Mamaki yakamata  hakan yana nufin tasan abinda yk faruwa tsakanin ta da kamal kenan, batayi magana ba ta mik'e a hankali da y'ar sanda a hannun ta suna tafe  suna hira ya lura idan ba maganar fk akayi mata ba bata sakin jiki tayi hira amma da yk ya gane lagon ta yanxu ma tanbayar ta yk Kayan address d'in fk da inda zai same shi, aikuwa ta zage tana masa kwatance hospital d'in harma da gidan su fk d'in Wanda xuwan ta d'aya amma ta haddace shi a k'wak'alwar ta, har suka iso gida suna hirar duk Wanda ya gansu sai yai murmushi a tunanin su sun fahimci junane.


_________________Lokacin da hjy Sara ta d'ibarwa momy ya cika haka tazo da motar 6oyayyun sojojin ta aka kwashi momy aka tafi da ita a gaban jama'a aka wulak'anta ta, tana kuka tana komai amma ko k'ofar gida kaltume bata bar dady ya lek'o ba balle momy ta sanya ran za'a taimaka mata, haka suka tafi da ita tana kuka tana komai amma hjy Sara bata tausaya mata don kuwa a cewarta baxata d'auki k'addarar rasa beauty ba.


Kubra kam tana can likafa taci gaba don yanxu babu kalar bad'alar da batayi ta nemi maza ta nemi mata shan giya bin clubs babu Wanda batayi yanxu ma haka bata k'asar tana can ta raka wani saurayin ta malesia a can d'in ma bata tsaya shi kad'ai ba duk Wanda ya taya yanda kasan karya haka tk bak'in fata da jar fata kowa mu'amala tk dasu.


To isah ma dai ta 6angarensa babu wani cigaba sai na me hak'ar rijiya don shima yaxama rik'ak'k'en d'an iska liwad'i zina bai mai dasu komai ba da yake fari ne gaba d'aya ya saje da turawa kamannin sa, shigar sa da d'abi'ar sa xaka iya rantsuwa shi d'in bai had'a jini da musulmai ba.




Y Sadeeq kam ana can ana gudanar da kasuwanci komai alhamdulillah ana samun cigaba da riba mai yawa abinda kawai yk damun sa shine rashin samun labarin gida baya samun numbers d'in dady gaba d'aya haka na momy, hatta fk yanxu ya daina samun wayar sa yasmeen ce kawai suke yin waya jefi-jefi ita kuwa ta fad'a masa basa ma Nigeria wannan shine abinda yk damun sa.



Aunty yasmeen kuwa tana can tare da masoyin ta farouq soyayya suke irin me tsaya wa a rai abinda kawai yk damun ta shine rashin jin lpyr y'ar k'anwar ta,kullum da ita tk kwana tk tashi gashi har yanxu da saura komawar su saboda farouq bai k'arasa karatun nasa, ganin da yayi ta sanya damuwa a ranta yayi mata alk'awarin zaiyi mata visa ko ita kad'ai ne taxo gida daga baya sai ta koma amma fa sai ta haihu don ciki ne da ita kusan six months (to aunty yasmeen Allah ya raba lpy dake da duk masu ciki musulmi)
