Pg41

"Kamal wai kuwa kun dai-daita da d'iya ta."?

Mama ta tanbaye shi lokacin da yazo gaishe ta, sunkuyar da kansa yai k'asa baya son ya fad'a mata gsky amma kuma bazai iya yi mata k'arya ba saboda ya riga ya saba babu abinda yk 6oye mata itace abokiyar shawarar sa sai kuma maryam k'anwar sa kfn rasuwar ta, don haka ya sanar da ita yanda suka yi da suhailat, babu shakkah ta tausayawa d'an ta saboda tafi kowa sanin halin sa muddin yace yana son abu to da iyakacin k'arfinsa yk yi bilhak'k'i sannan tunda tk dashi bata ta6a jin yace yau yana son wata yarinya ba sai akan Maryam shi yasa tayi farinciki sosae amma dai dukda haka soyayyar da tk yiwa d'an ta baxata hana ta yiwa Suhailat adalci ba tunda itama tana da y'anci, tayi masa addu'a da ptn alkhairi.

Ckn kwana 2 ya fara shirin tafiya kano don ya nemowa suhailat doctor fahad ya danne xuciyar sa zai iya sadaukar mata da farincikin sa, don yafi so ya ganta ckn kwanciyar hankali dukda cewa xuciyar sa nayi masa d'aci da suya a duk lokacin da ya tuna cewa Maryam tana da Wanda tk so bashi ba amma haka yk daure wa, daga mama babu Wanda yasan abinda yk faruwa don bai fad'a wa kowa ba haka itama Maman babu Wanda ta fad'a wa tsakanin ta dasu addu'a Allah ya za6a musu abinda yafi alkhairi  don duk su 2n babu bare a wajen ta.



Suhailat kam cike tk da farinciki girma da darajar kamal suka k'aru a xuciyar ta ga wani tausayin sa da tk ji amma dukda haka baxata iya sadaukar da soyayyar fk ba muddin tasan zata same shi, hansatu ce ke tsokanar ta lokacin da suka fita kiwo ta juyo ta kalle ta kfn tace. "Adda Maryam yau kam naga kina shikin farinciki hala kun sasanta da hamma kamalu." Murmushi Suhailat tayi ckn nishad'i tana wulla sandar dk hannun ta tana cafewa sannan tace. "Hansatu kenan ni fa akwai Wanda nk so ba y kamal ba yana can garin mu."


Sororo hansatu ta tsaya tana kallon Suhailat bakinta a bud'e cike da mamaki tace." Lallai adda Maryam yanxu har akwai Wanda zaifi hamma kamalu had'u wa da burgewa za kishe wai kai baka sonshi."? 

Dariya sosae Suhailat tayi kfn tace. "To ke banda abinki hansatu wa ya fad'a miki shi so yana duba had'u wa ko burgewa? Ai ko mahaukaci xuciyar ka ta xa6a haka zaka dinga ganin sa kamar yafi kowa na duniya kyau da had'u wa, balle ni kuma Wanda nk so, ko ke kk ganshi zaki ce ya kai k'arshe wajen had'u wa."

"Kina nufin yafi hamma kamalu." Hansatu ta fad'a tana rik'e baki, Suhailat ta kalle ta kfn tace. "Ni dai ban fad'a ba amma ki bari idan kin ganshi da kanki zaki banbance." 

Shiru hansatu tayi tana girgixa kai tare da yaba wauta irin ta Maryam yanxu ace kamar hamma kamalu zuk'ek'en saurayi mai ji da kud'i, ilimi, wayewa, ga kyau amma adda Maryam tace wai tana da Wanda yafi shi, gsky da ita hamma kamalu yace yana so babu abinda zai hana ta aure shi don ya had'a komai, saurin kawar da wannan tunanin tayi saboda tasan koda ita xata so shi shi ba zai so taba ita da ta tashi a k'auye dukda cewa akwai makarantu sai dai bazaka ta6a had'a su da na ckn binni ba.



Ta6a ta suhailat tayi ganin tayi nisa ckn tunani ta dubeta a tsanake kfn tace. "Ke kuma wanne irin tunani kk haka." Hmm ke dai bari adda Maryam ai tunani ne mara amfani kawai ke dai mu bar maganar bani labarin saurayin naki na can."

Aikuwa an ta6owa suhailat inda yk mata inda yk mata k'aik'ayi nan ta dinga zai yana wa hansatu labarin fk kyawawan halayensa da yanda yk nuna mata k'auna, ita kanta bata san haka tk son fk ba sai yanxu da yayi mata nisa amma tana ji a ranta duk inda yk zaizo gareta.

Har suka dawo daga wajen kiwo hirar fk tk yiwa hansatu, ita kanta hansatu ta fahimci adda Maryam tayi nisa a soyayyar dofta yanda taji tana fad'a.

_________________________Momy tana can hannun sojojin hjy Sara suna bata gwale-gwale hjy Sara ta basu umarnin kada su sassauta wa momy muddin bata fad'i inda beauty tk ba, momy ta rame ta k'anjame abinci ma sau d'aya suke bata a rana tunda tk bata ta6a shiga mawuyacin hali irin wannan ba, da ace tasan inda matsiyaciyar yarinyar nan tk da da kanta zata je ta d'auko musu ita duk ma abinda zasu yi mata suyi mata, ta hanga ta hango babu inda yarinyar ta sani batada wata k'awa ko aminiya balle tace ko itace ta 6oye ta, tayi kuka tayi rantsuwa tayi magiya amma duk sun k'i yadda da ita ta lura wad'annan mutanen basu san annabi ya faku ba aikin da aka basu yafi komai muhimmanci a gurin su, gashi bata da Wanda zai nemi inda tk balle a k'wace ta yaran ta duk basa kusa da ita, kubra ce kawai ita kuma ta guje ta bama tasan inda tk ba, "wayyo rayuwa na shiga uku na cuci kaina" ta fad'a lokacin da wani daga ckn sojojin ya kai mata mahangur6a a baki take bakinta da hanci suka fashe suna fita da jini idan ana sa bawa da wahala a ckn y'an kwanakin nan yakamata ace momy ta saba da ita saboda kullum sai an bata gwale-gwale sau uku byn horon yunwa da suke mata ga rashin wanka yau kusan sati bata watsa ruwa a jikinta ba, da wannan wahalar da tk sha a hannun k'atti ta k'wammace zama a k'ark'ashin kaltume tana yi mata bauta koba komai baxata rasa abinda zata ci ba, tunda tk a rayuwa bata ta6a jin wani abu ya d'arsu a xuciyar ta me Kama da nadama ba sai yanxu da tk shan gwabza.(kowa ya tuba don wuya ba lada su momy ansha wuya)

____________________Doctor fk ya fara binciken inda zai samu dangin mahaifin sa ta hanyar bin duk wasu k'auyuka da suke garin Adamawa kamar mayobalwa  yana shiga yana yawo tare da nuna hoton mahaifin sa, baiyi tunanin abun zai ja masa lokaci sosae ba amma sai gashi ya shafe kusan sati guda ba tare da ya samu wani k'wak'k'waran labari ba, duk Wanda ya tunkara da hoton sai suce basu sanshi ba, babban abinda yafi tayar masa da hankali shine bai san sunan k'auyen mahaifin nasa ba, sannan baisan sunan koda mutum d'aya daga ckn y'an uwan mahaifin nasa ba sai k'anwar sa wacce ta rasu,abinda zai iya tuna wa kawai shine ammy ta sanar dashi mahaifin nasa bafulatani ne hakan tasa ya fara bi duk wata ruga da yaji labarin fulani ne a ciki, dukda haka bai samu wani information akan mahaifin sa ba, shi kam ya d'auki alk'awarin muddin a ckn garin Adamawa dangin mahaifin sa suke ko a ina ne saiya nemo su, zai shiga lungu da sak'o k'auye da birni don ganin yasamo asalin dangin sa.



Babban abinda yk sanyaya mishi gwiwa akan lamarin shine yawan tunanin masoyiyar sa suhailat har yau ba'a san inda tk ba wannan tunanin yana dakusar masa da farinciki yana yawan train d'in number ta amma kullum amsa d'aya ce a kashe, gashi baya samun y Sadeeq da aunty yasmeen kwata-kwata balle ya sanar dasu abinda yk faruwa gashi dady ya zama wani jani talau sai yanda akayi dashi, wad'annan abubuwa guda biyu sune suka taru suka maida fk wani iri kullum ckn damuwa ba shi da wani sukuni ya rame yayi baki duk kyan nan nasa ya ragu saboda baya ckn nutsuwar sa, yasa a ransa bazai ta6a koma wa gida ba sai ya samo dangin sa, da kuma masoyiyar sa xuciyar sa da jikin sa na fad'a masa ya kusa had'u wa da ita kuma duk lokacin da yayi mafarki yana ganin su a tare shi yasa ya saki jiki suhailat tana ckn Adamawa idan kuwa bata nan tabbas tana kusa dashi haka yk ji a ransa sai dai bai san inda zai nemo ta ba.


Hakan tasa ya nemi a bashi hutu a  wajen aikin sa, har zuwa lokacin da ya gama abinda yakaishi, basu bashi hutu ba sai ma k'arin girma da akayi masa yaxama likita me xaman kansa zai iya aiki a ko ina a fad'in Nigeria, nan suka nemi ya sanar dasu a garin da yk ya fad'a musu a take suka mallaka masa dank'areren gida a ckn tsakiyar garin jimeta tare da dukkan abinda zai iya buk'ata na aiki ko na gida, da wannan damar fk yayi amfani ya cigaba da neman dangin sa da kuma masoyiyar sa a gefe guda kuma yana taimaka wa mutane ta 6angaren aikin sa musamman y'an k'auyuka da Fulani saboda a ganin sa ta hakane kad'ai zai sa ya samu abinda yk so.




____________________kamal ya isa garin kano dukda cewa babu Wanda ya sani neman doctor ne ya kawo shi, lokacin da ya iso dare yayi don haka ya nemi hotel ya kwana da sassafe byn ya gama duk abinda yk ya d'auki hanyar zuwa hospital d'in kamar yanda Maryam tayi masa kwatance, sai dai yayi rashin sa'a domin kuwa likita d'aya ne a ciki yana duba patients, byn ya gabatar da kansa ya sanar dashi Wanda yk nema, shiru doctor annur yayi kfn yace. "To gsky doctor fahad guda 1 ne a wannan asibitin dukda baka fad'a min full name d'in sa ba ina nufin sunan mahaifin sa."

Shiru kamal yayi don shi sai yanxu ya tuna cewa Maryam sunan sa kawai ta fad'a masa amma bata fad'a masa sunan mahaifin sa ya dubi doctor annur yace. "Gsky ban san full name d'in sa ba amma don Allah ka had'a ni da wannan d'in idan ba shi bane kaga shikenan." Doctor annur yace. "Maganar gsky doctor fahad ya kwashe tsawon 1 month baya zuwa asibitin nan amma dai naji ana cewa wai promotion akayi masa shi yasa ya daina don yanxu haka ina da yak'inin baya ckn garin kano."....... �

Kuyi hak'uri kwana 2 shiru data is low

      (Ummu Mashkur).

Pg42 

Kamal yayi shiru yana tunanin abinda yakamata yayi doctor annur yace. "Ina ganin kawai kaje gidan su watak'ila kasamu cikakkyan bayanin inda xaka same shi." Sai yanxu wannan tunanin yaxo masa kuma dama Maryam ta bashi full adress na gidan su fahad d'in amma dukda haka saida doctor annur ya k'ara yi masa kwatance ya mik'a masa hannu sukayi musabaha yayi masa gdy ya tafi, baisha wahala wajen neman gidan su doctor ba, sai dai yayi rashin sa'a domin kuwa an fad'a masa fahad baya nan bai samu damar ganin ammy ba me gadi ne yk masa wannan bayanin, hakan tasa dole ya shirya komawa Adamawa cike da tunanin yanda zai fuskanci Maryam da wannan maganar, ta riga ta yadda dashi akan zai nemo mata masoyinta amma yanxu gashi zai dawo ba tare da wani k'wak'k'waran bayani ba, haka dai ya daure ya hau motar Adamawa suka d'au hanya.



______________________Momy fa ta gama shan gwabza da wahala duk ta molak'e duk Wanda ma ya santa sai yayi da gsk kfn ya iya gane ta, gaba d'aya ta fita daga haiyacinta lokacin da sojojin hjy Sara suka ga bata ckn haiyacinta ta kusa mutuwa sai suka je suka sanar da ita, ita kanta hjy Sara da ta ganta saida ta tsorata da yanda momy ta koma nan take ta basu umarnin suje su jefar da ita a k'ofar gidan ta da daddare don kar ta mutu a hannun su, haka suka bari saida dare ya tsala sannan suka kawo ta k'ofar gida suka yasar da ita a sume, sukayi tafiyar su, momy kam bata san Wanda yk kanta ba, don kuwa kwata-kwata bata ckn haiyacinta, anan ta kwana a k'ofar gida ckn halin rai kwakwai mutu kwakwai, da asuba ne wani mak'ocin su alhj bashir ya fito domin tfy masallaci yana xuwa zai wuce sai yaga mutum a kwance ckn mawuyacin hali ya duba sam bai gane ko wa cece ba abinda ya iya ganewa kawai macece kuma da ranta sai dai a hankali xuciyar ta tk harbawa da alamar tana gab da mutuwa, da sauri ya koma gidan sa yafi to da mota sannan ya nemi taimakon mutane masu tfy masallaci aka taimaka masa suka sanya ta a mota sannan aka wuce da ita private hospital don a binciki lpyr ta, suna zuwa emergency aka nufa da ita nan doctors suka rufu a kanta don ceto rayuwar ta, ckn ikon Allah sai gashi an samu nasara numfashin ta ya dai-dai ta, ta hanyar sanya mata na'urar dai-daita numfashi ta oxygen sannan suka cigaba da binciken lpyr jikinta.

An d'auki kimanin awa d'aya likitoci suna kanta kfn daga baya wani daga ckn likitocin ya fito hannun sa rik'e da handkerchief yana goge gumin dk kan face dn sa, ya dubi alhj bashir Wanda yk jira a waje yace. "Kabiyo ni office." Bai jira amsar sa ba yayi gaba, shima alhj bashir yabi byn sa cike da zullumi.

Byn sun shiga office d'in doctor imran ya dubi alhj bashir kfn yace. "Am maganar gasky alhj wannan baiwar Allah tana ckn mawuyacin hali irin Wanda yk da matuk'ar hatsari saboda a binciken da mukayi mun gano ta samu karaya hud'u hannuwa da kafafu sannan mun gano ta kamu da cutar paralysis, wato shanyewar 6arin jiki don haka dole xamu rik'e ta anan domin mu bata kulawa ta musamman don haka ga wannan kaje maza yanxu ka siyo domin a fara yi mata gashi." Ya mik'a wa alhj bashir wata takadda wacce ya cika ta da rubutu.


Jikin alhj bashir a sanyaye ya juya domin kuwa bashi da ko sisi a hannun sa duk sun k'are wajen yi mata gwaje-gwaje sannan daman shima kansa yana ckn halin rayuwa saboda karayar arxik'i da ta same shi shekara 2 da suka ga bata, a da shi d'in hamshak'in mai kud'i ne Wanda wannan dalilin ne yasa ya gina katafaren gida a unguwar don duk mutanen unguwar masu hali ne, alhj bashir mutumin kirki ne Allah ne kawai ya jarrabe shi da karayar arziki Wanda a wannan lokacin ya gane masoya da kuma mak'iyan sa, a halin yanxu matarsa ma neman juya masa baya tk saboda a cewar ta baxata iya rayuwar talauci ba, yaronsa ne yk d'an taimaka masa da duk abinda yk buk'ata kasancewar sa babban ma'aikaci, a halin yanxu babu abinda ya rage masa a duniya daga wannan gidan sai motar sa da yk hawa.


Haka ya fito daga ckn office d'in jikin sa a sanyaye lokacin gari har ya waye tunani yk ta yanda zai samu damar taimaka wa wannan baiwar Allah, dole yaje ya samu matarsa jamila su tattauna yanda zasuyi dukda yasan da wuya ta amince su taimaka amma babu yanda zaiyi don ita d'in ce dai abokiyar shawarar tasa.



Lokacin da yaxo gidan ya tarar dasu hankalin su a tashe sakamakon rashin dawowar sa tun asuba nan yayi musu bayanin abinda yk faruwa, jamila ta dube shi ckn 6acin rai kfn tace. "Amma dai gsky alhj baka kyau ta mana ba yanxu tsakani da Allah daga ganin mace a kwance sai kawai ka wani d'auke ta ka kaita asibiti alhalin muna da buk'ata, kai kad'ai ne sarkin taimako dubi yanda yaran ka suke ckn buk'atar abinda zasu ci yau mun tashi ko abinci babu amma ka tafi ka kashe d'an kud'in naka a banxa."  Alhj bashir ya dubi matar sa cike da takaici kfn yace. " Wai jamila yaushe zaki zama me tausayi ne? Ko don baki ga halin da matar tk ciki ba, to kisani ku kuna da lpyr da har zaku ji yunwa ita kam batada buk'atar abinci a wannan halin da tk ciki addu'a da kuma lpy su kad'ai ne abinda tk buk'ata, idan har mutum bai daina hangen Wanda yafi shi ba to bazai ta6a godewa Allah ba, ni kam ngd wa Allah da ya bani lpy da kuma tawakkali, kuma indai ina da abinda zan taimaka wa me buk'atar taimako na to ba zan fasa ba har byn raina." Ya k'arasa maganar ckn 6acin rai, jikin jamila yai sanyi ckn nadama tace. "Kayi hak'uri alhj yanxu bari na shirya muje gurin ta." Ajiyar xuciya alhj bashir ya sauke kfn yace."Yanxu babban abinda yk damu na shine yanda zan samu kud'in da zan siyi wad'annan abubuwan da aka rubuta." Ya nuna mata takaddar dk hannun sa,


Kar6a tayi tare da dubawa taga mak'udan kud'in da aka rubuta ajiki ckn damuwa tace. "Gsky alhj bamu da hanyar samun wad'annan kud'in sai dai mu jira matar ta farfad'o ko zata iya fad'a mana inda dangin ta suke mu sanar dasu." Alhj bashir yace.''Na gamsu da wannan shawarar amma ki sani muddin bata iya fad'a mana komai da ya shafi y'an uwa ko dangin ta ba, nayi alk'awarin zan saida motata ayi mata duk abinda za'a yi mata." Ckn mamaki jamila ta kalli mijinta, tabbas xuciyar ta bata so hakan ba amma saboda tasan halinsa yasa tayi shiru don tasan duk abinda zata fad'a masa bazai yadda ba.


"To yanxu me zamu barwa yara." Ta fad'a tana kallon sa, kfn yayi magana suka ji k'arar mota ckn minti 1 aka fara shigowa da kayan abinci Wanda sako ne daga wajen d'an alhj bashir, ya dubeta ckn fara'a kfn yace. "Kin gani ko Allah yana tare damu kuma bai manta damu ba ke dai inaso ki zama mai taimaka wa duk Wanda yk buk'atar taimakon ki muddin kina da halin yin haka." Murmushi jamila tayi tare da d'aga kai.




To lokacin da sukaje asibitin momy ta farfad'o sai dai tana ckn rad'ad'in ciwo duk jikin ta ciwo yk mata abinda ta lura dashi kuma shine 6arin jikinta d'aya ya daina aiki ta yunk'ura da niyyar ta tashi zaune, ai bata san lokacin da ta zabga wata uwar k'ara tare da rikitowa k'asa ba saboda tsabar rad'ad'i da azaba a sume ta fad'o daga kan gadon, a dai-dai wannan lokacin ne alhj bashir da matarsa jamila harma da doctor imran suka shigo ckn d'akin sakamakon wannan gigitacciyar k'arar da ta saki, ckn sauri doctor imran da alhj bashir suka kamata suka maida ita bisa gadon, jamila ta matso ckn sanyin jiki tana lek'a fuskar matar saida ta k'ura mata ido tsawon minti 10 sannan ta iya gane mak'ociyar ta ce rabi, ckn matsanancin tashin hankali jamila tace."na shiga 3 yanxu rabi kece kk koma haka. "?

Alhj bashir da doctor imran suka kalleta lokaci 1 kfn likitan yace." Ku bani guri na duba ta."

Jikin su a sanyaye suka fita daga d'akin kowa da abinda yk sak'awa a ransa, alhj bashir ya dubi matarsa jamila byn sun fita yace. "Dama kin Santa ne."?

Jamila ta goge k'wallar da ta zubo mata, saboda ita kam ta k'ara yin imani da Allah saboda yanda taga hjy rabi y'ar k'walisa mai ji da kanta, yanxu dubi yanda Allah ya maida ita, lallai mutum ba'a bakin komai yk ba.

Ta dubi mijinta ckn sheshshekar kuka tace." Alhj hjy rabi ce fa matar alhj Ibrahim mak'ocin mu." Ckn tsananin mamaki yace. "Alhj Ibrahim mu'azzam dai na kusa damu."


D'aga kai jamila tayi tana share hawaye, alhj bashir yace. "Abinda yakamata kawai shine bari naje na shaida masa abinda yk faruwa."  Jamila tace. "To amma naji ance  yayi aure kuma matar bata barinsa ya fito ko ina."

"Aikuwa dole a sanar masa ke ki zauna ko za'a buk'aci wani abun ni bari naje." Alhj bashir ya fad'a tare da juya wa yana k'ok'arin fita.


To byn alhj bashir yaje yayi sallama da dady, shiru bai fito ba, tsawon minti 30 ya saki  aika  yaro, saiga kaltume ta fito, da tsananin mamaki alhj bashir yk binta da kallo, kfn yayi magana tace. "Mlm kaine kk son ganin miji na."?  Kansa kawai ya iya d'aga wa saboda tsananin mamaki, kaltume ta cigaba." To me yk tafe dakai saboda yanxu nice a madadin sa."


Alhj bashir yace."To ikon Allah, to amma idan ya kasance shi d'in nk buk'atar gani fa."  Tsaki kaltume taja kfn ta juya da niyyar koma wa, ganin zata tafi yasa alhj bashir yace. "Hjy minti 2 don Allah." Bata juyo ba sai dai ta tsaya sannan tace. "Ina jinka." Alhj bashir yace.

 "Na tsinci matarsa ne hjy rabi ckn mawuyacin hali yanxu haka tana asibiti." Kaltume ta juyo ckn zaro ido tace. "Babu ruwa na da abinda ya same ta ko zai same ta kuma  kai da ka kaita asibiti kaine kaga zaka iya don ni ko alhj bazan sanar wa wannan labarin ba."


Tunda alhj bashir yk ganin mata marasa kunya bai ta6a had'u wa da wadda ta tsare shi da ido tana fad'a masa maganganu ba sai wannan lallai rashin kunyar tata ta shahara kfn yayi wani yunkuri ta juya ta barshi nan tsaye.





Kamal ya koma Adamawa ya sanar da suhailat duk abinda ya faru tayi mamaki sosae da taji wai fk baya nan tunanin ta ina ya tafi bai ta6a bata labarin zaiyi wata tfy ba, "ko dai ya daina sona ya samu wacce tafi ni." Wata xuciyar ta fad'a mata yayinda wani 6angaren ke k'ara tuna mata tsantsar soyayyar da yk mata, tasani da wuya ace doctor ya juya mata baya  saboda irin soyayyar da yk nuna mata, tabbas sai dai idan akwai wani babban dalili, amma haka zata hak'ura ta cigaba da rok'on Allah ya had'a ta da masoyin ta.


Haka ta cigaba da rayuwa ckn y'an uwanta cike da k'auna da soyayya matsalar ta d'aya yawan tunanin doctor, dukda y kamal yana d'ebe mata kewa ta hanyar yi mata yawan maganar doctor saboda ya gane indai kana son kuyi hira me tsayi da ita to kayi mata maganar fk, wannan dabarar da kamal yayi ya sanya suhailat ta saki jiki dashi idan kaga suna hira da dariya kamar basu ba, da wannan iyayen su suka yi la'akari suka tattauna a tsakanin su a take suka sanya ranar auren kamal da Maryam wata 2 don su a ganin su yaran sun gama fahimtar junan su........

Mama ta samu yayyen ta tayi musu bayanin Suhailat fa tana da Wanda tk so a can kanon." 

Baffah hamxa yace. "Waye shi Wanda tk so d'in, kuma me yasa bai nemi inda tk ba indai da gsk yk sonta." 

Mama tace. "Wata k'ila bai San tana nan ba don lokacin da ta sanar masa kamal har can yaje amma bai same shi ba."

Ckn 6acin rai baffah haladu yace. "Kaji wani shirme don Allah, yanxu ke banda abinki indo jiran sa zamuyi tayi har sai ya nemi inda tk yaxo? Wa ya sani koma ya manta da ita, ke koma bai manta da ita ba mu ba zamu d'auki d'iyar mu mu bawa kad'o ba wad'anda basu iya rik'e aure ba yanxu dubi irin kuskuren da hamma kabiru yayi ya d'auki dije ya bada ita ga kad'o yarinyar nan har ta rasu bataji dad'in rayuwar auren ba, to yanxu so kk itama mu jefa ta irin wannan rayuwar? Koda kamalu bai ce yana sonta ba bazamu bawa bare d'iyar mu ba." 


Ckn sanyin jiki mama tace ba haka nk nufi ba hamma  kawai dai ina tausayawa yarinyar ne saboda batayi rayuwa me dad'i a gidan mahaifin ta ba yakamata ace ta auri Wanda tk so tayi rayuwar aure me dad'i, sannan hamma ba kowanne bahaushe ne baya rik'e aure ba ka manta nima bahaushen nk aure? Sannan itama adda dije bata da matsala da mijinta kishiyar ta ce me matsalar, amma dai tunda kunce haka shikenan ni addu'a ce kawai tawa Allah yasa hakan shine mafi alkhairi. " suka amsa da "ameen."


To shima kamal da yaji maganar rasa yanda zai kar6eta yayi saboda shi har ga Allah yana k'aunar Maryam amma zai iya hak'ura da ita muddin hakan zai samar mata da farinciki ya kasa tun karar kowa da maganar har saida mama ta kirashi tayi masa magana ganin lokaci yana tfy basu fara wani shiri ba tunda abun yazo musu on expected, lokacin da ta tuntu6eshi da maganar duban ta yayi sannan yace. "Mama wlh har yanxu na kasa tun karar Maryam da wannan maganar kuma nasan bata sani ba gani nk kamar ban kyau ta mata sannan kamar ban cika mata alk'awarin da na d'aukar mata ba."

Mama ta dafa kansa kfn tace. "Karka damu yaro na baka karya alk'awarin da kayi mata ba, saboda iyayen ku sun yanke hukuncin cewa koda yazo ba zasu bawa bare d'iyar su ba alhalin ga d'an gida yana so, abinda kawai nk so da kai shine ka dage da addu'a Allah ya za6a muku abinda yafi alkhairi da kai da ita nima zan taya ku,kuma naji dad'i da kaxamo ba son kansa ba inda wani ne ya samu wannan damar ba zai damu da halin da yarinyar zata kasance ba, yanxu inaso kaje ka same ta ku tattauna akan lamarin sannan kada ka manta da yawan yin addu'a."


"To mama insha'allah zanyi amfani da shawarar ki ina gdy da wannan k'warin gwiwa d kk bani."

Ya k'arasa maganar yana k'ok'arin mik'ewa mama tace."ba komai yaro na Allah yai muku albarka. " "ameen." Ya fad'a yana k'ok'arin tfy.


To a kwana a tashi ba wuya har anci tsawon wata guda da sanya ranar, amma har yanxu suhailat bata sani ba don kamal ya kasa sanar da ita, su kam iyayen shirye-shiryen su kawai sukeyi don har anfara gyara amare Maryam da hansatu wacce za'a had'a da nata ayi lokaci 1 itama kanta hansatun bata san abinda ake shirya wa akan su ba, sai dai kawai suga an basu magani an tsare su sai sun shanye, ita kam suhailat bata ta6a tunanin wani abu da xuciya d'aya tk kar6ar duk abinda aka bata, don mama ce mata tayi maganin sanyi ne ake bata.





Suhailat bata san da wannan maganar ba sai ana saura sati 2 biki, innar hansatu ce ta sanya d'iyar ta a d'aki ta fad'a mata dukda ance ba'a son su sani sai dai ta gargad'e ta akan kada ta fadawa kowa, hansatu kam ta shiga ckn tashin hankali saboda jin Wanda za'a aura mata wai ja'e d'an abokin baffan ta, Sam bata k'aunar sa kuma shima tasan ba lallai yana sonta ba, ta samu Suhailat da k'yar suka yi dabara suka fita saboda yanxu ba'a barinsu suje ki ina.



Sun ta6a y'ar tfy a ckn daji kfn su samu guri su zauna, tun kfn hansatu ta fara magana suhailat tace. "Hansatu yau na ganki ckn damuwa kuma tunda kk ce zamuyi magana nk jin gaba na yana fad'uwa don Allah ki fad'a min me yk damun ki."?


Hansatu ta goge hawayen da suka fara zuba ta dubi suhailat ckn ido sannan tace." Yau saura sati 2 auren mu ni da ke adda Maryam. "

Ckn tsananin mamaki da rud'ani Suhailat tace."wai me kk fad'a saura sati biyu auren mu kamar yaya ke wanene yk fad'a miki. "?

" innata ce kuma nasan babu wasa tsokana a tsakanin mu ni kam adda na shiga 3 wlh bana son ja'e wai kuma shine za'a aura min, ke gwanda ke hamma kamalu bashi da matsala kuma yana sonki ni kam bani da tabbacin ja'e yana sona me yasa baffah zaiyi min haka. "Ta dafe kai tare da fashewa da kuka.


Ita kam suhailat tamkar wata gunki ta zama a gurin don ta kasa ko motsi saboda tsananin tashin hankali, wai sai yaushe ne zata yi rayuwa ckn jin dad'i? Allah y sani tana tausayin kamal kuma tana ganin girmansa amma soyayya 1 ce kuma tana wajen fk d'in ta kuma babu wani d'a namiji a duniya da zata iya yi masa rabin soyayyar da tk wa fk. 

Ajiyar xuciya ta sauke lokacin da taji hansatu ta dafa ta tana goge mata hawayen da ita kanta bata san lokacin da ya fito ba, hansatu tace." Haba adda Maryam meye kuma na kuka? Nayi tunanin ni kad'ai kuka ya kama nayi tsammanin kece zaki zamto me lallashi na, amma kash sai gashi kema lallashin kk buk'ata." Suhailat ta dubi hansatu ckn damuwa kfn tace. "Bari na tanbaye ki hansatu shin kin ta6a soyayya."? Girgixa kai hansatu tayi alamar a'a suhailat ta cigaba." To bazaki ta6a fahimtar abinda nk ji a ckn xuciya ta ba, domin shi so ba ruwansa da kyau ko muni kud'i ko talauci hankali ko hauka duk Wanda Allah ya jarabce shi da son wani ko wata ba wad'annan abubuwa yk dubawa ba indai soyayyar ta gsky ce shi yasa nk tausayin y kamal saboda nasan irin abinda yk ji, hansatu wlh idan har aka aura min y kamal an aura masa gangar jiki na xuciya ta kuwa tana gurin doctor."


Hansatu ta d'anyi shiru tana jajanta lamarin ckn ranta lallai shi so babu ruwansa amma ba don haka ba menene hamma kamalu bashida shi da har za'a ki shi, ta dubi suhailat ckn tausayawa don itama yanxu ta fara jin wani abu na soyayya ga hamma kamalu don ma tasan baxata ta6a samun sa ba shi yasa ma baxata bari kowa ya gane ba, "tashi mu tafi kada a neme mu kinsan yanxu tsaron mu akeyi ba'a son muna fita yawo."


Batayi magana ba haka ta mik'e jiki babu kwari suka fara tfy a ranta tana zargin kamal da k'in fad'a mata abinda yk faruwa tunda tasan babu yanda za'a yi ace shima bai san abinda yk faruwa ba musamman kwanan nan ta lura yana yawan tafiye-tafiye dukda baya fad'a mata inda yk zuwa.


Da wannan tashin hankalin suka dawo gida lokacin da suka shigo gidan gaba d'aya mutanen gurin suka bisu da kallo tare da had'a bakuna wajen tanbayar "daga ina kuke." Ckn mamaki Suhailat da hansatu suka had'a ido kenan yanxu basu da damar fita sai ansan inda suka je, hansatu ce tabada amsa da cewa muna byn gari." Ita kam suhailat gaba tayi ta nufi d'akin inna kakarta, kamar yanda ta saba yau ma tana rik'e da carbi tana lazimi suhailat ta wuce ta ta kwanta bisa gadon k'arfen inna, inna ta juyo ta kalli suhailat da alamun akwai abinda yk damun ta tace. "Maryama lpyr ki k'alau kuwa naganki wani iri."? Suhailat batayi mata magana ba saboda a halin yanxu kowa ma haushin sa tk ji sannan idan tayi magana kukan da tk 6oyewa zai bqiyana, inna ta yunk'ura  a hankali ta isa wajen Suhailat ta zauna a gefen gadon tare da d'ago kan Suhailat ta fara lallashinta saboda ta lura kuka tk sosae, da k'yar ta samu tayi shiru sai ajiyar xuciya tk sauke wa, inna tace." Kiyi hak'uri maryama nasan dalilin kukan ki, kin samu labarin saura sati 2 a d'aura auren ki da kamalu ko, to inaso ki kwantar da hankalin ki ki kar6i wannan auren da daraja insha'allah bazaki yi nadamar hakan ba. " 


Ckn kuka Suhailat tace. "Inna na hak'ura kuma na yadda da hukuncin ku a kaina xuciya ta ce tk min zafi a duk lokacin da na tuna ba fahad ne mijina ba, nasan y kamal d'an uwa na ne kuma bana k'inshi amma gsky ba nayi masa soyayya irin ta aure, inna ku bani dama naje da kaina na nemo fahad idan har ban same shi ba zan hak'ura na kar6i kamal a matsayin mijin da Allah ya za6a min."


Inna tayi shiru don ta tuna irin wannan ta ta6a faruwa shekaru kusan 30 da suka wuce da d'anta kabiru akwai wacce suke so su aura masa y'ar uwarsa amma shi kuma akwai wacce yk so daban daga k'arshe yaje ya auri wacce yk so d'in Wanda dalilin hakan yasa inna ta tsani matar daga k'arshe tayiwa d'anta kiranye ya dawo gareta Wanda tun lokacin da ya dawo baiyi lpy ba kuma har ya koma ga mahaliccinsa maganar sa 1 ce matarsa da d'an sa Wanda basu san inda zasu nemo su ba takanyi nadama mara adadi a duk lokacin da ta tuna da wannan abun.

Inna ta sauke ajiyar xuciya byn ta tuna da wannan abun da ya faru maganar gsky baxata so a k'ara kwatanta irin sa ba, ta goge hawayen ta tare da mik'e wa a hankali tk takawa har ta isa wajen wata tsohuwar adaka ta bud'e ta d'auko tarin hotu na masu yawa Wanda daga ganin su kasan ba na yanxu bane don ko kala basu da ita, wajen suhailat ta dawo ta mik'a mata wani photo , Suhailat ta tsurawa hoton ido Inna tace. "Wannan itace Khadija mahaifiyar ki yarinya me ladabi da biyayya har ta koma ga mahaliccinta bata ta6a sa6a min ba a matsayi na na mahaifiyar ta."

Ta k'ara mik'a mata wani photo Wanda saida ta mik'e tsaye tana kallon sa wani mutum ne da wata mata da alama matarsa ce har da d'an su a tsakiya, Suhailat ta tsurawa matar ido don gani tk kamar ta ta6a ganin ta a wani waje, inna tace. "Wannan shine d'ana kabiru da matarsa da kuma dansa, abinda yk shirin faruwa dk yanxu ya ta6a faruwa dashi a baya, bana son tuna wannan labarin maryama don nice babbar me laifi kuma yanxu na dad'e da yin nadama dukda nasan baxata yi min amfani ba, amma bana so a sake yin irin kuskuren da na tafka." Ita kam suhailat shiru tayi idon ta nakan wannan matar ta jikin photo kallon sani tk mata amma ta kasa tuna a inda tasan ta, ganin ta kasa gane wa yasa ta mik'a wa inna hoton sai dai ta rik'e na mahaifiyar ta, ta dubi inna ckn d'an murmushi kfn tace."naji dad'i da nasamu hoton umma ta a nan kuma inna daga yanxu na d'auki alk'awarin baxan k'ara nuna rashin amince wa ta da auren y kamal ba zanyi koyi da mahaifiya ta zan kasance me biyayya ga dukkan umarnin ku."


Ckn jin dad'i inna ta rungume suhailat tana kukan farinciki koba komai maryama ta tuna mata d'iyar ta dije.

A Hankali Abubakar Sadeeq ya bud'e murfin motar ya fito yana kallon yanda gidan su ya dawo ko gate man babu, alhj bashir ya k'arasa wajen sa saboda ya gane Sadeeq ne dan alhj Ibrahim har k'asa Sadeeq ya tsugunna ya gaida alhj bashir, ya amsa ckn fara'a tare da fad'in. "Amma baka garin nan ko." Sadeeq yace."eh wlh ina can Canada yanxu ma mafarki nayi gida ba lpy shi yasa nazo nagani tunda bana samun number kowa." Alhj bashir yace. "Tabbas gidan ku ba lpy ba saboda mahaifiyar ku tana can rai a hannun Allah shi kuma mahaifin ku ya auri wata mata mara imani ta juyar masa da tunanin sa babu Wanda yk jin maganar sa sai ita, yanxu ma haka naxo ne akan maganar rashin lpyr mahaifiyar ku wacce tk can kwance a asibiti amma matar nan ta hana mu had'u da alhj haka ta gama yi min rashin kunya tai tafiyar ta, Allah ne ma ya tak'aita min wahala da kazo." Ckn matsanancin tashin hankali y Sadeeq yace. "Yanxu wanne asibitin momy tk."?

Pg44 & pg45

Double pages

Lokacin da suka isa momy ta samu bacci sakamakon allurar da akayi mata, hankalin y sadeeq ba k'aramin tashi yayi musamman yanda yaga mahaifiyar sa ta koma tamkar ba ita ba, shi ya biya duk wani abun da ake buk'ata a take aka shiga da ita wajen gashi, yayi wa alhj bashir da matarsa jamila gdy mai yawa sannan yayi musu alkhairi dukda alhj bashir yaki kar6a amma saida ya matsa masa  ya kar6a saboda ya samu labarin karayar arxikin da ta samu alhj bashir d'in, byn ya tabbatar an shiga da ita d'akin gashi, sai ya hau motar sa ya nufi gida don yaje ya sanar da mahaifin sa abinda yk faruwa, sannan yaga wacce isashshiyar ce tk juya dady kamar waina.



Lokacin da ya isa gidan bai tsaya knocking ba yai parking a waje, ya tako da k'afar sa ya bud'e gate d'in tare da kutsa kansa ciki, tun a farfajiyar gidan ya fahimci an samu sauye-sauye saboda wasu k'atti ya gani suna ta faman zirga-zirga kowa da aikin da yk yi wasu na shara wasu na mopping kai harda masu girki, y Sadeeq bai k'ara tsinkewa da al'amarin ba saida ya shiga babban parlourn, subhanallah manyan baligan maza majiya k'arfi ya gani tare da wata mata wacce bai gane ko wacece ce ba ita d'aya a ckn su suna buga lido runtse idon sa yayi tare da ambaton sunan Allah ckn tashin hankali.


Wanda wannan maganar itace tajawo hankalin kaltume da sauran gardawan da tk tare dasu suka juyo a tare suka kafe shi da ido, sai a lokacin sadeeq ya bud'e idon sa, ckn tsananin mamaki yk bin kaltume da kallo musamman yanda yaga irin shigar da tayi ta zauna ckn maza, ganin itama ta kafeshi da ido yai saurin d'auke nasa idon cike da mamakin yanda ta samu sake haka, yana ckn wannan tunanin yaji muryar d'aya daga ckn samarin nan yace. "Ya hjy wannan kolon fa ko shima d'an hannu ne ya wani xo ya tsare mu da ido."?  Kaltume tayi wani shu'umin murmushi kfn tace." No wannan fa yaron me house ne yanxu ya dawo. " wani daga ckn su yace."Ah kice ma d'an mu ne, kinsan dole mu yiwa me house kara."  Ckn  zafin nama da takaici Sadeeq ya nufi inda kaltume tk tsaye daga ita sai singlet da theree quater ya d'aga hannu zai bata mari yaji an rik'e hannun sa ta baya, yana juyo wa yaga dady tsaye ransa a 6ace, Sadeeq yace."Sannu dady ashe ka fito. " dady baiyi masa magana ba ya juya da nufin barin gurin, ckn tsananin mamaki da tashin hankali sadeeq yabi byn dady yasha gabansa kfn yace. "Dady wai baka ganeni ba, nine fa d'an ka abubakar, na lura kamar ba'a haiyacin ka kk ba." Dady ya juyo ya k'ura masa ido kamar mai son tuna wani abu,  kaltume tayi saurin xuwa gaban dady ganin kamar yana so ya gane d'ansa tace. "Alhj wannan fa shine Wanda ya shigo har ckn gidan ka ba tare da izini ba kuma ya nemi ya keta min rigar mutunci don kawai nayi masa magana, kada ka yadda dashi ba kada wani d'a abubakar." 

Ta k'arasa maganar tana kallon sadeeq tare da yi masa murmushin mugunta, dady ya kalleta  ckn kulawa kfn yace. "Na sani bani da d'a kawai so yk ya yaudare ni, don haka ki fad'a masa yai gaggawar barmin gida tun kfn na d'auki mataki."


 Tsan-tsar mamaki da al'ajabi gami da tausayin dady ne suka sanya Sadeeq hawaye, lallai akwai babban aiki a a kansa dady ne ya zama haka? Mutum me zafi da isa a gidan sa, shin kaltume itace matar da dady ya aura? Shin Ina suhailat da kubra sk? Wad'annan tanbayoyin da bashi da amsar su sune ke bijuro masa.

Kaltume tayi masa wani kallo na raini kfn tace. "To kaji sak'on me house da kunnen ka idan kuma ba haka ba yanxu na nuna maka isa ta ta matar gidan." Sadeeq baibi ta kanta ba ya k'arasa wajen mahaifin sa Wanda yk k'ok'arin barin parlourn ya tsugunna har k'asa sannan ya fara magana ckn muryar kuka.


"Dady ka dawo ckn haiyacin ka don Allah, ni d'anka ne, kuma byn ni kana da wandansu y'ay'an, shin ka manta yasmeen da isah ga kubra da suhailat duk mu d'in yaran ka ne wad'anda kk nuna mana soyayya a baya, don Allah ka tuna."

Dady ya dafe kansa da yk sara masa ba tare da yayi wa y sadeeq magana ba ya wuce d'akin sa saboda wani masifaffen jiri da yk kwasar shi, sadeeq ya durk'ushe a gurin yana gunjin kuka tunda yk bai ta6a shiga ckn tashin hankali irin wannan ba, ga mahaifiyar sa can a kwance babu mataimaki sai Allah sannan ga mahaifin sa ckn halin juyewar tunani wannan na nuna masa cewa dady baisan abinda yk yi ba, a halin yanxu, bai ta6a tunanin akwai wani abu a duniya da zaisa uba ya manta da y'ay'an sa.

Kaltume ta kalli abokan shashancin tace."Zaku iya tfy zuwa anjima saboda inaso nayi magana ta sirri da wannan yaron." Ta k'arasa maganar tana nuna y sadeeq, abokan nata suka had'a baki wajen fad'in. "Yeah sai hjy kall munsan ba kyayin tunanin banxa." Suna fad'ar hakan suka bar parlourn, kaltume tayi wani shu'umin murmushi tana kallon yanda sadeeq ya dafe kai a durk'ushe yana gurshek'en kuka.

Ta tako a hankali ckn gadara da isa ta tsaya akan sadeeq saida ta k'are masa kallo ba tare da yasan taxo kansa ba sannan ckn jin nuna ita d'in wata aba ce tace."Abubakar kk kowa? To koma dai wacce tsiya ce kaga yanda mahaifin ka ya dawo a tafin hannu na ko."??  Bai iya d'ago kansa ba balle ya bata amsa tayi dariya me sauti kfn ta cigaba da fad'in.

"Nasan kana ckn duhu kuma kana buk'atar wasu amsoshin tanbayoyi wad'anda a halin yanxu babu me baka amsar su sai ni." Ta fad'a tana nuna k'irjinta, sai a lokacin ya iya d'ago rinannun idonsa ya kalleta sai dai yakasa cewa komai, ganin haka yasa kaltume yin wani murmushin mugunta kfn tace. "Ina da sharad'i kfn na baka labarin abinda ya faru kasani ba wani abu ne yasa na tarwatsa wannan gidan ba sai burin duniya son kud'i shine abinda yasa na tarwatsa Ku,saboda na lura muddin banyi hakan ba, zan rayu ne a k'ark'ashin uwa da ubanka ina yi musu hidima suna biya na abinda bai taka kara ya karya ba." Zura mata ido kawai sadeeq yayi yana kallo gami da mamakin kalamanta, kfn yayi wani tunani yaji tana fad'in. " ka wani k'walalo min ido hala kana mamakin abinda na fad'a maka? To idan ma mamakin kk sai ka daina don zakaji abinda yafi Wanda na fad'a maka." Tana gama fad'ar haka ta juya ixuwa inda fridge yk ta d'auko lemuka sannan ta samu guri a d'aya daga ckn kujerun parlourn ta zauna, kfn ta juya ta kalli inda yk durk'ushe a gadarance tace."idan kana buk'atar jin abinda yk faruwa da labarin inda uwarka da y'an uwanka sk zaka iya zuwa nan." ta juya tare da zuba lemo a ckn cup ta fara sipping a hankali.



Bashida wani za6i sai zuwa inda tk don koba komai zai so yaji abinda ya faru don yasan ta inda zai fara yai dabarar kunna recording a wayar sa ba tare da ta lura da hakan ba, tsawon mintuna 30 yana zaune amma tayi kamar bata ganshi ba saida ta gama iskancin shan lemon ta kfn ta dube shi tace. "Tabbas nice na tarwatsa gidan nan saida na mallake rabi saboda son xuciya irin tata tace na rakata wajen boka ya mallake mata Ibrahim, ta manta ba ita kad'ai ke son jin dad'in duniya ba, da wannan nayi amfani na mallake ta nima sannan na sanya ta ta matsawa Ibrahim ya tura ka can k'asashen waje kuma ayiwa yasmeen aure saboda nasan idan kuna nan zan samu matsala, byn duk tayi wad'annan na bata umarnin ta sanya Ibrahim ya aure ni, daga nan kuma sai naje wajen shi wannan bokan aka warware nata asirin akayi min Wanda yafi nata zafi aka mantar da Ibrahim komai da kowa sai ni sai abinda nace masa, maganar y'an uwanka kubra da wannan mayyar yarinyar ban san inda suke ba, abinda kawai nasani rabi ta kaita wani gida wajen wata mata ita kuma yarinyar ta gudu har gida matar taxo ta tafi da rabi ina kallo kuma na hana Ibrahim yaje koda inda tk, to d'axu wani mutum yazo yana fad'a min wai sun tsince ta sun kaita asibiti, ni kam wannan ba matsala ta bace, abinda nk so dakai shine nasan a yanxu kusan rabin dukiyar Ibrahim tana hannun ka, inaso kabani 100 millions muddin ka bani zan warware asirin da nayiwa Ibrahim kuma zan rabu daku na bar ckn rayuwar ku gaba 1 saboda ni ba tsohonku ne a gaba na ba dukiyar sa ce a gaba na shi yasa tunda na aure shi ban yadda wani abu ya had'a ni dashi ba, kana bani wannan kud'in zanje in samu d'an k'aramin yaro ckn abokaina in aura muci duniyar mu da tsinke, amma fa kasani idan baka bani ta wannan hanyar ba zanje na sanar wa Ibrahim kai 6arawo ne ka sace masa dukiya kasan kuwa zaiyi iya k'ok'arin sa wajen ganin ya k'wato dukiyar sa, kuma ya mallaka min ita, don haka dabara ta ragewa me shiga rijiya."




Tana gama fad'in haka ta ja bakinta tayi shiru, sadeeq ya dubi wayar sa da tk faman aikin recording yayi murmushi kfn yace. "100 millions kawai kk buk'ata ki rabu damu."? "K'warai da gsk idan kuma xaka k'ara min ba zaiyi yawa ba." Ta bashi amsa ckn izgili, sadeeq yai murmushin gefen baki sannan yace."To ni dai yanxu bani da cash na wad'annan kud'in amma xan iya rubuta miki check idan kina buk'ata. " da sauri tace."me zai hana amma bazan ta6a yadda da kai ba sai naje na ciro kud'in na tabbatar suna hannu na kfn na cika maka alk'awarin da nayi maka. "



Y Sadeeq yace."OK ba matsala sannan ya zaro biro ya rubuta mata check na 100 millions sannan yajuya byn cheek d'in yai rubutu kamar haka :THIS WOMEN IT'S CRIMINAL Don't GIVE HER MY MONEY ARREST HER .
Ya mik'a mata cheek d'in, ta kar6a tare da dubawa babu abinda ta iya gane wa saboda bata san komai ba, sai dai ta tabbatar check d'in ne saboda tana yawan sanya dady ya rubuta mata irin sa idan tana buk'atar kud'i, murmushin jin dad'i tayi sannan a ranta tace. "Lallai baka da wayo ai ko na samu wad'annan kud'in bazan k'yale ku ba na samu hanyar da zan dinga tatsar ku,a fili kuwa cewa tayi "bari kaga na tashi naje na cire kud'i na saboda ba'a bori da sanyin jiki." Ganin ta mik'e Sadeeq yace. "Amma yakamata kfn  ki tafi kiyi wa dady bayanin ni d'an sa ne ko.''

Ta wani ta6e baki sannan tace." Alfarma 1 zan iya yi maka zan sanar dashi kai d'an abokin sa ne, saboda har yanxu ban yadda da kai ba sai na kar6i kud'ad'e na a hannu kfn  na warware komai. "
Bai damu ba ya amince da hakan, yasani duk momy ce ta janyo musu ba don ta d'auki kaltume sunje wajen boka ba da babu yanda za'a yi ta samu damar yin haka, ya dad'e da tsanar halayen momy yanxu dubi yanda ta sanya suhailat ckn gararin rayuwa baima san ta inda zai fara ne manta ba, ga kuma halin da ita kanta momyn tk ciki ba don uwa uwa bace wlh da bazai k'ara koma wa ta kanta ba.

Yana kallo kaltume ta shiga d'akin dady ba'a fi five minutes ba  suka fito tare da dadyn,  wajen sa suka nufa byn sun zauna kaltume ta dubi dady ckn kwantar da murya tace. "Alhj Ashe d'an abokin ka ne saida ga baya yk min bayani." A halin yanxu babu abinda dady yk fahimta sai abinda kaltume ta fad'a masa don haka ba tare da musu ba ya amince da abinda ta fad'a masa.


Haka ta tafi ta barsu ba tare da tunanin komai ba saboda tasan indai dady ne bazai ta6a yadda da duk abinda za'a fad'a masa ba muddin ba ita ce ta fad'a masa ba, gane hakan da sadeeq yayi yasa baiyi tunanin fad'a wa dady gskyr alak'ar dk tsakanin su ba yasan da zarar ya dawo ckn haiyacin sa ba sai anyi masa wannan bayanin ba, ckn dabara ya tofawa dady wasu adduo'i a ckn ruwa yayi sa'a kuwa Allah ya taimakeshi  dadyn ya shanye ruwan tass ko minti 10 baiyi da sha ba wani bacci me nauyi yayi awon gaba dashi, sadeeq ya tsura masa ido ckn tausayawa yana zubar da hawaye.





______________________Zuwa wannan lokaci Suhailat ta fara dangana ta sanya wa xuciyar ta salama saboda ta lura baxata ta6a ganin doctor ba dukda cewa kullum sai tayi mafarkin sun had'u don har gixo yk mata, yanda kamal yk damuwa da damuwar ta kuma yk nuna mata shima yana son aga fahad don yana son duk abinda tk so,hakan ya siya masa daraja mai girma a idonta Wanda tk zata iya rayuwa dashi a rashin masoyinta tasan ba kowanne namiji ne yana sonka ba sannan ya dinga taimaka maka wajen don ka auri wani ba shi ba, lallai kamal ya ciri tuta shi d'in na daban ne, ita kam hansatu ja'e yaxo kuma ya sanar da ita shi fa baya sonta don ba aure ne a gaban sa ba sannan yayi mata alk'awarin muddin ta bari aka aura mata shi sai ya zama ajalinta, tun lokacin da ya fad'a mata wannan maganar ta sanar da iyayen ta amma baffan ta ya dage akan dole sai anyi saboda yana tausayin mahaifin ja'e yanda ya damu dalalacewar yaron sa suna ganin da zarar anyi masa aure zai daina duk abinda yk ya nutsu shi yasa yk lallashin hansatu da bata hak'uri saboda shi a ganin sa duk barazana ja'e yk ba zai iya yin abinda ya fad'a ba.


Yau saura kwana 2 a d'aura auren gidan duk ya cika da bak'i y'an uwa da abokan arzik'i duk sun hallara amare sunsha gyara irin na fulani sai dai gaba d'ayan su babu farinciki ko kad'an a tare dasu duk lokacin da suka ke6e babu abinda suke sai kuka Suhailat ta k'ara tabbatar da cewa ta rasa doctor nan da gobe wannan tunanin shi yk sanya ta kuka ita kam hansatu ta gama saddak'arwa lokacin mutuwar ta yayi don ta tabbatar ja'e zai iya yin abinda ya fad'a.

Washe gari tunda safe akayi musu wankan lalle da na madarar shanu sannan aka yi musu kwalliya irin ta amaren fulani gsky ba k'aramin kyau sukayi ba, iyayen su maza suka tafi masallaci saboda juma'a ce kuma sai an sakko za'a d'aura auren.



_______________Tunda ya tashi yk jin jikinsa babu dad'i kamar kada ya fita amma haka kawai ya tsinci kansa da son fita, don haka yai wanka ya shirya ckn manyan kaya kamar yanda al'adarsa tk duk ranar juma'a manyan kaya yk sawa ko break fast baiyi ba hakanan yaji ba ya jin yunwa don haka ya d'auki key d'in motar sa ya fita. 

Tfy kawai yk ba tare da yasan inda ya nufa ba zuwa yanxu ya kusa haddace k'auyukan da suke ckn Adamawa don ko ina xagawa yk, yau kam tfyr tasa babu wani armashi don gaba d'aya jikinsa a sanyaye yk ga fad'uwa da gaban sa yk faman yi lokaci-lokaci innalillahi wa Inna ilaihirraji'un, shine abinda yk fad'a a duk lokacin da yaji fad'uwar gaban daga k'arshe ya kunna k'ira'ar karatun alk'urani haka ya cigaba da tfy a hankali yana bin karatun, ya wuce k'auyuka da yawa duk inda yaje zancen d'aya ne basu sanshi ba, saida ya shiga wani k'auye me suna jada anan ne wani mutum ya kwatanta masa wata ruga dadiri ya shaida masa amma shi Wanda ya sani me kama da hoton ya dad'e da rasuwa, jikin fk ya k'ara yin sanyi lik'is amma dukda haka bai fasa shirin tafiya ba saboda koba komai yau ya samu Wanda yace yasan shi Wanda tun lokacin da ya fito bai ta6a samun Wanda yace yasan me kama dashi ba ma, ganin anyi kiran sallar juma'a dole ya hak'ura da tfyr sai ya tsaya don yayi sallah a babban masallacin juma'a da yk garin jada.

Byn an idar da sallar ya gama duk addu'oin sa musamman yau da ya tsinci kansa ckn halin fad'uwar gaba yasani wani abun ke shirin faruwa dashi shi yasa ya rok'i Allah yasa duk abinda zai faru dashi ya zama alkhairi, haka ya biyo jerin masu fita daga masallacin ya nufi wajen motar sa yana k'ok'arin tfy ne ya tuna ashe baisan hanyar zuwa dadiri ba don haka ckn sauri ya nufi wajen wasu dandazon mutane da ya hango suna k'ok'arin shiga wata mota k'irar akori kura, sallama ya fara yi musu yawancin su Fulani ne hausarsu ma bata fita sosae wani dattijo ya amsa FK yace. "Baffah don Allah ina tanbayar dadiri." Dattijon yai murmushi kfn yace. "Wad'annan mutanen daka gani duk can zamu je hala kaima d'aurin auren zakaje."? Haka kawai ya tsinci kansa da cewa." Eh nima can zanje." 
Dattijon yace. "To ka biyo byn motar mu." Ckn girmamawa fk yace."Idan babu damuwa baffah kutaho kaida wad'ansu muje a motata naga wannan d'in kamar mutane sunyi yawa."  "Hakane yaro amma mu babu ruwanmu a haka muke yin lodi muje duk inda zamu je, to amma tunda ka nemi muje tare babu komai na yadda da kai dukda duniya ta lalace amma naji na yadda da kai." Ngd baffah. "  FK ya fad'a byn ya juya wajen motar sa yayinda baffah haladu ya samu wasu daga ckn abokan sa suka nufi wajen motar FK d'in wani daga ckn abokan nasa ya kasa hak'uri yace. "Moddibo hai nashe ina samu d'an birni." Baffah haladu yace. "Bansan shi ba amma ina tunanin ckn abokan ja'e ne kasan yana huld'a da y'an birni saboda ni dai bansan shi ba kuma yace wajen d'aurin auren zaije ko kuma abokin kamalu ne daga sokoto bansani ba gsky."







Ba wata tfy me nisa bace ta kawo su dadiri k'ofar gidan su baffah haladu inda anan ne za'a d'aura auren, wajen cike yk da mutane y'an uwa da abokan arxiki byn su baffah haladu sun fita daga motar FK sukayi masa gdy shima yayi musu sannan baffah ya kutsa ckn mutanen yana gaisawa da mutane, shi kam FK cigaba da zama yai a ckn motar sakamakon luguden dukan da xuciyar sa tk yi, ga yunwa da ta fara kawo masa hari sabodasaboda ko break baiyi ba, yana nan zaune yana hango jama'a heart beat d'in sa na k'aruwa.


Haka ya daure ya fita sai dai ya 6oye hoton sa saboda yayi tunanin sai angama d'aura auren sannan zai tanbaya yasan a lokacin za'a samu Wanda yasan mahaifin sa tunda wajen taro ne, baffah haladu ya fito d'auke da huhun goro , babu Wanda ake jira yanxu sai ango ja'e da waliyyansa shiru har yanxu babu labari daman shine waliyyin kamal da hansatu shi kuma baffah hamxa shine waliyyin Maryam, wajen FK ya nufa ganin sa a tsaye ya hard'e hannu yana kallon mutane, "yaro kai abokin ja'e ne ko kuma kamalu naga tun d'axu da muka xo baka samu wajen zama ba shi kanshi mahaifin ja'e da waliyyan basu k'araso ba, bamu san abinda yasa ba." Baffah haladu ya fad'a yana kallon FK, "a'a baffah ni ba abokin kowa daga ckn su bane hasali ma bansan kowa ba dama da akwai Wanda nk nema sai aka kwatanta min nan akace anan yk shi yasa naxo, don Allah baffah idan kun gama d'aura auren xan baka hoton sa a nuna wa mutane ko Allah zaisa a samu Wanda ya sanshi." Ckn damuwa baffah yace."karka damu insha'allah za'a dace bari a gama nayi maka alk'awarin ni da kaina zan nemo maka shi muddin yana rugar nan don babu Wanda ban sani ba a ckn ta don nine me gari sarautar a gidanmu tk kuma ni na gaje ta." Murmushi FK yayi ckn jin dad'i yace. "Ngd sosae baffah." 

Wajen zama baffah ya nuna masa sannan ya cigaba da kar6ar mutane wad'anda suke dandaxon zuwa wajen d'aurin auren.

Ganin mutane suna ta jira shiru ja'e da babansa basu zoba baffah haladu ya kira baffah hamxa suka tattauna akan a fara d'aura auren kamalu da Maryam kfn su xo, aka sanar da marok'a za'a fara nan fa suka cika waje da fad'in "za'a fara d'aura auren kamalu da Maryam." Haka kurum FK yaji bugun xuciyar sa ya k'aru ya sunkuyar da kansa yana ambaton sunan Allah sai yaji ya samu nutsuwa lokacin da ya d'ago kansa ya hango ango kamal yana ta faman washe baki ckn abokan sa sannan suka d'ungumo wajen d'aurin auren aka shiga gaisawa da jama'a kamal ya mik'a wa FK hannu sukayi musabaha yayinda a lokaci 1 kowanne yaji wani irin yanayi a tare dashi, a tare suka d'ago ido suka k'urawa juna ido, kamal gani yk tamkar ya ta6a ganin wannan face d'in yayinda shi kuma FK yaji angon ya shiga xuciyar sa lokaci 1 don haka yai masa murmushi kfn yace. "Ina yi maka ptn alkhairi Allah ya bada zaman lpy." Murmushi kamal ya maida mishi sannan yace. "Ngd sosae d'an uwa." Sai a wannan lokacin suka saki hannun junan su, 
Baffah haladu da baffah hamxa suka zauna yayinda gurin yai tsit ana shirin fara d'aurin aure, kwatsam sai aka ji k'arar motoci har guda 2 wad'anda kana gani kasn ba mutanen kirki ne a ckn su ba, ja'e ne ya fito daga ciki ya shak'o wuyan mahaifin sa Wanda duk jikinsa jini ne yk zuba a matuk'ar galabaice yk,d'ayan hannun sa rik'e da wata shar6e6iyar wuk'a kai tsaye wajen su baffah haladu ya nufa, tun kfn ya k'arasa duk mutanen gurin aka mimmik'e tsaye cike da tashin hankali, ckn zafin nama ya cakawa baffah hamxa wukar dk hannun sa babu Wanda yayi tunanin hakan kfn wani lokaci baffah hamxa ya fad'i k'asa sumamme ja'e ya d'auki speaker dk wajen ya fara magana kamar haka. "Nine ja'e Wanda ake shirin d'aura min aure yanxu dama saida na fad'a wa ubana bana son auren amma dashi da wannan abokin nasa suka dage sai anyi da farko nayi tunanin zan bari a d'aura auren byn nasamu abinda nk buk'ata sai na kashe yarinyar nayi tafiya ta sai abokaina suka bani wannan shawarar don haka yanxu xan k'arasa kashe ubana da kuma uban nata sai naga Wanda zai d'aura aur........" Kfn ya  k'arasa FK yai ta maza ya make zabgegiyar wuk'ar dk hannun sa sannan ckn zafin nama yafara naushin sa byn ya k'wace hannun sa daga shak'ar da yayiwa babansa, dama ba wani k'arfi ne da ja'e ba zarra ce kawai da xama ckn 6ata gari ckn k'aramin lokaci idonsa ya raina fata ganin abinda yk faruwa abokan sa da suka rako shi suka hau motar suka gudu, babu Wanda yabi ta kansu kamar yanda babu Wanda yaje inda FK yk jibgar ja'e ta ko ina, Wanda gaba d'aya kamannin sa sun canxa saboda azaba ko ihu kasa yi yayi, saida yaga ja'e ya suma sannan ya k'yale shi ya nufi wajen baffah hamxa da mlm buba mahaifin ja'e wad'anda suke kwance rai a hannun Allah ganin haka tasa suma mutane suka fara kawo agaji domin a ceci rayuwar su, FK ya koma motar sa ya d'auko kayan aiki dama dasu yk yawo da taimakon mutane aka sanya su a wata bukka shi kuma ya duk'ufa aikin sa, ckn 30 minutes suka farfad'o byn yayi musu treatment din ciwukansu.


Ya fito yana share gumi baffah haladu ya k'araso wajen sa yana masa gdy akan abinda yayi, FK yace. " yakamata a cigaba da d'aurin auren tunda komai ya lafa." 


Baffah haladu ya matso kusa da FK yace."babu shakka naji dad'in abinda kayi mana ka ceci rayuwar d'an uwa na Wanda shi kad'ai ya rage min a rayuwa bansan da abinda zan saka maka dashi ba sai addu'a amma ina me neman alfarma a wajen ka idan bazan takura maka ba inaso ka a d'aura auren nan da kai, haka kawai naji ina son had'a zuri'a da kai dukda bansani ba ko kanada aure, idan baka buk'ata kada ka CE cuci kanka ka fad'a min sannan idan kana buk'atar ganin yarinyar zan sanya a kirawo maka ita yanxu. "



FK yai shiru ckn tsananin rud'ani gsky idan ya amince ya fara auren wata ba Suhailat d'in sa ba akwai matsala babba don zai cutar da kansa, babban abun tashin hankalin shine ita Suhailat d'in tana ina? Kuma yaushe zasu had'u? Baffah haladu ya kura masa ido yana jiran jin abinda zai fad'a amma sai yaga ya sunkuyar da kai ya fad'a ckn tunani, FK ya d'ago ido ba zai iya k'in amince wa ba saboda yana ganin kima da darajar mutumin tun lokacin da suka had'u a jikin sa yk jin tamkar ma shine mahaifin nasa, ya bud'e baki da niyyar yin magana kamal dk gefen su yace. "Baffah ba hansatu yakamata a bashi ba Maryam ce ta dace dashi koba komai ita d'in tayi karatu sannan tana da wayewa ba kamar hansatu ba." Ckn mamaki baffah ya dubi kamal kfn yace. "Amma kai fa kamalu kana nufin kana nufin ka hak'ura da auren kenan."?


Kamal ya girgixa kai kfn yace.'' A'a baffah ba haka nk nufi ba ina nufin zan auri ita hansatun saboda dama can ita Maryam d'in akwai Wanda tk so kuma likita ne shi yasa nace ya aure ta wata k'ila ya dinga d'ebe mata kewar Wanda ta rasa ita kuwa hansatu na ta6a kama su wata rana tana fad'a wa ita Maryam d'in dama ni aka aura mata tana sona yayinda ita Maryam d'in tk bata labarin shi wancan likitan da tk so." yayi shiru sannan ya durk'usa k'asa yace. "Baffah kuyi min hak'uri nima zanso ace na auri wacce tk sona,a baya nayi tunanin zan iya shawo kan Maryam amma daga baya sai naga bazan iya ba saboda a jiya daddare ma nazo zanje wajen ta naji tana fad'a wa hansatu koda an aura mata ni gangar jikinta kawai zan aura amma banda xuciyar ta don babu Wanda zata iya mallaka wa xuciyar ta sai doctor d'in ta, hansatu ta tanbaye ta "yanxu da ace hamma kamalu doctor ne zaki so shi sai tace." Tabbas zata iya d'an sona saboda albarkacin wancan likitan amma yanxu ita gsky bata so na da aure amma tana sona irin soyayyar zumunci, ina gama jin wannan hirar tasu na koma d'aki na tun a lokacin auren ya fice min daga raina gashi lokaci ya k'ure min balle na sanar daku na fasa don Allah baffah kuyi hak'uri ba wasa da hankali nayi muku ba."

Murmushi baffah haladu yayi sannan yace. "Babu komai kamalu Allah ya tsara hansatu itace matar ka, shi kuma dofta idan ya amince sai a d'aura da Maryam." Ya k'arasa maganar yana kallon FK muryar sa can k'asa FK yace. "Na amince baffah Allah yasa hakan shi yafi alkhairi." suka amsa da "ameen."


A take baffah haladu ya sanar da mutane canjin da aka samu ba tare da 6ata lokaci ba aka d'aura auren kamal mukhtar da hansatu hamxa, akan sadaki 50k baffah ya tanbayi sunan FK ya fad'a masa shima a nan take aka d'aura auren FAHAD KABIRU (FK) & MARYAM IBRAHIM (SUHAILAT) motar sa ita ya bayar a matsayin sadaki saboda bai fito da kud'i ba.............

Pg46

Makad'a da marok'a suka fara aikin su, anan ne kamal yaji sunan mutumin da ya sadaukar wa soyayyar sa, har yanxu yana k'aunar Maryam sai dai zai iya komai don ya faranta mata, musamman da yaji sunan angon nata indai ba manta wa yayi ba wannan sunan irin shine sunan da Maryam d'in ta tk yawan fad'a masa a matsayin masoyin ta, a take kuma wani tunani ya fad'o masa, kodai shine? don Maryam na yawan fad'a masa doctor zaizo gareta komai dad'e wa, idan kuwa shine yasan tabbas za tayi farinciki, shi kam ya d'auki k'addarar auren hansatu dukda baya jin soyayyar ta a ransa amma zaiyi k'ok'ari ya sanya soyayyar ta a xuciyar tunda ita tana k'aunar sa, shi mutum ne me ra'ayin auren wacce tk sonsa don a cewar sa idan ka yadda ka auri wacce bata sonka duk abinda ya biyo baya kaine Wanda ka janyo, kuma tun lokacin da yaji suhailat na fad'in zai auri gangar jikinta ne ba xuciyar ta ba, auren ta ya fita daga ransa ya rasa hanyar da zaibi yace ya fasa sai gashi Allah y kawo masa mafita don, ya mik'e daga inda yk zaune ya nufi wajen FK burinsa kawai ya tanbaye shi ko shine fahad d'in Maryam.

Tun lokacin da aka d'aura auren FK ya tsinci kansa ckn farinciki ba tare da yasan dalilin hakan ba, shi dai kawai yana jin wani nishad'i a ransa yarasa me yasa yk jin hakan maimakon xuciyar sa ta taya shi jajen wannan auren da baizo masa a ckn shiri ba amma sai ta 6ige da yi masa murna, duk sanda ya tuna cewa ba suhailat ce wacce ya fara aura ba sai yaji gaban sa ya fad'i amma hakan bai hana xuciyar sa yi masa Allah y sanya alkhairi ya bada zaman lpy ba, ta hanyar jefashi ckn farinciki, byn mutane sun ragu FK ya shiga inda aka kwantar dasu baffah hamxa ya duba su ya tarar sun farka kuma ko wanne yana ckn haiyacin sa sai dai suna jin zafin ciwukan da aka ji musu, sannu yayi masu sannan ya koma ckn motar sa, au motar suhailat  ya d'auko ruwa da alluran kashe rad'ad'i yayi musu hadda allurar bacci ana gama wa kuwa baccin yayi gaba dasu.

Kamal ya duba ko ina baiga fahad ba don haka ya nufi ckn gida saboda an aiko mama tana kiranshi don taji yanda akayi ya koma angon hansatu sa6anin angon Maryam, ba ita ba duk mata y'an biki suna buk'atar k'arin bayani koda yk sun fara jin rad'e-rad'in abinda yk faruwa, yana shiga abokan wasa suka fara tsokanar sa suna gud'a shi kam murmushi kawai yk tare da kutsawa ciki don ya isa d'akin da mama tk, da k'yar ya samu ya wuce, a zaune ya samesu su 3 matar baffah haladu da matar baffah hamxa da mama da alama wani abun suke tattauna wa, don magana suke yi k'asa-k'asa, suna ganin shi suka kowacce ta fara wurgo masa tanbaya,

 "kamalu me yk faruwa ance ja'e ya kashe baffan hansatu? tanbayar innar hansatu.
Ya akayi ka auri hansatu ba Maryama ba? Tanbayar matar baffah haladu.

Waye shi Wanda aka aura wa Maryam d'in Allah yasa dai nagari ne?
Mama ke wannan maganar.

Guri kamal ya samu ya zauna kfn ya cire hularsa yana goge gumi sannan ckn murmushi yace." To yanxu wa zan fara baiwa amsa a ckn ku ."? Mama tayi masa dak'uwa kfn tace. "Gidan ku kamal, don kaga muna jiran kayi mana bayani shine zaka fara yi mana jan ran nan naka."? Dariya kamal yayi sannan ckn nutsuwa  ya warware musu komai tunda ga farko har k'arshe sannan ya k'ara da cewa." Ni gsky ina zargin shi wannan da aka d'aura auren dashi kamar shine asalin wanda Maryam d'in tk so saboda sunan su iri d'aya sannan kuma shima likita ne." Mama tayi shiru ckn tunani kfn tace. "Tabbas tunanin ka zai iya zama gsky don babu yadda Allah baya ikonsa, zata iya yiwuwa kuma ba shi bane sa'a akayi sunan nasu yaxama iri d'aya, mu dai ftn mu Allah yasa ya zamo mutum na gari, kuma Allah yasa hakan shi yafi muku alkhairi gaba d'aya." Duk suka amsa da ameen.



Amare suna can k'uryar d'akin inna tsohuwa sun haye gadon k'arfen ta kowacce ta kunshe kanta tana shar6ar kuka ba susan abinda yk faruwa ba, suhailat gani tk gaba d'aya an gama da ita idan aka d'aura mata aure da kamal, ba wai bata sonsa bane tasani kamal yana da duk wasu quality's da Mace zata so shi tayi kuma rayuwar aure me dad'i dashi, amma soyayyar FK baxata bari ta bawa duk namijin da ta aura kulawa ba, saboda har yanxu xuciyar ta bata fasa fad'a mata yana nan zuwa gareta ba, shi yasa tk kuka tasan ko yanxu aka d'aura auren FK ya baiyana babu yanda za'a yi a warware auren dole tayi hak'uri ta zauna da kamal.

Ita kam hansatu gani tk adda Maryam kukan dad'i tk saboda ita Wanda ake so a mak'ala mata d'an daba ne Wanda bashi da wata Sana'a sai kidnapping, kuma iyayen ta sunsan da hakan shine suke so su k'ak'aba mata shi don suna tak'amar idan anyi mishi aure zai nutsu, ita tasan babu wata nutsuwa a wajen ja'e mutumin da yace zai kashe ta muddin ta aure shi, taja majina ta juyo ta kalli suhailat zuwa yanxu ita ta daina kukan sai ajiyar xuciya tk faman saukewa akai-akai, hansatu tace.''adda Maryam ni fa mamaki kk bani nice wacce yakamata nayi kuka ni da ja'e yace zai kashe ni amma su baffah suka dage, ke kam ban san abinda kk yiwa kuka ba hamma kamalu ba shida makusa kuma yana k'aunar ki wlh da nice babu abinda zaisa nayi kuka sai dai nayi murna. "

Murmushin yak'e suhailat tayi kfn tace. "Hansatu kenan nasan bazaki gane irin halin da nk ciki ba wata k'ila ke baki ta6a yin soyayya ba shi yasa amma nima d'in na hak'ura da auren y kamal sai dai nasan zaiyi fama dani kfn na kar6e shi a matsayin miji na." Hansatu ta rausayar da kai xuciyar ta na mata suya ita kam ta sani baffan ta ba k'aunar ta yk ba.


Mama ce ta shigo d'akin tana fad'in. "Wai ina amaren nan ne kuxo maza ku shirya kowacce zata je wajen mijinta yanxu." Babu wacce ta iya yin magana har mama ta k'araso ta lura kuka sukayi  sai dai batayi musu magana ba ta kama hannayen su ta fita dasu, da kanta ta zubawa kowacce ruwan wanka tace. "Maza Ku gama ina jiran ku." Sannan duk suka shiga band'aki don su cika umarnin mama amma kowacce ba don ranta yana so ba. "



______________________Aunty yasmeen ta haihu ta haifi sankaceciyar yarinya aikuwa aunty yasmeen tace ita fa Khadija ta haifa mahaifiyar suhailat, farouq bai musa mata ba ya sanyawa yarinya Khadija suna kiranta sahla, ko 40 yasmeen batayi ba ta takurawa farouq ita dai tana son zuwa gida, don haka babu shiri ya fara yi musu shirye-shiryen tfy ita kam yasmeen tsaraba kawai tk loda kowa saida tayi masa nas daban amma na masoyiyar ta suhailat duk yafi na kowa yawa saboda ita d'in special ce a wajen ta, ta matsu taje taga yanda suhailat tk rayuwa a gaban momy don ita bata yadda cewa momy zata rik'e ta tsakani da Allah ba don tafi kowa sanin halin tsohuwar ta, farouq yana gama yi musu visa ya nemi izini a makaranta,nan take suka sanya rana tfy nan da kwana 2.



Baffah haladu yayiwa fk jagoranci har ckn gidan sannan ya kaishi d'aki me kyau ya sanar dashi amaryar shi zata zo suga juna, murmushi kawai FK yayi can k'asan xuciyar sa kuma yana jin fad'uwar gaba haka dai ya daure ya cigaba da zama yana jiran yaga ta inda matar da aka aura masa zata baiyana.


Mama da kanta tayiwa amare kwalliyar y'an birni sannan ta mik'a musu wani tsadadden less kala d'aya tace kowacce ta sanya su kam basu da za6i sai bin duk umarnin mama, byn sun gama shirya wa ta feshe su da wani turare me kamshi.


Saida ta kai kowacce k'ofar d'akin da angonta yk tare da yi musu umarnin su shiga, suhailat ta d'aga labule a hankali ta shiga d'akin tayi sallama sai dai a xuciyar  ta kawai tayi abarta shi yasa Wanda tayiwa bai amsa ba, bata d'ago kanta ba saboda yana k'asa tana wasa da zoben hannun ta, FK baiji motsi ba kwata-kwata amma sai yaji d'akin ya rikid'e da wani kamshi me dad'i, bayansa a juye yk don haka ya juyo don yaga Wanda ya kawo masa tsarabar wannan k'amshin.......



UMMU MASHKUR CE

Share don Allah.

            SUHAILAT

Bai iya hango face d'in ta ba saboda a rufe tk da mayafi me kauri, sai dai yana ganin ta heart beat d'in sa ya tsananta, ya k'ura wa surar ta da yanayin tsayuwar ta ido, gani yayi tamkar suhailat ce a gaban sa, ckn sauri ya kauda kansa k'wallah ta na neman  zubo masa ya sani tsananin soyayyar da yk wa suhailat ne yasa yk ganin wannan yarinyar kamar ita, juya bayansa yayi xuciyar sa tayi masa wani duhu lokaci d'aya, baya son kallon fuskar yarinyar wacce tk amsa sunan wai matarsa, tabbas ya yadda da k'addara Allah ne ya k'addara ba suhailat zai fara aura ba, shi bashida ra'ayin zama da mata 2 amma yanxu dolene ya zauna dasu, saboda bazai ta6a iya hak'ura da suhailat ba.

Ita kam suhailat gajiya tayi da tsaiwa kuma gashi kamal yak'i yi mata magana, wani 
haushin sa ya taso mata ba don tana ganin girmansa ba yau da ta nuna masa iyakarsa, don me zai shanya ta a tsaye don kawai ance taxo wajen sa, tayi k'wafa ckn ranta tana fad'in "ba komai ni na kawo kaina." Ckn sauri ta juya tare da barin d'akin hawaye yana bin kuncinta ashe duk soyayyar da kamal yk fad'in yana yi mata duk k'arya ne, daga d'aura aure zai fara yimata wulak'anci saboda yasan yanxu tana k'ark'ashin ikonsa, d'akin inna ta shiga, su mama suna zaune taxo ta wuce su fuu tana kuka, hankalin mama yai matuk'ar tashi, ta dubi innar hansatu tace. "Dama nasan za'a rina wai an saci zanin mahaukaciya, yanxu haka wannan canjin da akayi wa Maryam shine baiyi mata ba, ni bansan abinda yasa suka yanke hukunci ba tare da sun shawar ce mu ba." Innar hansatu tace. "Haka Allah ya nufa wani baya auren matar wani, matar mutum kabarin sa." Mama ta rausayar da kai kfn tace. "Hakane Allah y sanyaya mata xuciyar ta ta kar6i abun da hannu 2." Innar hansatu tace. "Ameen."


Juyo wa yayi don yayi mata magana koba komai yanxu matsayin matarsa tk, yana juyo wa yaga babu kowa a d'akin yasani bai kyauta mata ba, amma babu yanda ya iya yanayin da yk ciki bazai iya yi mata magana me dad'i ba don a lokacin da ya juyo zai iya fad'a mata komai Wanda zai iya janyo matsala, shima fitowa yayi daga d'akin yayi waje inda su baffah haladu suke zaune tare da y'an uwa, yana so ya yiwa baffah maganar abinda ya kawo shi amma sai yaga da mutane don haka ya wuce wajen patients d'in sa su baffah hamxa.


Baffah haladu ya fahimci magana yk so suyi don haka yabi shi gurin, byn ya zauna ya dubi fahad yace."d'ana ina ptn matar taka tayi maka ba matsala. " shiru fk yai don da kunya ya dubi baffah haladu yace masa baiga koda fuskar ta ba, sannan kuma ba zai iya yi masa k'arya ba, don haka ya dubi baffah yace. "Baffah ni ba ma wannan ba inaso muyi magana akan Wanda nazo nema ko Allah zaisa ka sanshi."

Murmushi baffah haladu yayi kfn yace. "To shikenan ya sunan sa kuma......" Katse shi fk yayi ta hanyar nuna masa hoton da yk yawo dashi koda yaushe, baffah yai saurin kar6ar hoton ya tsura mishi ido, take ya fara zubar da hawaye, ya kalli fahad ckn kuka yace. "Yaro a ina kasan wannan."? Ckn mamakin kukan da baffah keyi fk yace." Mahaifi na ne, tun ina dan k'arami ya tafi ya barmu ni da mahaifiya ta, lokacin da na fara girma na fara samun matsalar gori akwai wad'anda suke jifa na da Kalmar shege, nasha zuwa wajen mahaifiya ta akan ta fad'a min wanene mahaifina amma sai dai tace min nayi hak'uri ba yanxu zata fad'a min ba akwai lokacin da tk jira, na ta6a neman aure da akayi bincike sai aka fasa aura min yarinyar yanxu haka akwai wacce nk so kuma a dalilin ta ne, na fito neman asalina saboda dangina su nema min auren ta, dukda cewa itama yanxu haka ina ne manta ruwa a kallo." Baffah haladu ya gyara zama kfn ya fara magana da cewa."Alhamdulillah ngd wa Allah yaro kasamu daginka wannan da kk gani yaya nane kabiru, labarin yana da tsawo ban sani ba ko mahaifiyar ka ta baka labarin. "? "Eh,  baffah ta fad'a min." Murmushi baffah hamxa yai kfn yace. "Ashe kai d'in jini ne shi yasa tun lokacin da naganka naji ka shiga xuciya har na iya d'aukar diya ta na baka, nayi farinciki da ya kasance kai d'an uwanta ne,saboda Maryam d'iyar k'anwata ce wacce ta rasu ina ptn zaka rik'e min ita da amana, sannan maganar wacce kk so insha'allah da zarar ka shirya kazo ni da kaina zan tsaya maka ka aure ta." Fk yayi murmushi wani dad'i yana mamaye duk ilahirin jikinsa waya ya d'auka ya kira  ya shaida mata  ya samu dangin sa, baffah haladu yace. "Yakamata ka tashi muje ckn  gida don kaga sauran y'an uwa da kuma mahaifiyar mu wato ka karka, mik'ewa FK yayi duk wata damuwa da yk ciki ya nemeta ya rasa sai yanxu ya k'ara amsa sunan sa na ango, kai tsaye ckn gidan suka nufa suka shiga tare da sallama.


Kamal yana zaune hansatu ta turo k'ofa ta shigo tayi sallama ga dukkan alamu a tsorace tk, don jikinta banda rawa babu abinda yk, maimakon taji harigidon muryar ja'e yana mata ihu sai taji sanyayar muryar kamal ya amsa sallamar ckn tsananin mamaki da al'ajabi ta yaye mayafin dk fuskar ta ta k'ura masa ido shima ita yk kallo yana mata murmushi, bakinta har rawa yk wajen fad'in. " ham..hamma, ina ja'en yk."?

Murmushi kamal yayi kfn yace. "Zo ki zauna hafsat yanxu zan nuna miki inda ja'en yk." Shiru tayi don tunda tk bata ta6a jin hamma kamalu yayi mata magana da irin wannan muryar ba dukda cewa shi dama ba hansatu yk ce mata ba, ganin ta tsaya tana tunani ya mik'e a hankali ya kamo hannun ta, ya zaunar da ita kusa dashi ita kam ta zama wata butun butumi kamar babu mini a jikin ta binsa kawai tk da ido mamakin abubuwan da hamma yk mata yau tk yi don koda wasa bai ta6a koda rik'e mata hannu ba, amma yau shine hadda zaunar da ita tayi shiru tana jiran abinda zai fad'a mata, kallon ta yayi ckn nutsuwa kfn yace. "Hafsat nine Wanda na maye miki gurbin ja'e, yanxu haka dani aka d'aura auren ki."
Mamaki, tsoro, farinciki ne duk suka had'u a xuciyar hansatu, Wanda hakan yasa ta kasa magana, ckn nutsuwa kamal ya warware mata duk abinda ya faru, inda ya k'arasa maganar da fad'in. "Yanxu dai kinga nine mijinki ina ptn kina sona."? Yayi maganar yana lek'a fuskar ta, kunya ta rufe ta,tasa tafin hannun ta Wanda yasha jan lalle ta rufe fuskar ta, tana murmushi, nan dai ya dinga jan ta da hira tsawon lokaci, so yk ya sabawa kansa da zama da ita ko zai samu xuciyar sa ta fara k'aunar ta, ya fahimci a d'an wannan zaman da sukayi ta fara shiga ransa saboda shi mutum ne Wanda yk son mace me kunya, kuma hansatu ba dai kunya ba.


Saida ya tabbatar ya sanya ta ckn farinciki sannan ya k'yale ta ta fito sai ga hansatu an fito ana zuba murmushi, maimakon kuka da tk faman yi kwana 2.


Kai tsaye d'akin inna ta nufa don tasan suhailat tana can tana zuwa ta same ta, tana sallar la'asar itama alwala tayo taxo tayi tata sallar, byn sun idar hansatu ta dubi Suhailat ckn murmushi kfn tace." Adda Maryam kinga yanda Allah yk ikonsa ashe ke d'in ba matar hamma kamalu bace,duk kukan da kk akan bakya sonsa Ashe shi d'in ba mijinki bane."

Ckn tashin hankali suhailat tace. "Me kk nufi da kk ce kamal ba shine miji na ba to dawa aka d'aura min aure.!?

Hansatu tace." Nima bansani ba amma dai hamma kamalu yace min ba dashi aka d'aura ba, maganar da nk miki a halin yanxu nice matar hamma kamalu, suhailat ta zare ido kfn tace. "To ni kuma wa aka aura min!"? Innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji'un." Sakin baki hansatu tayi kfn tace. "Kina nufin baki ga Wanda aka aura miki ba, byn mama da kanta ta kaiki har k'ofar d'akin."? 

Nan suhailat ta fad'a mata duk abinda ya faru byn shigar ta d'akin har xuwa fitowar ta, hansatu tayi murmushi kfn tace." Kinyi rashin wayo adda Maryam, idan shi yana jin kansa ai ke  sai kiyi masa magana koda sallama ce da gaisuwa, kin san dole ya amsa." Nayi masa sallama fa amma dai gsky nasan bazai ji ba don a xuciya ta nayi. " ckn nutsuwa hansatu ta warware wa suhailat abinda ya faru kamar yanda kamal ya fad'a mata.



________________Cike da izza da gadara kaltume tk takawa a hankali komai na jikinta motsi yk saboda irin banzar shigar da tayi baxaka ta6a cewa ta ta6a zama da musulmai ba, balle kuma xuciyar ka ta raya maka ita d'in musulma ce, haka ta cigaba da rangaji zuwa ckn harabar bankin byn ta mik'a wa me aiki a lokacin envelope d'in hannun ta ya bud'e, ganin mak'udan kud'in da aka rubuta a jiki yasa da sauri ya d'ago ido yana kallon ta, kaltume ta kashe masa ido sannan ckn ya mutsa fuska tace. "Kayi mamaki ko."? Yakamata ka hanxarta ka ciro min kud'i na, nayi gaba." Baiyi mata magana ba ya cigaba da duba check d'in, yana juya byn ya hango rubutun da akayi da manyan baki'. THIS WOMEN IT'S CRIMINAL DON'T GIVE HER MY MONY ARREST HER.
Ckn sauri ya kira security suka kama ta kaltume sai k'unduma musu zagi tk amma a banza saida suka yi gaba da ita, ma'aikacin bankin ya kira number ya sadeeq da tk rubuce a jikin check d'in, y sadeeq yace masa a kaita can headquarters d'in police zaije can haka kuwa akayi, kaltume hankalin ta ya tashi sai yanxu tk ganin wautar ta da tai saurin amince wa da sadeeq alhalin bata iya karanta komai a boko ba ta san shine yasa akayi mata wannan wulak'ancin koda yk ai dashi da mahaifin sa suna tafin hannunta sai yanda taga dama zatayi dasu, wlh sai sadeeq da iyayen sa sun gane kurensu ta fad'a a fili lokacin da ake danna ta a guardroom.



____________________Y sadeeq abubuwa sun taru sunyi mishi yawa musamman ga rashin lpyr Momy sannan ga dady Wanda har yanxu baya ckn haiyacin sa, asibitin ya koma byn y'an wasu cike-cike aka bashi transfer ya maida private hospital Wanda basa buk'atar mai jinya nurses ne suke kula da mara lpy y'an uwansa sai dai su xo su duba shi su tafi, haka sadeeq ya zuba mak'udan kud'i a asusun hospital d'in duk abinda ake buk'ata a ciki ake d'auka, washe gari tunda safe ya d'auki dady suka tafi wajen wani babban malami masanin addini, byn an shiga dasu sadeeq yayiwa malam bayanin komai, shiru mlm yayi tsawon lokaci yana kallon dady sannan ya dubi sadeeq yace. "Gsky mahaifin ka ya had'u da hatsabibiyar mace wannan lamarin nasa tsafi ne ba asiri ba, amma insha'allah zamu yi k'ok'ari muyi masa addu'a babu abinda yafi k'arfin Allah." Haka mlm ya dinga zuba wa dady adduo'i na musamman, sannan ya rubuta musu wasu ayoyi na karya sihiri wad'anda za'a tofa a ruwa a dinga sha ana yin wanka tsawon  kwana 7, 

Tunda suka taho daga wajen malam y sadeeq nemi ganyen magarya Wanda dashi za'a had'a tun a ranar aka fara kuma ckn ikon Allah an fara ganin sauk'i saboda yanxu haka dady ya dawo ckn haiyacin sa kuma ya gane sadeeq, sannan yana yawan tanbayar sa sauran y'ay'an sa musamman Suhailat duk lokacin da ya tanbaye shi inda suhailat tk y sadeeq k'arya yk masa yana ce masa tana can gidan yasmeen.



Jikin Momy kullum sai a hankali don kuwa likitoci suna k'ok'arin kula da lpyr ta amma abun kullum k'aruwa yk maimakon raguwa daga k'arshe ma likitocin da kansu suka bada shawarar a koma maganin gargajiya don haka aka d'auko ta ranga-ranga daga asibiti zuwa gida.


Yasmeen sun sauka a Nigeria kai tsaye gidan su suka sauka allah-allah tk taje gida taga y'ar k'anwar ta, babu Wanda ta matsu ta gani irin suhailat, gajiya ce kawai tasa bata tafi a ranar da suka dawo ba,so tk tayi musu bazata shi yasa babu Wanda ta kira ta fad'a masa tana hanya, da sassafe suka shirya itada farouq da yarinyar su sahla wacce ta murmure tayi kyau abinta.


Tun daga harabar gidan ta fuskanci akwai matsala gaba d'aya ya hargitse, kamar ba gidan su ba, kai tsaye babban parlourn gidan ta nufa, babu kowa a ciki tayi sallama shiru babu amsa hankalin ta ya k'ara tashi ta nufi d'akin Momy babu kowa da alama an dad'e ba'a amfani da d'akin, a guje yasmeen ta fito saboda yanda hankalin ta ya tashi ko ganin gaban ta bata yi sai ji tayi tayi karo da mutum, a razane ta d'ago tana kallon y sadeeq Wanda ya fito daga d'akin sa, ckn mamaki sadeeq yk kallon ta, yasmeen ta bishi da kallo kfn ckn damuwa tace. "Y sadeeq me yk faruwa a gidan nan naga kamar ba gidan mu da nasani ba." Murmushin k'arfin hali sadeeq yayi kfn yace. "Ki fara nutsuwa tukunna yasmeen kfn nayi miki bayani, ina farouq d'in koba tare kk ba."? Yasmeen tace." Yana waje. " ta fad'i hakan lokacin da tk juya wa saboda ta shigo dashi,tare suka shigo yana d'auke da baby sahla wacce tk ta wasan ta, da sauri y sadeeq ya tari farouq yana dariya tare da kar6ar yarinyar yana fad'in."lallai mutanen nan baku da kirki yanxu har new baby muka samu amma ba labari gashi kunyi mana xuwan baxata unexpected. " farouq yai murmushi kfn yace. "Ayi mana afuwa babban yaya, gata nan mun kawo muku ita har gida." Byn sun zauna sun gama gaisawa farouq ya tanbayi ina Momy tk, sadeeq yace. "Momy ba lpy kuxo muje kuga jikin nata sai a hankali." Gaba d'aya suka tafi 6angaren da ake kula da momy, saboda sadeeq ya samu wasu mata guda 4 suna kula da ita yana biyan su don baxata iya yiwa kanta komai ba, haka suka shiga gurin, abun tausayi momy duk ta fita haiyacin ta komai sai anyi mata, aunty yasmeen banda kuka babu abinda tk yi, shi kansa farouq saida ya zubar da hawaye Momy kam kallon su tk ckn xuciyar ta banda nadama babu abinda tk, ta kasa furta komai sai dai ta tsurawa baby sahla ido wacce ke hannun y sadeeq, ganin haka tasa ya matsa kusa da ita yana nuna mata yarinyar hawaye kawai suka gani yana zuba daga idonta saboda ba baki balle hannun da zata d'auki jikarta d'iyar yasmeen.



Byn sun fito kai tsaye part dn dady suka nufa, alhamdulillah shi kam yanxu ya dawo normal kamar babu abinda ya faru dashi sai dai lokaci-lokaci yana tunawa da wasu abubuwan shi yasa koda yaushe zaka same shi yana karatun alk'urani ko sallar nafila Wanda yanxu haka ma sun same shi yana salatud-duha, ne guri suka samu suka zauna saida ya idar sannan suka gaishe shi, dady fa d'ad'a murmushin sa ckn barkwanci yace."kaga y'an k'asar waje wannan zuwa babu sanarwa haka. "? Suka yi dariya gaba d'aya y sadeeq yace." Kuma dady harda tsarabar mata suka kawo maka. " 

Dady ya mik'a hannu ya kar6i baby sahla yana mata wasa ita kam sai faman 6angala dariya tk, yace. "To naga taku y'ar ni kuma ina tawa y'ar da kk tafi da ita."? 

Kallon-kallo suka tsaya yi a tsakanin su ckn sauri y sadeeq yace." Ai dady basu taho da ita ba saboda tana karatu kada a katse mata karatun dady ya had'a ransa kfn yace. "Yanxu saboda Allah a wajen wa kk bar min yarinya wlh na matsu naga mamana." Farouq ya duba wayar sa saboda jin shigowar sak'o yana dubawa yaga sadeeq ne, ckn second 2 ya d'au haske yace. "Am dady na barta ne a wajen wani aboki na kuma insha'allah babu abinda zai faru, sannan tana gama jarabawar ta za'a yi mata visa ta dawo gida gaba d'aya." Dady yai shiru na d'an wani lokaci kfn yace."Waima wanene ya baku ixinin tpyr min da yarinya, nasan ba laifin kowa bane illah ke yasmeen. " ya k'arasa maganar yana nuna ta.

Ckn sauri yasmeen tace wlh dady ni dai babu ruwa na ni banma fahimci maganar da kk yi ba.


______________Suhailat ta dubi hansatu tace. "Na shiga 3 ni Maryam me yasa aka yi min haka!? Xuciya ta bata gama kar6ar kamal a matsayin miji ba, amma yanxu za'a bijiro min da wata damuwar."? Saurin kallon ta Suhailat tayi lokaci guda xuciyar ta ta harba da k'arfi, "don Allah hansatu ki taimaka min ko farkon sunan nasa ne ki tuna." Hansatu ta d'aga kanta tana tunani tare da fad'in "ko faisal kai ba haka ba far- far.." Suhailat tayi saurin fad'in ko dai *fahad*."? Hansatu ta d'aga kai "yauwa kamar dai haka hamma kamalu ya fad'a min."Ai Mik'e wa Suhailat tayi tsaye tana fad'in."hansatu kaini wajen sa don Allah ki kaini." Sakin baki hansatu tayi tana kallon suhailat wacce tk k'ok'arin mik'e wa, hayaniya sukaji daga tsakar gida amsu koke-koke nayi masu gud'a da hamdala nayi basu tsaya 6atawa kansu lokaci ba suma sukayi waje don gane wa idon su abinda yk faruwa, wani matashi suka hango an baibaye shi ana kallon sa, hansatu ta k'arasa wajen da sauri, baffah haladu ne yayi magana da cewa. "Ku matsa mana ya shigo sosae a nuna masa dangi a tsanake." Kowa ya matsa ya bada hanya sai dai shi uban gaiyar ya kasa motsa wa sai ma wani guri da ya k'urawa ido hannun sa d'aya yana nuna wajen bakinsa yana kar-karwa, duk mutanen gurin suka juya don ganin abinda ya jefa shi ckn wannan mood d'in, Suhailat ce itama tana tsaye ckn irin yanayin da yk ciki sai dai ita ta had'a da zubar da hawaye bakinta banda rawa babu abinda yk yi, nan fa kallo ya koma wai shaho ya d'au giwa, Suhailat ta shek'o a guje............nima fa na shek'a a guje

Kai tsaye wajen sa ta nufa ta fad'a jikinsa tana wani irin kuka me tsuma xuciya gaba d'aya mutanen gurin jikin su yayi sanyi, suna neman k'arin bayani, amma babu meyi musu wannan bayanin saboda shi kansa FK d'in idonsa a rufe yk yana xaubar da hawaye, sai dai shi idan ma ba kallon sa kk ba bazaka fahimci abinda yk yi ba, sa6anin ita da kukan ya taho mata gaba d'aya tana yi tana jan ajiyar xuciya, kamar me shirin had'iye xuciyar.

Kowa a gurin yayi cirk-cirko ana kallon ikon Allah, sun d'auki tsawon 20 minutes a haka kfn nutsuwa ta sauka a zuciyoyin su sai a wannan lokacin ne, suhailat ta tuna cewa ita d'in fa matar wani ce kuma taxo ta rik'e fahad, wata k'ila ma mijin nata yana gurin tunda ita bata sanshi ba ckn sauri ta saki fk Wanda shima zuwa lokacin ya fahimci abinda suka aikata, sunkuyar da kanta tayi har yanxu hawayen bai daina kwarara daga idonta ba, baffah haladu ya matso kusa da ita ya kama hannun ta kfn yace. "D'iya ta menene yk faruwa shin kin sanshi ne daman." Shiru tayi don da kunya ta dubi jama'ar dk wajen, wata k'ila harda mijin da aka aura mata, ta fad'a musu matsayin fk a wajen ta da xuciyar ta, ganin tayi shiru baffah ya maida tanbayar ga fahad, shi kam tuni xuciyar sa ta lula tunanin me ya kawo suhailat d'in sa wannan garin kuma a ckn wannan gidan ko kad'an xuciyar sa bata ta6a kawo masa hasashen zai ganta a garin Adamawa ba, koda yk yana yawan mafarkin sun had'u da ita, sannan xuciyar sa na yawan fad'a masa yana kusa da ita Ashe ma gata a ckn dangin sa.
