Kowa a wajen saida ya furta kalmar "alhamdulillah." Suhailat kam tsintar ta akayi ta sanya goshin ta a k'asa tana yin sujjadar shukur, haka shima fk don kusan lokaci d'aya suka xube a k'asa sun d'auki tsawon lokaci kfn fk ya d'ago ya durk'usa a gaban baffah haladu sannan ckn farinciki yace. "Ngd baffah Allah yasa ka maka da alkhairi dama itace wacce nk fad'a maka ina Neman ta ashe itace matar da aka aura min ngd sosae". Baffah haladu yai murmushi kfn yace." Allah kenan babu yanda baya ikon sa don haka shi zamuyi ta godewa sannan kayi gdy ga kamalu domin ya taka muhimmiyar rawa wajen kasancewar aurenku, saboda ni hansatu nace ka aura amma da yk shi ya san wani abu game daku yace shi ya hak'ura ya bar maka Maryam d'in. " fahad ya juya ga kamal yace. "Ngd sosae d'an uwa na." Suhailat ma ta kalli kamal tace. "Y kamal tabbas ka cika min alk'awarin da ka d'aukar min kuma ngd Allah y bar zumunchi sannan Allah yasa hafsa tafi ni zama alkhairi a wajen ka." Gaba d'aya gurin aka amsa da "ameen." Mama cike da tsokana ta dubi Suhailat tace. "Shikenan kuka ya k'are ko d'iya ta."? Hannu tasa ta rufe face d'in ta tana fitar da wani k'ayataccen smylin.




To baffah haladu ya gabatar da fahad a matsayin d'an kabiru yayansa inna banda kuka babu abinda tk yi, tana Neman yafiyar fahad sannan tace zata bishi har kano ta baiwa mahaifiyar sa hak'uri saboda tasan itace wacce ta cutar dasu, don har Allah ya d'auki ran kabiru bai daina anbatar sunan ruk'ayya ba.


 Shi kam doctor farinciki yayi masa yawa gashi dai Allah y taimaka masa burin sa sun cika lokaci d'aya, dole ya kira ammy yayi mata wani sabon albishir d'in, mutane sunyi masa yawa kowa zuwa yk don ganin yaron kabiru me rasuwa, shi kam ya gaji da yawa gashi yana son ke6ewa da matarsa don su d'an tattauna, amma babu hali mik'e wa yayi, yace." Bata daiyi magana ba sai murmushi da tk faman yi,hannun ta ya kamo suka zauna ya dube ta ckn farinciki yace. "My patients ki bani labarin gwa-gwarmayar da kk sha mana." Tayi dariya sam ta kasa sakewa dashi sosae tk jin kunyar sa, ganin taki sakin jikinta dashi yace. "To yaushe kk shirya mana koma wa kano." Ckn sanyin murya tace. "Nima ban sani ba." Lips d'in ta ya tsaya yana kallo ji yk tamkar ya cafko shi, take ya fara shiga ckn wani new mood ya kamo hannun ta Wanda yasha jan lalle yana d'an murzawa a hankali yana lumshe ido, ckn wata irin rikitacciyar murya yace. "Kin k'ara kyau and your skin very soft lotion my hayaty." Da sauri ta k'wace hannun ta, sannan a hankali ta yunk'ura da niyyar tashi saboda ta tuna abinda mama ta fad'a musu kfn su fito tace. "Kada wacce a ckn ku ta yadda mijinta koda hannun ta ya rik'e mata, ba'a ce haram bane amma kuyi idan aka kai musu ku sai kunfi daraja a idonsu." Ta lura wannan idan ta k'yale shi ba a rik'e hannu kawai zai tsaya ba. Ganin tana k'ok'arin tashi ya sa hannun sa ya zagaye ta dashi yanda ko motsin kirki baxata iya yi ba, sannan ya tsareta da manyan idonsa masu kwarjini, sunkuyar da kanta tayi don baxata iya jure kallon k'wayar idonsa ba, hakan ne ya bashi damar k'are mata kallo bai san lokacin da ya manna mata kiss a for head d'in ta ba, idonta yai rau-rau kamar zata yi kuka tace."don Allah doctor ka barni na tafi." Ya wani kashe mata ido ckn salon tsokana yace. "Nan ba hospital bane, don haka ni ba doctor bane idan kin shirya fad'in suna na to yanxu zaki tafi idan kuma baki fad'a ba zamu kwana a haka."

K'wallar da tk rik'e wa ta zubo a check d'in ta yabi tears d'in da kallo yana ji kamar yasa harshen sa ya lashe, amma dai ya daure saboda ya lura ta tsorata dashi sosae, muryar ta a dakushe tace."kayi hak'uri don Allah yaya ka barni in tafi kada aga na dad'e. "
Murmushi yayi jin abinda ta fad'a yace. "to ko anga kin dad'e ba'a wajen mijinki kk dad'e ba, kuma ni ba yayan ki bane don naga alamar kina so ki dinga rage min matsayi, amma dai zanyi hak'uri na kar6i yayan na yau kawai sai ki shirya canxa min suna." Da sauri ta d'aga kanta har yanxu idonta a lumshe yk don baxata iya had'a ido dashi ba ya d'ago ta tsaye ya rakota har k'ofar d'akin bai barta ta tafi ba saida ya k'ara kissing hannun ta, ita dai tunda ta samu ya cika ta tai waje da sauri babu ko waiwaye.

Wata dariyar farinciki fk ya saki yana k'ara godewa Allah da ya mallaka masa ita a matsayin matar sa, sannan xuciyar sa fari k'al ya bar d'akin.


Koda ta koma d'akin inna ta tarar hansatu ta dad'e da dawowa, hansatu ta dube ta. "Adda Maryam sai yanxu likitan naki ya barki kk dawo."? "To sa idnawa ina ruwan ki." Dariya hansatu tayi kfn tace. "Yauwa don Allah adda ki koya min yanda zan kula da hamma kamalu kinga shi ya rayu a birni ni kuma a k'auye kinga muna da ban-banci kuma yace min can zasu tafi dani sokoto." Dariya Suhailat tayi sannan tace. "Karki damu zan koya miki wad'ansu abubuwan tunda nima kinga ba wai na iya bane sosae dama aunty yasmeen ce tk koya min, nan dai ta fara da koya mata yanda ake tsafta da kwalliyar jiki da ta d'aki.




_________________Kubra ta dubi k'ofar gidan su Wanda rabonta dashi har ta manta, a hankali tk takawa saboda yanda cikin jikinta ya fara tsufa, hawaye ne kawai yk sauka a fuskarta, kai tsaye babban parlourn gidan ta nufa, aunty yasmeen da y sadeeq ne zaune suna tattauna wa akan inda zasu samu labarin inda Suhailat tk, kullum sai yasmeen ta tanbayi momy idan ta shiga duba ta amma babu amsa saboda har yanxu bata iya magana sai dai taga tana hawaye duk lokacin da tayi mata wannan tanbayar, suna ckn magana kawai sai ganin kubra sukayi ckn mawuyacin hali ba don jini-jini bane da babu yanda za'a yi su gane ta saboda ta kod'e tayi bak'i sai ciki a gaba, dukda tayi musu sallama amma yanayin kukan da tk bai bari sun ji sallamar tata ba,  tsura mata ido sukayi suna kallon ta cike da mamaki, ita kam k'afafuwanta ma kasa d'aukar ta suka yi ta durk'ushe k'asa tana gunjin kuka kamar ranta zai fita, ckn zafin rai aunty yasmeen ta mik'e ta isa wajen ta kfn ta d'aga hannu ta wanka mata mari sannan ckn karad'i tace. "Me kk dawo gidan nan kiyi kubra byn kin tafi yawon barikin ki shekara da shekaru baki dawo ba sai yau da kk kwaso abun kunyar ki zaki doshi gidan mu ki shafa mana masifa ko"!?  to maza ki kama hanya ki fita don gidan nan ba gidan mazinata bane." Marin da aunty yasmeen tayi mata da kuma maganganun da tk mata baisa taji zafi ko ciwo ba, dama ta shirya tarar duk abinda za'a yi mata fiye da wannan ma tunda itace wacce ta jawa kanta, hannu biyu ta had'a tana rok'on aunty yasmeen "don Allah don annabi aunty kuyi min rai kada ku kore ni, nasan nayi mugayen laifuka a wajen ku amma don Allah ku yafe min, na gane kuskure na, bazan k'ara ba wlh nayi da nasani da nadama marar amfani na cuci rayuwa ta......."kuka ne ya k'wace mata ta cigaba da yi tana shesheka kamar zata shid'e, jikin su yai sanyi musamman y sadeeq baya so yaga mutum ckn damuwa balle kuma y'ar uwar sa, ita kam yasmeen ko kad'an bata ji tausayin kubra ba sai dai jikinta yayi sanyi sosae, a hankali y sadeeq ya k'arasa wajen da suke ya mik'ar da kubra tsaye ya kaita wani d'aki yace ta zauna a nan,   byn ya dawo ya kalli yasmeen wacce tk ta faman huci kfn yayi magana tace."haba yaya me yasa zamu kar6i yarinyar nan."? " saboda jinin mu ce kuma hannun ka baya ru6ewa ka yanke, haba yasmeen wanne irin tunani ne dk yanxu idan muka kore ta munsan inda zata tafi." "Inda ta baro mana can xata koma." Ta fad'a ranta a 6ace, "shikenan ni dai na kar6e ta kuma kinsan koba komai zamu iya samun labarin inda little sis tk tunda da ita aka shirya komai." Ckn sauri Aunty yasmeen tace."hakane bari naje na tanbaye ta. " ta fad'a tana k'ok'arin juya wa, y sadeeq ya tare ta yana fad'in. " me yasa kk da garaje ne wai, ki duba halin da tk ciki na tabbata ko kinje babu abinda zata iya fad'a miki, ki bari a hankali ckn siyasa sai mu tanbaye ta. "

Yasmeen ta koma ta zauna jikinta a sanyaye xuciyar ta na mata suya, sahla ta d'auka wacce tk zaune tana wasanta yarinyar ta 6angale mata baki tana dariya dukda cewa bata jin dad'in xuciyar ta amma saida ta mayar mata da murmushi ta dubi yarinyar tace."Sahla ina k'aunar maman ki Suhailat wannan dalilin ne ma yasa na sanya miki sahla don na dinga tuna wa da ita a koda yaushe, nasani suhailat duk halin da ta tsinci kanta baxata lalata rayuwar ta ba kamar yanda kubra tayi ba. " y sadeeq da ya tsaya yana kallon ta murmushi yayi kfn yace. "Addu'a zamu dage dayi Allah yasa tana hannu na gari." Idonta fal hawaye tace. "Ameen." 

Satin su d'aya farouq ya koma wajen karatun sa ya bar Yasmeen saboda yasan tana ckn damuwa baxata iya binsa ba kuma shima 3months ne ya rage masa gaba d'aya.


Saida kubra tayi kwana 2 a gidan sannan dady yasan ta dawo aikuwa ya tubure baxata zauna masa a gida ba, ya sadeeq da aunty yasmeen suka dinga bashi hak'uri amma yak'i, yace. "Rabi ma da kk ga na k'yale ta a nan albarkacin ku tk ci wlh, saida y sadeeq yayi masa dogon bayani ckn nutsuwa sannan ya amince, amma da sharad'in ko parlour bai yadda ta fito ba, to itama hukuncin na dady baiyi mata tsauri ba saboda ita kanta ba son fitowar tk ba, don aunty yasmeen bata sakar mata fuska kwata-kwata, lokacin da suka tanbaye ta inda aka kai Suhailat kuka ta dinga yi kamar ranta zai fita saida suka yi mata da gsk sannan ta fad'a musu gskyr abinda ta sani, ba aunty yasmeen ba hattah y sadeeq saida ya fitar da hawaye saboda yanda suka ji irin rashin imanin da mahaifiyar su ta aikata, gashi kuma kaltume ta fad'a musu an nemi yarinyar an rasa shi yasa ma matar ta tafi da momy aka yi mata wannan abun, ranar dai haka suka wuni duk gidan babu wal-wala hatta baby sahla da ta lura babu meyi mata wasa sai ta wuni tana sharar baccinta.


___________________Baffah haladu ya kira kamal da fahad akan maganar tariya kamal yace." Ni baffah ina da gida na a can sokoto dama kuma hutu na ya k'are a wajen aiki a can nk so ta tare. " Baffah yai murmushin su na manya sannan ya juya wajen FK yace. "To kai bokan turai me kace." Sosa k'eya yayi sannan yace. "Ai baffah ina ganin nima kano zamu tafi da ita." gyara tsaiwa baffah yai kfn ya fara magana. " To babu matsala tunda kunfi so ku tafi dasu sai ku shirya nan da kwana 2 ku tarkata ku bar mana gida." Suka yi dariya Kamal yace. Au korar mu ake yi kuma baffah."? "Eh,  mana tunda mun gama aikin mu ai sai ku tafi."




Ckn kwana 2 suka shirya mama kamal da hansatu suka hau motar sokoto, ita kam suhailat da FK a ckn motar ta, zasu tafi, babu Wanda zai raka amare baffah haladu yace daga baya sai aje tunda da nisa sai dai ya saida shanaye kusan goma ya damk'awa fk da kamal yace kowanne ya siyawa matarsa kayan d'aki, da farko k'in kar6a sukayi amma baffah yace. "Ni ba ku na bawa ba hakkin y'ay'a na na sauke, don haka babu ruwan ku."



Haka kowa ya sanya matarsa a gaba suka tafi shi dai kamal bai samu sakewa ba saboda idon mama sannan gashi motar haya ce,shi kam FK tunda suka fara tfy ya takura mata da y'an ta6e-ta6e, ba shiri ta k'irk'iri baccin k'arya ta lumshe ido har baccin gskn yazo, ganin tayi bacci sai ya kwantar mata da kujera saboda tafi jin dad'in baccin shi kuma ya maida hankalin sa wajen tuk'i amma duk byn y'an mintuna sai ya juyo ya kalleta.




FK&SUHAILAT sai mun had'u a kanon dabo............



UMMU MASHKUR CE MANAZARTA

Misalin 4:30 suka shigo kano, kai tsaye gidan ammy ya nufa da ita, kallon sa tayi cike da gajiya tace. "Ba gidan mu zaka kaini ba."? Shiru yai mata kamar baiji ba saida ta k'ara mai-maitawa, sannan yace." Shi kuma gidan mu aikin me yk."? Ya fad'a ba tare da ya kalleta ba saboda haushi yaji da tace ya kaita can, wace rayuwa me dad'i ta tsinta a gidan da har zata yi d'okin a kaita can, ganin ya had'a ransa itama sai ta ja bakinta tayi shiru, sai yanxu ta tuna idan ma taje gidan wajen wa xataje byn momy ce da kaltume kawai a gidan sai dady Wanda aka rabata dashi, koba komai tayi missing mahaifin ta zataso ta ganshi ko daga nesa ne, da wannan tunanin suka iso gate d'in gidan yai horn gate man yaxo ya bud'e yana ta faman yi masa sannu da zuwa, byn yayi parking ya fito ya bud'e mata a hankali ta sauko da alama ta gaji, ckn tsokana yace. "Idan bazaki iya tfy ba na d'auke ki na shiga dk ciki." Turo d'an cute lips d'in ta tayi kfn tace. "Zan iya." Murmushi yayi a ransa yana tausayawa lips d'in nan nata duk randa ya damk'eshi, ya lashi nasa lips d'in da ya bushe sannan ya kama hannun ta ya jata ciki, duk k'ok'arin ta na k'wace hannun ta kasa saboda ya rik'e sosae,  haka ta hak'ura suka shiga.


Ammy ce zaune ita da me aikin ta suna hira, ckn tsananin mamaki tk bin tilon d'an nata da kallo saboda bai fad'a mata yana hanya ba,juyo da kallon ta tayi wajen suhailat wacce kanta ke k'asa har yanxu bai sakar mata hannu ba, ammy ta mik'e ta taro su tana fad'in. "Gsky son baka kyauta min ba me yasa baka shaida min kuna hanya ba." Murmushi yayi kfn yace. "Ammy surprise din ki nake so nayi." Itama murmushin tayi sannan tace. "Lallai kam kayi surprise d'ina amma dai tunda kasan ba kai kad'ai bane ai sai ka fad'a min ko." Ta fad'a tana kallon Suhailat, ita kam tsugunna wa tayi tace. "Ammy ina wuni."

Ammy ta d'ago ta tana fad'in. "Haba d'iya ta ki bari idan kk huta sai mu gaisa yanxu taso muje kinji." Har lokacin fk bai saki hannun ba da ta tsugunna ma shi sai ya sunkuya, kallon sa ammy tayi kfn tace. "Mlm ka sakar mata hannu na kaita d'aki." Shagwa6e fuska yayi sannan yace. "Yanxu saboda Allah ammy don kin ganmu tare ni ko tani bakya yi gaba d'aya kin tattara kulawar ki kin bata."


"Eh, d'in ai na fad'a maka na daina wahalar da kaina akan yi maka wani abu idan kaji haushi a ckn satin nan ayi auren ka huta." Dariya yayi ammy ta sosa masa inda yk masa k'aik'ayi don ma baya son fad'a mata da wuri, muryar shi cike da fara'a yace."yanxu dai ammy komai yazo k'arshe na huta da gorin ki tunda gashi na dawo na samu dangi na sannan na nemo mata ta gata nan ma a gaban ki."

"To fa zaka fara shirmen naka ba, to ai daman nasan itace wacce zaka aura, kaga ni ka tsaida mu ka wuce kaima kayi wanka, idan kun huta sai ka bani labarin inda ka nemo min d'iya ta."


Murmushi FK kawai yayi ya wuce part dn sa don yin wanka, ita kam suhailat dad'i ne ya ishe ta da ya kasance ammy bata san komai akan maganar ba, saida ammy ta kaita har d'aki, ta nuna mata komai d'akin ya had'u sosae dama saboda special bak'i aka tanade shi, ta ajiye hijabinta a kan d'an mtsakaicin gadon ta shiga bathroom don yin wanka, byn tayi wanka ta d'auro alwala saboda yin sallar la'asar, tana fitowa ta samu wata sabuwar doguwar riga da sabon hijabi, bata tsaya 6ata lokaci ba ta d'auka ta sanya a jikinta tayi sallar sannan taxo wajen mirror ta d'an yi light make-up, byn ta gama kasa fita parlour tayi sai ta samu guri ta zauna tana bin d'akin da kallo tana d'an murmushi.

Ammy ganin har lokacin suhailat bata fito ba ga fk tuntuni ya fito sai tanbayar ammy yk tana ina, ammy ta k'walawa talatu y'ar aikin ta kira tace."kije ki kira bakuwar nan tana can d'ak....."

Kfn ammy ta k'arasa maganar fk ya mik'e tsaye ya doshi k'ofar d'akin yana fad'in. "Na hutar dk talatu yi tfyr ki." Ammy bata samu damar yin magana ba saboda har ya shiga sai ta kad'a kai tana murmushi.


A hankali ya tura k'ofar d'akin hango ta yayi zaune tayi shiru bayanta a juye,  lalla6awa yayi a hankali sai ji tayi an rungume ta ta baya, ckn tsananin tsoro ta bud'e baki zata k'wala ihu, yai saurin sanya mata hannun sa a bakinta sannan ya juyo da ita facing d'in sa ya tsare ta da ido, kirjin ta ya cigaba da harbawa ckn sauri, itama idonta a kansa yk, sai dai ta kasa furta komai, iskar bakinsa ya hura mata a ido har lokacin bai cika ta ba, tayi saurin d'auke idonta tana k'ok'arin k'wace jikinta, sai dai ya kwakume ta sosae haka ta hak'ura ta zuba wa sarautar Allah ido kamshin turaren sa na kashe mata jiki, tayi lamo a jikin sa tana shak'ar k'amshinsa, a saitin kunnenta ckn rad'a yk fad'in. "Dama ke matsoraciya ce hayaty."? 

" ko dai mijin naki kk tsoro. "? Bata bashi amsa ba saboda batada amsar da zata bashi, ya lek'a fuskarta idonta a rufe yk don baxata iya kallon k'wayar idonsa ba.

Ammy suka ji tana fad'in." Wai ya akayi naji ku shiru ne daga kira sai ka zauna ka cika ta da surutu ko." Ckn sauri ya saketa ya mik'ar da ita tsaye yana gyara mata rolling d'in mayafin rigar ta, sannan ya kamo hannun ta, a dai-dai lokacin ammy ta turo k'ofar suka had'u a bakin k'ofa, kallon sa tayi tace."Amma dai ka cika shirme yaro na, me yasa zaka zo ka dameta byn gajiyar da ta kwaso ko abinci bata ci ba amma kazo kana mata surutu ko." Murmushi yayi sannan yace. "No ammy na same ta tana yin sallah ne." Ya fad'a lokacin da yk kallon suhailat suka had'a ido ya d'aga mata girarsa d'aya, tayi saurin d'auke kanta, ammy ta kamo hannun ta tana fad'in. "Sakar min y'a ta taci abinci tunda kai ba yunwa kk ji ba." Ta fixge hannun suhailat daga nasa ta ja ta suka fita murmushi yayi yana shafa kwantacciyar sumar da tayiwa fuskar sa k'awanya, sannan yabi byn su.



Ammy da kanta tayi serving d'in suhailat wacce ta kasa nutsuwa taci abinci, saboda kunyar ammy da tk ji ga kuma fk da yk takura mata da wani shu'umin kallo Wanda yk jefa ta ckn wani yanayi, lura da haka da ammy tayi yasa tace."d'iya ta d'auki abincin ki tafi d'akin ki kici kinji." Sai kuwa fk yai saurin cewa. "Bari na kai miki d'aki." Ammy ta rik'e mishi hannu Wanda yk k'ok'arin d'aukar plate d'in, tace. "Ba'a so bazaka kai mata ba, salon kaje ka k'ara takura mata ko."? Tashi kinji y'a ta, suhailat ta d'auki plate d'in ta nufi d'akin har ckn ranta taji dad'in yanda ammy ta hana fk binta saboda ita yanxu har ta fara tsoron sa saboda salon da yk mata.


Ammy ta kalli fahad kfn tace." Ina jinka fahad baka ce min komai ba ina ta jiran ka kana ja min rai." Murmushi yayi sannan ya gyara xama."kada ki damu ammy yanxu zaki ji labari mai matuk'ar dad'i, dama a waya na fad'a miki yanda nasha wahala kfn na samu dangin mahaifi na har zuwa lokacin da na gansu to a lokacin na fad'a miki na gansu amma ban fad'a miki auren da aka d'aura min da y'ar uwata a can ba." Zare ido ammy tayi kfn tace. "Aure fa kace!"? To ita kuma wannan ya zaka yi da ita."?

Dariya yayi kfn yace." Ai itace y'ar uwar tawa kuma da ita aka d'aura min auren, a lokacin da aka d'aura auren daga ni har ita bamu ga juna ba saida ga baya." Nan dai ya shaida mata duk abinda ya faru da yanda zumunchin su yk da suhailat.


Ckn tsananin mamaki ammy tace."ikon Allah, ashe ita d'in y'ar uwarka ce ta jini, dubi yanda aka d'aura auren ku ba tare da kowanne a ckn ku yasani ba, lallai auren ku yaxama wata ishara Allah ya sanya alkhairi ya baku zaman lpy. "

"Ameen ammy." Ya fad'a yana murmushi, sannan ya fad'a mata sak'on inna na neman yafiyar ta da tk, ammy ta goge k'wallar da tk ta 6oyewa tun lokacin da ta samu labarin mahaifin fahad ya rasu, dama tana yawan mafarki da hakan, ckn dabara ta goge k'wallar tace. "Ai ni tun tuni na yafe mata kuma ban rik'e ta a raina ba." Haka suka cigaba da hira fk yana bata labarin yanda aka nuna masa soyayya ckn dangin sa.


Satin su d'aya  da dawo wa ammy tace fk ya kaita gidan su, baiyi musu ba saboda koba komai shi kansa zaiso momy ta ga yadda Allah yk ikon sa, sannan gashi y sadeeq da aunty yasmeen basa nan kuma dady yana buk'atar taimako don haka dole yaje ya nema wa dady magani abinda yasa bai yi tunanin hakan a baya ba saboda shima tasa matsalar tasha kansa, yanxu kam ya gama da tashi matsalar saura wannan, ya d'auki wayar sa ya danna nomber y sadeeq kamar da wasa sai yaji ta shiga, rabonsa da ya same shi a waya har ya manta, mamakin sa bai k'aru ba saida yaji muryar sadeeq yana sallama, ckn zak'uwa fk ya amsa y sadeeq yace. "Idonka kenan doctor ka manta da rayuwar mu gaba d'aya, yau wata na 2 a Nigeria amma babu kai babu hk, ka, ni kuma abubuwa sun taru sunyi min yawa, shi yasa ban neme ka ba." Fahad yayi murmushi kfn yace. "Ayi min hak'uri babban yaya, wata matsala ce ta sha gaba na, amma dai zancen bazai yiwu a waya ba kana ina nazo na same ka."?

Y sadeeq yace." Ina gida yanxu haka. " "OK gani nan xuwa." FK ya fad'a lokacin da ya kashe wayar, keys d'in sa kawai ya d'auka ya fito ganin ammy bata parlour ya lalla6a ckn sand'a ya nufi d'akin suhailat, saboda tunda ammy taji an d'aura auren tk sa masa ido akan shiga d'akin tace yayi k'ok'ari ya gyara gidan sa sai ta bashi matarsa can ya k'arata amma baxata iya da wannan rashin kunyar ba.


Ckn sa'a ya yana murd'a k'ofar ta bud'e, can bisa gado ya hango ta a kwance tana bacci ckn nutsuwa a hankali numfashin ta yk sauka, k'ura mata ido yayi k'aunar ta na k'ara huda xuciyar sa, komai nata special ne, duk abinda tk yi kyan ta k'aruwa yk, musamman da ya kwana 2 bai ganta ba saboda ammy 6oye masa ita tk, ita kanta ya lura tafi sakewa idan baya nan, yayi saurin sanya wa kofar sannan a hankali yk takawa har yazo bakin gadon kai tsaye ya haye bisa gadon ya kwanta a bayanta yana shak'ar k'amshin ta.


Ckn kwana biyun da suka dawo wani sabon gyara na musamman ammy tk mata cimar ta ma daban ake mata aikuwa ta k'ara yin 6ul-6ul da ita fatar ta ta k'ara laushi sai shek'i tk, janyo ta jikin sa yayi yana d'an shafa ta, kamar a mafarki taji ana ta6a ta, ba tayi saurin bud'e idonta ba saboda tasan babu Wanda zai shigo d'akin ta, fk ne kuma ammy tana kula mata dashi, juyi ta k'ara yi ta cigaba da baccinta amma sai bacci yak'i zuwa saboda new massage d'in da jikinta yk kar6a Wanda tunda tk bata ta6a koda mafarkin hakan ba balle kuma a gsk, jin abun yana k'ara yawa ta bud'e yalwatattun idonta ta zuba su a kansa, shi kam tuni ya fara rasa nutsuwar sa idon sa a lumshe yana k'ara manna ta da jikin sa, k'ok'arin k'wace jikinta ta farayi amma ya hana saboda ba karamar runguma yayi mata ba, kuka ta sanya masa ganin hankalin sa ya fara tafiya Wanda wannan kukan nata ne ya dawo dashi daga duniyar da ya lula, ya sassauta ruk'on da yayi mata yana k'ok'arin bud'e idonsa wad'anda yk ji su manne tamkar an sanya musu super glue, tsura mata kyawawan idonsa wad'anda suka riked'e suka koma jajaye yayi, ita kam nata idon hawaye ne kawai yk fitowa daga ckn su,suna ckn wannan halin suka ji ammy na nocking k'ofar, tana kiran sunan suhailat, a firgice FK ya sake ta ya sauka daga kan gadon yana zare ido, ita kanta a ysorace tk don bazata so ammy ta ganshi a d'akin ba.

"Yarinya ta ki tashi haka baccin ya isa." Muryar ammy ta k'ara fad'a, a kunne ya rad'a mata."kada ki bari ammy ta fahimci ina d'akin nan, ki tashi ki bud'e mata bari na shiga toilet. " d'aga masa kai kawai tayi tana goge hawayen dk kan face d'in ta, mik'ewa yayi ya shiga bathroom, ita kuma taje ta bud'e k'ofar tana d'an murza ido, ammy ta bita da kallo kfn tace. "Hala har yanxu baccin kk gashi anyi sallah tun d'axu kiyi sallah kixo kici abincin ki gashi can an gama." "To ammy ngd." 

Harar ta ammy tayi ckn wasa kfn tace. "Ba na hana ki yawan yi min gdy akan komai ba."? Sunkuyar da kanta tayi tana d'an murmushi, kfn tace." Kiyi hak'uri ammy na daina. " "Yauwa ko ke fa, amma me ya samu idonki kamar kinyi kuka ko." Gaban ta ya fad'i ckn in-ina tace."um um ba kuka nayi ba don nayi bacci da yawa ne shi yasa yai haka." Ammy tace. "Are you sure."? 
Ta k'ara d'aga kanta, ammy tace." To maza kije kiyi wanka kiyi sallah kizo ina jiran ki. " to." Ta fad'a tana k'ok'arin juya wa ta tsinci muryar ammy na fad'in."son bai shigo ba tun d'axu na lek'a part dn sa ban ganshi ba. " ckn sauri tace. " bai shigo ba ni ina bacci kuma d'akin a rufe yk." Murmushi ammy tayi ganin yanda duk ta rikice, sannan tace."Shikenan. " ta juya ta tafi ita kuma ta rufe d'akin, fk ya fito daga bathroom yana goge gumi ya dubi suhailat yace. " kinyi k'ok'ari bari na tafi dama wani guri zanje shine nazo nayi sallama da my wife. "

"Don Allah ka daina xuwa kada ammy ta ganka." D'an k'aramin tsaki yaja kfn yace. "Itama ammy banda abun ta ya za'a yi ta hana ni xuwa wajen ki, koda yk nasan abinda tk tsoro kada a sama mata jika ba ayi tariya ba." Ya k'arasa maganar yana lek'a fuskarta, da gudu ta fad'a bathroom tana rufe fuska, murmushi yayi sannan ya bud'e k'ofar ckn sand'a yayi waje, yayi sa'a kuwa ammy bata ganshi ba don haka kai tsaye ya wuce gidan su suhailat wajen y sadeeq kamar yanda suka yi dashi.



Lokacin da ya iso gidan yasha mamaki da yaga aunty yasmeen, nan suka dinga hira yana d'aga baby sahla yana mata wasa, yasmeen ta dube shi tace. "Yanxu doctor don Allah kana ina aka salwantar da rayuwar masoyiyar ka."? Fk ya zaro ido kamar da gsk yace." Me kk nufi aunty yasmeen, ban fahimci inda maganar ki ta dosa ba, kina nufin bata ckn gidan nan ni fa tun d'axu kawaici kawai nk muku shi yasa ban tanbeku ita ba."



Y sadeeq yace. "Kai dai bari doctor." Nan ya bashi labarin da kubra ta basu yanda suka d'auki suhailat ita da momy suka kaita gidan y'an lesbians, dukda cewa a halin yanxu yana tare da suhailat, amma saida ransa ya 6aci da yaji inda momy ta kaita, musamman da ya tuna lokacin da yazo momyn da kanta tk fad'a masa suhailat ta tafi yawon ta.

Ya furxar da wani huci me zafi tabbas ba don momy uwa ce a wajen su sadeeq ba da sai yayi shari'a da ita, amma yanxu bazai yi gaggawar sanar dasu yana tare da suhailat ba sai ya tabbatar da sun matsu da ganin ta.

Kallon y sadeeq yayi sannan ya fara magana. "Gsky ban ji dad'in wannan labarin ba kwata-kwata, kuma nima abinda yasa wasu abubuwan suka faru ban sani ba saboda bana ckn garin nan,kasan nazo na tarar da dady ckn wani mummunan hali shi da suhailat, sai nayi tunanin nayi maganar auren mu da ita, lokacin da na tuntu6i dady da maganar sai ya nuna min baima ganeni ba kai harma ita kanta cewa yayi ya manta ta, amma sai yace min naje na turo iyaye na zai aura min ita, lokacin da naji haka hankali na ya tashi kasan dai yadda ake ciki ammy bata sanar dani wane mahaifi na ba, da dady na dogara jin hakan yasa nazo na tayar wa ammy hankali saida ta fad'a min inda zan nemi asali na, wannan dalilin yasa na tafi can jihar Adamawa watana 3 ina bincike amma ban samu ba, ckn ikon Allah sai gashi daga baya na samu, sai dai kuma na samu ana wata rigima akan auren wasu yara a gidan kakanni na, to nayi k'ok'arin sulhunta abun d'aya daga ckn Wanda za'a aura wa yaran yace shi baya son auren gaba d'aya shine kanin mahaifi na ya maida auren kaina aka d'aura min aure da y'ar uwa ta." Su duka suka d dube ckn sauri aunty yasmeen ranta a 6ace tace. "Lallai ma doctor ashe soyayyar da kk fad'in kana yiwa y'ar uwa ta duk k'arya ne tunda gashi ka iya auren wata alhalin ita bama asan inda tk ba." Fk ya marairaice fuska kamar gsk yace. "Haba yasmeen Ku yin min uxuri mana ni kaina abun ba a ckn shiri yazo min ba, sannan ban yadda da auren ba saida na fad'a wa shi baffan nawa inada wacce nk so, ya amince sannan yace naje zai nema min auren ta da kansa idan na dawo." Haka kurum aunty yasmeen taji bata son ganin fk don haka ta zuba tsaki tabar parlourn, fk ya dubi sadeeq Wanda shima mood d'in sa ya canxa yace. "Babban yaya kasa baki yasmeen ta fahimce ni mana." Sadeeq ya had'iye nasa 6acin ran sannan yace. "Karka damu ka rabu da ita zata sauko ne yanxu tana ckn halin rad'ad'in rashin y'ar uwar ta ne, kuma sai kazo da wannan maganar kasn su mata tana taya ta kishi ne." Fk yai murmushi kfn yace. "Na sani komai zai dai-dai ta insha'allah, ina dady ne."?



Sadeeq yace." Ya d'an fita ." "OK da daddare idan dady ya dawo zan taho da matar tawa ya sanya mana albarka." Da k'yar y sadeeq ya amsa mishi don shima ya fara jin haushin fk d'in.






Lokacin da ya dawo gida ya sanar da ammy yanda suka yi dasu, ammy tayi dariya kfn tace. "Lallai son baka da dama yanxu suma sai kayi musu wasa da hankali kamar yanda kayi min."? Dariya yayi." Itama bazan fad'a mata inda zan kaita ba kawa kice mata zata raka ni unguwar."


Haka kuwa akayi byn sallar magrib ammy ta shirya suhailat ckn tsadaddun kaya, shima irin color nata ne sai dai shi waniyadi ne me laushi ita kuma less ne amma kala d'aya, sunyi bala'in kyau, haka suka jera ya rik'e hannun ta ammy tana k'ara fad'a masa amanar y'ar ta ta bashi shi kam sai murmushi yk, saida ya biya wani shopping mall yayi mata siyayya wai tayi tsaraba inda zasu je, ckn siyayyar harda wani zungureren hijab da kuma nik'ab yace ta sanya, bata yi masa musu ba ta cire mayafin jikinta ta sanya hijabin da kansa ya d'aura mata nik'ab d'in sannan yaja motar suka nufi ainihin gidan su suhailat........

Da kansa ya d'aura mata nik'ab d'in sannan yaja motar suka nufi ainihin gidan su suhailat.....


*continues*
Isah ya dawo gida shima ckn wani irin yanayi mara dad'i domin kuwa ya kwaso cutar sida wacce aka fi sani da AIDS a jikin matan turawa wad'anda yk bibiya, ganin irin ciwukan da yk yi abokan sa suka tattaroshi suka dawo dashi Nigeria, lokacin da aka kawo shi gida dady cewa yayi ba zai zauna masa a gida ba, ya nemi wani uban ba shi ba, sauran yaran suka dinga yi masa magiya da kuka sannan ya hak'ura ya bashi d'aki a BQ na gidan can kusa da inda ake kula da momy yace suje can su k'arata, sannan be yafe ba idan aka d'auki k'wandalar sa aka nema wa isah ko kubra ko momy magani,da wannan sharad'in suke zaune y sadeeq ne ya d'auki nauyin su.

 lokacin da isah yaga halin da momy da kubra suke ciki kuka ya dinga yi yana fad'in."momy kin cuce mu." Ita kanta momyn sai dai aga hawaye yana zuba ta gefen idonta don har yanxu bata iya magana sai dai nuni.

Su aunty yasmeen da sadeeq kullum sai sun shiga sun duba mahaifiyar su dukda cewa suna jin tausayin ta amma d'acin abinda tayiwa Suhailat ya kasa goge wa daga ckn zuciyoyin su.

----------------------------------------
Tunanin ta bai hasaso mata inda zasu je ba, kuma gashi ya sanya mata nikab, don haka har suka iso gidan bata san inda ya kawo ta ba saida yayi parking ya bud'e mata k'ofar motar sannan ta fahimci a harabar gidan mahaifin ta tk ckn farinciki ta kalle shi kfn tace. "Dama nan zamu xo."? Yayi dariya
"Ko kin d'auka siyar dk zanyi." Murmushi tayi duk farinciki ya isheta ta matsu taga mahaifin ta, dukda tana fargabar yanda mutanen gidan zasu kar6e ta, ckn nutsuwa ya kalleta yace. "Zamu shiga dk ciki amma fa da sharad'i ina so ki rufe idon ki koda mun shiga karki bud'e har sai nace ki bud'e idan kuma baki yadda ba yanxun nan zamu koma ko shiga baza muyi ba." Ckn rashin fahimta tace. "Saboda me zan rufe ido ko so kk nayi tuntu6e na fad'i." "Ya za'a yi ki fad'i kina tare da bodyguard d'in ki, kulle idonki maza mu shiga." Bata k'ara magana ba tayi kamar yanda ya umarce ta, shi kuma ya kama hannun ta suka shiga, kai tsaye babban parlourn gidan suka dosa banda fad'uwa babu abinda kirjin ta yk yi,fk ne yai sallama dady d y sadeeq suka amsa suna bin shi da kallo yayinda yasmeen ta mik'e tana k'ok'arin barin parlourn ranta a 6ace saboda ganin doctor yazo da matar tasa har gida don ya raina musu sense, ckn fara'a dady yace. "A'a sannun ku da zuwa yaro na, d'iyar tawa ce wannan aka kawo min d'axu abubakar yk sanar dani xuwan ka to Masha'allah ku zauna mana." Har gaban dady  fahad yaja ta suka tsugunna ya sanya hannu ya d'aga nik'ab d'in da ya rufe fuskarta, dady ya zabura ya mik'e tsaye tsaye kfn da k'arfi yace. "Mama na." Aunty yasmeen wacce ta kusa shiga d'aki ta juyo da gudu, shima y sadeeq mik'ewa tsaye yayi yana fad'in. "Little sis ina kk shiga." Ita kam suhailat wasu hawayen farinciki ne suka kawo wa idonta ziyara, ta fad'a jikin dady tana rera kuka, shi kansa dady daurewa kawai yayi amma saura kad'an yayi hawayen, jikin aunty yasmeen har rawa yk ta k'arasa itama ta rungume suhailat suna kuka fk ya koma gefe ya hard'e hannu yana kallon su, ckn tausayawa ba k'aramin tausayi suka bashi ba, dady yayi k'ok'arin lallashin su suka d'an yi shiru, sannan yasmeen da Suhailat suka kalli juna sai kuma suka fashe da dariya.


Dady ya kalli yasmeen da sadeeq ckn yanayin tuhuma yace. "Amma abubakar ya akayi naga mama na tare da fahad byn kunce min tana can tana karatu a wajen y'ar uwar ta."? Sunkuyar da kansa yayi ckn jin kunyar k'aryar da yayi wa dady, dady ya juyo ya kalli yasmeen wacce tk ckn tsananin farinciki yace." Fatima ko da akwai wani abinda kk 6oye min."? Kfn tai magana y sadeeq yace. "Kayi hak'uri dady ni ne na shirya hakan saboda bana so hankalin ka ya tashi mu kanmu bamu san tana tare da d'an uwa fahad ba, don ko d'axu da yazo bai fad'a mana ba." Yai shiru yana kallon fahad, murmushi doctor yai kfn yace. "This is surprise." Ya fad'a yana duban Aunty yasmeen wacce kunyar abinda tayi masa jiya yakamata, y sadeeq yace. "Dady ranar wanka ba'a 6oyon cibi maganar gsky abinda ya faru ba zaka ji dad'in sa ba dukda cewa ya wuce, momy itace tayi silar 6acewar little sis ta hanyar kaita gidan y'an lesbians amma bari na kira maka kubra kaji daga bakinta tunda da ita aka had'a baki aka kai little sis d'in." D'aga kai dady yayi ckn tsananin mamakin mugun hali irin na rabi, y sadeeq da kansa ya tashi ya kira kubra, a tsorace tk sosae saboda Sam dady baya kula ta aunty yasmeen ma bata sakar mata fuska y sadeeq ne kad'ai yk d'an kula su sama-sama, tana shigo wa parlourn idonta a kan Suhailat gaban ta ya yanke ya fad'i haka ta k'ura mata ido ita da fk da yaki matsawa daga kusa da ita dukda aunty yasmeen tana d'ayan gefen sun sanya ta a tsakiya.

Haka kubra ta cigaba da turo k'aton cikinta tana k'ara kallon Suhailat tayi mata wani azababben kyau da kwarjini ga kuma mutumin da taso irin son da bata ta6a yiwa wani mutum ba, jikinta a sanyaye ta k'arasa ta zauna y sadeeq ne yace. "Kubra ki nutsu ki fad'a musu labarin da kk sanar damu akan abinda kk shirya wa little sis ke da momy." Ckn kuka ta fad'a musu komai, ran dady yai k'ololuwar 6aci ya kalli suhailat ckn tsananin tausayin ta sannan yace. "Mama na ki fad'a mana yanda akayi kk ku6uta daga sharrin wad'annan azzaluman." Itama ta basu labari tunda ga farko, ranar da momy ta d'auke ta a mota ta kaita gidan hjy sara, har sanda ta ku6uta da taimakon zainab (zeeza) sannan ta basu labarin had'u warta da mama da yanda akayi taje Adamawa ta had'u da dangin ta, daga nan ta tsaya bata basu labarin had'u war su da fahad ba.

Kowa a gurin saida ya tausayawa Suhailat don ma Allah ya tak'aita mata wahala ya had'a ta da dangin mahaifiyar ta ckn sauri ba tare da ta tagayyara ba.

Dady ne ya kalli doctor Wanda ya shiru gumi duk ya tsatsafo masa saboda tunanin irin halin da masoyiyar sa ta shiga, dady yace. "To fahad saura kai sai ka bamu labarin yanda aka yi kk had'u da mama na a can Adamawa d'in." Doctor ya gyara zama ya fara basu labarin tun lokacin da yazo wajen dady a lokacin ya tarar da dadyn ckn wani irin yanayi da yanda sukayi dashi akan yaje ya turo iyayen sa akan maganar auren su da Suhailat, haka ya fad'a musu irin wahala da gwa-gwarmayar da yasha kfn ya samu ganin dangin sa, da yanda yaje ya tarar da badak'alar auren hansatu da ja'e xuwa yanda akayi aka d'aura musu aure da suhailat ba tare da kowannen su yasan Wanda aka aura masa har zuwa yanxu da suke zaune tare da ammy.


Kowa a gurin mamaki sosae yk yi saboda jin yanda Allah ya juya al'amarin Ashe doctor d'an uwan suhailat ne na jini shi yasa suke sonsa tamkar jinin su, babu yanda Allah baya ikon sa ka duba yanda ya juya mugun nufin su momy akan Suhailat ya xamo mata alkhairi: alkhairi mana tunda gashi silar hakan ta had'u da dangin mahaifiyar ta sannan gashi burinta ya cika na auren masoyin ta.


Dady ya kalli fk yace. "Yanxu alhj kabiru shine mahaifin ka."? D'aga kansa fahad yai tare da cewa." Eh dady shine mahaifi na. " girgixa kansa dady yayi kfn yace.''Ai shi d'in abokin kasuwanci nane ta dalilin sa na had'u da k'anwar sa Khadija mahaifiyar Suhailat na kuma shine Wanda ya tsaya min har na aure ta, naji mutuwar sa kamar yanda har yanxu mutuwar k'anwar sa ba ta daina cin xuciya ta ba, ubangiji Allah yayi musu rahma gaba d'aya dasu da sauran musulmai." Suka amsa da "ameen."



Dady ya dubi y sadeeq yace."A wanne irin hali kasameni lokacin da ka dawo abubakar. "?

Y sadeeq ya bashi labarin yanda ya same shi, sannan ya  fad'a masa duk abinda ya faru tsakanin sa da kaltume da yanda yasa aka kamata yanxu haka tana can tana girbar abinda ta Shuka a gidan d'an kande, dady yai shiru yana tunani babu shakkah rabi itace babbar wacce ta fi cutar dasu dashi da suhailat sannan itace ta bawa kaltume damar yin duk abinda tayi don haka dolene ya d'auki mataki na k'arshe a kanta, dubi yanda ta sanya y'ay'an sa 2 suka tarwatsa rayuwar su kubra da isah gashi yanxu ita ta kwaso ciki shi kuma ya kwaso ciwo.



Ya mik'e tsaye yana huci ya dawo asalin Alhj Ibrahim mu'azzam me kwarjini da isa a gidan sa ba jani talau ba kamar yanda momy da kaltume suka maida shi tsawon lokaci, ya kalli yaran sa da suke zaune yace." Ku tashi muje wajen rabi da akwai maganar da zamuyi a gaban ta."

Haka suka d'unguma suka shiga inda ake jinyar momy suhailat ta tsorata da yanda ta ga momy, dady yasa aka kira isah wanda shima yana can yana fama da kansa byn kowa ya zauna dady ya kalli gaba d'aya yaransa  yace. "Ina so Ku zama sheda na k'arasa yanke igiyar aure na 2 da tk kan rabi sannan a yau ba sai gobe ba Ku nema mata wajen zama amma ba'a gida na ba, su wad'annan zan iya hak'ura na rik'e su tunda y'ay'a nane." Ya fad'a yana nuna kubra da isah wad'anda suka raku6e a gefe suna faman zare ido, yana gama fad'in haka ya juya da niyyar barin d'akin,muryar momy ce ta dakatar dashi wacce tk fita a hankali tace."Don Allah alhj kayi hak'uri ka saurare ni akwai maganar da nk so na fad'a maka."tsaye wa yayi ba tare da ya juyo ba, ita kam momy yunk'ura wa tayi da niyyar ta tashi zaune amma ta kasa, y sadeeq da aunty yasmeen ne suka kama ta suka zaunar da ita byn sun jingina byn ta a jikin pillow, yau kam ana ganin jikin nata da sauk'i tunda gashi har magana tk yi, momy ta matse k'wallar da tk ta faman yi mata anbaliya a kunci ta dubi mutanen gurin inda kallon ta ya tsaya akan Suhailat wacce ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tausayin momy na huda xuciyar ta,a hankali ckn sautin kuka momy ta fara magana. 


"Hak'ik'a a rayuwa ta ban amfana da komai ba sai tarin abun kaico, na cutar da miji na na cutar da yarana sannan nayiwa marainiyar Allah cutar war da bazan iya lissafa wa ba." Ta d'an tsagaita da maganar tana kallon dady Wanda ya juyo shima yana sauraron abinda tk fad'a, sannan ta cigaba da cewa. "Ina me fargaba da danasanin sanar da ku cewa a ckn yara na akwai bara gurbi." "Innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji'un." Shine abinda gaba d'aya gurin suka had'a baki wajen fad'a, jikin dady yana rawa yayo kanta ckn zafin nama xuciyar sa tana tafarfasa, shak'e ta xuciyar sa tk bashi shawarar yayi amma sai ya danne koba komai zai so yaji waye ba d'an sa ba a  ckn yaran suma kansu yaran fargaba da tsoro ne suka kama su kowanne yana jiran k'arin bayani daga wajen Momy ita kam tuni wani tari me k'arfi ya sark'e ta.


Jiki a sanyaye yasmeen ta bata ruwa sannan tarin ya d'an lafa, momy ta cigaba da cewa."Na samo ciki ne a wajen wani boka na Wanda naje akan ya mallake min Alhj shine yace sai ya kusance ni kuma na amince masa tunda ga wannan lokacin na fahimci ina da shigar ciki, na koma wajen boka don mu nemi mafita amma sai yace min dolene na haifa masa d'an sa kuma kada nayi kuskuren sanar da wani ba d'an miji na bane idan kuma nayi gan-gancin sanar da wani sai ya haukatar dani, haka dole na hak'ura na haifi d'an da sunan d'an miji nane." Kuka ne yaci k'arfin ta, kowa a wajen kuka yk zuwa yanxu aunty yasmeen da kubra sun fita daga rud'ani tunda sunji ba a ckn su bane, hawaye har da majina y sadeeq yace. "Me kk so ki fad'a Momy."? Yanxu tsakanin ni da isah wani daga ckn mu d'an boka ne."?! Kin cuce mu momy kin cutar damu wayyo Allah wannan wacce iriyar rayuwa ce......"


Hatta dady kuka yk yanxu sosae tabbas yau ya k'ara gasgata rashin tunani da rashin imani irin na rabi, '' *ki fad'a min wanene a ckn su*" dady ya fad'a da k'ara ji, saida tabi kowa da kallo kfn ckn in ina ta iya cewa."Is.. isah ne ba d'anka ba alhj ka yafe min don Allah."

Dady ya goge hawayen fuskar shi sannan ckn dakakkiyar murya yace. "Babu yafiya a tsakanin mu rabi ni bazan ta6a yafe miki cutar damu da kk yi ni da y'ay'a na ba, kuma banyi bak'in ciki da ya kasance isah ba d'a na bane, dama na dad'e ina zargin haka saboda yanayin halayen ni ban ta6a aikata zina ba amma sai gashi shi harda cutar k'anjamau ya d'auko nayi nazari me yawa na kasa gane inda suka gaji wannan d'abi'ar shi da kubra by ashe ke suka gada,ba komai kije na barki da Allah sannan ina k'ara fad'a miki kiyi gaggawar bar min gida ke da shegen danki don bazan zauna da d'an boka a gida na ba." Isah ya rarrafa ya rik'e k'afar dady yana kuka me sauti yace. "Kayi min rai dady kaine gata na babu Wanda na sani bayan kai." Ya juyo ya kalli momy yace.

 "momy kin cuceni me yasa.. Me yasa kk haife ni byn kin san ba ta hanya me kyau kk samar da ni ba na tsaneki na tsaneki...." Da sauri y sadeeq ya rufe masa baki sannan ya fara yi masa fad'a. "Ba kada hankali ne isah duk abinda tayi maka ta gama da kai duk lalacewa ita d'in mahaifiyar ka ce sannan da kk cewa ta haifeka ba ta hanya me kyau ba, ita kai kad'ai ne ta samar ta wannan hanyar shin kai zaka iya tuna y'ay'a nawa ka Samar."? Jikin isah yai sanyi don kuwa gsky y sadeeq ya fad'a kawai sai ya xube a k'asa xuciyar sa na masa suya yana tirr da irin halinsa da na mahaifiyar sa.


Momy tayi murmushin takaici kfn tace." Sadeeq ni d'in nan da kk gani ban can-canci ayi min biyayya ba saboda ku kanku y'ay'a na na cutar daku sosae, kaga wannan halin da kubra tk ciki nice wacce na janyo mata, saboda naje gurin boka na inaso Suhailat ta fara yawon bin maza ya bani wani garin magani Wanda na had'a yaji dashi na ajiye sai gashi d'iya ta kubra itace tasha  saida ga baya na fahimci hakan, na d'auki yarinyar nan da hannu na na kaita gidan y'an lesbians na kar6i mak'udan kud'i don kawai rayuwar ta ta lalace, amma sai gashi hakan bata samu ba daga baya kuma nk jin labarin kubra ma tana ckn k'ungiyar y'an lesbians, sannan a dalilin haka na tsinci kaina ckn wannan halin saboda matar da na kar6i kud'in ta tace baxata yi asara ba ta had'a ni da wasu mutane marasa imani suka dinga kinga ta daga k'arshe suka yasar dani da wannan larurar, nayi nadama mara amfani saboda lokacin nadamar ya wuce ni."  Nan ta fara to nawa kanta asiri tunda ga kashe Khadija mahaifiyar suhailat da tayi da duk abinda ta aikata, basu fahimci haukacewa tayi ba saida su kaga ta fara cixgar gashin kanta, itada bata iya motsa komai nata sai gashi ta mik'e tsaye tana y'an dube-dube tana cigaba da tonawa kanta asiri.

Tun suna sauraron ta har suma hankalin su ya fara tashi ganin ta fara cire kayan jikinta, duk suka yi kanta aikuwa ta fara ihu tana shek'a dariya nice nayi kaza nice nayi kaza, har xuwan ta garin su kaltume saida ta fad'a, dady kam ficewa yayi daga d'akin don ba zai iya jinta ba su kuma suka fara k'ok'arin rik'e ta saboda tana son ta gudu ne.



Kuka dai kam suhailat da yasmeen kubra isah y sadeeq kai harma da fahad ranar sunsha shi, da k'yar mazan suka iya rik'e ta suka sanya ta a mota suka nufi gidan kula da mahaukata da ita, yayinda suka bar matan ckn yanayin kuka, kubra ta kalli suhailat ckn hawaye tace." Kiyi hak'uri y'ar uwa ta ki yafe mana nida momy mun cutar dk sosae itama cikin kuka tace. "Na yafe muku wlh kubra har ckn xuciya ta dama ban rik'e ku a raina ba, fatana Allah y bawa momy lpy." Suka amsa da "ameen. " yasmeen ta kalli suhailat ."ban ta6a ganin mutum me mugun hali irin mahaifiya ta ba sannan ban ta6a ganin mutum me kyakkyawan hali irinki mungode da kk yafe mata dukda tarin abubuwan da tayi miki keda mahaifiyar ki."

Girgixa kai Suhailat tayi kfn tace. "Babu gdy a tsakanin mu a halin yanxu addu'a ce ta dace mu dinga yi mata Allah y bata lpy."  Yasmeen tace. "Kayya suhailat amma ni nasan momy bazata ta6a samun lpy ba......."

UMMU MASHKUR CE MANAZARTA

Kayyah Suhailat amma ni nasan momy bazata ta6a samun lpy ba.....

"Haba aunty yasmeen ki daina fad'ar haka Allah ne ya d'ora mata wannan rashin lpyr kuma yanxun idan yaga dama sai ya yaye mata ba'a ja da ikon Allah.'' Suhailat ta bata amsa.

" hakane y'ar uwa ta ngd da tunatar dani da kk yi astagfirullah."


Sai wajen 12:pm suka dawo gida zuciyoyin su cike da alhinin abinda ya faru, ammy tana ta yiwa fahad waya akan ya dawo mata da yarinyar ta, a tunanin ta ko wani gurin ya kaita sukayi wannan daren don ta lura yaron nata ya cika rawar jiki, "Kiyi hak'uri ammy yanxun nan zamu dawo akwai abinda ya tsayar damu." Amsar da yk bata kenan byn sun dawo ko zama baiyi ba ya kalli suhailat yace. "To taso mu tafi ko."? Yasmeen ta kalle shi "haba doctor ka barta mu d'an gana mana idan ta kwana sai kaxo ku tafi."

Girgixa kai FK yayi sannan yace. "Ta6 lallai kina so yau ammy ta xaneni, kina gani nima aronta aka bani ki bari idan na mayar mata da y'ar ta sai kije ki aro da kanki.'' Aunty yasmeen tace."To shikenan tunda haka kace zanje da kaina wajen ammyn nasan bazata hana ni ba." Haka suka je wajen dady ya sanya musu albarka sannan suka dawo parlourn sukayi sallama da su  mijin ta ya sanya ta a gaba suka tafi kowa xuciyar sa ba dad'i.


Saida ya biya yai musu takeaways saboda basu samu damar cin komai ba a can gidan, sannan ya wuce dasu gidan ammy zaune suka same ta a parlour ranta a had'e, kallo d'aya fk yayi mata ya gano abinda tk nufi, mik'ewa ammy tayi ta kamo hannun suhailat hankalin ta ne ya k'ara tashi saboda ta lura yarinyar tana ckn damuwa a fili tace. "Subhanallah yarinya ta me nene ya faru ko yayi miki wani abun ne."? Fk yayi murmushi." Haba ammy ni me zanyi mata kawai dai wani issue d'in ne ba abinda kk nufi ba." Ita kam suhailat k'asa tayi da kanta kunyar ammy tk matuk'ar ji, saboda ta lura ita da danta basa jin kunyar jiunan su, har d'aki ammy ta raka ta shima a baya ya biyo su rik'e da ledar takeaway d'in da yayi musu, ammy ta juyo tare da harar sa. "Bashi kk binmu ne kazo kar6a."? Ya rausayar da kai sannan ckn shagwa6a yace."tun ranar da yarinyar nan taxo gidan nan na fahimci kin d'auki duk wata soyayya da kulawar da kk bani kin damk'a a hannun ta, ni yanxu tsakanin mu dk sai kora bakya so mu zauna waje d'aya." Dariya ammy tayi tare da jan dogon karan hancin sa tace. "Ja'irin yaro ai kai d'in ne na lura idan ba haka nayi maka ba rawar kanka tayi yawa, yanxu dai kaje parlour ka jira ni."

Bai ce komai ba ya mik'a mata leda d'aya daga ckn ledojin dk hannun sa, ya juya, ita kuwa ammy saida ta raka suhailat har ckn d'akin sannan ta a ajiye mata ledar, ta d'ago ta kalleta ."ki cire damuwa daga ranki don Allah d'iya ta ki kula da kanki sannan kici abinci me yawa plss." Tana gama fad'in haka ta juya ta bar suhailat wacce tabi byn ammy da kallo cike yaba kyawawan halayen ta.



A zaune ta samu fahad ya zuba uban tagumi, yayi nisa ckn tunanin yanda zaiyi k'ok'arin d'auke matarsa tunda ya lura indai a gidan nan ne ammy bazata bari ya shak'ata ba,haka kawai a dinga yi masa iyaka shi da matarsa? Ko da yk laifinsa ne da ya kawota gidan ammy, da ya wuce da ita gidan sa ai babu Wanda zai takura masa sai lokacin da yaga dama sannan zai kawo ta ta gaida ammy, amma babu komai ckn kwana 2 nan zai nema wa kansa mafita tunda ya lura ita suhailat d'in bata damu ba.


Ammy ta d'an ta6a shoulder d'in sa ganin yayi nisa ckn tunani tafi 10 minuets amma bai san taxo ba, firgigit yayi yana kallon ammy, "son wanne irin tunani kk haka me zurfi."? Ko dai kana tunanin yanda zaka d'auke matar ka ne tunda na takura maka."? Da sauri ya kalli ammy saboda baiyi tunanin zata gano shi da wuri haka ba kansa ya fara sosawa yana neman k'arya ko dabarar da zaiyi wa ammy sai yaji tace." Fahad nasan zaka damu da yanda nk taka tsan-tsan da kai akan yarinyar nan, to ina so kasani ba don wani abun nk maka haka sai don ina son yarinyar tafi haka daraja a wajen ka, nasani kana sonta amma bana buk'atar wani abun ya shiga tsakanin ku yanxu, nafi son hakan ta faru a can gidan ka daka tanadar muku kuyi rayuwa a ciki, saboda yarinyar tana da kunya kai kuma na lura so kk sai ka k'ure ta, don haka ka kiyaye ni sannan kayi k'ok'ari ka gyara gidan naka kfn nasan ya muku lokacin tariya don kasan bikin da kk yi a can da dangin ku bai isa ba muma zamuyi namu anan ina ptn ka fahimce ni." Ta k'arasa maganar tare da tsare shi da ido, wata kunyar ammy yaji ta lullu6e shi don haka ya kasa magana, "ko baka ji abinda nk fad'a ba."? Ammy ta k'ara tanbayar sa, " naji ammy na fahimta kuma insha'allah zan kiyaye." Ya fad'a kansa a sunkuye.


"Then ka fad'a min abinda ya tsayar daku kk yi dare haka." Ammy ta fad'a tana kallon sa, fk yace. "Abun dai babu dad'i ammy, nan dai ya bata labarin duk abinda ya faru har abubuwan da momy tk fad'a tayiwa suhailat da mahaifiyar ta da kuma shi kanshi dadyn, lokacin da ammy taji irin wannan rashin imanin da momy tayi saida tayi kuka ta kalli fahad ckn hawaye tace." Fahad ko byn raina ban amince maka ka wulak'anta wannan yarinyar ba, koda kuwa kayi wani auren inaso ka rik'e ta da amana kaga dai irin rayuwar da tayi a gidan mahaifin ta yakamata ace tayi rayuwar y'anci a gidan mijin ta. " fk yai murmushi kfn yace. "Insha'allah ba zan ta6a wulak'anta ta ba don Allah kema ki dinga yi min addu'a akan hakan." Ammy ta goge hawayen ta sannan tace. "Karka damu indai addu'a ce kullum a ckn yi maka ita nk kuma zan k'ara akan wacce nk yi, yanxu tashi kaje ka kwanta dare yana k'ara yi." Ba tare da musu ba ya mik'e ya nufi part dn sa.




__________________byn tafiyar su suhailat y sadeeq ma ya wuce d'akin sa haka aunty yasmeen, itama ta d'auki d'iyar ta tafi d'akin ta, kubra da isah kawai aka bari, isah ya kalli kubra sannan yace. "Kubra na fahimci duk wannan abubuwan da suka faru akan mu suka k'are, tabbas na cuci kaina da ban biyewa abokaina da kuma muguwar xuciya ta ba da abun yazo min da sauk'i, nasani da na kasance mutum na gari kamar yanda y sadeeq yk dady ba zai k'yamace ni ba dukda ta baiyana ni d'in ba d'an cikinsa bane ni d'an fasik'i maxinaci kuma mushriki ne." Kuka yaci k'arfin sa anan saida yayi mai isar sa sannan ya cigaba, "basu yi min adalci ba da suka samar dani ta hanyar zina, ni kaina ina yawan tanbayar kaina akan yawan aikata xina da nk yi koda ace nayi k'ok'arin daina wa saina kasa ashe nima asali na kenan kaico.".....
Kuka suke yi sosae shi da kubra babu me lallashi, sun dad'e a haka kfn kubra ta fara magana." Ni kam duk alhakin Suhailat ne ya k'are a kaina, dukda cewa nima ina da nawa laifin,bazan manta lokacin da nasha wannan maganin da momy ke fad'a ba wata rana na dawo daga makaranta na ganshi a ajiye kawai na d'auka naci awara dashi tunda ga wannan lokacin na tsinci kaina ckn wani irin hali Wanda ban ta6a fuskantar irin haka ba, babban kuskuren da na tafaka shine sanar da k'awa ta danayi ita kuma ta d'ora ni a hanya mara 6illewa, yanxu gashi ita kanta ta guje ni balle kuma samarin da nk bi, ga kuma wannan cikin da yak'i fita ko za'a kashe ni bansan wanene uban shi ba."ta k'ara fashe wa da kuka ckn kukan tk fad'in."Allah na tuba ka yafe min yanxu na k'ara tabbatar wa hassada ga me rabo taki ce tunda gashi wacce muke yiwa hassadar Allah y d'auka ka ta akan mu, wayyo Allah....... "!!!!!

Da k'yar suka lalla6a kowa ya nufi wajen kwanan sa, amma fa a ranar duk gidan babu Wanda ya runtsa, aunty yasmeen d y sadeeq haka suka kwana sallah suna rok'on Allah y yafe wa mahaifiyar su kuma ya bata lpy, ita kam kubra cikinta yayi nauyi baxata iya sallah ba.

Dady shima kwana yayi yana sallah tare da addu'ar Allah y yafe masa, saboda a ganin sa dukda cewa asiri akayi masa amma yayi sakaci musamman wajen wulak'anta marainiyar Allah Suhailat dukda cewa mahaifiyar ta da kuma inna sun damk'a masa amanar kula da ita kuma ya d'auki alk'awarin zai kula da ita sai gashi ya kasa saboda ya had'u da marasa imani, wai d'iyar cikinsa aka siyar don tayi lesbians to siyarwa mana tunda ta kar6i mak'udan kud'i, shi kam tsakanin sa da rabi Allah ne kawai zaiyi masa sakaiya.




____________________Yau da gobe sai Allah babu abinda yake tabbata sai ikon Allah kamar yanda farinciki baya dauwama haka shima bak'inciki ba zai ta6a dauwama ba, hakan ce ta faru a gidan alhj Ibrahim, ckn kwana 3 rad'ad'in abun da ya faru ya fara raguwa a zuciyoyin su, yanxu haka shirye-shiryen sabunta shagalin bikin suhailat da fk suke, har guda aunty yasmeen taje ta rok'i ammy alfarmar ta bata Suhailat ba tare da musu ba ammy ta bata ita, aikuwa aunty yasmeen ta dage kai da fata wajen gyara y'ar k'anwar ta ciki da waje (hmm Suhailat kin gamu da aiki y'an Adamawa sunyi nasu ammy tayi ga kuma babbar yaya yasmeenfahad kam zai m.....)

Suhailat tayi wani irin sihirtaccen kyau na mamaki, zuwa yanxu aunty yasmeen ta hana fk zuwa wajen ta saboda a cewar ta ya bari ya ganta a wajen biki, shi kam hakan sam baiyi masa dad'i ba don dai babu yanda zaiyi ne don itama ammy ta goya musu baya, hakan ce tasa ya siya mata tanfatsetsiyar waya don su d'an dinga jin muryar juna.



Gida kam yasha gyara dama ba wani gyara yk buk'ata ba tunda sabo ne, sai dai an zuba kaya sababbi, ammy tace kada su wahalar da kansu itace uwar d'iya don haka itace ta zuba duk wani abinda ake buk'ata a kitchen, shi kuma fk ya zuba tsadaddun furniture's a d'akunan bacci, tare da luntsuma-luntsuman kujeru a parlours, dady kam lefe yk had'a wa fk na gani na fad'a.



Duk byn kwana 2 suna xuwa suga jikin momy, ba laifi ta d'an fara samun lpyr paralysis d'in sai dai hauka kam kamar dad'a mata shi akayi, haka kawai za'a ga tana kuka tana fad'in. "Na kashe Khadija matar alhj nayi masa asiri don na mallake shi babu mugun abinda banyi ba, sai ta fara fixge-fixge har sai an rik'e ta, kasancewar y sadeeq da fk sun saki kud'i shi yasa ake kula da ita yanda yakamata.




Ansa ranar biki sati 1 me zuwa duka 6angaren biyu suna cike da farinciki ba kamar fk Wanda shima ake masa gyaran jiki na musamman kasancewar sa likita yasan abubuwan da suka dace dashi.


" ni dai gsky aunty na gaji da shan wad'annan abubuwan ai sai su lalata min hanji." Suhailat ta fad'a lokacin da yasmeen ta tsareta sai ta shanye wani magani, dariya yasmeen tayi kfn tace."Waya fad'a miki yana lalata hanji, ai sai dai ya gyara shi, koba kyaso ki zamo tauraruwa a wajen doctor yaxama babu wacce yk so da gani sai ke." Murmushi tayi kfn tace. "Ina so mana aunty yanda nk jin kishin sa wlh ko da wasa bana so a had'a shi da wata mace." Kubra ta shigo tana turo tsinin cikin ta Wanda ya tsufa haihuwa ko yau ko gobe, kallon abinda yk hannun suhailat tayi ckn xuciyar ta tace. "Tabbas macen da ta kare mutuncin ta y'ar gata ce,yanxu dubi yanda kowa yk alfahari da Suhailat ake lallashinta akan tasha magani alhalin kowa yasan amfanin da maganin yk yi, ita kuwa da ta xubar da nata mutncin a titi babu yanda za'a yi ta kwaso shi, wasu hawaye masu zafi suka gangaro kan check d'in ta tasa hannu ta goge don bata so su fahimci halin da tk ciki..........

Aunty yasmeen ta d'ago ta kalleta xuwa yanxu ta rage d'aure mata fuska, tun lokacin da taji cewa momy ce silar lalacewar rayuwar kubra sai ta dawo tana jin tausayin kubra, tace. "A'a uwar 2 an lalla6o kenan." Guri ta samu ta zauna da k'yar sannan ta d'an k'wakulo murmushi kfn tace. "Wlh kuwa na gaji da zama ni kad'ai shi yasa nace bari naxo nan." Ta6e baki aunty yasmeen tayi har yanxu tana jin haushin abinda kubra tayi, tace. "Kin kyau ta wa kanki."
Ita kam suhailat cewa tayi."Ai dama dole kad'ai ci ya dameki kuma a halin da kk ciki ma bai kamata ki dinga zama ke kad'ai ba. "

"To suu daa  ke a ina kk san hakan."? Aunty yasmeen ta fad'a tana kallon suhailat, dariya tayi kfn tace." A wajen ku manya mana."
"To naji na lura doctor ya koya miki surutu, da yawa ni kuma bari na koya miki yanda zaki sace xuciyar sa." Ckn tsokana suhailat tace. "Hmm aunty kenan ai na riga na sace xuciyar sa sai dai ki koya min yanda zan mallake ta kamar yanda kk mallake xuciyar y farouq." Sakin baki kubra tayi tana kallon suhailat ita kanta aunty yasmeen wacce tk k'ok'arin d'auko wani littafi Wanda abubuwan suke ciki saida ta juyo ta kalli suhailat, ita kam ko a jikinta kamar ba ita tayi ba, aunty yasmeen ta k'araso inda tk zaune tana jujjuya cup dn da yk hannun ta tak'i shanye maganin, saida ta zauna sannan tace. "Lallai yarinyar nan na lura nan gaba sai kinfi k'arfi na." Dariya tayi kfn tace. "Lah da wasa fa nk miki aunty."

Nan dai yasmeen ta fara fad'a mata yanda rayuwar aure tk, (abinda wasu iyayen basa yi yakamata idan y'ar ki ta isa aure ki zaunar da ita babu maganar kunya a ciki ki koya mata yanda zata kula da mijinta, ki sani idan ke baki yi mata wannan bayanin ba, k'awaye zasu sanar da ita ta hanyar da bata dace ba, su bata shawarwari marasa kyau sannan ga wayoyin nan na zamani, amma idan kk zaunar da ita da kanki kk yi mata kyakkyawan bayani zata fi gamsuwa kuma koda kawaye sun bata mugayen shawarwari bazata d'auka ba, Allah dai yasa mu dace ameen.)

  Aunty yasmeen tace." Ya ke y'ar uwa ta ina so ki rik'e wad'annan abubuwan da zan fad'a miki, da farko dai shi aure gaba d'ayan sa ba wani abu ne yk tfyr dashi ba sai hak'uri, ki kasance mace me hak'uri da kauda kai, kada ki zamo mace me yawan mita da kai k'orafi, nasan baki da k'awa to don Allah ki dinga rik'e sirrin gidan ki,  kuma duk rintsi kada ki bari wani yaji sirrinki keda mijin ki, ki zamo me magance matsalar ki da kanki ba me yawan kai k'ara ba, ki kauda kanki daga wayar mijin ki ba ruwan ki da bincike a ciki,  sannan ki tsaya ki fahimci halayyar mijin ki, mutum ne me sauri fushi ko bashi da saurin fushi hakan zai taimaka miki wajen control d'in sa a duk lokacin da kk samu wani sa6ani, idan yazo miki da damuwa ki nuna masa kinfi shi shiga ckn ta, haka ma idan farinciki ne, a duk lokacin da wani abun ya had'a ku ya d'auki zafi to ke ki sassauta xuciyar ki koda shine me laifin ki lallashi  abinki daga baya idan ya huce saiki sanar dashi kuskuren sa ckn dabara.


Tsafta kwalliya iya magana nasan duk bani da matsala dk ta wannan 6angaren, amma ki k'ara dagewa musamman ke da mijin ki ya kasance likita dole sai kin xage damtse wajen tsafta da kwalliya.

Kinga yanxu lokaci ya k'ure zan samu lokacin da zan koya miki abubuwan gyaran jiki wad'anda xakiyi da kanki ba ki dinga siyen kowanne tarkace kina amfani dashi ba, ni da kk ganni bana yadda nayi amfani da abinda bansan yanda aka had'a shi ba musamman Wanda ba nasha ba, haka kurum naje na tura abinda mijin ma xai guje ni, shi yasa nk taka tsan-tsan, sannan na koyi had'a abubuwan da kaina, ki bari a hankali zan koya miki. " 

Yasmeen ta gama maganar tare da kallon Suhailat wacce ta maida hankali sosae tana jin bayanin da aunty yasmeen d'in tk yi mata.

D'ago wa tayi ckn murmushi tace.''ngd aunty na insha'allah zanyi amfani da wannan shawarwarin d kk bani."

Aunty yasmeen tayi dariya kfn tace. "Babu komai k'anwata akwai ma wasu sirrika na musamman da na tanadar miki amma sai daga baya zan sanar dk."

Ckn tsokana suhailat tace. "Haba ba dole y farouq yakasa tfy karatu shi kad'ai ba, shi kad'ai yasan....." Bata k'arasa maganar ta ba aunty yasmeen ta kai mata duka tare da cewa. "Lallai yarinyar nan kin raina ni da yawa." Dariya Suhailat tayi sannan tace. "Babu raini aunty sai fad'ar gsky ko kubra." Ta tanbayi kubra wacce tk zaune jugum tunda ta shigo shawarwarin da aunty yasmeen ta fad'a wa suhailat keyi mata yawo a k'wa-k'walwar ta yanxu itama da ta tsare mutuncin ta wata k'ila da hadda ita ckn wad'annan shawarwarin, yanxu kam tasani koda ta haihu ba lalle bane ta samu mijin aure, tunda ba kowa ne zai yadda ya auri wacce ta ta6a haihuwar shege ba, ta d'an k'wakulo murmushi ta kalli suhailat wacce tk kallon ta sannan tace. "Eh" yanayin yanda tayi maganar kad'ai ya isa ya sanya tausayin ta a xuciyar mutum, aunty yasmeen ta ta6e baki kfn tace. "Ke kk jawa kanki kema da yanxun wata k'ila hadda ke za'a had'a ayi  bikin." Kuka me k'arfi ne ya k'wace wa kubra, Suhailat ta kalleta ckn tausayawa sannan tace." Haba aunty yasmeen zuwa yanxu yakamata ki dinga sassauta wa kubra, bai kamata ki dinga jifanta da irin wad'annan maganganun ba, itama fa ba laifin ta bane k'addara ce ta fad'a mata ko kin manta labarin da momy ta bamu."? Ckn 6acin rai yasmeen tace. "Ko kad'an ban manta ba, amma dai kinsan koda da dak'addara itama tana da nata laifin, ko kin manta lokacin da nk mata fad'a akan abinda tk yi tana min rashin kunya."  "Kiyi hak'uri aunty ki yafe mata ki d'auki abinda ya faru da ita a matsayin kaddara sannan a lokacin saboda momy tana goya mata baya ne shi yasa tak'i jin maganar ki." Aunty yasmeen tayi murmushin takaici kfn tace. "To ai dama Wanda baiji bari ba xaiji hoho yanxu ina momyn da tk goya mata bayan." Haba aunty don Allah kiyi hak'uri mana ko mahaliccin mu muna yi masa laifi kuma ya yafe mana balle kuma mu y'an Adam, wannan abun da ya faru da kubra ni na tabbatar kaddara ce saboda wannan rabon da Allah ya kaddara dole sai an same shi, babu za'a yi tunda haka Allah y tsara, aunty don Allah ki zamar wa kubra uwa kamar yanda kk dad'e da zamo min kin rungume ta y'ar uwar ki ce, kuma duk duniya batada kamar ki, don Allah ki barta da abinda yk damun ta plss aunty na. "


Ta had'a hannun ta waje d'aya alamar rok'o, jikin aunty yasmeen yayi sanyi sosae saboda tasan gsky suhailat ta fad'a mata koda yaushe idan suhailat tana magana ita har mamaki tk a ina yarinyar nan tasan abubuwan da yakamata, koda yake wannan baiwa ce ta musamman wacce Allah ya bawa suhailat d'in, kallon ta tayi da d'an murmushi kfn tace. "Shikenan k'anwata insha'allah zan rik'e ta tamkar y'a ta amma fa kinsan bazata kaiki ba."  

Kubra wacce tk ta faman kuka ckn kukan tace. "Na yadda aunty dama nasan ko rabin ta ba zanyi a wajen ki ba amma dukda haka ngd da kk yadda zaki kar6eni."  Ta juyo ta kalli suhailat "ngd y'ar uwa ta tunda nk ban ta6a nuna miki soyayya ba sai gashi yanxu kece kk fi kowa tausaya min ngd sosae Allah y bar zumunchi." 

Suka had'a baki wajen cewa."ameen. "




To amarya fa ansha gyara ciki da waje, tayi wani asirtaccen kyau fatarta kamar fulawa don laushi, kwarjini da haibar ta suka k'ara bai yana, aunty yasmeen ta toshe duk wata kafa da fk zaiga suhailat, gidan wata k'awar ta ta kaita sannan ta kar6e wayar ta idan ya kira yayi ta mata magiyar ta fad'a masa inda takai masa mata amma tak'i fad'a masa.


Ranar party da safe ya kira wayar kamar kullum tana wajen yasmeen byn sun gaisa yace. " yau dai yakamata ki cire min wannan takunkumin da kk sa min yasmeen in so naga matata." Aunty yasmeen tace. "Haba doctor wannan wanne irin azar6a6i ne idan kayi hak'uri ckn kwana 2 ta zama taka har abada sai dai ka bamu aro."

Ni dai don Allah aunty yasmeen ki taimaka min na ganta ko sau d'aya ne, ido na fa ya kusa makancewa saboda rashin ganin ta ko so kk d'an k'anin naki ya zama makaho ne."? 

Aunty yasmeen ta kwashe da dariya. "Lallai fa k'anina kace yau har aunty na samu, to gsky kayi hak'uri idan ma ka makance nasan kana ganin ta idon zai bud'e." Haushi ya kama fk ckn jin haushin yace. "To ai sai ki kai mata wayar muyi magana ko."?

Murmushi yasmeen tayi saboda ta fahimci haushi yaji don haka tace." A'a dai a bini a hankali ko nak'i kai mata wayar. " tsabar takaici yai mata shiru, jin yak'i magana ta kashe wayar tana dariya.


D'akin da ake gyara Suhailat ta nufa, shi kanshi d'akin k'amshi ya gama kama shi balle wacce aka tanadi k'amshin domin ta, ganin aunty yasmeen da wayar ta jikinta ya bata fk ne ya kira, kfn suyi magana wayar ta shiga k'ara: aunty yasmeen ta kalli suhailat tace. "Koda wasa kada ki bashi damar zuwa nan ya ganki a wajen party haka na tsara kin gane."? 

Kanta ta d'aga sannan yace mik'a hannu a hankali ta kar6i wayar, tayi picking, shiru baiyi magana ba don a zaton sa yasmeen ce, ckn sanyayyiyar murya tace." Assalamu alaikum warahmatullah angon Maryam."?


Jin saukar muryar ta a kunnen sa saida ya lumshe ido saboda har ckn xuciyar sa ta shiga tayi shawagi, ckn kasalalliyar muryar sa ya amsa."Wa'alaikis salam warahmatullah, amaryar fahad. " shiru sukayi a lokaci guda suna sauke ajiyar xuciya "ke bakya missing d'in mijin ki ko."? Ya fad'a kasa-kasa kamar me jin bacci.

" Me yasa ka fad'i haka. "? Itama ta tanbaye shi 

" Saboda aunty yasmeen ta hanamu ganin juna amma ke baki damu ba. "

"Kayi hak'uri gobe i yanxu ina Gian ka." Ta fad'a da wata irin murya wacce tasa ya kasa magana.

Ckn xuciyar sa yk tunanin yanda akayi ta iya fad'a masa haka ita da tk jin kunyar sa, ko maganar tariyar yayi mata shiru tk amma shine yau ta iya fad'in haka.

Jin yayi shiru tace. "Kaga in anjima ma zamu had'u wlh nayi missing d'in ka nima." Sai a lokacin ya samu k'arfin gwiwar cewa. "Ni ban yadda kinyi missing d'ina ba, saboda ni kad'ai nk yiwa yasmeen magana amma ke bakya yi." 

"Haba *nafsy* duk abinda fa aunty yasmeen tk yi tana yi ne saboda mu, to me zaisa mu kuma mu kasa hak'uri na d'an lokaci.'' 

Sunan da ta kirashi ya sanya shi ckn nishad'i har yanxu bai daina mamakin yanda yau tk magana dashi ba tare da jin kunya ba, yayi murmushi Wanda itama saida taji sautinsa sannan yace." Naji dad'in wannan sunan ki cigaba da kirana dashi ko a gaban wane, yau naji sauyi a tare dk menene sirrin ne."? Dariya tayi kamar tana gaban sa ta d'an juya ido kfn tace. "

"Aunty na ce sirrin." ckn dariya yace. " Ah kice nabar fad'a da ita gyara tk min." Tayi murmushi kfn tace. "Wani gyaran ma sai mun had'u zaka gani."


Wani irin farinciki ya mamaye shi tunda yk da ita bai ta6a hira me dad'i da ita irin ta yau ba, lallai kuwa idan hakane ya daina jin haushin yasmeen da ta hanasu ganin juna tunda gyara tk masa, da haka suka yi sallama byn ya aika mata da sak'on kiss ta ckn wayar har ckn k'ahon xuciyar ta taji saukar shi, ta dad'e da wayar a kunnen ta sannan ckn farinciki ta ajiye tana murmushi ita kanta taji dad'in hirar su ta yau, kuma tayi alk'awarin ta daina jin kunyar fk da yawa kamar yanda aunty yasmeen ta fad'a mata lokacin da ta same ta wata rana tana wayar ta kasa magana daga um sai a'a shine byn ta gama wayar aunty yasmeen ta zauna tace. "Ki cire wannan banxar kunyar ki farantawa mijin ki da kalamai masu dad'i, kunya itace tk cutar mata da yawa a gidajen auren su, to ki sani tsakanin ki da mijin ki babu kunya duk abinda kk yi masa lada zaki samu shi yasa matan waje suke k'wace mana maza saboda su basa jin kunyar aikata komai a wajen d'a namiji don haka kema ki dage."


Murmushi tayi lokacin da ta gama tuna wa a fili tace. "Ai kuwa xan dage don sai na bawa fk mamaki." Tayi saurin rufe baki tare da fad'in. "Au *nafsy*."


Misalin 5:30 an gama shirya amarya zuwa wajen party saboda 6:00pm to 8:pm za'a tashi, hmm maganar na tsaya rattaba muku kyan da suhailat tayi k'auyanci ne don haka ku k'iyasta da kanku haka shima fahad, abokin ango doctor muhseen ya tuk'o sabuwar motar da FK ya siya ango fahad ya hakimce a byn mota sai lokacin aunty yasmeen tk yi wa muhseen kwatancen gidan sajida inda suhailat tk, lokacin da suka iso tuni angama shirya amarya, fk ya yunk'ura zai fita doctor muhseen yace. "Haba my man karka bada y'an maza mana ka bari a kawo ta mana." Murmushi FK yayi kfn yace. "Doctor Ba zaka gane yanda na matsu naga yarinyar nan ba yau fa kusan sati rabona da ita." "To koma dai kwana nawa ne ai dai yanxu kayi hak'uri a fito maka da ita." Haka ya koma ya zauna idonsa akan k'ofar gidan, k'anwata aunty sajida hanan itace ta kamo Suhailat wacce tasha lullu6i da wata tsadaddiyar alkyabba, dukda cewa ba'a ganin face d'in ta amma FK suman zaune yayi da ya hango ta, saida hanan ta k'wank'wasa glass d'in motar kfn ya dawo dai-dai a hankali ya sanya hannun sa ya bud'e motar ta shiga, wani irin k'amshi me rikitarwa ya cika motar, tana k'ok'arin zama taji ya kama ta ya zaunar da ita bisa cinyar sa, hanan ta shiga gaba muhseen yaja mota, sauran motocin suka kwashe y'an uwa da abokan arxiki yawanci k'awayen aunty yasmeen ne da k'annen su saboda ita amaryar batada k'awa ye gwanda ma hanan yanxu sun d'an fara sabawa saboda zaman sati d'aya da sukayi a tare.

Har aka isa wajen partyn babu Wanda ya iya yiwa d'an uwansa magana ita dai tsananin kunyar sa ce da kwarjininsa yasa ta kasa koda gaishe shi, shima kansa yau wani kwarjini tayi masa sannan kuma ga kwalliyar ta da tasa yashagala da kallon ta baima san sun iso ba saida yaji motar ta tsaya, hanan da muhseen suka fita, yabi bayansu da kallo sannan ya dawo gareta lips d'in ta dayake yawan tsokane masa ido yk kallo kfn a hankali yace. Da sauri ya dubi gurin, doctor muhseen ya gani a tsaye, ya d'an yi k'asa da glass d'in sannan ya lek'o da kansa kfn yayi wata magana muhseen yace."Mlm ka fito ka tsaya kana wani 6ata mana lokaci ku ake jira fa." Fk yayi murmushi kfn yace. "Kaga ni kada ka takura most min wlh zanja mota nayi tfy ta da matata ai partyn ba dole bane."

Muhseen yasan FK zai iya aikata abinda ya fad'a don haka ya sassauta murya "Haba doctor me yasa kk hakane taron nan fa naka ne kai muka xo tayawa murnar samun hayatyn ka."

Wani dad'i ya xiyarci xuciyar fk ya juya ya kalleta idonta a rufe yk saboda bata son had'a ido dashi, ya juyo ya kalli muhseen kfn yace."Bamu five minutes yanxun zamu fito. "

Yana gama fad'in haka ya zuge glass d'in ya juyo gareta "hayaty" ya kira ta a hankali bata amsa ba kuma bata bud'e idon ba.

"Ki bud'e idonki ki fad'a min yanda za'a yi a gyara kwalliyar nan don gsky na 6ata musamman ta nan." Ya fad'a tare da manna mata kiss a lips d'in ta, 6ata fuska tayi sannan ckn shagwa6a tace. "Don Allah ka daina kaga jiran mu ake.'' Ckn rad'a yace." Bud'e idonki sannan ki fad'a min. " 

A hankali ta bud'e idon sai dai ta kasa kallon sa ma balle ta iya magana, 

"Kinga yanxu dai ba wannan ba muhseen yana jira na, ya za'a yi a gyara kwalliyar don za'a iya gane wa." Ba tayi magana ba ta bud'e k'aramar jakar dk hannun ta, ta juya masa baya sannan ta gyara kwalliyar tata, kamar babu abinda ya faru, lokacin da ta juyo ya kalleta sai yaga har tafi da yin kyau, "Bari na bada tukui ci." Kfn tace komai ya manna mata kiss a for head d'in ta.

Turo baki tayi "gsky karka k'ara 6ata min kwalliya." Ta fad'a tana gyara alkyabbar ta, "karki damu tukuici ne kawai na bayar."

 shima ya d'auko tasa alkyabbar ta maza  kalar tata ya sanya sannan ya bud'e musu k'ofar motar yana rik'e da hannun ta.


"Wow." Shine abinda mutanen gurin suka had'a baki wajen fad'a, (Nima da naci nawa adon hannu na rik'e da mash kur nace."Masha'allah.")
