An gudanar da party lpy anci ansha an cashe naga mutane da yawa musamman y'an group d'in ummu mash kur novels fan's da y'an kishin mace fan's ds duk kowacce saida ta k'ure adaka taxo naga mum munnir hadda wawa ummu suhailat kuwa tace. "Wannan bikin d'iyar ta ne dole ta cashe, Karaduwas kuwa ta shige ckn k'awaye don tace ba za'a barta a baya ba, oh' su husna an kafe y sadeeq da ido ko kyafta wa ba'a yi duk motsin sa kina kallo, saida nazo hawa mota na lura da makociya ta lauratu Maman haneef nik'i-nik'i da kaya banda Wanda taci harda tsaraba


Byn an tashi a motar aunty yasmeen  suka tafi gidan ammy wannan umarnin ammy ne tace a gidan ta za'a yi wuni, aunty yasmeen taji dad'in haka saboda bata yadda da doctor ba zai iya gudu wa da amaryar gidan sa.

Aikuwa kamar tasan abinda ya shirya kenan tunda har yama fad'a wa muhseen haka zaiyi yace masa." Muhseen daga nan gida na zaka kaimu idan aka tashi a k'arasa bikin tana gidan ta." Dariya muhseen yayi. "Mutumi na baka da dama meye ban-bancin yau da kuma gobe, ka hak'ura mana a kawo maka ita." Hakane amma ni gsky bazan iya hak'ura ba, ni banga abinda za'a yi ba tunda an riga an d'aura aure to me kuma ya rage. " 

Shiru muhseen yayi masa saboda shi bazai iya da rigimar FK ba.

 suna ckn haka aunty yasmeen da ammy suka zo wajen da suke zaune, ammy ta bud'e face d'in suhailat wacce kanta yk k'asa tace. "Masha'allah yarinya ta kinyi kyau Allah y albarkaci wannan auren naku." "Ameen y rabbi." Fk yai saurin amsa wa ita kam ckn xuciyar ta ta amsa, ammy ta harareshi "To rasa kunya tunda an tashi daga partyn sai ka bamu amaryar mu tafi da ita."

A daburce ya kalli ammy ckn lokaci kad'an ransa ya 6aci yasan wannan duk shirin yasmeen ne, kuma wlh ba don suhailat ba da sai ya fad'a mata magana don me zata dinga sanya mishi shamaki tsakanin sa da matarsa akan wani dalili mara tushe, ya d'ago jajayen idonsa wad'anda suka canxa launi ba tare da yayi wa kowa magana yabar gurin, aunty yasmeen tace."ammy ko dai mu hak'ura mu bashi matarsa sai muyi yinin a can." Ammy tace. "Rabu dashi sai mun gama bikin mu, banda ma rashin hankali irin nasa da yau da goben ai duk d'aya ne." Doctor muhseen yace. "Hakane ammy bari naje gurin sa."

Da haka suka tafi da ita gidan ammy d'akin ta na da anan aka sauke su, komai ckn wadata da kwanciyar hankali, byn sun yi wanka sunci , sukayi shirin kwanciya a nan ne aunty yasmeen ta dubi suhailat tace.''gsky sai kin dage saboda na lura mijin nan naki yana da saurin fushi." Suhailat ta sauke ajiyar xuciya kfn tace. "Nima abinda yk damu na kenan tun d'axu, aunty dubi yanda ya 6ata rai akan abinda bai kai ya kawo ba, ni wlh da na kalle shi har tsoro ya bani."

Murmushi yasmeen tayi. "Ba tsoro yakamata ki dinga ji ba, hanyar da zaki shawo kansa zaki nemo, ammafa ki kula kada ki dinga tunkarar sa idan yayi irin wannan fushin ki bari sai xuciyar sa ta d'an yi sanyi kfn ki lallashe shi, yanxu d'auko  wayar ki ki kira shi kada ki bari ya kwana da damuwa."  Suhailat ta d'auko wayar ta danna masa kira ringing d'aya ya d'aga sai dai baiyi magana ba, Suhailat ta kalli yasmeen wacce tayi mata alama da ta lallashi mijinta.


"Amincin Allah y tabbata a gareka mijina ina ptn zuwa yanxu ka huce daga fushin da kayi, idan kuma baka huce ba, ga sak'o daga xuciyar abar k'aunar ka, a duk inda kk ka nemi ruwa me sanyi kasha sannan ka jira abinda xan fad'a maka."

Fk da yayi shiru yana jinta cike da tsananin mamaki da al'ajabin maganganun ta ya mik'e a hankali ya d'auko ruwa a ckn fridge ya zauna ya sha, don har yanxu xuciyar sa batayi sanyi ba, wata ajiyar xuciya ya sauke lokacin da ya gama shan ruwan ya lumshe idonsa.


Ita kuma ta cigaba "Ngd wa Allah da ya sassauta maka fushin da yk ckn xuciyar ka, *nafsy* ya kamata ka sanya wa xuciyar ka salama ni fa banga abun jin haushi ba,  da yau da gobe duk d'aya ne a wajen Allah."

Ya busar da wata iska daga bakinsa yanxu kam ya samu nutsuwa kuma xuciyar sa tayi fari amma dukda haka bazai 6oye  mata yanda yk jin haushin yasmeen ba wacce ya d'ora mata alhakin komai a kanta don haka yace. "Ai dama nasan bazaki ga abun jin haushi ba tunda auntyn ki ce ta shirya hakan ke kuma bata ta6a yin laifi a wajen ki, koda yk baki damu dani bane shi yasa." 

Ckn xuciyar ta taji haushin yanda yake fad'ar laifin aunty yasmeen sai taji kamar ma ta kashe wayar amma da ta tuna abinda yasmeen ta fad'a mata, cewa."Babu abinda yk kawo mummunan sa6ani sai rashin bayani a lokacin da mutum ya yanke maka hukunci ba tare da yayi bincike ba, kai kuma kada kayi fushi kabi duk wata hanya da kasan zaka fahimtar dashi har sai ya fahimta.

Da wannan ta lallashi xuciyar ta ta fara yi masa bayani.

"Kada ka xargi aunty na saboda ba itace ta shirya hakan ba itama umarnin ammy tabi,itama kanta ba taji dad'in abinda kayi ba sannan itace ta bani shawarar na kiraka yanxu na lallasheka, aunty yasmeen tamkar uwa tk a waje na duk abinda ta umarceni zanyi don haka kaima bana son kana sanya duk abinda zata yi a matsayin laifi sai dai gyara, domin zan iya yi maka rantsuwa aunty yasmeen tafi kowa sona a duniya nasan bazata cutar damu ba."


Shiru yayi sai yanxu ya gano kuskuren da ya tafka tabbas yasmeen ba wacce zai dinga jin haushin ta a koda yaushe bane yakamata shima ya d'auke ta tamkar uwa yanda suhailat ta d'auke ta indai da gsk yana k'aunar ta.


Ckn sassauta murya yace. "Na gane hayaty kuma ngd da kk tunatar dani matsayin auntyn mu a wajen mu, insha'allah daga yau duk hukuncin da zata yanke a kanki nima zanyi biyayya, amma fa yana da ja da kk ce tafi kowa sonki gsky ni dai a cire ni don yanda nk jinki a xuciya ta daga ammy sai ke, ita kuma fa dole akwai wad'anda zasu tarayya dk a xuciyar ta byn iyayen ta ga mijinta da kuma d'iyar ta." "Hmm baka san yanda nk a wajen aunty yasmeen bane shi yasa." 

"To naji abar maganar amma dai nasan ni nafi kowa sonki, yanxu dai kuna inane ki bani aunty yasmeen na bata hak'uri saboda mummunar fahimtar da nayi mata."

Taji dad'in hakan sosae ta juyo inda yasmeen tk sai dai ta tarar da gurin babu kowa murmushi tayi saboda tasan guri yasmeen ta bata don ta sake tayi waya wata k'ila kuma farouq d'in ta ne ya kira ta suna can suna yayyafa wa tasu flower ruwa.

"Ba kya ji nane." Ya fad'a saboda shiru da yaji tayi,

"A'a ina ji dama ina duba aunty yasmeen ne naga ashe ta fita daga d'akin." 

"Kuna inane."? Ya tanbaya, " Muna gidan ammy, ta bashi amsa kai tsaye, "OK gani nan xuwa gwanda nazo da kaina."

Ta duba agogo 11:30pm "amma yanxu naga kamar dare  yayi ka bari saida safe." 

"No waya fad'a miki yanxu dare yayi sannan idan na zauna ko bacci bazan iya ba tunda babu ke."

"To shikenan nima xanso kazo ko don ka bawa ammy hak'uri." Yai ajiyar xuciya kfn yace. "Ranta ya 6aci sosae ko."?

" ka kwantar da hankalin ka bata nuna 6acin rai sosae ba amma dai gsky bata ji dad'i ba." 

"OK bari nazo ki kashe wayar idan naxo ke zan kira don nasan halin ammy bazata saurare ni ba sai kin sa baki."

"To sai kazo ka kula min da kanka sannan kayi tuk'i a hankali."


Da haka sukayi sallama dashi ckn farinciki, ta kashe wayar, ta gyara wa baby sahla kwanciya sannan ta sauko daga bed ta fito, a parlour ta tarar da aunty yasmeen tana waya daga ganin yanda tk lumshe ido kasan da farouq take waya ta wutsiyar ido ta kalli suhailat, tace. "Yauwa gata bari na bata." Ta fad'a lokacin da tk mik'a mata wayar.


Tasan y farouq ne don haka tayi sallama ta gaida shi, nasiha sosae yayi mata akan aure sannan daga K'arshe yayi musu ptn alkhairi da samun zaman lpy yace shima yana hanya nan da sati d'aya, tayi masa gdy sannan ta bawa aunty yasmeen itama sukayi sallama ta kashe wayar, anan Suhailat tk shaida mata FK fa yana nan xuwa, murmushi aunty tayi kfn tace."Ai kinyi k'ok'ari da kk iya shawo kansa."


Lokacin da yazo yana shigo wa ckn babban parlourn yaci karo da ammy, ya k'arasa da sauri yana mata magana tayi masa banxa, ko kallon inda yk ba tayi ba ta cigaba da abinda tk yi jera kayan drinks a fridge, a hankali ya xo inda tk shima ya fara taya ta har suka gama amma bata ko kalle shi ba sai ma k'ok'arin barin wajen da tk yi,  hannun ta ya kamo cike da marairaicewa yace. "Plss ammy na ki saurare ni don Allah." Ta juyo "me zaka fad'a min byn kana so ka nuna wa duniya ban isa dakai ba."? 

"Ayyah ammy bafa haka bane rashin fahimta ne kawai wlh ban san kece kk bada wannan umarnin ba kiyi hak'uri na tuba." 

Murmushi ammy tayi kfn tace. "To shikenan ka wuce ka kwanta dare ya fara yi."

Kamar yace yana son yaga matarsa amma dai yai shiru gudun kada ya k'ara janyo wa kansa wani laifin, don haka ya wuce d'akin sa, byn ya gama shirin kwanciya ne ya kira suhailat ya fad'a mata yanda suka yi da ammy, sannan ya nemi ta had'a shi da aunty yasmeen itama ya bata hak'uri tace. "Babu komai." Sun dad'e suna hirar su me dad'i da matarsa kfn suyi sallama cike da so k'aunar juna.



Washe gari anyi wuni lpy amarya tasha kyau ita da yasmeen da kuma ammy tun safe ake hada-hada, anraba abubuwa masu yawa masu d'auke da picks d'in amarya da ango Wanda duk ammy ce ta d'auki nauyi.

Tun byn la'asar aunty yasmeen ta sanya suhailat a d'aki ta tsare ta sai ta cinye ferfesun da aka yi mata wajen kala 4 dukda taci wadansu wad'anda yasmeen tayi mata amma ammy tasa aka k'ara yi mata saboda tace basa yawa.


Dakyar ta iya cin 2 a ciki aunty yasmeen tace. " Suma Wad'annan d'in da kk k'i ci dasu zaki tafi ki sanya su fridge babu abinda zasu yi. " turo baki tayi kfn tace. "Ni don Allah aunty ki cinye wlh nagaji da cin irin wad'annan abubuwan." Harara yasmeen ta watsa mata kfn tace. "Bazan cinye d'in ba ke zaki ci shi." Bata k'ara magana ba ta mik'e ta shiga bathroom tayi wanka lokacin da ta fito aunty yasmeen ta ajiye mata kayan da zata sanya, wata tsadaddiyar atanfa ce me kyan gaske anyi mata d'inki dai-dai jikinta, yasmeen tayi mata light make-up, tayi kyau sosae, a dai-dai lokacin doctor muhseen ya shigo wai sunxo d'aukar amarya, gaban ta ya fad'i tunda ga lokacin ta fara share hawaye, ammy tayi mata nasiha me ratsa xuciya akan zaman aure, haka suka tafi tana faman kuka.

Aunty yasmeen saida ta kaita har katafaren gadon ta ta kalli gidan sannan ta juyo ta kalli suhailat wacce duk mayafinta ya jik'e da hawaye ta k'ank'ame yasmeen wai bazata tafi tabar ta ba,ita kanta Suhailat hawayen farinciki tk saboda ganin yau Allah ya nufe ta da kawo y'ar k'anwar ta gidan aure, da k'yar aka 6an6are Suhailat daga jikinta sannan suka tafi kowa a wajen saida ya tausayawa y'an uwan,saboda irin kukan da sukeyi.

Byn kowa ya watse suhailat ta dunk'ule a waje d'aya saboda tsoro da ta fara ji har yanxu bata daina kuka ba, tana ckn wannan halin taji tsayuwar mota kfn tayi wani yunk'uri taji dadda d'ar muryar mijinta na mata sallama, dukda halin da tk ciki baisa ta kasa amsa sallamar ba, muryar ta a dakushe saboda kukan data sha.

Yasha wani tsadadden yadi me laushi sai k'amshi yk zuba wa, ta d'ago idonta a hankali suka had'a ido da sauri y k'arasa gareta yana fad'in. "Subhanallah kukan me kk yi hayaty byn kuma kinsan yau d'in rana ce ta musamman a gare  mu."?

Wani kukan ne taji ya sake k'wace mata, ya zauna a gefen ta tare da janyo ta jikin sa yana lallashinta har ya samu ta d'an yi shiru sannan ya kamo hannun ta zuwa parlour inda su doctor muhseen suke jiran su, nan dai abokan nasa suka fara tsokanar shi yana rama wa, daga baya suka yi musu addu'ar samun zaman lpy me d'orewa a rayuwar auren su sannan suka yi musu sallama, muhseen yace."Waiko raka mu ba zaka yi ba." Wani daga ckn abokan yace. "Mu dai mun yafe."


"Koma Baku yafe ba babu inda zani." Ya fad'a yana gyara zama, muhseen ya sunkuyo dai-dai kunnensa yace. "To mlm farin shiga kabi y'ar mutane a hankali dai don na fahimci ka cika rawar kai kada kaje Kayi ta6argaza." Duka ya kai masa yana dariya shima muhseen d'in dariya yayi sannan ya fice yabi byn abokan sa suka tafi.


Fk ya juyo ya kalli suhailat duk ta takure a waje d'aya yasa hannun sa a hankali ya cire mata mayafin da ta k'udundune dashi sannan ckn taushin murya yace. "Kije kiyi alwala muyi sallah mu godewa Allah da ya nuna mana wannan ranar me albarka." Batayi magana ba kuma bata tashi ba saboda kunyar sa da tk ji bai ta6a ganin jikinta babu hujabi ba sai yau, ganin taki tashi ya sunkuce ta ya nufi d'akin da ita bai direta a ko ina ba sai a bathroom sannan ya juya ya fita alwalar tayi sannan ta fito ta zauna a gefen gado byn ta sanya hijabinta, d'akin tk bi da kallo Wanda yasha kyau komai na d'akin white&blue ne, tana ckn wannan halin ya shigo tare da sallama har ya canxa kayan sa zuwa doguwar rigar bacci, kallo d'aya tayi masa ta sunkuyar da kanta, ya k'araso a hankali da kansa ya shinfid'a musu carpet d'in sallah, ya dad'e yana xuba musu adduo'in samun zaman lpy a sabuwar rayuwar da zasu farayi byn sun idar da sallar, sannan ya dafa goshin ta nan ma ya dinga kwararo musu adduo'i sannan suka shafa.


Ya janyo ledojin da suka shigo dasu ya fara bud'e wa, da kansa yaje kitchen ya d'auko plate,spoon da knife ita dai kallon sa take saida ya gama ya yanko naman ya nufo bakinta dashi,  a hankali ta girgixa kai, shima kansa ya girgixa kfn yace. "Dole kici abinci ki k'oshi hayaty saboda ki samu k'arfin iya d'auka ta, kinsan dai nayi hak'uri tsawon lokaci ko."?

Gaban ta ya fad'i abinda tk tsoron ji kenan Ashe shi yana nan manne ckn xuciyar sa, ya k'ara nufar bakinta da naman haka ta dinga kar6a tana ci dukda ba wai dad'in sa tk ji ba kuma hankalin ta a tashe yk, shi kansa ya lura da hakan don haka ya dinga janta da hira ckn salon mantar da ita damuwar dk ranta, koda yk yasani kamar yanda bazai iya cire wa kansa abinda yk ransa ba haka itama sai dai yayi k'ok'arin nuna mata abun ba na tashin hankali bane.


Haka ya gama ciyar dasu dukkan su ba da wani yawa suka ci ba, ya had'a kayan ya kai kitchen ya d'aura ye sauran abubuwan kuma ya sanya su a fridge, kfn ya dawo ta wanke bakinta ta hate gado ta kwanta ba tare da ta cire kayan jikinta ba, saida ya tabbatar ya rufe ko ina sannan ya shigo d'akin, hango ta yayi a can k'arshen gadon ta mak'ure, yai murmushi kfn ya cire rigar da ke jikin sa daga shi sai singlet da boxer, ya nufi wajen ta, ckn nutsuwa ya hau gadon ya fahimci ba bacci tai ba sai dai idonta a rufe yk ya janyo ta jikinsa tare da rungume ta tsam, hakan yasa ta bud'e ido kirjin ta na cigaba da har bawa da k'arfi, da sauri ta k'ara runtse idonta ganin yanda yk ba kaya tunda tk bata ta6a ganin namiji baligi a haka ba sai taga ya k'ara girma ya cika mata d'akin.


Ckn muryar rad'a yace. "A haka zaki kwanta da wad'annan kayan basu yi miki nauyi ba." Da sauri ta d'aga kanta don ita tsoron sa kawai tk ji, murmushi yayi sannan yace. "Mijin ki baya buk'atar ganin ki da komai idan yana tare dk a irin wannan lokacin don haka bari na rage miki kayan da kaina, kfn tayi magana har ya fara cire hijabin da ta duk'unk'une a ciki, saida ya cire mata komai shima ya cire nasa, sannan yaja musu bargo byn ya kashe fitilar d'akin, suhailat ta runtse idonta hawaye ya fara sauko mata yayinda shi kuma ya fara nisa baya jin kira.(da sauri na na fice daga d'akin nasu kar naga abinda yafi k'arfi na kuma.)



Kiran sallar farko a kunnen suhailat ta yunk'ura xata tashi amma ta kasa saboda wani rad'ad'i da ya ziyarci k'asan ta, bata san lokacin da ta saki y'ar siririyar k'ara ba, FK Wanda yk zaune bisa sallaya yana nafila yai sallama, ckn sauri yaxo wajen ta duk ya rikice." Me ya faru hayaty ya akayi. " kasa kallon sa tayi balle ta iya yi masa magana saboda haushi da kunyar sa da suka yi mata yawa.

Kamar k'wai haka yk lalla6ata ya d'auko ta a hankali byn ya tanadar mata ruwa me zafi a bathroom, bai tsaya 6ata lokaci ba ya sanya ta a ciki tasa ki wata k'ara saboda zafin ruwan da ya ratsa ta har yanxu bata bud'e idonta ba, saida ya canxa mata ruwa 3 sannan ya sulla mata wanka da kansa, ita dai tana jinsa ta kasa komai banda hawaye da tk faman yi, ya d'auko ta ya d'ora ta kan bed ya shafa mata mai sannan ya dubo wata doguwar riga ckn karanta yasa mata, har yanxu bata daina kukan ba, nan ya zauna ya fara aikin lallashi,da k'yr tayi shiru ya mik'e jin ana assalatu ya sakar mata pic a for head sannan ya yafi, sai a lokacin ta bud'e idon sa byn taji k'arar rufe k'ofar, ta mik'e a hankali ta hau kan sallaya ta fara sallah.........


            SUHAILAT

Saida tayi raka'atul fijr sannan ta gabatar da sallar asuba, byn ta gama adduo'in ta, ta koma gado tai kwanciyar ta bacci me nauyi yayi gaba da ita.

Lokacin da fk ya dawo ya lek'a fuskarta yaga baccinta tk yi tsakanin ta da Allah, murmushi yayi a fili yace. "Allah sarki hayaty na wahalar dk dole kiyi baccin gajiya."

Gadon ya hau shima tare da janyo ta jikinsa ya rungume ta tsam, a haka shima bacci ya kwashe shi.

Ta riga shi farkawa taji ta a jikin sa, a hankali ta zare hannun sa daga jikinta ta lalla6a ta sauko daga gadon byn ta gyara masa bargo, bathroom ta shiga tayi wanka byn ta k'ara gasa jikinta har yanxu tafiyar ta bata dawo normal ba, byn ta fito ta zauna ta tsala kwalliya sannan ta sanya kayan ta wata doguwar rigar less d'inkin yayi mata ciff dai-dai ita, tana ckn fesa turare taji ana knocking k'ofar parlourn, saida ta sanya hijabi sannan ta tafi don ganin Wanda yk knocking d'in, driven ammy ne d'auke da kaya nik'i-nik'i wai inji ammy, ta bashi hanya yazo ya ajiye a dining area, tace. "Don Allah ka gaida ammy kace mata mungode." Yace. "Insha'allah xan fad'a mata." Byn tfyr sa ta zauna a parlourn tana kallo, sam bata ji motsin sa ba sai ji tayi ya rungumo ta ta baya, ta bud'e baki zata saki k'ara saboda ta tsorata, fk ya juyo da ita gare shi tayi k'asa da kanta cike da kunyar sa, murmushi yayi kfn yace."ashe amaryar tawa matsoraciya ce." 

Ta turo baki ckn shagwa6a tace."to ba kaine ka tsorata ni ba. "

"OK ayi min afuwa bari naje nayi wanka na dawo sai mu gaisa ko."? Ya fad'a yana k'ok'arin mik'ewa, d'aga kanta tayi  tana binsa da kallo cike da so da k'auna, kashe mata idonsa d'aya yayi ganin yanda tk binsa da kallo cike da jin kunyar sa ta sunkuyar da kanta tana murmushi, shima murmushin yayi sannan ya wuce zuwa d'akin sa.

 30 minutes ya dawo ckn shigar k'ananan kaya sunyi masa matuk'ar kyau, bata parlourn don haka kai tsaye ya wuce d'akin ta, ya same ta ta gama gyara komai na d'akin tamkar ba yanxu ya fita ya barshi a hargitse ba, murmushi yayi saboda ba k'aramin burgeshi tayi ba shi d'in mutum ma'abocin son tsafta, ya k'arasa inda tk, kfn yayi magana ta tsugunna tace." Ina kwana. "? Hannun ta ya kamo ya mik'ar da ita tsaye sannan ya had'e bakinsu waje d'aya, sun dad'e a haka k'afafuwan su na neman kasa d'aukar su sannan a hankali ya zare bakinsa daga nata tare da k'ura mata ido ckn tsananin buk'atar ta, ita kam idonta a lumshe yk matsananciyar kunyar sa ke damun ta, ckn dakusashiyar murya yace." Daga yau a haka zamu dinga gaisawa kin gane."?

Batayi magana ba sai bud'e idonta da tayi tana kallon sa, yayi murmushi kfn yace. "To xo mu shiga kitchen mu samu abinda zamu yi break fast ko." Muryar ta a hankali tace. "Ammy ta aiko mana da break fast tun d'axu." Ckn fara'a yace. "Ah to muje muci mana." Ya fad'a tare da janyo hannun ta, sai a lokacin ya lura da yanayin tafiyar da tk yi, ya sunkuya dai-dai kunnenta yace." Ko dai sai na duba ki ne ? Allah dai yasa banyi wa kaina 6arna ba." Batayi masa magana ba, har suka zauna a dining, da kansa yai serving d'in su a plate d'aya haka ya ciyar da ita a baki, wannan karon itama ta saki jikin ta dashi don itama itace ta ciyar dashi har suka k'oshi byn sun gama ta had'a kayan ta kaisu kitchen ta gyara ko ina sannan ta zauna a kusa dashi, matso wa yayi ya kwantar da kansa a cinyar ta, ya zuba mata ido itama shi tk kallo, sai dai maganganun aunty yasmeen yana ta yawo a k'wa-k'walwar ta da tace."Ki cire dukkan kunya ki kula da mijin ki kunya na cutar da mu wajen kula da mazajen mu shi yasa matan waje basa shan wahala wajen k'wace mana su, namiji yana buk'atar kulawar matarsa a kowanne lokaci kada ki ta6a nuna wa mijinki kin gaji dashi ko a fuska balle ki kai ga fad'a masa, hakan yana rage miki k'ima a wajen sa,koda yaushe ki nuna wa mijinki kina son kasancewa tare dashi." Tayi ajiyar xuciya byn ta gama tunowa a hankali d'ora hannun ta a kan kwantacciyar sumar kansa tana d'an shafawa a hankali, murmushi yayi ckn jin dadin abinda tk yi masa, yace."Ngd wa Allah da ya nuna min wannan lokacin, gashi dai burika na sun cika lokaci d'aya. " ya tashi zaune tare da rik'e face d'in ta yace."Kin shayar dani farincikin da ban ta6a shiga ckn irin sa ba, nasha jin labarin aure a wajen friends dina amma ban ta6a sanin haka yk ba tabbas bahaushe yayi gsky da yace mutum baya xama cikakkyan mutum sai yayi aure, ni kaina yau jina nk na zama cikakkyan mutum kuma kece sila, ina rok'on Allah yanda kk kiyaye min kanki da mutuncin ki, dukda had'arin da kk shiga na rayuwa a gidan y'an lesbians amma kk kare min kanki, wannan kad'ai ya isa na jinjina miki, ina rok'on Allah ya bani ikon rik'e ki da amana, sai dai yanxu xan fad'a miki yanda nk son rayuwar auren mu ta kasance, idan akwai abinda kk so mu k'ara kema saiki fad'a tunda mun shiga zauren tattauna wa. "



Nasan abubuwa da yawa bani da matsala a kansu don ke gwana ce musamman 6angaren tsafta girki da duk abinda ya shafi wad'annan, nasan kin dad'e da k'ware wa ta wannan fannin, kula dani shine bak'on abu a wajen ki, ni d'in nan da kk gani inaso ki shagwa6a ni da soyayya da kulawa koda yaushe inaso ki kasance a tare dani koda yk a hankali zan koyar dake yanda zamu rayu da juna ckn kulawa da tsananin farinciki. "

Hannun ta ta sanya a kyakkyawar sumar da ta zagaye ha6arsa (saje) tana d'an shafawa a hankali tace. "Karka damu nafsy nayi maka tanadin irin wannan rayuwar da taimakon aunty yasmeen nasan zaka ji dad'in irin tanadin da nayi maka." Ya lumshe ido cike da jin dad'in abinda tk yi masa sannan yace. "Ashe dai nayi gajen hak'uri anyi min special tanadi wata k'ila ma yafi Wanda nk tunani, koda yk gsky zanyi mamaki idan naga kin saki jikinki dani kina bani kulawa da soyayya duk da tarin kunyar da kk da ita." Ya k'arasa maganar cike da tsokana.

 Ta d'an yi smiling kfn tace. "Ai don ban saba da irin wannan rayuwar bane amma da zarar na saba zaka sha mamaki na." Ta k'arasa maganar tare da juya fararen idonta.




Dariya yai sannan yace. "Allah yasa dai ba cika min baki ake ba." Ta ta6e baki "idan ma cika bakin ne ai zaka gani lokaci ne zai nuna."

Ya lura kamar ta d'an ji haushi sai ya kamo face d'in ta yace. "To idan da gsk ne muje ki d'an k'ara sammin zumar da kk  bani jiya." Ya k'arasa fad'a yana d'age girar sa d'aya.

Gaban suhailat ya fad'i don gskyr magana a tsorace tk dashi, don ba karamar wahala tasha a daren jiya ba, xuciyar ta ce ta tunatar da ita shawarar aunty yasmeen.


"Kada ki ta6a gujewa mijin ki a duk lokacin da ya buk'ace ki,koda ranki baya so kiyi k'ok'arin faranta masa nasa ran, rashin bashi kulawa a wannan lokacin yana iya haifar muku da matsala ke dashi dole ne a ckn 2 yayi d'aya, kodai ya nemawa kansa mafita ta hanyar samun wacce zata biya masa buk'ata, ko kuma ya bawa xuciyar sa hak'uri,  amma ki sani ba zai ta6a manta hakan a ransa ba, ke kuma zaki zauna cikin fushin mahaliccin ki, (Wasu matan sun d'auki wannan hanyar ta hana mijin su Kansu a matsayin hanyar da zasu rama wani abu da yayi musu, to ki sani kanki kk cutar saboda zaki kasance ckn fushin ubangiji har sai kin samu yardar mijinki, sannan shi kansa mijin naki zai d'auke ki a matsayin muguwa mara tsoron Allah, a duk lokacin da mijin ki yayi miki wani laifi akwai hanyoyi masu yawa da zaki fahimtar dashi abinda yayi har ya baki hak'uri, ba sai kinbi wannan hanyar ba saboda zaki cutar dashi sannan kema ki jefa kanki ckn matsala).





Ta d'an yi murmushi kfn tace." Da jikin da me jikin mallakin nafsy ne, to me zaisa kuma nafsy ya nemi izini don zaiyi amfani da abinda yk nasa ne."






Mik'ewa zaune yayi cike da tsananin mamaki bai ta6a tunanin abinda zai fito daga bakinta kenan ba yayi zaton zata tsorata ne, ya shafi gefen check d'in ta kfn yace. "Allah sarki hayaty Ashe ke din jaruma ce, nasani kin sha wahala jiya, to me zaisa kuma na k'ara wahalar dk wlh tsokanar ki kawai nk, nayi miki alk'awarin bazan k'ara yi miki komai ba har sai na tabbatar kin warke gaba d'aya yanxu dai tashi muje ki tayani bacci don bacci nk ji."




Saida ta sauke 6oyayyiyar ajiyar xuciya saboda taji yace da wasa yk, sannan a hankali suka mik'e rik'e da juna suka nufi d'akin sa.



To ranar dai haka suka wuni cikin farinciki, babu abinda suke sai faranta ran junan su, ammy ce tk aiko musu da abinci.





BAYAN SATI D'AYA


Kubra ta haihu ta haifi d'iyar ta mace aunty yasmeen itace tk kula dasu, lokacin da kubra ta d'auki d'iyar ta, haka ta tsurawa yarinyar ido a k'ok'arin ta na son gano uban yarinyar, babu wani abu da ta iya fahimta hakan yasa ta fashe da kuka tare da rungume y'ar ta, soyayyar yarinyar na shiga ckn xuciyar ta.


Lokacin da aka kaiwa dady yarinyar sam baiyi tunanin d'aukar ta ba, yace. "Allah ya raya y sadeeq ne yk faman dawainiya dasu.


Ranar suna yarinya taci sunan momy rabi, y sadeeq ne ya sanya mata wannan sunan, haka shi ya d'auki nauyin yanka rago da duk abinda ya dace ayi sai dai babu wani taro da akayi, suhailat ma tazo tayi barka.




Ckn sati d'aya sunyi wata shak'uwa ta ban mamaki, dukda cewa har lokacin fk bai k'ara kusantar ta ba, yana so sai ta k'ara sakin jikinta dashi, tsawon wannan lokacin ammy ba ta daina wahalar kawo musu abinci ba, har saida suka cika sati d'aya ciff sannan tace suhailat ta fara shiga kitchen.


Zama ne me cike da soyayya da k'aunar juna tare da baryyrywa juna kulawa ta musamman.

Bata bari ya bukaci wani don kfn ya buk'ata tayi masa haka shima yana k'ok'arin bata kulawa ko a office yk duk byn awa d'aya sai ya kira yaji lpyr ta.



Momy kam ta fara samun sauk'i haukan ya d'an ragu yanxu anfi damuwa da paralysis d'in da tk fama dashi da kuma ciwukan da ta jiwa kanta a lokacin da tk ganiyar haukanta.

Lokacin da aka kawo mata rabi y'ar kubra kuka ta dinga yi tana surutai dakyar aka kar6i yarinyar daga hannunta.


Isah kam jikinsa ya tsananta yanxu haka yana kwance a gadon asibiti ana kula dashi y sadeeq ne ya d'auki nauyin shi don shi dady cewa yayi bazai wahalar da kansa a kan d'an  boka ba.



Kwanan sa 5 a asibiti yace ga garin ku nan, haka aka yi masa sallah aka kaishi gidan sa na gsky.. (Allah y jik'an dukkan musulmi ameen)...

UMMU MASHKUR CE MANAZARTA

            SUHAILAT

(Allah ya shirye mu y shirya zuri'ar mu ameen).


Y sadeeq aunty yasmeen har ma da suhailat duk sun ji rad'ad'in mutuwar isah amma duk kubra tafi su, haka dai aka gama zaman makoki, yasmeen da suhailat duk suka tafi gidajan mazansu yayinda y sadeeq ya koma bakin aikin sa, gidan daga kubra sai dady da kuma y'ar ta momy karama.

Kfn tayi wani yunk'uri tuni ya had'e bakinsu waje d'aya, nan ta fara mutsu-mutsu tana k'ok'arin k'wace kanta don ba a hankali yk yi ba gaba d'aya ya fita haiyacin sa, dole ta hak'ura ta k'yale shi saida yai son ransa sannan ya d'aga ta a hankali ya nufi d'akin sa da ita yana mata magana ckn rad'a a kunne, aikuwa sai ta tintsire da dariya, da haka suka fad'a duniyar ma'aurata.


HMm WAI INA LABARIN KAMAL DA HANSATU ?

Suna can sokoto birnin shehu suna gudanar da rayuwar ckn jin dad'i, tuni hansatu ta waye tamkar wacce ta girma a birni tasan duk hanyar da zata bi ta faranta ran mijin ta, shi yasa ta shiga xuciyar kamal tayi kane-kane har a ransa yk jin gwanda da Allah yasa ta zamo mallakinsa, itama mama ta taimaka kwarai wajen koya wa hansatu girke-girke da yanda xata kula da mijinta shi yasa hansatu tk matuk'ar girmama mama koba komai ita d'in tamkar uwa tk a gurin ta ba sirika ba.



Kamal yayi k'ok'arin sanya ta a wata makaratar matan aure wacce ake koyar da ilimin addini har ma da na xamani, da yk kuma hansatu tana da k'wa-k'walwa tana fahimtar karatu sosae, duk abinda bata gane ba kamal yana koya mata, yanxu haka abinda ya dakatar da karatun nata shine ciki da tk dashi Wanda yk matuk'ar bata wahala shi yasa kamal yace ta d'an dakata da xuwa makarantar shi yk mata lesson a gida shi yasa karatun nata bai koma baya ba. Wannan kenan.

A tare suka yi wanka suna tsokanar junan su wannan ya watsawa wannan ruwa shima d'ayan ya rama, kusan awa d'aya wankan da baifi suyi shi a 30 minutes ba, haka dai suka gama shiriritar suka fito, wajen shirya wa ma saida suka 6ata lokaci kfn su gama, tare suka fito rik'e da juna bakinsa a dai-dai kunnenta yana magana ckn rad'a ita kuma tana faman murmushi da alama tana jin dad'in abinda yk fad'a mata, kai tsaye dining area suka nufa a tare suka ciyar da junan su, fk yana ta zuba mata santi tana masa dariya, haka dai suka gama cin abincin suka dawo parlour don suyi kallo.


To rabin kallon nasu duk soyayyar su suke sha ba wai sun maida hankalin su ga kallon ba, fk ya shafa d'amammiyar marar ta ckn tsokana yace. "Wai yaushe zaki kar6i ajiyar da nk baki."?

"To shikenan tunda kinyi musu tashi muje d'akin can na duba ki."

Ckn minti 5 da sanya tsinken gwajin result ya bayyana positive pregnant, wayyo dad'i fk ya juyo ya sunkuci suhailat yai juyi da ita yana dariya kamar ba yanxu shine Wanda yk had'e mata rai ba, a hankali ya d'ora ta bisa d'an k'aramin gadon dk d'akin sannan ya kalleta cike da farinciki yace. "Hayaty zargi na ya zamo gsky kina d'auke da ciki na sati 3 ngd wa Allah hayaty kema ngd miki don Allah ki dinga kula da kanki da kuma abinda kk d'auke dashi idan kina so kuma muyi fad'a ki dinga yin gan-ganci yanxu zaki ga 6acin raina." Yai shiru yana zuba mata kyawawan idonsa ganin tana hawaye mamaki yk akan hawayen me kuma xata yi ? Ko dai na farinciki ne?


"Hayaty me ya sanya ki kuka a wannan lokacin kuma, ko kukan farinciki kk yi."? Ta girgixa kai alamar a'a, ckn fargaba da damuwa yace." Ba kyason babyn ne." 

Muryar ta ckn kuka tace. "Ina so nafsy tsoro nk ji tsoron haihuwa nk ji, ance da wahala kaga umma ta ma a wajen haihuwa ta rasu." 


Tana gama fad'ar haka wasu hawayen suka k'ara 6alle mata, ya janyo ta jikinsa yana shafa yalwataccen gashin kanta Wanda ya sauko zuwa shoulder d'in ta, ckn sigar lallashi ya fara magana." Haba hayaty da ba'a haihuwa ai da ba'a same mu ba kuma duk wacce kk ga ta rasu wajen haihuwa kwananta ne ya k'are, Allah y jik'an umma itama kinga ai saida ta haifa min ke, sannan ta haifa mana Adam kinga kenan kwananta ne ya k'are lokacin ta ne yayi da taxo waccan haihuwar Allah y kar6i abarsa, addu'a yakamata mu bita da ita sannan mu tara mata jikoki masu albarka wad'anda zasu taya mu yi mata addu'a, wannan shine abinda zaki saka mata dashi." 


Jikinta yayi sanyi kuma ta yadda da abinda ya fad'a don haka tayi murmushi kfn ta kalli marar ta tace." Yanxu baby ne a wannan gurin. "

Ta fad'a tana danna gurin da sauri ya janye hannun ta, yana fad'in. "Baby's dai, ki daina danna wa mana sai kin nane musu hanci."?

Tayi dariya kai baban baby's ai yanxu ko hancin ba'a yi musu ba." 


Ya rik'e baki."la la la tun  yanxu za'a canxa min suna to ban yadda ba ki bari sai kin kawo min su sannan. "  dariya tayi kfn tace.''to Allah y kawo su lpy." Ya amsa da "ameen.




To fa suhailat anyi ciki fk har d'aukar hutu yayi na sati d'aya baya zuwa ko ina yana koya mata yanda zata kula da kanta, ita kam kallon sa kawai tk don har yanxu gani tk kamar ba ta da komai tunda babu abinda yk sanya ta, haka kawai fk sai ya sanya ta kwanta yayi ta yi mata tausa har bacci ya d'auke ta, yanxu ya hana ta duk wani aiki me wahala sai dai idan ya fita tayi sauri tayi kfn ya dawo.

"Ammy ki kula min da my patient"....


*continues*
Murmushi ammy tayi sannan tace." Karka damu patient d'in ka tawa ce, kuma banda shish-shigi irin naka meye na shiga tsakanin uwa da d'iyar ta. "


Suhailat ta faki idon ammy tayi masa gwalo, murmushi yayi bai ce komai ba ya shiga motar sa yayi mata key, ammy ta kama hannun suhailat suka shiga ciki.


To wuni ammy tayi tana faman d'awainiya da suhailat, yau kam taci abinci saboda d'an wake ammy tayi mata yasha kayan had'i, kuma Allah y taimake ta da taci bata yi aman sa ba, haka suka wuni hira da ammy wata shak'uwa da soyayyar junan su ta k'ara shiga ckn zuciyoyin su, ckn hikima ammy ta dinga koya mata yanda zata yi rayuwar auren ta ckn kwanciyar hankali, a haka fk ya same su, suhailat ta saki jiki tana kwasar dariya, ya manta yaushe rabon da ya ganta ckn irin wannan nishad'in don tun lokacin da ta fara laulayi tayi bankwana da wal-wala kullum ckn yi masa kuka take yau kam yaji dad'in yanda ya same ta.


Wasu files ne a hannun sa ya ajiye su, ya k'araso ckn parlourn yana fad'in. "Wash yau na gaji da yawa gashi kuma ba'a gida nk ba balle a d'anyi min tausa."


"To rasa kunya ka kwanta ayi maka tausar anan mana ." ammy ta fad'a tana harar sa, sosa k'ayataccen gashin kansa yayi yana murmushi yace. "Ammy ya  gida, y wannan rigimammiyar koda yake naga yau har wani sabon nishad'i tk." 


Ya k'arasa maganar yana kallon suhailat wacce tun maganar sa ta farko kunya ta sanya ta sunkuyar da kanta tana mamakin halin mijin nata.

Ammy tace. "To ina ruwan ka? ko sa idon naka ne ya tashi."? 

"Shikenan ammy na fahimci indai hayaty na kusa dk bakya ta tawa duk abinda nayi ban iya ba." Ya k'arasa maganar ckn shagwa6a sannan ya mik'e tsaye tare da kallon Suhailat "Muje ko." Abinda kawai ya fad'a kenan yana k'ok'arin juya wa."

"Kuje ina."? Ammy ta tanbaya, ya juyo " gida mana ammy. "


"Au bazaka tsaya kaci abinci ba sannan sai ku tafi." Ya d'an gyara tsaiwa shi gsky baya son tsayawar nan don dai babu yanda zaiyi ne, koma wa yayi ya ajiye files d'in dk hannun sa sannan yace. "To ammy ki bani aron d'iyar taki ta d'an taya ni shirya wa." Bai jira amsar ta ba ya nufi 6angarensa na gidan.





Ammy ta juyo ta kalli suhailat wacce ta k'ura tv ido kamar bata jin abinda suke yi, "d'iya ta tashi kije mijin ki na jiran ki a 6angarensa." 

Ckn damuwa tace. "Amma ammy...." "A'a d'iya ta karki damu tashi kije ki kula da mijin ki kinji, kada ki yadda yanayin ciki ya hana ki kula da mijinki."

Ita dai suhailat ckn kunya ta mik'e ta nufi 6angaren nasa, a kwance ta same shi a d'aya daga ckn kujerun dk parlourn, yana ganin ta shigo ya janyota ta fad'a kansa rungume ta yayi yana shinshina wuyan ta ckn rad'a yace. "Yau me ammy ta baki ne naga kamar a k'oshe kk." Tayi murmushi kfn tace. "Ni gsky ka dawo dani wajen ammy don ta fika iya kula, yau d'an wake fa ta yi min kaji dad'in sa kuwa? Wlh ko kaine kaci shi sai ka nemi k'ari."


Ya shafi gefen face d'in ta yace. "Ai nima na iya d'anwaken don baki ce nayi miki bane."

Ta ta6e baki kfn tace."Ba dai irin na ammy ba, ka tashi kayi wankan kada ammy taga mun dad'e."

Harar ta yai ckn wasa sannan yace. "Ai tunda ita ta tsayar damu babu abinda zata ce duk abinda mu kayi don haka ni bazan yi wanka mara dalili ba dole a samu dalilin yin wakan nan."


"Kaje aiki ka gaji da yawa wannan ba dalilin da zaisa kayi wanka don kaji dad'in jikinka bane."? Ta bashi amsa tamkar bata fahimci abinda yk nufi ba.


Ya gyara kwanciyar sa ya d'ora ta akan ruwan cikin sa, sannan ya kafa mata kyawawan idanun sa yana son tura mata wasu sak'onni ta ckn k'wayar idon, tabbas ya samu nasara don gaba d'aya jikinta ya mutu murus ta lura idan batayi da gsk ba abun kunya yk so su tafka a gidan ammy don haka ta zabura da sauri zata mik'e, ya gane nufin ta hakan tasa ya fisgo ta ta fad'a k'irjin sa.


" Me yasa kk min wasa da lpyr ku keda baby?

"Am sorry" ta fad'a tana kama kunnenta, "shikenan amma gsky ki dinga kula.".


Ya d'ora bakinsa a saitin kunnenta yace." Da gsk nk fa hayaty sai kin kawo a gaji don am in feeling." 

Ta zaro ido sannan ckn damuwa tace."Don Allah nafsy kayi hak'uri muje gida karka janyo min na kasa fita daga ckn d'akin nan."


Yasan da gsk tk har ckn ranta, amma sai yace. "To kisan dai yanda xakiyi dani don gsky ni ko driving bazan iya ba."

Ganin zata 6atawa kanta lokaci yasa dole ta bada kai bori ya hau.

A tare suka yi wanka sai wani cika tk yi k'asan ranta kuma tana tunanin yanda zata kalli idon ammy matsananciyar kunyar ammy tk ji, shi kam ko a jikinsa harkar gaban sa kawai yk har y'an wasu wak'ok'i yake yana mata dariya, tamkar tai kuka ya kalleta.''Kinga yarinya idan ma zaki saki ranki gwanda ki saki domin ni ban ma san kina yi ba, sannan hakan da kk yi ne zai nuna wa ammy anyi wani abun amma idan kk saki fuskar ki baxata gane komai ba, ke ita fa babu ruwan ta ko a gaban ta zan iya rungume ki."  


Harara ta watsa masa batayi magana ba haka ya gama tsokanar ta ya janyo hannun ta suka fito daga 6angaren nasa, suhailat dai tunda suka fito bata d'aga kanta ba kwata-kwata saboda ji tk dama k'asa ta tsage ta shiga ciki tsabar kunya.


"Ammy kinga mun dad'e ko wlh feeling ne ya dame ni shi yasa na rik'e y'ar taki saida ta a gaxa."


Ckn sauri gami da razana ta d'ago kanta tare da dafe k'irjin ta, kfn tayi magana ya kwashe da dariya. "Duba kiga." Ya fad'a yana nuna mata wajen da ammy tk ta juyo a hankali har yanxu kirjinta bai daina harbawa ba, sai taga wayam babu kowa ta sauke wata ajiyar xuciya a fili, aikuwa ta dage ta manna masa mintsini a hannu.

"Auch da zafi fa." Tai saurin guduwa tana murmushi, ya bita suka fara zagaye a parlourn yana cewa sai ya rama.

 Ammy ce ta shigo hannun ta rik'e da wani d'an ma dai-daicin basket an shirya kulolin abinci a ciki, suhailat na ganin ta ta gudu wajen ta.

Ammy ta kalli fk tace."meye kuma kk yi haka fahad sai ka sa ta fad'i. "?

Murmushi yayi yana sosa k'eya yace." Itace fa ta gallara min mintsini ammy. "

Harara ammy ta watsa masa kfn tace. "To zo ka rama." Suhailat ta lek'o tare da yi masa gwalo, ya girgixa kai tare da yin k'wafa.


"Kinga daughter taho muje dining alala nayi miki Allah yasa ki iya cinta kamar yanda kk ci d'an waken." Suhailat wacce yawunta har ya tsinke tace. "Zanci ammy." "Yauwa to xo muje." Ammy ta fad'a tare da ruk'o  hannun ta.


"Wai ni yau babu Wanda zai bani abinci." Fahad ya fad'a tare da rungume hannun sa a k'irji yana kallon su, ba tare da ta kalle shi ba ammy tace. "Ai kitchen in ba bak'on ka bane, kaje ka duba abinda zaka iya ci."

Ya rausayar da kai ckn nuna kalar tausayi yace. "Yau kuma da kaina zan zuba abinci Allah sarki ni ." 

Suhailat ta yunk'ura xata tashi tana fad'in." Bari na kawo maka nafsy. " ammy ta zaunar da ita tare da cewa. "Rabu dashi daughter duk neman magana ne yau ya fara zuba abinci da kansa? Mutumin da har girkawa yk shine zai tsaya yana wannan maganar yunwar ce bata dame shi ba." Murmushi suhailat tayi lokacin da ta d'auko lomar alala ta nufi bakinta.


Ganin da gsk ammy bazata bari a bashi abincin ba yasa ya tafi kitchen da kansa ya zubo wainar shinkafa da miyar agushi, ko kallon inda dining d'in yk baiyi ba ya zauna a kujera yaci abincin sa, sai wani 6ata rai yk shi a dole yayi fushi.

Rabin hankalin suhailat na kansa tana cin alalar tana satar kallon sa, ammy tana lura dasu duk tayi musu banxa itama ta cigaba da cin abincin ta.


Yana gama wa ba tare da ya d'auke plate d'in da yaci abincin ba ya d'auki files d'in ya nufi hanyar waje yana fad'in. "Ni na tafi ammy ki sanya driver ya kawo ta in ta gama." Ya fad'i hakan babu walwala a face d'in shi.


Dariya ammy tayi kfn tace. "Wlh ngd fahad da ka kawo min ita amma ka sani kana sanya k'afa ka tafi ita da ta koma gidan ka sai ta haihu."

Da gudu ya dawo yana dariya. "Wlh da wasa nk ammy tsokanar ta fa nk."

Ammy tayi dariya "hmm idan ma da gsk kk kaje."

"Hayaty kina jin abinda ammy tk cewa kinyi shiru."? Ya fad'a yana kallon suhailat wacce tk faman k'unshe dariya.

Ta d'ago tace." Ni wlh da ma zaka taimaka min kabarni anan ammy ta dinga yi min d'an wake da alalar ta masu mugun dad'i. "

"To kaji dai don haka kayi tfyr ka idan an kwana 2 sai kazo ku tafi."

"K'arya tk ammy ta fad'a ne kawai." Ya fad'a yana harar suhailat

"OK abinda kk yi mata kenan ko sai naga Wanda zai baka ita ku tafi."

Ya samu guri ya zauna. "Wlh ammy sai dai mu zauna tare."

Ammy tayi dariya kfn tace. "Daughter tashi ki shirya ku tafi indai d'an wake da alala ne zan dinga aiko miki dasu har ma da wasu abubuwan."

Dama itama a xuciyar ta addu'a tk Allah yasa ammy tace su tafi saboda muddin suka zauna a gidan ta tabbatar sai doctor ya janyo mata abun kunya a wajen ammy, don haka ta mik'e ta nufi bathroom ta wanke hannun ta tare da wanke bakinta sannan ta d'auko hijabinta ta fito, ta same su shi da ammy suna hira ammy ta dube ta "har kin fito daughter"?

"Eh ammy ngd da d'awainiyar da kk yi dani Allah y..." Rufe mata baki ammy tayi da hannun ta tare da fad'in. "Kull kada ki k'ara yi min gdy don nayi miki wani abun indai kin d'auke ni a matsayin mahaifiyar ki." Tayi k'asa da kanta k'walla na k'ok'arin kawowa idon ta ziyara, tace."insha'allah bazan k'ara yi ba ammy. " fk ya kamo hannun ta "To ammy sai kinzo mun tafi." Wata k'atuwar leda ta mik'a masa "rik'e wa daughter wannan." 

Suhailat ta dubi ledar babu damar tayi gdy don haka sai kawai tayi murmushi suka wuce, ammy har bakin mota ammy ta rako su byn sun shiga ta mik'a musu basket d'in dk hannun ta, fk yace. "Mun gode ammy Allah y k'ara girma."

Haka yaja motar suka tafi ammy na d'aga musu hannu, saida ya kaita gidan su ta gaida dady shima ya sanya musu albarka, kubra ce kawai a gidan sai y'an aiki d'iyar kubra momy k'arama ba lpy sai rigima tk suna ta faman lallashinta itada tabawa me aiki.


Tunda ga ranar kullum sai ammy tayi mata abinci na gargajiya an kaimata kuma babu laifi duk abinda taci yana zama amma idan tayi da kanta sam bata iya ci da taci sai amai, ko girkin fk sai ta toshe hancin ta tk iya yi masa, yace ta hak'ura ya samo y'ar aiki amma tak'i yadda da hakan.


Wata rana byn fk ya tafi office ta gama duk aiyukan da tk sai ta kwanta a kujera take bacci yai gaba da ita, sam ta manta bata rufe k'ofar gidan ba,saboda tsabar baccin da tk ji idonta har rufewa yk.

FK ya dawo daman ba dad'ewa zaiyi a hospital d'in ba wasu patients da yayi wa aiki jiya ya duba, tunda ga waje yaga gate a bud'e don haka sai yai parking a waje ya fito, kai tsaye ya shiga, a parlour ya same ta tana sharar baccin ta ko motsin shigowar sa bata ji ba, yayi murmushi a hankali ya k'arasa wajen ta don baya so ya tashe ta, ya sumbaci goshinta sannan ya wuce d'akin sa don ya d'an watsa ruwa.

Sallama ake ta k'wala wa a k'ofar parlourn hakan yasa suhailat ta farka daga baccin ta, ta tashi zaune tana murza ido wata mata ce a tsaye tana kallon suhailat d'in, a hankali tace. "Baiwar Allah ki shigo mana." 

Matar ta shigo tana k'are wa parlourn kallo daga ganin ta kaga wa yayyun y'an duniya, "ga waje ki zauna." suhailat ta katse ta daga kallon k'urillar da tk bin parlourn dashi.

Ta samu guri ta zauna mik'ewa suhailat tayi ta shiga bathroom ta wanke face d'in ta, sannan ta fito ta d'auko wa matar lemo da snacks, sannan taxo ta zauna, matar ta dube ta. "Baki gane ni ba ko."? Murmushi suhailat tayi kfn tace." Eh wlh. " ta d'auki cincin  ta kai bakinta tare da korawa da lemo sannan tace."suna na lubabatu amma zaki iya kira na da lubna, ni makociyar ki ce, tun lokacin da aka kawo ki nk ta zuba ido naga ko zaki shigo mu gaisa amma naji shiru, shine nace yau bari ni nazo tunda ni na damu nayi k'awancen dk." Suhailat ta d'an yi murmushi tana kallon lubna wacce tk faman cin abubuwan da aka ajiye mata, ko kad'an bata kwanta mata a rai ba "ta6 wannan matar da daga ganin ta kasan ta girmi aunty yasmeen ma balle kuma ni." Ta fad'a ckn xuciyar ta a fili kuwa cewa tayi. "Ba haka bane lubna kinsan kowa da ra'ayinsa mijna baya son shige-shige shi yasa ma bana shiga ko ina."

Lubna ta ta6e baki."Yo dama idan zaki biyewa namiji ai da kowa sai ya rabaki." Sai kuma tace."wai wanne irin bacci ne me mugun nauyi na same ki kina yi ko dai har ciki ne dk."r suka kama suhailat, daga had'uwar su yau sai kawai ta fad'a mata tana da ciki alhalin bata san wacece ita ba.

"Ta6 yaushe ma aka kawo ki da har xakiyi ciki."

Lubna ta katse suhailat daga tunanin da tk ta hanyar jefa ta ckn tsananin mamaki zuwa yanxu ta fara tsoron lubna, da sauri tace. "Ni bani da fa komai nayi aiki na gaji ne shi yasa na kwanta na dan huta kuma ban dad'e da kwanciya ba kk xo shi yasa banji ba."

"Ok to ni dai shawarar da zan baki kada ki yadda yarinya k'arama kyakkyawa dk ki tsufa da wuri saboda haihuwa tsofar da mace tk yi, kin ganni nan shekara ta 5 da aure amma ban yadda na haihu ba, saboda bana son tsufa yazo min ban shirya ba." Ta zaro wani tablets daga y'ar k'aramar jakar dk hannun ta tace. "Kinga wannan maganin ko ciki ya kai 4 months indai kinsha sai ya zube, bana rabo dashi saboda ina da sakacin shan maganin hana d'aukar ciki, idan ciki ya shiga ina shan wannan ya gama a take zanyi ministration, idan kina so gashi kema ki ajiye saboda tsaro." 


Kfn suhailat tayi yunk'urin yin magana tuni fahad Wanda yk la6e yana sauraron su ya isa gaban lubna ransa a matuk'ar 6ace, fitowarsa kenan kunnensa yayi masa mugun ji ganin abun yana so ya wuce tunanin sa yasa ya fito don yayi wa tufkar hanci, ya dubi lubna ckn tsananin 6acin rai yace. "Malama tashi ki barmin gida kuma kada ki kuskura na sake ganin k'afarki a gida na nonsense kawai."

Lubna ta mik'e dukda ta tsorata amma hakan baisa bakinta ya mutu ba tace."Dakata mlm saboda nazo gidan ka bashi ne ya baka damar ci min zarafi ba, me ake da namiji Wanda yk la6e, ke ashe mijin naki bai yadda dk bane shi yasa ya hana ki shiga ckn jama'a." Ta k'arasa maganar tare da kallon suhailat wacce jikinta yk rawa tsabar tsoro bata ta6a ganin nafsyn ta ckn mugun fushi irin wannan ba, mari fk ya kawo wa lubna kamar walk'iya suhailat ta shiga tsakanin su aikuwa ji kk tass a kumatun suhailat k'arfin marin yasa saida ta durk'ushe a k'asa, ganin haka da gudu lubna ta bar gidan.




Maimakon fk ya tausayawa suhailat inaa xuciyar sa bata bashi wannan damar ba a wannan lokacin saboda yanda tk tafarfasa aikuwa ya rufe ta da masifa."Dama idan na fita da irin wad'annan kk mu'amala?
To ki sani wlh wlh duk abinda ya samu ciki na bazan yadda ba daga ke din har su zan iya shari'a daku akan wannan cikin."


Yana gama fad'ar haka ya wuce d'akin sa ya barta a wajen durk'ushe ckn mugun yanayi.

Kuka tk sosae me yasa doctor zai yanke mata hukuci ba tare da yayi bincike ba, haka ta d'auki tsawon lokaci tana kuka kfn ta lallashi kanta ta mik'e tsaye, wata mummunar fad'uwa gaban ta yayi sakamakon ganin jini yana bin k'afarta bata san lokacin da ta saki wata k'ara ba tare da durk'ushe wa a wajen.............



UMMU MASHKUR CE MANAZARTA

            SUHAILAT

Bata san lokacin da ta saki wata k'ara ba tare da durk'ushe wa a wajen........


*continues*
Yana shiga d'akin sa yarasa abinda yk masa dad'i suhailat ta bashi mamaki ashe zata iya yin huld'a da irin wad'annan mutanen? Lallai ta shayar dashi mamaki da bak'inciki me yawa, bai ta6a zaton zata yi hakan take kuma maganganun matar suka fara dawo masa" *kinga wannan maganin ko ciki ya kai 4 months indai kinsha sai ya zube*

Anya ma ba itace ta sanya matar ta kawo mata maganin ba? Wata xuciyar tace masa suhailat baxata yi haka ba, kayi dai bincike.

Ya furxar da wani huci me zafi koma dai menene dole ya d'auki k'wak'k'waran matakin rabata da wannan matsiyaciyar matar.

Bathroom ya shiga ya watsa ruwa ko zai samu sassauci a ransa,yana fitowa yaji suhailat ta k'wala k'ara, dukda yana ckn yanayin 6acin rai hakan bai hana yaji karar tata har ckn k'ok'on xuciyar sa ba, don haka da gudu ya fita daga shi sai boxes.

A durk'ushe ya same ta ckn matsanancin kuka, kfn ya tanbaye ta abinda ya faru ta fara nuna masa da hannu, ai lokacin da yaga jini tk zubarwa, innalillahi daskarewa yayi a tsaye kfn kace meye wannan sai ga hawaye yana kukan jikinsa har rawa yk ji yk kamar ya shak'e ta itama, ckn kukan ya fara magana. "Me yasa? me yasa kk yi min haka Maryam? Tabbas kin cutar dani cutar da bazan ta6a iya yafe wa ba, wato kfn nazo kin riga kinsha maganin da tsinanniyar matar ta kawo miki ko? Koda yk ba laifin ta bane tunda ke kk bata izinin ta siyo ta kawo miki, me yasa kk aure ni byn kin san bakya son had'a zuri'a dani?Ashe duk soyayyar da kk nuna min yaudara ce me nayi miki kk yi min irin wannan tsanar why why...." Gunjin kuka ya hana shi k'arasa maganar, ya durk'ushe shima a wajen yana cigaba da kuka, itama kukan tk tabbas ta shiga ckn zargi kuma babu Wanda zai fitar da ita sai Allah ta rasa wacce hanyar zata bi ta sanar dashi bata sha komai ba hasali ma bata san matar da yk zargin ta kawo mata maganin ba itama yau ta fara ganin ta.


Rarrafo wa tayi tare da zuwa wajen sa ta rik'e k'afarsa tana kuka tace. "Don Allah nafsy ka yarda dani wlh ban sha komai ba, sannan wlh ban ta6a ganin lubna ba sai yau."

Yai shiru yana mata wani kallon banxa."Yau kk fara ganin har kk san sunan ta ko? Wlh Maryam kin bani mamaki da wani ne ya fad'a min abinda ido na ya gane min, wlh sai na hukunta shi, sai gashi ni da kaina na kama ki an kawo miki maganin da za'a zubar min gudan jini na, byn kinsha kin zubar kuma kice baki sha komai ba, a baya bansan ki da k'arya ba amma yau gashi na kama ki da ita kuma duk abinda zaki fad'a min bazan ta6a yadda dk ba."


Ya tukud'e ta gefe aikuwa  goshinta ya bugu da bango ta saki kukan azaba, ko kallon ta baiyi ba ya mik'e tsaye yana fad'in. "Wlh sai kun biya ni jinin d'a na, dole in shigar da k'ara." Ya fad'a d'akin sa tamkar mahaukaci.


Ita kam tan kwance inda ya jefa ta goshinta na fitar da jini yayinda jinin dk zuba a k'asan ta shima ya k'aru, a hankali ta rarrafa ta d'auko wayar ta a xuciyar ta tana shawarar Wanda yakamata ta kira tsakanin aunty yasmeen da ammy, haka ta yanke shawarar kiran ammy koba komai tasan ita babba ce zata shawo musu kan al'amarin sannan tasan halin aunty yasmeen zata iya d'aukar zafi da fk a lokacin da ta fahimci abinda ya faru.


Ringing 1 ammy ta d'aga tare da sallama, bata tsaya amsa sallamar ba ckn kuka tace. "Ammy don Allah kizo jini ne ya6alle min na rasa yanda zanyi." Ai ammy bata k'arasa jin k'arshen maganar ba ta ajiye wayar tare da k'wala wa driven ta kira bata tsaya d'aukar komai ba sai hijabi, saida ta shiga motar ta fad'a masa inda zai kai ta.


Motar ammy na k'ok'arin shiga ta fk tana k'ok'arin fita,ganin ammy ce sai ya tsaida tasa motar har yanxu bai daina kuka ba, tabbas yaji bakincin rabuwa da gudan jinin sa,


Ammy ta sauko daga motar tana bin tilon d'anta da kallo Wanda yk zubar da hawaye hankalin ta ya k'ara tashi tasan indai ba mutuwa suhailat tayi ba babu abinda zai sanya jarumin yaronta kuka, "menene ya faru."? Ammy ta tanbaya ckn matsanancin tashin hankali." Ta zubar min da d'a na ammy. "

Da sauri ammy ta shiga ciki dukda bata fahimci abinda yk nufi ba, ta gane wata k'ila 6ari suhailat tayi tunda ta kira ta, a kwance ta same ta shame-shame ckn jini, "innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji'un fahad me yasa ka barta a nan baka kaita asibiti ba ta suma fa."

"Ammy itace fa da kanta tasha maganin zubar da ciki ta zubar min da d'a." Ya fad'i haka yana lek'a fuskar suhailat wacce ta daina numfashi, mari ammy ta zabga masa."baka da hankali ne d'auke ta maza ka duba ta meye amfanin zaman ka likita amma matar ka bata mori hakan ba, da inane zaka je kabar y'ar mutane ta mutu, ko itace ta sha maganin da gsk to so kk itama mu rasa ta kenan saboda an rasa d'an tayi Wanda bai zo ma duniyar ba. "


Yaji ciwon kiran d'ansa d'an tayi da ammy tayi, amma shima sai yanxu hankalin sa kawo akan zata iya mutuwa fa, don haka ya kinkemeta dukda jini dk jikinta ya nufi charging room dk gidan, ya fara bata taimakon gaggawa ckn 30 seconds ta farfad'o dukda bata bud'e idonta ba numfashin ta ya dawo ya jona mata k'arin ruwa sannan ya bar d'akin, ba zai iya duba cikin yana nan ko baya nan ba, yasani muddin ya bincika yaga cikin ya fita ba zai iya control d'in kansa ba zai iya aikata komai shi yasa ya kira doctor muhseen, ammy tace ."Me yasa kai ba zaka duba ta da kanka ba."?

Nan ya bata labarin abinda ya faru tunda ga abinda yaji lubna ta fad'a mata har zuwa faruwar abun.

 
Ammy tayi shiru "gskyr magana ban yadda yarinyar nan zata aikata haka ba, sai dai idan 
ba kayi bincike ba." "Ni kaina ammy da fad'a min akayi wlh bazan ta6a yadda ba sai gashi ni da kaina na gani."

Ammy tayi shiru tana mamaki har yanxu bata yadda ba ta dube shi. "Ka tanbaye ta kuwa kaji ta bakinta."? " kinsan  idan raina ya 6aci bana sauraron komai, xuwan ki ne ma yasa na d'an samu sassaucin abinda nk ji."
"Tabbas kayi kuskure ka bari ta samu lpy muji ta bakinta inada tabbacin daughter bazata yi hakan da gan-gan ba, kaine mijinta yakamata ace kafi  kowa sani abinda zata iya yi da Wanda bazata iya yin saba saboda kai ne mutum na farko da za'a fara tanbayar halayyar ta, don haka ka sani ni ban yadda ta aikata wannan abun ba."

Jikin fk yayi sanyi sosae da maganar ammy yasan duk maganar ta gsky ce sai dai fa shima yakamata ammy ta fahimce shi, ba wai wani ne yazo ya fad'a masa abinda ya faru ba shine ya gani hakan da idonsa kuma ganin da yayi kawai ya isa hujja don haka yace."Na fahiceki ammy amma ina so kisani bazan ta6a yi mata sharri ba, kuma a yanda na fuskanci maganar da suke yi sun gama sa bawa da juna, banda haka yaushe ne ma tasan suhailat tana da ciki? Sannan abinda yasa na k'ara gasgata hasashe na shine duk lokacin da wahalar cikin ta ishe ta tana yawan ce min na cire mata shi ta huta, ni kuma sai nayi ta lallashinta to ina tunanin don taga ni bazan yi mata abinda tk so bane shi yasa ta nemi wacce zata iya yi mata hakan." 

Yaja numfashi xuciyar sa na k'una  "Ammy ina matuk'ar k'aunar hayaty amma son da nk mata ba zai rufe min ido na kasa hukunta ta akan laifin da tayi min ba saboda gaba."

Ajiyar xuciya ammy ta sauke zuwa yanxu ta fara yadda da d'anta amma dai har yanxu akwai ko kwanto ckn ranta, ta dube shi. "Na fahimci abinda kk nufi fahad amma kasani ni ba irin ka bace bazan ta6a yanke mata hukunci alhalin banji ta bakinta ba wata k'ila ba yadda muke tunani bane, dole ne mu bari ta samu lpy kfn mu yanke hukunci, idan ya tabbata da gan-gan tasa aka zubar min da jika na tabbas nima saina hukunta ta idan kuma bata da laifi kai ne Wanda zan hukunta."


Ckn 30minutes sai ga doctor muhseen yazo, ammy ta dube shi byn sun gama gaisawa tace."muhseen don Allah ina so ka duba min suhailat it's pregnant 4month's amma yau sai kawai ta fara bleeding mun rasa dalilin hakan abokin ka bazai iya duba ta ba saboda yana ckn damuwa shi yasa aka kira ka, kayi min bincike sosae ko zaka gano abinda tasha ko taci ya sanya ta bleeding. "

Doctor muhseen ckn girmamawa yace." insha'allah zan bincika ammy tana inane."? 

Fk da ya dafe kansa ya mik'e suka nufi charging room d'in, tana kwance kamar yanda ya barta amma yanxu bacci tk yi, ganin doctor muhseen ya tsurawa fuskarta ido sai kishi ya turnik'e shi."Mlm kayi aikin ka."

Muhseen yayi murmushi haba aboki na me kk tunani? ina kallon yanda yarinyar ta rame lokaci d'aya ne, dubi goshin ta fa ka gani a kumbure yk wata k'ila fad'uwa ma tayi har haka ta faru."  Sai yanxu ya kula ma da goshin nata, ya d'an ciji lips d'in sa yana tuna lokacin da ya hankad'e ta ta bugu da tile's ya runtse idonsa koma menene itace wacce ta janyo wa kanta.

Muhseen yace "ina buk'atar nayi testing blood d'in ta tunda babu damar ayi testing fitsarin ta, da kansa ya d'auko sirinji ya zuk'i jinin ya mik'a wa doctor muhseen saboda yana kishin ya ta6a masa mata, haka sukayi aikin tare duk abinda ya shafi sai an ta6a jikinta shi yk yi haka suka zauna jiran result kasancewar da akwai kayan aiki a d'akin ya zuba komai da za'a buk'ata.

Lokacin da result d'in ya fito doctor muhseen ya mik'a mishi takaddar yana murmushi, fk ya kauda kansa " me kk so na gani? Tabbacin na rasa d'a na? Ni nasan ya riga ya fita so kk sai na gani a rubuce ko me."?

Doctor muhseen yace. "Haba aboki na be a man karka yi sa6o fa ubangiji ne ya baka, to idan ma ya kar6i abinda ya baka ya zaka yi dole sai dai kayi hak'uri, ni na manta ma ba kaine ka sanya ni aikin ba, ammy yakamata na damk'awa result d'in." Ya fad'a tare da mik'ewa ya nufi parlourn.


Fk ma ya tashi jikin sa a sanyaye ta tabbata cikin ya zube, yaxo wajen da suhailat ke kwance tare da kamo hannun ta ya rik'e tsam yana fitar da wani numfashi."kin rabani da farinciki na Maryam me yasa kk za6i sanya ni ckn bak'inciki? Bazan iya yi miki hukunci ba hayaty amma zan iya hukunta tsinanniyar da ta kawo miki wannan maganin." Ya k'arasa maganar tare da kifa kansa a jikinta yana fitar da hawaye, "Ka yadda dani nafsy ban sha komai ba, ban ta6a yi maka k'arya ba kuma ba zan fara daga yanxu ba Allah shine shaida ta."

Muryar suhailat yaji tana fad'in haka a hankali, sai dai har yanxu idonta a rufe yk da alama mafarki ma tk.

Mik'e wa yayi tare da fitowa daga d'akin ya nufi parlour, a hanyar shiga ya  had'u da doctor muhseen zai tafi muhseen ya d'an daki shoulder d'in sa yace. "Mutumi na na taya ka murna amma dai gsky ka dinga kula sosae kai likita kasan duk abinda ya dace game ciki." Yana gama fad'ar haka ya wuce ya tafi sam dk bai fahimci maganganun sa ba kuma bai tanbaye shi ba, haka ya k'arasa shiga parlourn ammy ya gani da result d'in a hannun ta tana murmushi, kansa ya d'aure me zaisa ammy tayi murmushi byn abinda yk cikin wannan takardar abun bak'inciki ne.

Kfn yayi magana ammy ta ya fito shi da hannu tana cewa."zo kaga result Allah y tak'aita babu abinda ya samu cikin." Da gudu ya k'arasa ya kar6i takadddar yana dubawa, a take nadama da danasanin abinda ya aikata ya fara bijirowa xuciyar sa, ba wani abu bane ya haifar mata da bleeding d'in ba sai firgita da tashin hankali da ta tsinci kanta a ciki, amma babu alamar wani abun tasha don zubar dashi, yai shir yana nazarin takaddar sam ya manta tashin hankali ko firgita yana sanya mace me k'aramin ciki shiga irin wannan yanayin, zama yayi a d'aya daga ckn kujerun yana sauke ajiyar xuciya tabbas yayi kuskure da bai saurari ta bakinta ba ya yanke mata hukunci kai tsaye.


Ammy ta kalle shi ckn mamaki kfn tace. "Ya naga kayi haka ko ba kayi farinciki bane."?  Ammy suhailat bata aikata komai ba ba tasha maganin zubar da ciki ba hasali ma nine Wanda na aikata kuskure ta hanyar jefa ta ckn tsananin tashin hankali da firgici Wanda shine ya haifar mata da bleeding."

Ammy ta dube shi "ka gani ko? meye amfanin yanke hukunci ckn fushi."?

Fk ya sunkuyar da kansa cike da damuwa sannan yace." Amma ni wannan lamarin ya bani mamaki, da kunne na fa naji komai sannan ni da kaina na kori matar! Kai gsky abun ya bani mamaki." 

Ammy ta mik'e tana fad'in."yanxu dai ita kad'ai ce wacce zata fahimtar damu." Bai samu damar yin magana ba har ta fice daga parlourn ta bar shi ya had'a kai da gwiwa yana faman tunani.


Lokacin da amma ta shiga d'akin idon suhailat 2 tana kallon sealing tana ganin shigowar ammy ta fara k'ok'arin tashi zaune, ammy tayi saurin k'arasa wa tare da kwantar da ita tana fad'in. "Kwanta ki huta daughter baki ga ruwa aka sanya miki bane."?

Ta koma ta kwantar da kanta tayi shiru, ammy ta zauna a gefen gadon ta d'ora kan suhailat a cinyar ta tana shafa gashin kanta.


"Ammy ki fad'a masa wlh bansha komai ba yak'i sauraro na balle ya yadda dani." Muryar Suhailat ke fita a hankali dukda cewa idonta a rufe yk." Ki kwantar da hankalin ki daughter babu abinda ya samu babyn ku abinda ya faru kuma ba laifin ki bane shine ma Wanda yk da laifi, ki kwantar da hankalin ki idan kin k'ara samun sauk'i sai muyi maganar. " 

Farinciki ne ya mamaye xuciyar  ta jin cewa babyn yana nan babu abinda ya same shi ta k'ara lumshe idonta tana sauke 6oyayyir ajiyar xuciya.



Shi kuwa yana can parlour yana tunanin abinda ya faru tabbas shine me laifi da ya kasa fahimtar komai ya furxar da huci yana jin tsanar kansa da duk abinda ya aikata ya dubi hannun sa Wanda dashi ne ya mari suhailat sai yaji kamar ya gutsire hannun ya huta.


Sai la'asar ta tashi daga baccin da tk, jikinta yayi mata dad'i bata jin ciwon  komai sai goshin ta da yk mata d'an zafi kad'an, ta mik'e zaune tana k'ok'arin saukowa daga gadon ammy ta shigo, itace ta taimaka mata har d'akin ta ta kaita suhailat sai faman raba ido tk inda zata ga fk amma bata ganshi ba wata k'ila har yanxu yana fushi dani ne ta fad'a ckn ranta. "Idan kin shirya ki fito kici abinci." Ammy ta fad'a byn ta gama had'a mata ruwan wanka.........

"Yo dama idan zaki biyewa namiji ai da kowa sai ya raba ki, wai wanne irin bacci ne me mugun nauyi na same ki ki nayi ko dai har ciki ne dk."?

Ammy ta fad'a tare da nuna fk Wanda yakasa d'ago idonsa saboda tsabar kunya, shi dama tun lokacin da akace masa babu abinda yasamu babyn jikinsa yayi sanyi yasan kuma haka zata faru.


Gaban sa yana fad'uwa ya tsugunna gaban ammy yace. "Kiyi hak'uri ammy don Allah, nasan nayi kuskure ayi min afuwa."



"Ba ni zaka bawa hak'uri ba don ni baka yi min komai ba ita da kayi wa sharri itace zaka nemi afuwar ta ba ni ba, sannan kuma kasani daga yau zan tafi da ita gida na har sai ta haihu don na lura baxaka iya kula min da ita ba."



Ckn tsananin damuwa ya dubi ammy tare da rik'e hannun ta, "don Allah Ammy  kiyi min duk irin  hukuncin  da zakiyi min amma kada ki raba ni da matata plss."

"Nasani nafsy ba da gaiyya kayi ba amma me yasa kak'i sauraro na sam baka bani damar kare kaina ba, shin a haka zamu cigaba da rayuwa duk lokacin da nayi maka laifi baxaka saurare ni ba da tun farko ka tsaya kaji ta bakina wata k'ila da ban shiga tashin hankalin da ya haifar min da bleeding ba."?

"Suhailat ni kad'ai ammy ta haifa bani da wa ko k'ani inaso ki tara min zuri'a wad'anda zanyi alfahari dasu, ina son yara da yawa suhailat shi yasa na haukace miki a lokacin da naga kamar za'a raba ni da yaro na kiyi hak'uri, gani tsugunne a gaban ki kiyi min duk irin hukuncin da ya dace dani, amma don Allah ki rok'ar min ammy akan kada ta d'auke min ku, kune farinciki na."

"Ammy ki dawo min da hayaty." Ya durk'ushe a gurin da k'yar ya mik'e ya koma gidan shima ya had'a kayan sa ya nufi gidan Ammy a cewar sa sai dai ta rik'e su tare.






To tunda ga wannan lokacin suka koma gidan Ammy k'ark'ashin kulawar ta, da farko bata basu damar kasance wa da juna ba saboda likita ya fad'a mata kasancewar su tare zai iya bada wata matsalar.




Kulawa sosae Ammy tk bawa suhailat xuwa lokacin cikin ya fito sosae, da akayi mata scanning aka gano y'an 2 ne, murna a wajen Ammy da fk ba'a magana ita kam suhailat tsoron haihu wa take, ammy ce ke kwantar mata da hankali.



A kwana a tashi ba wuya a wajen Allah cikin suhailat ya shiga wata 9 sai dai yayi girma na ban mamaki da k'yar take tfy kullum Ammy da fk suna faman kula da ita babu abinda ta nema ta rasa Amma dai ta matsu ta rabu dashi saboda komai nata ya canxa k'afafuwan ta sun kinbura suntum, da farko tana zuwa asibiti, kfn daga baya fahad ya dinga dubata da kansa ya kawo kayan aiki gidan Ammy harda na kar6ar haihuwa.


Wata ranar juma'a ta tashi da ciwon baya tun tana daure wa har ammy ta lura haka dai maimakon ciwon yayi sauk'i sai yayi gaba, marar ta ma ta fara ciwo, ammy ta kira fahad Wanda har ya fita office ya dawo yana zuwa ya duba sai yaga haihuwa ce amma da saura, haka ciwon ya dinga k'aruwa tana faman kiran Allah, su kuma ckn tausaya mata suke k'arfafa mata gwiwa.


Sai wajen la'asar nak'uda ta tashi sosae, da taimakon Allah da taimakon fk ta haifo santalelen d'an ta yana fad'owa wani ya biyo bayansa, wani irin numfashi tk fitar wa da sauri-sauri saida yai mata wata allura kfn numfashin ta ya dai-dai ta, mik'a wa Ammy yaran yayi ganin har yanxu Suhailat bata dawo haiyacin ta sosae ba.

Shi kam yana can yana kula da matar sa saida ya tabbatar ta samu bacci sakamakon allurar da yayi mata yana aikin xuciyar sa na kwad'ayin zuwa ga yaran sa, tabbas suhailat alkhairi ce a tare dashi.


Ganin tayi bacci ya fito ya samu Ammy zaune tana kallon yaran tana goge hawayen farinciki.

"Ina ma kabiru yana da rai yaga jikokinsa Allah ya jarabce shi da son yara kamar yanda nima nk so, Ashe ba mu zamu tara yaran ba, d'an mu ne, shi yasa akace ka rok'i Allah ya baka y'ay'a masu albarka ba masu yawa ba, yanxu gashi kai kad'ai kasamu y'ay'a har 3 a lokaci 1 kuma bamu san wad'anda zasu xo a gaba ba, Allah mun gode da wannan kyauta Allah y raya mana ku ckn musulunci."


"Ameen Ammy." Fk ya fad'a lokacin da ya d'auki na farko yana kallo kamar su d'aya dashi babu abinda ya baro nashi, haka ma na 2 gwanda ita macen ta d'an yi yanayi da mamanta.




Nan aka fara kiraye-kirayen waya ana sanar da y'an uwa da abokan arxiki kfn kace meye wannan tuni gidan ya fara cika da y'an barka, aunty yasmeen ana kiran ta kamar me jira sai gata, Ashe dama tana hanya zataxo ta ganta da yk tana yawan zuwa, sai gashi yau tazo a dai-dai.


Lokacin da taga yaran kamar ta cinye su don farinciki haka ta shiga d'akin da suhailat tk har yanxu bata farka ba.


Don haka suka koma aka cigaba da kar6ar y'an barka............

Suhailat bata farka daga bacci ba sai wajen magriba, lokacin da ta farka aunty yasmeen ce zaune a gaban ta tana rik'e da baby girl d'in a hannun ta.

Zuba wa yarinyar ido Suhailat tayi tana kallon ta a dai-dai wannan lokacin Ammy da fk suka shigo kowanne d'auke da yaro d'aya a hannun sa.

Da saurin su suka k'arasa ganin ta farka, Ammy ta shafa kan suhailat tace. "Sannu daughter kinji Allah dai ya raya mana su ckn musulunci." Yasmeen da fk suka amsa da "Ameen." Ita kam k'asa tayi da kanta cike da kunya, Ammy ta d'ora mata yaron dk hannun ta a gefe sannan ta fita aunty yasmeen ma mik'ewa tayi "Bari na had'a miki ruwan wanka."


Fk ya k'araso gadon hannun sa rik'e da babban, ya zauna kusa da ita ckn matsanancin farinciki, "hayaty kin gama min komai a rayuwa tunda kk haifa min wad'annan yaran ban ta6a tsintar kaina a ckn matsanancin farinciki irin wannan ba ngd ngd ban san da me zan saka miki sai dai nace Allah yayi miki albarka."


Suhailat tayi murmushi kfn tace. "Nafsy mu godewa Allah shine ya bamu wannan kyautar kuma shi ya bani ikon haifar su, wlh har yanxu gani nk kamar ba gsk ba, dukda cewa ga yaran a gaba na, amma nafsy da akayi scanning 2 aka gani ko."?


" Eh mana ai kad'an kenan daga ikon Allah ansha yin scanning aga namiji amma ckn ikon Allah a samu mace mu gashi munga 2 ashe 3 ne."


Ta d'an yi shiru tare da k'ura wa yaran ido dukkan su kamar su d'aya musamman mazan, ta dube shi. "Nafsy ya za'a yi mu dinga gane su."?



Ya mik'a mata na hannun sa, ya d'auki macen yace." Wannan shine babba sai kuma wannan."

 ya nuna mata d'ayan, "wanne suna kk za6a musu." Suhailat tayi murmushi cike da farinciki k'aunar yaran da mahaifin su na k'ara huda xuciyar ta, tace."A sanya musu sunan iyayen mu mana."


"To shikenan." ya d'auki babban yayi masa hud'uba, yace. "Na sanya masa kabiru." (Sunan mahaifin sa) 

Ya d'auki na biyun shima yayi masa hud'uba yace."shi kuma wannan Ibrahim." (Sunan dady)

Ya kalli baby girl d'in yace. "To ita kuma wanne sunan za'a sa mata."? Suhailat tayi murmushi kfn tace. "Ayi mata hud'uba da rukayya." (Sunan Ammy)

Ya kalleta "amma ni nayi tunanin sanya mata Khadija fa."

"Ka manta aunty yasmeen ta sanya ai ni ina ganin ta ishe mu duka." 

"Hakane amma tamu daban tasu daban don haka ki shirya haifa min khadija ko da agaba ne."

Harara ta watsa masa." wace zata k'ara haihu wa ai na gama. "

Yai dariya cike da tsokana yace. "Ta6 aikuwa ki shirya don sai kin hafa min yara 12, ko kuma na samo miki y'ar uwa ta taya ki."


Cike da kishi tace."ka samo 3 ma ba 12 ba ko 20 kk so zaka samu."

Dariya yai kfn yace. "Kwantar da hankalin ki hayaty babu wacce zan iya had'a ki da ita a xuciya ta, koda Allah ya kaddara min k'ara aure lallai matar zatayi zaman hak'uri don bazan iya kwatanta adalci a tsakanin ku ba."

Taji dad'in wannan maganar har xuciyar ta amma hakan ba zai sa ta tauye mijinta ba, don haka tace."nafsy duk Wanda kaga ana kishin sa lallai ana k'aunar sa, amma ni ba zan tauye kaba duk lokacin da kaji kana buk'atar k'ara aure k'ofa a bud'e tk abinda kawai nasani zanyi k'ok'arin kafa gwamanati wacce bata gushewa a xuciyar miji na."

Murmushi yayi tare da shafo gefen fuskarta yace. "Tabbas kin riga kin gama kafa wannan gwamnatin taki me cike da adalci a xuciyar mijinki."

Dai-dai lokacin aunty yasmeen ta shigo "to angon k'arni abar amaryar k'arnin  tayi wanka ta huta ko."

Ta fad'a lokacin da tk shigowa, yayi murmushi tare da mik'ewa yace."aunty don Allah ki bi min matata a hankali naji ance wankan da zafi."

"Karka damu ba irin shi za'a yi mata ba ai doctors kune kk hana wankan jego yanxu."

Aunty yasmeen ta kamo suhailat yayinda suka bar fk da yaran sa yana kallon su soyayya yaran da ta mahaifiyar su tana k'ara 6ula kowanne sashe na xuciyar sa, tabbas suhailat ta gama masa komai.


Ammy ta shigo d'akin tana fad'in. "Kazo ka sanya yara gaba mutane kuma suna ta zuwa ganin su, 6ata fuska yayi " Ammy gsky ni bana so a dinga yawan jagwal-gwala min yara."

"Kai Ammy wanne irin aiki kuma."?

" To an sanya min sunayen da bazan iya tsokanar suba, Allah stark ina ma mahaifin ka yana da rai shima yaji irin farincikin da na tsinci kaina a ciki, mungode my son." Ta fad'a tare da shafa kan fahad.

Shima yaji dad'in yanda ammy tayi farinciki yace."Ammy dukkan mu Allah zamu cigaba da godewa da kuma Suhailat itace ta bada shawarar a sanya sunayen ku."

Ammy tayi murmushi tana yaba kaifin hankalin yarinyar.

Me jego ansha wanka ta fito sharr  sai wani annurin haihu wa da tk fitar wa, ta dawo y'ar caras da ita duk girman da cikin nan yasa tayi ya gudu jinta tk sakayau.


Haka suka cigaba da kula da ita tare da yaran lokacin da aka kawo mata yaran ta shayar dasu harda kukanta da k'yar aka samu ta basu dukda cewa dama ansan bazai ishe su ba dole a had'a musu da madara.


Dady yaxo yaga takwaransa da na abokin sa yaji dad'i tare da yi musu adduo'i, a ranar suka had'u da ammy suka dinga hira tabbas xuwan dady ya tunawa ammy mijinta kabiru sai dai babu yanda zata yi tunda ya rasu don tun lokacin da taji wannan labarin ta shiga yi masa takaba tuntuni kuma ta gama.



(A gurguje)

Ranar suna ansh budiri raguna 6 fk ya yanka haka kaya akwati 6 yayi me jego 3 yaran kowa da tasa dukda cewa komai iri d'aya ne a ciki, Ammy ma tayi nata bajintar ta bawa Suhailat za6i akan ta za6i duk k'asar da tk so idan tayi 40 suje, dady kam takaddun gudaje ya mallakawa yaran kowa da nasa, ba maganar y'an uwa ake ba kowa da irin abun alkhairin da zai kawowa y'an 3, y'an Adamawa ma sunzo da nasu kayan arxikin inna ma tazo ta k'ara neman yafiyar ammy, haka dai akasha taro ckn farinciki da walwala hansatu ma tazo itama da goyon yaronta namiji.

Y'an group d'in ummu mash kur kowa saida yaje aka kwashi ganima, haka duk wad'anda aka gaiyato da ma gaiyyar sod'i kowa ya koma gidan sa cike da farinciki.


Y'an Adamawa basu tafi ba sai sun kwana 2 , fk ya d'auko takadar gidansa na jumeta yace."gashi nan ya mallaka musu su kwaso iyalansu su dawo ckn birnin Adamawa murna da farinciki ba'a magana, adduo'i kam yasha su, haka itama suhailat ranar da zasu tafi ta cika su da kayan arziki ciki kuwa harda motar da fk yabayar a matsayin sadakinta tace su d'auka su tafi da ita tunda baffah hamza ya iya mota.


Byn kowa ya watse aka bar me jego da yaran ta k'ar-k'ashin kulawar ammy sai aunty yasmeen dk zuwa lokaci-lokaci, ganin aikin yana yiwa ammy yawa yasa fahad ya nemo masu kula da yaran.

********

Momy k'arama y'ar gidan kubra Allah yayi mata rasuwa sakamakon kashin hak'ori da tayi fama dashi, haka ta gama alhinin ta ta warware, dady ya maida ita makarantar islamiyyah tare da gargad'i me zafi akan biyewa kawayen banxa, ita kanta ma bazata fara ba saboda ta gane babu riba a ckn harkar banza, yanxu abinda yk damun ta shine rashin aure tana iya bakin k'ok'arin ta wajen kare kanta daga koma wa ckn musiba.

Tayi alk'awarin bazata ta6a koma wa sa6on Allah ba, don haka ta k'udiri niyyar ko tsoho ne yazo da niyyar auren ta bazata k'i ba.

Aikuwa haka aka yi wani bawan Allah ya ganta matansa 2 da yazo bata tsaya 6atawa kanta lokaci ba ta amince kuma ta fad'a masa gsky ta ta6a haihuwa ya amince nan nan da nan aka yi bincike akansa aka sha biki ba tare da samun wata matsala ba.



Shima y sadeeq ya samu wata yarinya me suna husna me hankali da nutsuwa, ya yaba da hankalin ta shima ba'a wani 6ata lokaci ba wajen yin auren kasancewar itama iyayen ta a shirye suke.

Gida ya zamo saura dady kawai da y'an aikin sa, dukda cewa hakan yana d'an damun sa amma dai haka yk zaune abinsa bayayin tunanin aure kwata-kwata saboda matan ma yanxu tsoro suke bashi.


Lokacin da Suhailat suka yi 40 taxo gaida shi tk yi masa wata magana wacce ta tsaya masa a xuciyar sa, byn ta gaishe shi tayi shiru tana wasa da yatsunta, dady ya kalleta ckn kulawa kfn yace. "Mama na naga kamar da magana a bakinki ki fad'a min duk abinda yk xuciyar ki."

Murmushi tayi sannan ckn nutsuwa tace. "Dady don Allah wata alfarma nk nema a wajen ka."

Ckn rashin fahimta yace. "Alfarma kuma mama na."?

" Eh, dady ina so kayi aure ne."

Da sauri ya kalleta kfn yace. "Haba mama na yanxu ni wacce maganar aure kuma ai ni na hak'ura da auren don tsoron matan ma nk ji nasani bazan k'ara samun mace ta gari kamar mahaifiyar ki ba.''

Suhailat ta d'an gyara zama ckn kwantar da murya tace." Ba haka bane dady nasani akwai mata nagari masu kirki wad'anda suka fi ma umman tawa, ka duba magana ta dady bana so kana zama kai kad'ai a gidan nan sai masu aiki."

Yai dariya kfn yace. "Mama na kenan yanxu kina ganin akwai wacce zata aure ni byn na tsufa haka."? Itama dariyar tayi don tana ganin hak'anta ya kusa cimma ruwa tace." Haba dady ai baka tsufa ba ni idan ma ka amince min sai na za6a maka matar da kaina. " murmushi yayi ckn mamaki kfn yace. "Na amince mama na ki za6a min nasan bazaki za6a min wacce bata dace dani ba."

Murmushin samun nasara suhailat tayi kfn ta k'ara matsawa kusa da dady ta kamo hannun sa ckn marai-raicewa tace."dady ka auri Ammy don Allah ita kad'ai ce wacce nk ganin ta dace da kai."

Shiru dady yai tare da lumshe idonsa tabbas tun kfn yarinyar tayi masa wannan maganar shi kansa tun lokacin da yaga rukayya yayi sha'awar auren ta amma ganin cewa gurin kunya ne yasa yabar abun a ransa ba tare da ya fad'a wa kowa ba.

Ganin yayi shiru jikin suhailat yayi sanyi ta d'an k'ara shagwa6ewa tace. "Plss dady."

Muryar sa can k'asa yace."To kin tun-tu6i ita rukayya d'in. "?

"A'a dady na bari ne sai na fara fad'a maka kfn na fad'a mata kuma nasan zata amince insha'allah."

Yayi murmushi. "To shikenan na amince Allah yasa hakan shine mafi alkhairi."

Cike da murna tace. "Ameen dady ngd."

Byn ta koma gida ta samu fahad tayi masa bayanin abinda ta tsara da kuma yanda sukayi da dady, shima yayi farinciki da wannan kyakkyawan tunanin da tayi har mamaki yk yanda akayi shi baiyi wannan tunanin ba.

Nan yayi mata alk'awarin tare dashi za'a je wajen ammy da maganar, saida suka bari da daddare byn sun gama dinner yaran duk sunyi bacci, hira suke d'an ta6awa dukkan su an rasa Wanda zai yi juriyar kawo maganar, fk sai zungurin suhailat yk yi wai ta fara ita kuma nauyin ammy tk ji akan wannan maganar, can dai tayi k'arfin halin tashi taje gaban ammy ta tsugunna shima fk da yaga tayi hakan shima sai yayi yanda tayi, ammy ta d'ago ta kalle su ckn murmushi tace. "Da alama akwai maganar da kk so ku fad'a min ko dai so kk ku tafi gidan ku tunda y'an 3 sunyi 40 ai tun tuni na fahimci take-taken ka shine itama ka hure mata kunne ko."

Fahad yayi dariya kfn yace. "Kai ammy bafa wannan maganar bace dama akwai maganar da muke so muyi dk me muhimmanci."

Ammy tayi dariya. "To ina sauraron ku."

Suhailat ta d'an muskuta don gsky kunyar ammy tk ji amma babu yanda zata yi dole su fad'a mata muddin suna so burin su ya cika, ta d'an saci kallon ammy wacce tayi shiru tana jiran jin abinda zasu fad'a tace."Ammy dama munxo neman wata alfarma ne a wajen ki." sai kuma tayi shiru, ammy tace. "Wai tsoron me kk ji ne ku fad'a min abinda yk zuciyoyin ku."

Fk yai ta maza yace."Ammy so muke kiyi aure saboda shine cikar darajar ko wacce mace duk girman ta."

Tunda ammy taji ya anbaci aure ta had'e ranta ckn 6acin rai tace. "OK saboda an fad'a muku ni bazan iya kare mutunci na ba? To tun kfn kasan kanka nk zaune ba tare da mahaifin ka ba, sai yanxu saboda kayi aure kasan dad'in aure sannan zaka fito ka fad'a min wai nayi aure? To wlh ku kiyaye ni nasan laifin ba naki bane Suhailat duk shine Wanda zai shirya hakan saboda ya gaji da zama dani to idan ma gajiyar kayi ai fitowa zakayi ka fad'a ba wai kace nayi aure ba ku tashi Ku bar min wajen."

Ta k'arasa maganar ckn fad'a suhailat tunda tk bata ta6a ganin ammy na fad'a irin haka ba jikinta har rawa yk, shi kam fahad yasan haka zata iya faruwa tun farko saboda yasha jin ammy tana fad'in ita da aure har abada, maganganun da ta fad'a sunfi komai tayar masa da hankali don haka ya matsa kusa da ita sannan yace. "Don Allah ki janye wad'annan kalaman naki a kaina sunyi min tsauri da yawa ko kad'an babu abinda kk zargi ko tunani ammy, ba don komai muke so kiyi aure ba sai don maslahar kanmu mun sani zaki iya tsare mana kanki har k'arshen rayuwar ki, amma ammy don Allah ki fahimci maganar mu."

Suhailat itama ta matso ta rik'e hannun ammy ckn nutsuwa tace. "Don Allah kiyi hak'uri ammy ki yafe min ban san cewa hakan zai 6ata miki rai ba, wlh babu ruwan sa nice na k'irk'iro wannan maganar amma kiyi hak'uri koda wasa bazan k'ara yi miki makamancin irin wannan maganar ba insha'allah."

Jikin Ammy yayi sanyi koba komai bai kamata tayi musu irin wannan fad'an ba yakamata ace ta fahimtar dasu dalilin ta na k'in yin auren. 

Ta dube su gaba d'aya sun koma kalar tausayi, tace. "Babu komai na hak'ura amma ina so ku sani abinda yasa bana so nayi aure shine nafi so na mutu ban k'ara yin wani auren ba saboda ranar alk'iyama na tashi a matsayin matar mahaifin ka."

Shiru sukayi zuwa can fk yace. "Tabbas mun gamsu da hujjar ki amma dai munso ace kin auri dady koba komai ni zan cika gurbin mahaifi na da na rasa yayinda ita kuma hayaty da y'an uwanta zasu cike gurbin mahaifiyar su."

Suhailat tace. "Ammy saboda wannan dalilin ne kawai yasa muke so ki auri dady amma dai tunda kina da hujjar ki mu kam mun hak'ura da wannan maganar."

Shiru ammy tayi babu shakkah yaran sunyi tunani me kyau to amma idan ta amince shikenan fa burinta ba zai cika ba.

Ajiyar xuciya ta sauke kfn tace."to kun sanar da alhjn ko sai ni da kk raina.''? Tayi maganar fuskar ta babu yabo babu fallasa hakan yasa suka d'an samu k'arfin gwiwar bata amsa fk ne yace."Ai shi muka fara sanar wa kuma yace indai ke kin amince shima bashi da matsala ya amince. "

Shiru ammy tayi tana d'an nazari kfn ckn sanyin jiki tace."sai ku sanar masa na amince,amma da sharad'in kune zaku zauna a wannan gidan saboda bana son a rufe shi. "


"Wow" suka fad'a lokaci d'aya sannan a tare suka rungume ta cike da tsananin farinciki mara misaltuwa, ture su tayi tana fad'in. "Ni ku d'aga ni karku ji min ciwo." Suhailat tace. "Mungode sosae Ammy Allah ya k'ara girma."

Fk yace. "Ameen." Ita dai ammy bata k'ara yi musu magana ba saboda abinda yk ckn xuciyar ta a yanxu tafi son ta ke6ance ita kad'ai don ta samu damar yin nazari akan wannan al'amarin, ta mik'e ta nufi d'akin ta, shima fk cike da jin dad'i yaja matarsa suka wuce part dn sa a cewarsa zai d'an rage zafi ne saboda ammy bata barin shi ya rage zafin, dukda cewa babu abinda ya had'a su amma dai kam ya rage zafi sosae sai wajen k'arfe 2:00am ya rako ta d'akinta saboda kada yaran su tashi me kula dasu ta neme ta, sukayi sallama ya koma d'akin sa.

Byn dogon nazari da ammy tayi ta fahimci yin auren ta da alhj Ibrahim shine cikar k'ima da mutuncin ta sannan koba komai yayyen ta zasu ji dad'in hakan saboda dama sun dad'e suna yi mata mita akan auren gwanda kawai ta yadda.


To ckn sati 2 aka d'aura auren ammy da dady ba wani biki akayi ba tunda su ba yara bane tariya kawai akayi aunty yasmeen y sadeeq har ma da kubra sunji dad'in faruwar hakan suka dinga sanya wa suhailat albarka,saboda fk ya sanar musu itace tayi creating abun, haka yaran suka kai Ammy amarya gidan ango dady kfn ko wacce ta tafi nata gidan.
