Sun zagaye duniya k'asa kusan 6 suka je, rayuwar turai ta kar6e su duk k'asar da suka je kamar kada su dawo don sai sunyi 1 month sannan zasu tafi wata k'asar, sunyi kyau fatarsu ta murje kamar masu rayuwa a ckn k'ank'ara, sabon shafi na soyayya suka bud'e sabo dall ita kanta talatu me kula da yaran tayi kyau abunta balle kuma yaran ckn wata shida sunyi 6ul-6ul dasu tamkar sun shekara ga wayo musamman rukayya wacce ake mata lak'abi da afnan amma arfat da affan sun fi ta girma.

Lokacin da suka je k'asar malesia a nan Suhailat ta had'u da xeexa wacce ta taimaka mata lokacin da aka kaita gidan hjy Sara, sun sha hira nan suhailat ta bata labarin yanda ta had'u da dangin ta byn rabuwarsu itama ta bata nata labarin yanxu haka ma tayi aure kuma tare da mijin suka xo harda yaron su ammar.

Saida suka shafe tsawon 6 months kfn su dawo gida, lokacin da suka dawo xuwa aka dinga yi ganin yanda y'an 3 suka koma tamkar y'ay'an larabawa rarrafen su suke ga wani kyau da suka k'ara kamar ka sace su ka gudu.

A gidan dady suka sauka Wanda a halin yanxu ya koma gidan ammy, d'aki guda aka basu kfn a gyara musu gidan ammy da zasu koma kwangila fk ya bayar ta gyaran gidan gaba d'aya aka rushe shi akayi masa sabon design na y'an gayu, kasancewar gidan babban guri ne, suhailat ita kad'ai saida ta tashi da 3 Parlour's 2 bedroom komai sabo aka zuba ko wanne da irin color d'in sa, hatta yaran saida akayi musu d'akin su arfat da affan k'aton parlour da d'akunan bacci 2 an zuba musu duk wani abun da zasu buk'ata tun daga kan kayan wasa da duk abinda zai d'ebe musu kewa haka d'akin afnan an k'awata mata shi da kayan makeup tamkar wata budurwa ba Teddy's masu kyau da duk abinda d'iya mace zata iya buk'ata har zuwa girmanta, lokacin da suhailat taga yanda aka kashe mak'udan kud'i a d'akunan yaran ita dariya ma abun ya bata ta kalli fk tace. "Haba nafsy yaran da ko tafiya basu fara ba amma kayi musu irin wannan tanadin."?
   Harara ya watsa mata sannan yace." To idan suka girma kuma fa? Ko so kk yara na su dinga ganin abubuwa a gidajen wasu alhalin kuma mahaifin su yana da halin da zai iya yi musu komai. "
"A'a ni dai bance haka ba nafsy mungode Allah y k'ara arziki amma ni da cewa nayi a bari su d'an girma kfn mu raba d'aki dasu."

Ta k'arasa maganar ckn kwantar da murya, murmushi yayi kfn yace. "To ke waye ya fad'a miki yanxun zasu dawo, ni kaina ba xan iya bacci ba tare dasu ba a halin yanxu shi yasa duk d'akunan gidannan na sanya bed's d'in su kinga duk inda muke muna tare da y'ay'an mu." Tayi murmushi yace. "Kinga haka zamu cigaba da kwana dasu har sai sunyi wayo  sannan mu sallame su su koma dakunan su kfn lokacin kuma kin haifa musu y'an kannen su." Ya k'arasa maganar cike da zolaya zaro ido tayi sannan tace. "Ka rufa min asiri nafsy haihuwar fa da wahala da kk kawo min ita a kusa" "karki damu addu'a xan dinga yi miki Allah y baki me sauk'i." Tace. "To ameen kuma masu albarka ba."


To zaman dady da ammy babu wani matsala a ckn sa zama ake na girmama juna da faranta ran juna lallai dady ya zamo d'an gata a wajen ammy babu abinda ta rage shi dashi ta cike masa gurbin Khadija da ya rasa, haka shima ya maye mata gurbin baban fk Wanda  a baya tk ganin baxata ta6a samun kamar sa ba wannan dalilin ne yasa tak'i yadda ta k'ara yin wani auren sai yanxu ta fahimci ashe tauye kanta tayi shi yasa tk yawan sanya wa suhailat da fahad albarka don ba don sun matsa ba da bata auri dady ba.

Fahad ya maida suhailat makaranta inda ta cigaba da karatun ta, da yk itama science tk karanta hakan yasa fk ya dage yana koya mata abubuwan da bata gane ba duk abinda akayi musu zai zaunar da ita ya koya mata in practically yana d'aukar ta suje asibiti don taga yanda ake abubuwa, hakan yasa ta samu wata irin gogewa ta musamman nan da nan tayi suna a department d'in su kowa so yk ya dinga huld'a da ita yayinda ita kuma bata bada fuskar hakan ba, ko k'awa ba tada ita yaran ta sune k'awayen ta don tare dasu tk zuwa idan zata shiga lecture sai ta barsu wajen talatu me kula dasu.

To haka dai rayuwa ta cigaba da garawa yau da gobe babu wuya a wajen ubangiji, yanxu haka suhailat ta kammala karatun ta tayi degree 1&2 a 6angaren gerny haka ta fito da kyakkyawan results a lokacin ne kuma ta fara laulayin wani cikin tunda ta fahimci ciki ne da ita hankalin ta ya tashi shekarar yaran ta 5 amma gashi har ta samu wani cikin koda yk wasu ma tun kfn d'an ya shekara ake samo wani cikin, ba wai bata son y'ay'a bane amma ita a ganin ta kamar sunyi k'anana ayi musu k'ani, dukda cewa yaran sanyi wani irin girma na ban mamaki tamkar ba su uku aka haifa lokaci d'aya ba, ga 6arna duk inda suka shiga da 6arnar da zasu yi musamman arfat da affan ita kam afnan tafi k'warewa wajen shegen surutu da wayon tsiya, haka fk ya zauna ya dinga lallashinta tare da kawo mata misalai iri-iri, har saida ta fahimci tara y'ay'an ma wata baiwa ce ta musamman Wanda ba kowa Allah yk yi mishi irin ta ba.

 Sannan ya bud'e mata wani katafaren private hospital a jikin gidansa Wanda ya sanya masa suna SUHAIFAD HOSPITAL ya zuba mata k'wararrun doctors wad'anda suka san aikin su, yayi hakan ne don ya faranta mata sannan kuma shi kansa yace bazai iya barin ta tana yawon zuwa aiki wani asibitin ba, shi yasa ya bud'e mata wannan yayi mata k'ofa ta ckn gida.

To haka dai ta cigaba da rainon cikin ta har zuwa lokacin haihuwa ta haifi k'atuwar d'iyar ta kyakkyawar gsk fk ya dinga murna kamar ba'a ta6a yi masa haihuwa ba ya sanya wa yarinyar Khadija suna kiranta afra, afnan fa murna ba'a magana ta samu y'ar uwa haka suma affan da arfat dukda cewa su sunce "basu yadda ba sai ummyn su ta siyo musu namiji" fahad ya dube su ckn fara'a kfn yace. "Karku damu yara na ummyn ku me k'ok'ari ce nasan xata siyo muku maza masu yawa, ku dai ku cigaba da yi mata addu'a Allah y k'ara mata lpy." Gaba d'aya suka amsa da ameen.
Affan yace. "Abiy kaima zaka siyo mana."? Afnan sarkin wayo ta fashe da dariya, " yaro abiy namiji ne mata ne suke siyo y'ay'a. " ckn mamaki suka kalli yarinyar, "yarinya abiy yafi ummy kud'i shima zai iya siyo mana maza."
Arfat ya fad'a yana harar afnan, suhailat tace. "To ya isa kowa yayi mana shiru, dani da abiy babu Wanda ya isa ya siyo y'ay'a Allah ne kad'ai me iya bawa mutum y'ay'a kyauta ba tare da ya siya ba, Ku dai addu'a zaku dinga yi mana Allah y k'ara mana lpyr d'awainiya daku." Yaran suka amsa da ameen.



Kai jama'a duniya kenan haka tk juya wa yau da dad'i gobe babu, sannan ita mutuwa bata nuna wa mutum alamar ta kusa zuwa gareshi sai dai kawai mutum ya tsinci kansa ckn kabari idan ya aikata alkhairi yaga abinsa haka ma idan sharri ka aikata Allah dai yasa mu dace ameen, to kamar yanda na fad'a haka ce ta kasance da momy domin kuwa bokanta boka me garkuwa mahaifin isah shine ya turo mata da wani mugun asiri Wanda yai sanadiyyar mutuwar ta, saboda ya d'ora alhakin mutuwar d'an sa a kanta shi yasa ya d'auki fansa a kanta tayi mutuwa irin ta wulak'anci su Kansu asibitin da suke kula da ita sai gawar ta suka tsinta har ta fara kumbura ba'a San yanda aka yi ta mutu ba, haka aka kira sadeeq aka sanar dashi wannan mummunan labari, sunsha kuka lokacin da suka ga gawar mahaifiyar su, Da k'yar dady ya amince aka shigar masa gida da ita, sai kuma aka rasa me yi mata wanka saboda aunty yasmeen da kubra duk tsohon ciki ne dasu itama husna haka, daga k'arshe dai Ammy da suhailat ne sukayi mata wankan da taimakon aunty yasmeen, aka sallace ta aka mik'a ta gidan ta na gsky, Allah sarki duniyar kenan, Yasmeen da y'an uwanta sun jajantawa kansu irin wannan mutuwar da mahaifiyar su tayi sai dai zasu cigaba da rok'a mata sauk'i a wajen Allah.



Watan momy 1 da rasuwa yasmeen ta haihu y'an 2 duk maza itama sunan dady ta sa musu d'aya ana kiransa Khalil d'ayan mu'azzam , ko 40 batayi da haihuwa ba itama kubra ta sankato nata d'an al'ameen ita kuma tsakanin su da husna kwana 8 ita kam d'iya mace ta haifa y sadeeq ya sanya mata sunan momy suna kiranta muwadda.



5 years letter
Lokaci ya tafi rayuwa ta fara nisa, suhailat an zama iyaye yara don yanxu yaranta 6 3 maza 3 mata, arfat, affan, afanan, afra sai kuma wad'anda tk goyo muhseen da haseenah y'an 2 mace da namiji, rayuwar gidan ta abun sha'awa ta koya yaranta girmama na gaba dasu a tsakanin su ma baxaka ta6a ganin k'arami yana yiwa babba rashin kunya ba, dama dai y'an 3 sun d'an fi rikici to suma dai babu raini a tsakanin su.

Tsakanin ta da mijinta xama ake irin na fahimtar juna soyayyar da son faranta ran juna, uwa uba da hak'uri da juna, sun gama fahimtar junan su hakan tasa kowanne yk gudun abinda zai 6ata ran d'an uwansa kfn su samu sa6ani a tsakanin su suna dad'e wa idan kuma sun samu sun san hanyar da zasu gyara matsalar su.
Yasmeen itama yaranta 5 uku maza 2 mata baby sahla an zama y'an mata tayo gadon son kwalliya a wajen yasmeen koda yaushe ka ganta tsaf da ita ckn kwalliya, kubra kam yaranta 4 2 maza 2mata haka shima y sadeeq.

To shima dady ba'a barshi a baya ba wajen samun haihuwa domin kuwa ammy ta zankad'o masa yaransa guda 2 maza umar da usman.

Suhaifad hospital ya samu d'aukaka daga state-state ake kawo musu patient, suhailat ta zama babbar doctor don ta halarci makarantu kala-kala don k'ara gogewa a 6angaren da ya shafi mata gerny doctor, sannan dukkan ma'aikatan ta k'wararru ne,shi yasa suhaifad hospital yayi suna wajen iya kula da patients.

Wata rana suhailat tana gida don yau batayi niyyar fita asibiti ba sakamakon bata jin dadin jikinta kuma tayi test ta fahimci tana da shigar ciki na 2 weeks mamaki da al'ajabi gami da kasala duk suka lulle6e ta a lokaci 1 bata ta6a kawo hakan a ranta ba, duka fa watan su haseenah 8 amma gashi ta k'ara samun wani cikin, su arfat saida suka shekara 5 ta haifi afra, ita kanta afra saida ta shekara 4 kfn ta haifi su muhseen amma gashi yanxu ckn 8 months ta sake d'aukar wani cikin!

Kwanciya tayi tana juya abun a ranta, wayar ta tayi kara alamar kira, ckn kasala ta mik'a hannun ta tare da d'auko wayar ta kara a kunne tun kfn tayi sallama taji manager din ta, ta suhaifad hospital tana cewa." Hello ranki ya dad'e don Allah idan babu damuwa muna buk'atar ki yanxu a asibiti." Ta d'an yatsuna fuska tasani indai ba wata matsalar da tafi k'arfin su ba  basa kiranta muddin bata shiga ba don haka ta d'an yamutsa fuska kfn tace."menene ya faru manager."?
"Wata patient ce aka kawo duk mun kasa kula da ita saboda halin da take ciki is too much." Suhailat ta sauke ajiyar xuciya kfn tace."OK ku fara bata emergency care kfn na shigo nima jikina ne babu dad'i."
"Yes ma Allah ya kara miki lpy, muna gdy."
Ta amsa da ameen kfn ta ajiye wayar ta shiga bathroom ta d'an watsa ruwa zuwa yanxu ta fahimce baiwar yawan haihuwa Allah yayi musu ita da mijin ta don haka ta saki ranta tana me godiya ga Allah da wannan baiwar da yayi musu don wasu suna neman koda d'a d'aya ne amma Allah bai basu ba.
 Ta shirya tsaf ckn kamala da haiba gami da kwarjini ta gyara yaranta sunyi kyau abinsu kasancewar sana da k'iba y'an ku6i6i dasu, ta sa6a yaran ta a kafad'a ta fito talatu me aiki ta tarar sai faman gyare-gyare tk matar akwai tsafta shi yasa ta samu gurbin zama a gidan doctors.

Suhalat ta dube ta byn ta amsa gaisuwar ta tace."talatu ki dafa muku abinci zan shiga hospital nasan yanxu zasu dawo." Talatu ta amsa "to ranki ya dad'e amma banda ku ."?
Dariya tayi kfn tace." Talatu kenan amma kinsan dai bake kk yi min girki ba yau ma don ina da uxuri ne yasa nace kiyi muku amma koda yake zaki iya yi har da ni amma banda nafsy shi sai na dawo zanyi masa nasa."
"To ranki ya dad'e."

Byn ta isa aaibitin ta samu dukkan manyan doctors d'in sun taru akan wata mata wacce tk ta faman fixge-fixge kwata-kwata bata ckn haiyacin ta, ga wani wari da yk fita a gurin tayi saurin sanya facemask d'in ta kamar yanda dukkan su kowanne da nasa mak'ale a hancin sa, suna ganinta suka bata hanya ta k'araso a hankali byn ta d'aga  musu hannu sakamakon gaida ta da suke.

Kai tsaye ta wuce office din ta ta ajiy yaran ta a gadajen su saboda sunyi bacci, sannan ta fito ta dawo wajenda patient din tk.
 Nan take ta bada umarnin a bata allurar bacci aikuwa ana yiwa matar tayi baccin ckn y'an sakanni, sai a lokacin suma doctors d'in suka ga fa idar yin hakan don za'a samu damar yin checking d'in ta yanda yakamata, nan ta fara aikinta tare da taimakon sauran doctors d'in, abinda ya d'aure mata kai shine tana ganin matar sai taga kamar ta ta6a ganin amma dai ta manta inda ta santa, haka dai suka cigaba da checking d'in matar, sun gano matar y'ar lesbian ce hakan ce ta janyo mata matsalar lalacewar gaba tk fitar da wani irin ruwa me mugun wari harda tsutsotsi ne suke fita daga wajen.

Suhailat tayi shiru ckn tsurawa matar ido byn ta gama gane wacece, hjy sara ce wacce momy ta kaita gidanta da niyyar an siyar mata da ita, hawaye ne suka xubo mata tsron Allah ya kara d'arsuwa a zuciyarta yanxu dubi hjy Sara mace mai k'iba dubi yanda ta koma mace me ji da kud'i da soldiers yau da kud'in da sojojin sun kasa tsare ta daga wannan cutar kuma basu isa sun bata lpy ba, kai jama'a mutane muji tsoron ubangiji.

Lokacin da hjy Sara ta farfad'o ta d'an samu sauk'i sai dai har yanxu tana zubar da wannan ruwan me tsananin wari da k'arni, ta juya takalli gefen daman ta.

Wata kyakkyawar mata tagani hannun ta rik'e da sirinji da alama ma'aikaciya ce,
Suhailat ta tako a hankali ta k'arasa wajen ta ganin ta farfad'o kuma da alama ckn haiyacinta tk, saida ta k'are mata kallo tsawon mintuna 2 kfn ta iya yi mata magana ganin hjy Sara na yunk'urin tashi tace."hjy sara ki kwanta mana saboda kina buk'atar ku

Hjy sara wacce a yanxu ta koma tamkar wata k'warangwal tsabar rama ta k'ure suhailat da ido jin ta ambaci sunan tsanki baya Wanda ita da a iya sanin ta yau kusan shekara 2 kenan rabon da taji an kira ta da wannan sunan.

A hankali take motsa bakinta Wanda ya haifar da wani had'ed'd'en wari a wajen, ba don suhailat na sanye da safar hanci ba da tuni warin ya cutar da ita, ckn muryar tausayi tace." Yarinya a ina kk sanni naji kin ambaci suna na Wanda a halin yanxu ban ta6a tunanin zan iya had'uwa da Wanda yasan shi ba."

Murmushin takaici suhailat tayi don bata son tuna abinda ya had'a ta da hjy sara tace."nasani ke d'in ba mutuniyar kirki bace, kin cutar da yara da yawa kin gur6ata rayuwar su, shi yasa nk jin k'arin tsanarki na ninkuwa a zuciya ta fiye da lokacin da na sanki." Ckn tsananin damuwa tare da rad'ad'in xuciya hjy Sara ta dubi suhailat tace."Tabbas abinda kk fad'a gsky ne ni d'in ba mutuniyar kirki bace nasan bazan iya tuna yara maza da mata wad'anda na lalata rayuwar su ba nayi nadama kaico!.."

Sara ta k'arasa maganar ckn matsanancin kuka, wani abu me kama da tausayi suhailat taji a zuciyar ta dukda tasan hjy Sara itace ta janyo wa kanta amma da yk xuciyar ta me tausayi ce, take itama ta fara zubar da hawaye.

Hjy Sara ta cigaba da cewa." Ni ba abar tausayi bace saboda na sa6awa mahalicci na kuma na kasa tuba har saida lokacin tuban ya wuce min, gashi yanxu ina yin nadama mara amfani, a lokacin da bani da kowa sannan bani da komai wlh nasan Allah bazai yafe min ba saboda na cutar da bayin sa wad'anda ni kaina ban san yawan su ba, duk lokacin da aka kawo min yarinya bazan ta6a barinta ba har sai naga na lalata rayuwar ta na koya mata abinda nk yi, wata yarinya ce guda 1 da matar babanta ta kawo min ita itace kawai ta tsallake tarko na kuma tunda ga wannan lokacin ban k'ara samun nutsuwa ba."

Suhailat ta dubi Sara ckn taahin ha nkali kfn tace." Ba kowa bace wannan yarinyar face ni nice aka siyar miki dani don ki 6ata min rayuwa amma Allah ya tsare ni daga sharrin ki.

Hjy Sara ta dubi suhailat da sauri tabbas wannan beauty ce, kyau kwarjini da kamalar ta duk suna nan sai ma k'aruwa da sukayi, da farko ma zafin ciwo da  zrikicewa yasa ta kasa gane ta, da k'arfi tace beauty dama kina duniyar nan? Na dad'e ina neman ki gari-gari ammananban ganki ba, na kama wacce ta kawo min ke na azabtar da ita amma itama tace min bata san inda kk ba, wlh beauty tun lokacin da na hadu dk kuma kk kufce min ban k'ara samun nutsuwa ba, duk wacce na kusanta bana samun nutsuwa daga nan kuma cututtuka suka yi caa a kaina daga wannan sai wannan tin yara da sojoji na suna kula dani har suka gaji rana 1 suka had'a bani suka kwashe min duk abinda na mallaka suka gudu suka barni ba lpy ba dukiya haka na siyar da kangon gida na Wanda suka kwashe min duk abinda yk ciki hatta glasses Capet babu abinda suka bar min, to dama kin san gidan babba ne, byn na saida shi na siyi k'aramin gida sauran kud'in na cigaba da neman lpy ta, to yanxu dai bani da komai don kuwa komai ya k'are hatta gidan da na siya na saida shi, bara nk yi a bakin titi mutane na zagi na tare da hantara sakamakon wari da nk k'asa na har tsutsa yk fitar wa kuma nasan hakan bazai rasa nasaba da yawan amfani da nk yi dashi ba ta kowanne hali."

Ta k'arasa maganar tare da rushewa da wani matsanancin kuka ckn kukan ta cigaba da cewa." Na cuci kaina nasani wannan masifar da nk ciki kad'an ce daga azabar da zan fuskanta a wajen ubangiji,duk wani aikin sa6o da Allah ya hana na aikata shi, beauty nayi xina, nayi mad'igo, nayi luwad'i nabi bokaye, na cutar da rayuwar yara mata da maza,na sanya bak'inciki me yawa a zuk'atan iyaye da dama, shin wanne laifi ne ubangiji zai yafe min. "?? Ta fad'a ckn matsanancin tashin hankali tabbas hjy Sara tayi nadamar abinda ta dad'e tana aikatawa, a lokacin da nadama bata da amfani a wajenta.

Suhailat ta gyara tsaiwar ta tausayin hjy Sara ya fara shiga ckn zuciyar ta dukda cewa tsanar ta ta k'ara ninkuwa a zuciyar ta musamman da taji mugayen abubuwan da tk aikatawa, ita kanta hawaye tk saboda duk laifin da hjy Sara ke aikatawa babu k'arami a ciki balle a sanya ran zata samu sassauci, ckn sanyin murya tace.''tabbas hjy kin sa6awa Allah fiye da tunani na kuma kin aikata manya-manyan kaba'ira musamman shirka da kuma hakkin mutane da kk d'auka kisani Allah baya yafewa me shirka sannan baya yafe laifin da aka shiga hak'k'in wani amma dukda haka ki dage da yawaita istigifari babu Wani bawa da zai shiga tsakanin Allah da bawansa wata k'ila ya yafe miki,amma fa banda shirka.

Hjy Sara ta fara istigifari ckn matsanancin kuka tace."ko wannan larura da ta same ni nasani alhakin jama'a ne.

Suhailat tace ki dage da neman gafarar ubangiji wata k'ila ki samu sassauci,sannan wannan matsalar taki nayi bincike akan2i a wanke gurin saboda tsu-tso-tsi sun ci jikinki sosai, don haka ki shirya zuwa gobe za'a yi miki aiki." Ckn matsanancin kuka hjy Sara take yiwa suhailat gdy saboda taimakon da tayi mata.sdsre

Aranar aka gama duk cike-ciken da za'a yi aka sanya sunan ta a ckn jerin wadanda za'a yiwa aiki gobe saida ta bata magunguna wadanda zasu rage mata rad'ad'in ciwo sannan tayi mata sallama ta shiga gida.

Kai tsaye tana shiga kicin ta nufa byn ta watsa ruwa tayiwa fk had'ad'd'en girki dukda ta gaji amma hakan be hana ta tsayawa ta cancara kwalliya ba byn ta gama koerremai,sai a lokacin ta samu damar zama tare da yaranta suna da kyakkyawar fahimta dasu don wani lokacin zama tk su dinga wasannin su tare suyi karatu tare da hirar abinda ya dame su, wani lokacin dasu tk shawara, haka shima fk yana zama tare da iyalin sa suyi hira, shi yasa yaran basa 6oyewa iyayen nasu komai.

Suna zaune suna hirar su, affan yace." ummi yau ina kk mak'ale ne mun dawo bakya nan gidan duk babu dadi." Arfat yace."ummi rabu dashi ko damuwa fa baiyi ba wasansu sukeyii shi da afnan, ni da afra ne kawai muka damu da rashin ki." Murmushi suhailat tayi kfn tace" to naji yanxu dai ba gashi na dawo ba ai sai ku bani labarin makaranta." Nan suka shiga bata labarin wani tayi dariya wani kuma ta yi musu fad'a da nasiha, suna ckn haka suka ji tsayawar motar abban su, ckn ya'r rige-rige suka nufi waje kamar yanda suka saba duk Wanda ya riga zuwa gare shi, shi za'a d'aga sama koda suhailat ce don da ita ake rige-rigen, yau kam a hankali take takawa saboda yanayin yanda tk jin jikinta, fk a tsaye byn ya fito daga mota ya ware hannunsa yana jiran isowar yaransa, affan darfat ne suka riga zuwa ya had'a su ya rungume, afnan ta ja hannun afra cike da jin haushi suka juya zasu koma ckn sauri ya k'araso gare su yana fad'in."haba my beauty's ina kuma zaku je byn ga abban ku ya dawo gareku." Afnan ta turo baki kfn tace."abiy kullum su affan gudu sukeyi suna riga mu zuwa wajen ka ni na daina ma fitowa idan kadawo." Ta k'arasa maganar tare da matso k'wallah, fk ya janyo su jikinsa yana goge mata hawaye."haba sarauniya ta meye kuma abun kuka duba kiga my soldiers zasuyi miki dariya." Ya fad'a yana nuna mata su affan wadanda suka fara yi mata dariya, harara ta watsa musu tana gyara fuskarta, fk yace."yauwa ko ke fa sarauniya ta, kinga ginbiya ta ita bata kuka"  ya fad'a yana kallon afra wacce itama idonta tk faman murxawa a dole sai ta k'wak'ilo kuka, ganin idan ya biye musu zasu 6ata mishi lokaci ya kama hannun afnan rare da d'aukar afra ya juya ya kalli su affan wadanda suka tsaya a bayansa yana fad'in. "Ku taho muje wajen ummi naga kamar ba ta jin dad'i ko laifi kk yi mata ne."? A tare suka ce." bamuyi mata komai ba wlh abiy."

A haka suka k'arasa wajen suhailat wacce ta tsaya tana kallon su tana murmushi a gabanta ya dire afra tare da kamo hannun ta yana fad'in. "Ranki y dad'e hayaty yau lpy ba ki taro nafsyn ki ba."? Ya fad'a yana aika mata da wani irin kallo, k'asa tayi da kanta ckn k'ok'arin danne abinda yk ranta tace." Haba nafsy kazo mu shiga ka huta mana amma dubi yanda ka 6ata lokacin ka a ajen yaran nan." 

Baiyi musu ba yayi gaba suna bayan sa ya fahimci akwai abinda yk ckn zuciyar ta, kai tsaye d'akinsa suka nufa ita dashi yaran kam a parlor suka yi zaman su suna hirar su.

Byn ta taimaka masa yayi wanka ta d'auko masa kayansa marasa nauyi yasanya sannan suka sauko daga saman already afnan ta gama shirya wa abiy table suhailat ta koya mata duk yanda zata yi don yanxu har kichin tare suke shiga idan babu makaranta.

Byn sun gama cin abinci fk ya fara tanbayar yaransa matsalolin su kamar yanda ya saba haka yakeyi musu kullum yana zama da iyalinsa yaji abinda suke buk'ata, saida ya gama da yaran ya dubi suhailat yace."to ummy saura ke naga yau duk babu wal-wala a tare dake." Bata bashi amsa ba ta kalli yaran kfn tace maza aje ayi homework duk suka tashi suka nufi dakin su.

Fk duk ya matsu yaji abinda yk damunta kfn tayi magana yace."wai menene matsalar ne ina ta tanbayar ki baki fad'a min ba. "?

Ajiyar xuciya ta sauke kfn tace."Wasu abubuwa 2 ne suka faru dani yau na farko abun farinciki ne na 2 kuma abin mamaki da tausayi."

"To a bani nasha." Ya fad'a tare da kwantar da kansa a cinyar ta, a hankali tk shafa kwantaccen gashin kansa Wanda yasha gyara,ckn sanyin murya ta bashi labarin had'uwar su da hjy Sara da duk abinda ya faru har aikin da ta shirya yi mata gobe, shima yayi mamaki sosae sannan ya jinjina wa matarsa me rama alkhairi da muguntar da aka yi mata kuma ya bata k'warin gwiwa akan haka.

Suhailat taji dadin samun goyon bayan da tayi daga wajen tauraronta don haka ta saki ranta sosae,
3dariya kfn tace." To da ka fad'a musu rama maka zasuyi ne."? Yace. "Eh mana zan fad'a musu idan suna shan laban su cije min ke kinga ai sun rama min."

Ta6e baki tayi ckn dariya tace." To ai ni daga yau ma na yaye su." Ckn sauri ya gintse fuskar sa kfn yace. "Saboda me zaki yaye su yanxu."? Cike da tuhuma yk maganar, ta rausayar da kai." Because I'm pregnant 1 week." Baki bud'e yk kallon ta cike da mamaki kfn ya samu damar cewa."are you sure.'' Gira ta d'aga mishi."yes." Ckn tsananin farinciki ya ajeye yaran tare da rungume ta yana dariya.
Ture shi tayi ckn dariya tana fad'in." Kai nafsy ka manta a parlour muke koda yaushe yaran nan zasu iya fitowa ko talatu.'' Sai lokacin ya tuna da hakan ya cika ta yana k'ok'arin tashi "taso muje na baki goron albishir." Ya fad'a byn ya d'auki haseenah bata da za6i byn ta d'auki muhseen ta bishi sama.

Suna zuwa da kansa ya had'a wa yaran abinda zasu sha ya zuba musu a fidojin su ya mik'a mata na muhseen shi kuma yana bawa haseenah nata, saida yaran suka k'oshi ta watsa musu ruwa take suka koma bacci ta kwantar dasu a gadon su da yk d'akin sannan ta sauka wajen yaran ta dakin su affan ta shiga arfat yayi bacci affan ne ke ta faman rubutu, "yakamata ka ajiye aikin nan haka affan da safe sai kayi kada ka makara da asuba." Babu musu ko jayayya tsakanin ta da y'ay'an ta don haka ya ajiye byn yayi addu'a ya kwanta haka ta k'ara tofe su da addu'a kfn taja musu k'ofar ta nufi dakin sunyi bacci su kam addu'a kawai tayi musu tajanyo musu k'ofa.

Saida ta watsa ruwa ta sanya kayanta na bacci sannan ta nufi d'akin oga, Wanda shima yana can ita kad'ai yk jira, yana jin motsinta ya taho ya tarbe ta ya rungume ta tsam a jikinsa yana shinshina wuyanta Wanda yk fitar da sassanyan k'amshi me shiga xuciya.

A haka suka k'arasa cikin d'akin fk ya dubi suhailat ckn tsananin shauk'i da k'auna yace. "Ke y'ar baiwa ce ta musamman kullum kyau da k'uruciya kk k'ara wa dukda cewa kin haifi yara amma sam kyau da k'uruciyar suna nan yanxu haka mun kusa shekara 10 da aure amma kullum jinki nk tamkar yau aka kawo min ke I love you."

Suhailat ta shafa kwantaccen gashin da ya zagaye kyakkyawar fuskar sa cike da salon soyayyar wacce tk tfyr masa da nutsuwa tace. "Ina alfahari dakai miji na uban y'ay'ana tabbas Allah kad'ai zamu godewa da ya mallaka mana junan mu kuma yasa bama k'osawa da juna."

To da haka dai suka fad'a duniyar da babu kamar ta wajen kwatance.


Tunda asubah wayar suhailat tk k'ara Wanda k'arar ce ta tashe su, da sauri ta d'aga ganin cewa daga suhaifad hospital ne."ranki ya dad'e Allah yayiwa wannan patient din rasuwa wacce kk sanya sunan ta za'a yi mata aiki yau." Manager ta fad'a ba tare da ta tsaya gaida shugabar tata ba saboda tana ckn matsanancin tashin hankali, "innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji'un." Abinda tk ta fad'a kenan tsoron Allah yana k'ara huda zuciyar ta lallai mutum ba a bakin komai yk  ba Allah yasa mu dace.

Shi kansa fahad yaji mutuwar don babu dad'i musamman da ta mutu a hannun su gashi babu wani d'an uwan ta da xasu nema.

Haka dai aka yi mata sutura aka mik'a ta gidan ta na gsky Allah sarki duk me rai sai ya d'an-d'ani d'acin mutuwa Allah ya bamu ikon cikawa da imani ameen.

10 years latter
Ckn wadannan shekara anyi abubuwa masu yawa masu dad'i da aka sinsu, yanxu haka suhailat yaran ta 12 6mata 6maza ckn amma haihuwar ta 6  ce yawancin yaran y'an 2 ne, anyiwa afnan aure haka arfat da affan duk sun nema ckn yaran yasmeen suka aura yayinda muhseen ya gano y'ar gidan y sadiq muhibba itama haseenah khalid d'an kubra ne yk so za'a had'a ayi gaba d'aya, ammy da dady tsufa ya fara kama su amma dai basu da matsalar komai koda yaushe ckn sanyawa yarnsu da jikokin su albarka suke kakar suhailat inna ta Adamawa ta rasu yayin da adam k'anin suhailat ya dawo wajen dady anan ya samu wata yarinya me kirki yai aurensa yanxu haka yaransa 3.

Suhailat kam babu abinda zata yi sai gdy wajen Allah ya bata miji na gari ga y'ay'a na gari ga iyayn ta da y'an uwa kowa k'aunar ta yk yi ita kam duk lokacin da ta tuna da tayi sallah tana rok'awa momy gafara a wajen Allah kamar yanda tk yiwa mahaifiyarta, har gidan yari taje ta fanshi kaltume byn ta bata kud'i masu yawa ita kam kaltume duk ta tsufa a gidan yari haka ta tattara ta tafi garinsu dukda ta samu labarin rasuwar mahaifiyarta.....

Ummu Mashkur

Alhamdulillah

Ina gdy ga Allah da y bani ikon kammala suhailat daga farko har k'arshe kuskuren dake ciki Allah ya yafe min abinda na fad'a na alkhairi Allah y bamu lada dani da duk Wanda ya karanta.

Gdy me yawa gare Ku masoya na musamman y'an ummu Mashkur novels.

Jinjina gareku

Shafa'atu: kema kin bada gudummawa musamman wajen comments.
Missy:ina gdy kin bani k'warin gwiwa
My Meelat kanwaty: hmm ke kam kinyi kokari wajen Bibiyar book din daki-daki
Wayyo kuna fa da yawa bazaku lissafu ba duk wacce bata ji sunan ta ba tayi min afuwa, sannan duk wacce ta karanta littafin daga farko har k'arshe itama na jinjina mata.

Mu had'a a sabon littafi.

Ummu Mashkur CE
